### [1] [Finite generation of relative log canonical algebras of semi-dlt pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36018)
*Alberto Landi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明半dlt对$(X,\Delta_X)$的相对对数典范代数是有限生成的（即存在相对稳定模型），给出了一个绕开Kollár粘合理论的直接证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对稳定模型的存在性是稳定对模空间理论的核心问题。已有方法依赖Kollár的粘合理论，本文希望给出不依赖该工具的直接证明，并借此为Kollár–Shepherd-Barron–Alexeev稳定对模空间紧致性的简洁证明奠定基础。

Method: 采用直接的代数几何论证（而非Kollár粘合理论）。在假设：正规化在$T$上存在对数典范模型、$(X,\Delta_X)$在稠密开子概形$T^0\subset T$上存在稳定模型且其例外迹不包含导子的任何层（stratum）、以及包含于导子中的对数中心像与$T^0$相交的条件下，证明相对对数典范代数的有限生成性。

Result: 证明了半dlt对的相对对数典范代数有限生成，等价于相对稳定模型存在；由此重新得到Hacon–Xu与Birkar结果的demi-normal版本，并导出slc对的若干MMP步骤的存在性，重现Ambro与Kollár的结果。

Conclusion: 给出了不依赖Kollár粘合理论的相对稳定模型存在性直接证明，推广并统一了若干已有结果，并为Kollár–Shepherd-Barron–Alexeev稳定对模空间紧致性的简化证明奠定基础（后续工作中完成）。

Abstract: We prove that the relative log canonical algebra of a semi-dlt pair $(X,Δ_X)$ projective over a scheme $T$ is finitely generated, equivalently, that $(X,Δ_X)$ admits a relative stable model, provided that the normalization admits a log canonical model over $T$, that $(X,Δ_X)$ admits a stable model over an open dense subscheme $T^0\subset T$ whose exceptional locus does not contain any stratum of the conductor, and that the log centers contained in the conductor have images meeting $T^0$. Our main contribution is a direct proof that avoids Kollár's gluing theory entirely.
  As a consequence, we recover demi-normal versions of results of Hacon--Xu and Birkar. Furthermore, we derive the existence of certain MMP steps for slc pairs, recovering results of Ambro and Kollár. Finally, these results lay the groundwork for a streamlined proof of the properness of the Kollár--Shepherd-Barron--Alexeev moduli space of stable pairs, to be completed in forthcoming work.

</details>


### [2] [Deep Points of Cluster Algebras II: Punctured Marked Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38068)
*James Beyer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对带边界的带孔标记曲面的丛代数丛簇的deep points进行了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丛簇的deep points刻画了丛簇理论中不满足某些良好性质的特殊点，其在丛代数几何中具有重要意义。本文旨在针对带边界的带孔标记曲面这一重要曲面类型，对其丛簇的deep points给出完整刻画。

Method: 方法上，利用带边界的带孔标记曲面的组合拓扑与丛代数理论的对应关系，通过曲面的标记点、孔洞及边界结构来刻画丛簇中deep points的分布与特性，从而完成分类。

Result: 给出了带边界的带孔标记曲面的丛代数丛簇中deep points的分类结果。

Conclusion: 该分类结果为理解带孔标记曲面丛簇的几何结构提供了新的刻画，有助于进一步研究丛簇理论中的相关性质。

Abstract: We classify the deep points of cluster varieties of cluster algebras of punctured marked surfaces with boundary.

</details>


### [3] [Global Sections and Birational Geometry of Calabi-Yau Type Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36034)
*Andrei Constantin,Andre Lukas,Elijah Sheridan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文研究klt Calabi-Yau型簇上除子整体截面与双有理几何的关系，证明整体截面由控制D-MMP的双有理数据支配，并给出大锥上的分段拟多项式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典基于正性的方法（如Kawamata-Viehweg消没定理）主要适用于可动锥或nef除子，无法系统处理任意大除子的整体截面。作者希望将整体截面的计算推广到大锥上，并揭示其与D-MMP双有理数据的深层联系。

Method: 对klt Calabi-Yau型簇X，利用控制D-MMP的双有理数据来支配整体截面；通过消没定理，将任意大除子的整体截面系统地约化为合适双有理模型Y上的Euler特征χ(Y,O_Y(D))；约化过程通过直接减去或双有理收缩去掉不贡献整体截面的固定除子分量来实现。

Result: 得到大锥上整体截面的分段拟多项式公式，其定义域为D-MMP的Mori chamber；对Fano型簇，该类公式可推广至整个有效锥；这些公式仅由X的小双有理类即可构造，却能编码X所有双有理收缩的性质。同时研究了逆过程：有限的h^0(X,O_X(D))计算可确定chamber分解及相应双有理模型的若干方面。

Conclusion: 整体截面与双有理几何之间存在双向关系：一方面双有理数据决定整体截面的分段拟多项式结构，另一方面整体截面的有限计算反过来刻画chamber分解与双有理模型，从而将正性方法从可动锥推广到大锥乃至有效锥。

Abstract: We investigate aspects of the relationship between global sections of divisors and birational geometry. We show that for varieties $X$ of klt Calabi-Yau type, global sections are governed by the same birational data that control the $D$-minimal model program ($D$-MMP). As a consequence, global sections of arbitrary big divisors can be systematically reduced to Euler characteristics $χ(Y,\mathcal O_Y(D))$ on suitable birational models $Y$ using vanishing theorems, extending classical positivity-based methods beyond the movable cone. The reduction proceeds by removing fixed divisorial components, which do not contribute to the space of global sections, either directly by subtraction or birationally by contraction to a suitable $D$-minimal model. We obtain piecewise quasipolynomial formulae for global sections on the big cone, whose domains are the Mori chambers of the $D$-MMP. Restricting to the subclass of Fano type varieties extends such formulae to the entire effective cone. These formulae can be constructed from just the small birational class of $X$, yet encode properties of all birational contractions of $X$. Alongside the computation of global sections from birational geometry, we study the inverse process, wherein finite computations of $h^0(X,\mathcal O_X(D))$ determine aspects of the chamber decomposition and the associated birational models.

</details>


### [4] [Realizability of Cohomology Classes in Orthogonal Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36078)
*Naima Nader*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在Coskun与Ross工作的基础上，研究正交Grassmannian $OG(k,n)$ 中哪些上同调类可由不可约子簇实现，给出了正交Grassmannian与Grassmannian可实现类一致性的判别准则，刻画了低维与低余维情形的可实现性，并给出了若干正交Grassmannian中可实现性的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在射影簇的上同调理论中，判别哪些上同调类可由不可约子簇的闭包（即"可实现类"）表示是一个核心问题。Coskun与Ross已在Grassmannian情形取得了重要进展，而正交Grassmannian作为其重要变体，其可实现类结构是否与Grassmannian一致尚不清楚，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 沿用Coskun–Ross的研究框架，通过分析正交Grassmannian的上同调环与Schubert类结构，建立判别准则以比较正交Grassmannian与Grassmannian中可实现类的异同；并针对低维、低余维情形进行显式刻画，对特定正交Grassmannian推导可实现性的必要条件。

Result: 给出了正交Grassmannian中可实现类与Grassmannian中可实现类相一致的判别准则；完成了低维与低余维情形下可实现性的完整刻画；并对某些正交Grassmannian给出了可实现性的必要条件。

Conclusion: 正交Grassmannian的可实现类问题部分可归约到Grassmannian情形，但在一般情形下存在差异；本文的准则与必要条件为进一步研究正交Grassmannian的Schubert几何与可实现性提供了基础。

Abstract: Building on the work of Coskun and Ross, we investigate which cohomology classes of the orthogonal Grassmannian $OG(k,n)$ can be realized as irreducible subvarieties of $OG(k,n)$. We give a criterion for when the realizable classes in the orthogonal Grassmannian agree with those in the Grassmannian. We give a characterization for realizability in low dimension and codimension and give necessary conditions for realizability in certain orthogonal Grassmannians.

</details>


### [5] [Rigidity conditions for binomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36135)
*Veronika Kikteva*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式满足的三个条件（LND核的代入性质、不属于任何非零LND的核、商代数刚性），证明对不可约二项式三条件等价，并厘清可约二项式情形下所有存在的蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部幂零导子（LND）及其核的刻画是多变量多项式环研究中的核心问题，涉及刚性、嵌入与消去等经典课题。作者希望统一此前分散的若干相关性质，明确它们之间的逻辑关系，并针对二项式这一具体且便于计算的多项式类给出完整的等价/蕴含刻画。

Method: 定义三个条件：(1) 对任意仿射整环，若将代数无关元素代入多项式所得结果属于某局部幂零导子的核，则这些元素本身也在该核中；(2) 多项式不属于多项式代数的任何非零LND的核；(3) 多项式生成的理想对应的商代数是刚性的（无非平凡LND）。随后对不可约二项式证明三者等价，对可约二项式系统判定并建立三者间所有成立的蕴含关系。

Result: 对不可约二项式，(1)、(2)、(3)三者等价；对可约二项式，给出了三条件之间全部成立的蕴含关系，从而完全厘清其逻辑结构。

Conclusion: 二项式情形下，LND核的代入性质、非核成员性质与商代数刚性具有高度一致性：不可约时完全等价，可约时呈现确定的蕴含格局。该结果统一了相关刻画并为后续研究其他多项式类的类似问题提供了范本。

Abstract: We consider the following three conditions for polynomials in several variables. Condition \textbf{(1)} holds for a polynomial if, for every affine integral domain, the following is true: whenever the result of substituting algebraically independent elements into the polynomial lies in the kernel of a locally nilpotent derivation, the elements themselves must lie in that kernel. A polynomial satisfies condition \textbf{(2)} if it does not belong to the kernel of any nonzero LND of the polynomial algebra. It satisfies condition \textbf{(3)} if the quotient algebra modulo the ideal generated by the polynomial is rigid, that is, it admits no nontrivial LNDs. For irreducible binomials, we prove that all three conditions are equivalent. For reducible binomials, we establish all existing implications between these conditions.

</details>


### [6] [Calculating Hodge and weight filtrations on localizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36180)
*András C. Lőrincz,Michael Perlman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 介绍 Macaulay2 软件包 MixedHodgeModules，用于计算多项式环局部化及扭曲局部化上的 Hodge 滤过与权滤过，并支持 Hodge 理想、乘子理想、p-函数等相关不变量与复形计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式环的局部化及扭曲局部化 S_f、S_f f^{-α} 上蕴含丰富的 Hodge 理论与权滤过信息，但相关不变量（如 Hodge 理想、权层、p-函数等）及各类 de Rham、Du Bois 复形的计算缺乏统一的软件实现，作者希望填补这一工具空白。

Method: 基于 Macaulay2 实现软件包 MixedHodgeModules，对非恒定既约多项式 f 的局部化 S_f 及有理指数扭曲局部化 S_f f^{-α}（α∈QQ）进行计算，提供 Hodge 滤过与权滤过的相关算法与程序接口。

Result: 软件包可计算 Hodge 理想、加权 Hodge 理想、高阶乘子理想、Hodge 滤过的生成层级、元素的权层级、p-函数以及权滤过的长度；并提供计算分次 de Rham 复形、Du Bois 复形与超曲面交集 Du Bois 复形的例程。

Conclusion: MixedHodgeModules 为局部化与扭曲局部化上的 Hodge/权滤过研究提供了系统化的计算工具，覆盖多种理想、层级、p-函数及复形对象，便于相关代数几何与 D-模计算的应用。

Abstract: We describe the Macaulay2 package MixedHodgeModules, which computes Hodge and weight filtrations on localizations S_f of a polynomial ring S at a non-constant reduced polynomial f, as well as on twisted localizations S_f f^{-α}, where α\in QQ is rational. The package includes functionality for computing Hodge ideals, weighted Hodge ideals, higher multiplier ideals, generation levels of Hodge filtrations, weight levels of elements, p-functions, and lengths of weight filtrations. It also provides routines for computing graded de Rham complexes, Du Bois complexes, and intersection Du Bois complexes of hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [7] [Vanishing of degree $3$ unramified cohomology over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36188)
*Federico Scavia,Fumiaki Suzuki*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上Fermat三次曲线E的三次幂E^3的三阶非分歧上同调消失性，并证明整系数循环映射的满射性；在Tate猜想假设下将该满射性推广到具备Witt向量环光滑提升的任意维光滑射影簇；结果应用于Colliot-Thélène关于整体函数域上零循环局部-整体原则的猜想，并给出Fano簇反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上代数簇的整系数循环映射满射性以及非分歧上同调的消失性，是算术代数几何的核心问题。Colliot-Thélène提出整体函数域上零循环的局部-整体原则猜想，需要刻画循环映射的像；同时，代数闭包上的消失性是否可下降至有限子扩张，此前认识不足。本文旨在推进这些问题。

Method: 以有限域k（char p≠3）上的Fermat三次曲线E为具体对象，取ℓ≠p（当p≡1 mod 3时要求ℓ>3），直接计算H^3_nr(k(E^3)/k, Q_ℓ/Z_ℓ(2))并分析循环映射CH^2(E^3)_{Z_ℓ}→H^4(E^3,Z_ℓ(2))。对一般情形，在Tate猜想（有限域上曲面）假设下，对特征≠2且可光滑提升到Witt向量环W(k)的光滑射影簇X（dim d），证明除有限多个ℓ外，1-循环的整系数循环映射满射。方法结合非分歧上同调、Tate猜想与提升技术，并构造具体例子说明代数闭包上消失性不可下降。

Result: 得到H^3_nr(k(E^3)/k, Q_ℓ/Z_ℓ(2))=0，且CH^2(E^3)_{Z_ℓ}→H^4(E^3,Z_ℓ(2))为满射；由此推出H^3_nr(\bar{k}(E^3)/\bar{k}, Q_ℓ/Z_ℓ(2))=0。在Tate猜想假设下，对特征≠2、可光滑提升的光滑射影簇，几乎所有ℓ上1-循环的整系数循环映射满射。并构造例子证明：即使对Fano簇，代数闭包上三阶非分歧上同调的消失不能推出任意有限子扩张上的消失。

Conclusion: 本文验证了Fermat三次曲线三次幂情形下循环映射的满射性与非分歧上同调消失性，并在Tate猜想下将满射性推广至一类高维光滑射影簇；结果对Colliot-Thélène零循环局部-整体原则猜想有应用，同时表明代数闭包上的消失性不能下降至有限子扩张，纠正了可能的直觉预期。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a finite field of characteristic $p\neq 3$, let $E/k$ be the Fermat cubic curve, and let $\ell\neq p$ be a prime. If $p\equiv1\pmod3$, assume moreover that $\ell>3$. Then $H^3_{\mathrm{nr}}(k(E^3)/k,\mathbb{Q}_\ell/\mathbb{Z}_\ell(2))=0$ and the cycle map \[CH^2(E^3)_{\mathbb{Z}_\ell}\longrightarrow H^4(E^3,\mathbb{Z}_\ell(2))\] is surjective. In particular, $H^3_{\mathrm{nr}}(\overline{k}(E^3)/\overline{k},\mathbb{Q}_\ell/\mathbb{Z}_\ell(2))=0$.
  Assuming the Tate conjecture for surfaces over finite fields, we prove an analogous surjectivity result, for all but finitely many primes $\ell\neq p$, for the integral cycle maps for $1$-cycles on every smooth projective variety of dimension $d$ over a finite field of characteristic different from $2$ which admits a smooth projective lift to the ring of Witt vectors.
  We apply our results to a conjecture of Colliot-Thélène on the local--global principle for zero-cycles over global function fields. To further illustrate these results, we exhibit examples showing that vanishing of degree-$3$ unramified cohomology over the algebraic closure of the ground field does not imply vanishing over any finite subextension, not even for Fano varieties.

</details>


### [8] [Motivic classes of Fano schemes of lines on intersections of two quadrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36233)
*Lena Ji,Fumiaki Suzuki*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 对光滑两二次超曲面的完全交及其Fano线簇，在Grothendieck环中给出类公式，并证明Belmans等人猜想的前两个情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Belmans等人提出的关于光滑两二次超曲面完全交X及其Fano线簇的类在Grothendieck环中的猜想（前两个情形），并进一步刻画曲线上的二次纤维化中相对Fano线性子空间簇。

Method: 利用Grothendieck环中的类公式，在简单退化假设下，研究曲线上的二次纤维化的纤维中任意维线性子空间的相对Fano簇，并计算其有理Chow动机。

Result: 给出了X及其Fano线簇在Grothendieck环中的类公式，证明了Belmans等人猜想的前两个情形；在简单退化假设下给出了相对Fano线性子空间簇的类公式；计算了相对Fano簇的有理Chow动机；为Shah关于相对Fano线簇半正交分解中剩余范畴的猜想提供了证据。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅证明了Belmans等人猜想的前两个情形，还推广到曲线上的二次纤维化情形，并为Shah的猜想提供了支持。

Abstract: Let $X\subset\mathbb P^N$ be a smooth complete intersection of two quadrics. We find formulas in the Grothendieck ring of varieties for the class of $X$ and for the class of its Fano scheme of lines, thereby proving the first two cases of a conjecture of Belmans et al. We also find a formula for the class of the relative Fano schemes of linear subspaces (of any dimension) in the fibers of quadric fibrations over curves, under a simple degeneration assumption. As consequences, we compute the rational Chow motive of the relative Fano scheme, and we provide evidence for a conjecture of Shah on a residual category in a semiorthogonal decomposition of the relative Fano scheme of lines.

</details>


### [9] [The $F$-signature Function on the Big Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36321)
*Seungsu Lee,Suchitra Pande*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在正特征射影簇上定义了big除子的$F$-signature，证明该函数在大锥上连续，并刻画其正性与整体$F$-正则性的等价关系，给出了双有理变换规则及模$p$约化下的下界传播结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于$F$-signature的工作主要局限于ample锥，作者希望将其推广至更大的big锥，从而在更一般的big除子框架下理解$F$-signature的连续性与正性，并刻画其与整体$F$-正则性的联系。

Method: 定义big除子的$F$-signature；通过研究大锥上的Frobenius-alpha不变量分析$F$-signature的正性；结合graded ideal sequences刻画双有理变换规则；在模$p$约化情形下利用特征无关下界进行推广。

Result: $F$-signature函数在大锥上连续；任意big $\mathbb{R}$-除子的$F$-signature正性等价于$X$的整体$F$-正则性；得到以graded ideal sequences表示的$F$-signature双有理变换规则；模$p$约化下，ample除子的特征无关下界可传播至任意big除子。

Conclusion: 该工作将$F$-signature理论从ample锥系统地拓展到big锥，建立了正性、连续性与整体$F$-正则性之间的深刻联系，并为双有理几何及模$p$约化提供了新的工具与下界传播机制。

Abstract: We define and study the $F$-signature of big divisors on projective varieties in positive characteristics. For a normal projective variety $X$, we show that the $F$-signature function defines a continuous function on the big cone of $X$, extending our previous results on the ample cone. Abstract: We study well-formed weighted projective threefolds that admit $\mathbb Q$-Gorenstein smoothings to $\mathbb P^3$. Two families are known: the $\mathbb P^2$-type and the $Q$-type, and it is conjectured that these are the only possibilities. We derive numerical and local necessary conditions for such a smoothing. In addition to the anticanonical volume equation, constancy of the anticanonical Hilbert polynomial yields a further identity when all codimension two singularities are of $A$-type. We also obtain a semigroup condition governing the existence of global smoothing directions along codimension two curves with transverse $A$-type singularities. We apply these conditions to prove the expected classification in several cases. In particular, for every fixed square-free integer $d$, there are only finitely many $\mathbb Q$-Gorenstein smoothable spaces $\mathbb P(1,a,b,c)$ such that $\gcd(a,b)=d$. Our method reduces the possible weights to a finite exact computation; for every prime $p\le100$, the computation produces only members of the two expected families. Abstract: We study well-formed weighted projective threefolds $X=\mathbb P(w_0,w_1,w_2,w_3)$ that admit a $\mathbb Q$-Gorenstein smoothing to a smooth Fano threefold of Picard number one. For del Pezzo threefolds $V_d$ and prime Fano threefolds $Y_g$, we derive three necessary numerical and local conditions: the anticanonical volume equation, an identity obtained from the linear term of the anticanonical Hilbert polynomial, and a global section condition for smoothing the transversal $A$-singularities along coordinate curves. Abstract: We present new constraints on sub-GeV dark matter (DM) annihilation and decay using twenty years of diffuse hard X-ray observations from the INTEGRAL/IBIS telescope. By performing a joint analysis of the spectral and morphological information contained in the IBIS dataset, we simultaneously fit Galactic longitudinal profiles and energy spectra, exploiting their complementary signatures to disentangle potential DM signals from the dominant astrophysical backgrounds. We use physically motivated background models combining inverse-Compton (IC) emission from Galactic cosmic-ray electrons with thermal emission from unresolved accreting white dwarfs, and consistently include the secondary IC emission generated by DM-induced electron and positron populations propagated through the Galaxy. We conservatively derive 95% upper limits on the annihilation cross section $\langleσv\rangle$ and decay half-life $τ$ for the $e^+e^-$, $μ^+μ^-$, and $π^+π^-$ final states across the mass range 1 MeV to 5 GeV. Our constraints are in the range $10^{-28}$ cm$^3$/s $\lesssim \langleσv\rangle \lesssim 10^{-26}$ cm$^3$/s for annihilation and $τ\gtrsim 10^{25}$ s for decay at DM masses of a few MeV, improving upon existing X-ray limits by up to two orders of magnitude over much of the mass range. Our bounds are translated into constraints on the kinetic mixing parameter $ε$ of a dark photon and the scalar-Higgs mixing angle $\sinθ$, and compared with existing indirect, direct and accelerator limits available, providing a comprehensive picture of the allowed parameter space for light portal DM.

Abstract: We analyze Higgs production at the LHC with a jet veto at central rapidities and present the first next-to-leading logarithmic resummation of non-global logarithms for this process. Abstract: We analyse the process $e^+e^-\to hγ$ in the Standard Model (SM) and in generic extensions with heavy new physics, using the framework of Higgs pseudo-observables. Motivation: 高阶微扰QCD计算（如NNLO）面临红外发散与相空间积分的双重困难。传统切片方法依赖切片参数，引入残余不确定性且数值效率受限。作者希望借助AI（LLM在人的物理指导下）构造局部红外减除方案，避免切片参数，同时复用已有的低阶辐射计算与EFT奇异性预测，并在有限算力下验证可行性。

Abstract: We present AI-developed local infrared subtraction, building on projection to Born and EFT matching. The framework separates an integrable radiation term from a finite contribution at Born kinematics, referred to as the Born contact. The contact is determined using the EFT singular distribution in a resolution observable such as N-jettiness $τ_N$. Under human physics guidance, an LLM develops two implementations. One uses a neural network for phase space projection and fits the contact by matching to EFT cumulants. The other uses an analytic construction that keeps the Born momenta fixed while integrating over radiation. It combines the EFT $δ(τ_N)$ coefficient with finite 4-dimensional radiation integrals to calculate the contact term directly. This gives a local subtraction formula without a slicing parameter, while reusing existing lower-order radiation calculations and EFT singular predictions. As a demonstration, we reconstruct the full NLO correction for massless 3- and 4-jet production in electron-positron annihilation. The attempt to the NNLO dijet production is also made by recursively using the NLO P2B construction with the LLM designing machine-learning controls to reduce the variance of the contact integral. The tested predictions are in good agreement with EERAD3. The numerical calculation and projection-network training use a 2020 Apple M1 MacBook, without GPU acceleration, illustrating the feasibility of the construction with modest computing resources. The appendices develop an extension of the local subtraction to 3-jet NNLO, giving explicit radiation maps and a proposed contact formula. We also show how to integrate over NNLO radiation while keeping the Born momenta fixed, for any number of massless final-state jets. Our results demonstrate how AI can help higher-order calculations by constructing infrared subtraction and improving its numerical integration.

Abstract: Electric dipole moments (EDMs) provide some of the most sensitive probes of CP-violating interactions beyond the Standard Model (SM). Abstract: We develop two hybrid techniques that approach the performance of neural simulation-based inference (NSBI) analyses while substantially reducing the computational cost of inference and preserving some or all of the reliability guarantees of parametric methods. Based on our comparison studies, we recommend the use of our first approach, Latent Categories, for robust and efficient inference.

By contrast, models for elastically colliding DM are less impacted, and some remain viable candidates -- for example, the simple light Singlet-Doublet Majorana DM candidate at the Higgs blind spot. Our work confirms that accurately accounting for LMC effects is essential when high-velocity DM is probed, rather than relying on the simplified Standard Halo Model.

Abstract: Extracting the intrinsic transverse-momentum structure of quarks from deep-inelastic scattering data requires separating nonperturbative effects from perturbative radiation, which broadens the measured transverse-momentum distributions. Abstract: We investigate self-interacting dark matter in an $O(N)$-symmetric real scalar multiplet model with Higgs-portal interactions involving the Standard Model Higgs and a real singlet scalar. Abstract: The familiar wave kinetic theory revolves around turbulent energy cascades characterized by random phases of the individual normal modes. By contrast, recent years have revealed turbulent cascades where the transfer of energy is mediated by phase-coherent dynamics. Such coherent cascades have been previously established in deterministic systems with a tightly controlled algebraic structure of the mode interactions, such as nonlinear Schrödinger equations.
  Here, we show that the presence of phase-coherent cascades is robust with respect to substantial alterations of the mode couplings. We prove that, in infinite-dimensional resonant Hamiltonian systems with fully resonant normal mode spectra and random nonlinear couplings, a structured subset of couplings --- an infinitesimal fraction of the total --- suffices to support coherent cascades characterized by phase alignment, the formation of power-law spectra, and either unbounded growth of Sobolev norms or a finite-time blow-up. Numerical simulations further demonstrate the emergence of coherent cascades from random initial data through a process of phase locking, suggesting a mechanism for their emergence in broader classes of initial data and systems. We thus show that disordered mode interactions can sustain, rather than block, coherent energy transport across arbitrarily distant scales.

Abstract: The order $m$ antisymmetrised matrix product ensemble is represented by the product $X_1^T\cdots X_m^TJX_m\cdots X_1$, where $X_1,\ldots,X_m$ are independent real Ginibre matrices and $J$ is the elementary antisymmetric matrix, while the order $m$ Hermitised matrix product ensemble is represented by $X_1^\dagger\cdots X_m^\dagger HX_1\cdots X_m$, where $X_1,\ldots,X_m$ are now independent complex Ginibre matrices and $H$ is a Hermitian matrix drawn from the Gaussian unitary ensemble. These ensembles have recently been shown to be related to certain Muttalib--Borodin ensembles and integrals of Harish-Chandra--Itzykson--Zuber type, thereby motivating further investigation into their eigenvalue statistics. In this work, we construct ribbon graphs and constellations that are enumerated by the mixed cumulants of these ensembles and give loop equation characterisations for the generating functions of said cumulants when $m=1$.

A unified transform-domain formulation is also established by relating the proposed OLCT approach to its Fourier transform counterpart. Numerical and graphical results demonstrate the accuracy and effectiveness of the proposed method and validate it through comparisons with the Fourier transform based formulation of Ohira . The proposed framework may provide a promising foundation for solving non linear delay differential equations involving transcendental terms, as both the OLCT and the resulting Volterra integral equation possess the essential properties required to handle non linearities and transcendental terms.

Abstract: To address commons dilemmas under costly social cooperation, this paper develops a tripartite coevolutionary system that integrates social behavior, ecological resource dynamics, and institutional regulation. The results show that the system admits a sustainable operating equilibrium in which cooperation, ecological resources, and institutional regulation coexist. In addition to these stable regimes, the system exhibits bistability and limit cycles, indicating that alternative long term outcomes may arise from different initial conditions and parameter configurations. These nonlinear mechanisms generate a persistent crackdown rebound policy trap, in which intensified intervention temporarily improves cooperation and resource conditions but eventually produces recurrent oscillations and prevents system wide consensus. The proposed framework provides a system level explanation of governance failure and offers analytical insights for designing adaptive institutional regulation in coupled human environment systems.

Abstract: We investigate the a posteriori controllability of remodelling within the framework of Geometric Control Theory applied to continuum systems. Remodelling is described through an isochoric Bilby--Kroner--Lee decomposition, where the internal structural transformation is represented by a remodelling tensor and its evolution is governed by a stress-driven constitutive law. Assuming infinitesimal deformation and remodelling stretches, while retaining finite rotations of the principal remodelling directions, we derive a nonlinear state-space representation in terms of principal remodelling stretches and orientation variables. This formulation allows the remodelling process to be interpreted as a finite-dimensional nonlinear control system parametrised by the material position. Equilibrium configurations are characterised, and under-actuated controllability problems are studied by means of the tools of Geometric Control Theory. The three-dimensional setting shows that the nonlinear coupling between remodelling stretches and orientations enables complete steering of the internal state through a reduced number of independent controls, while the planar reduction reveals a loss of controllability despite the presence of nonlinearity. Abstract: Scattering theory is at the core of gaining information about most quantum systems. In general, analytic progress is difficult and the investigation of universal features using statistical methods is of interest. Commonly this is carried out within the Heidelberg approach which models the Hamiltonian of the target by a random matrix. Hitherto, such investigations were performed for systems that are effectively spinless or have total angular momentum conservation. In light of recent experimental progress it has become relevant to also investigate systems with half-integer spin. Using supersymmetry, we derive the distribution for the real and imaginary parts of the scattering matrix elements and the corresponding cross sections in the case of symplectic symmetry. These results complete the derivations for Dysons threefold way.

</details>

Result: 成功导出了非对角散射矩阵元与截面的精确分布表达式。结果显示：分布本身是对称的，而散射矩阵元并不对称。

Abstract: Scattering theory is a key tool for the investigation of quantum systems. Universal approaches are of interest, particularly in the framework of Random Matrix Theory. Dyson's Threefold way classifies systems according to their behavior under time reversal. In these three cases the distribution of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross sections were recently calculated. However, in many applications systems neither display full nor fully broken time reversal invariance, such as systems in tuneable external magnetic fields. The study of these systems has a long history, also in Random Matrix Theory. We investigate the influence of time reversal invariance breaking on the universal behavior of the mentioned distributions. Using supersymmetry we succeed in deriving exact expressions for the distribution of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross sections in systems with half-integer or integer/no spin in the absence of further symmetries. We find that the distributions are symmetric while the scattering matrix elements are not. Furthermore, our results might considerably facilitate the data analysis of time reversal invariance breaking.

</details>

Abstract: Recently derived exact expressions for the distributions of scattering matrix elements show that all combinations of ingoing and outgoing spin orientations are equally distributed. We propose a model suitable for experimental realization that breaks this degeneracy by introducing a spin preference in the channels. With the help of supersymmetry we derive a corresponding nonlinear sigma model. We exactly calculate the distributions of real and imaginary part in first order of the parameter measuring the preference. Quite remarkably, while breaking the spin degeneracy leads to different distributions for the favored and unfavored spin orientation, the real and imaginary parts of these altered distributions are the same as in the case of unbroken spin degeneracy.

Abstract: We establish convergence of minimizers for a minimal one dimensional atomic-scale model for relaxation in moiré materials to those of a one-dimensional continuum limit functional analogous to those commonly used in practice to model relaxation. The atomic-scale model consists of two periodic chains containing $N$ and $N+1$ atoms per common cell and is exactly moiré-periodic at the atomic scale. Its energy combines harmonic nearest-neighbor intralayer interactions with nonlocal interlayer interactions generated by an even $C^2$ pair potential with polynomial decay. The associated continuum energy couples linear elasticity to a periodic misfit potential, obtained by periodizing the pair potential, which penalizes unfavorable local stackings.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类自旋链的相互作用项生成环参数d=2s+1的Temperley-Lieb代数，是低维量子多体与代数方法交叉的重要模型。已有工作（如Nachtergaele-Spitzer-Starr 2004）仅对自旋-1/2 XXZ链证明能级铁磁序，缺乏对一般自旋-s情形的谱结构与间隙行为的系统刻画，作者旨在推广并补全这一理论。

Method: 1）对任意对称扇区证明能级铁磁序，推广Nachtergaele-Spitzer-Starr的结果；2）由此导出有限体积谱间隙的一致下界；3）结合变分估计与Knabe有限尺寸判据；4）分析完全极化态GNS哈密顿量；5）证明开链基态投影算子满足Jones-Wenzl递推，并用q+q^{-1}=d的量子整数描述简并。

Result: 证明了跨对称扇区的能级铁磁序；给出有限体积谱间隙的一致下界；确定完全极化态GNS哈密顿量的精确谱间隙为1-2/d，并证明该值也是最大混合基态间隙的下界；开链基态投影算子满足Jones-Wenzl递推，简并度为量子整数[L+1]_q（q+q^{-1}=d）。

Conclusion: 高简并基态使模型能够在保持有隙的同时破缺连续对称性，并导致该间隙不稳定；结果为Temperley-Lieb代数框架下自旋-s链的谱理论提供了统一图景。

Abstract: We review mathematical results about the spectrum of spin-$s$ chains with a nearest neighbor interaction given by the projection onto the singlet state of two spin-$s$ degrees of freedom. Up to normalization, these interaction terms generate a Temperley-Lieb algebra at loop parameter $d=2s+1$. We also present several new results. First, we prove a ferromagnetic ordering of energy levels across symmetry sectors of the model, thus generalizing a previous result of Nachtergaele, Spitzer and Starr (2004) for the spin-$\tfrac12$ $XXZ$ chain. This enables us to determine a uniform lower bound on the finite volume spectral gap for these spin chains. By combining a variational estimate with Knabe's finite size criterion, we show that the exact spectral gap of the GNS Hamiltonian of the fully polarized state is $1-2/d$. Furthermore, this value serves as a lower bound for the gap of the maximally mixed ground state. Finally, we show that the ground state projectors on open chains satisfy the Jones-Wenzl recursion, thereby recovering a known result that the degeneracies are the quantum integers $[L+1]_q$ for $q+q^{-1}=d$. We discuss how the high degeneracy of the ground states allows the model to break a continuous symmetry while remaining gapped, and renders that gap unstable.

</details>

Abstract: Cao and Wang recently classified a class of locally strongly convex centroaffine hypersurfaces whose non-vanishing Tchebychev vector field $T$ satisfies $K(T,X)=αX$ and $X(α)=0$ for every $X\perp T$, together with $K(T,T)=βT$. In the degenerate case $β=2α$, their higher-dimensional argument assumes in addition that the $T^\perp$-component of the difference tensor $K$ vanishes identically. We show that this extra assumption is unnecessary and the theorem can be strengthened.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Tachibana 型刚性定理通常要求曲率算子的整体非负性，条件较强。本文旨在将条件弱化为部分和（partial-sum）形式，从而放宽刚性定理的适用范围。

Result: 证明：维数 n≥4 的 Einstein 流形若曲率算子为 2(n-1)/3-非负，则局部对称；复维数 n≥2 的 Kähler-Einstein 流形对应阈值为 2(n+1)/3；实维数 4n≥8 的四元数-Kähler 流形对应阈值为 2(n+2)/3。

Conclusion: 在弱化的部分和非负条件下建立了 Tachibana 型刚性定理，并确定了上述三类流形的具体刚性阈值。

Abstract: We prove Tachibana-type rigidity theorems for closed manifolds under weakened partial-sum conditions on the eigenvalues of curvature operators. Specifically, an Einstein manifold of dimension $n\geq4$ is locally symmetric if its curvature operator is $\frac{2(n-1)}{3}$-nonnegative. The corresponding thresholds are $\frac{2(n+1)}{3}$ for the primitive curvature operator of a Kähler-Einstein manifold of complex dimension $n\geq2$, and $\frac{2(n+2)}{3}$ for the curvature operator of a quaternionic-Kähler manifold of real dimension $4n\geq8$.

</details>

*Wenqi Li,Jianyu Ou,Guoqiang Wu,Detang Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了常数量曲率的完备梯度Kähler--Ricci收缩孤立子具有刚性，可分解为Kähler--Einstein因子与欧氏因子的乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究常数量曲率条件下梯度Kähler--Ricci收缩孤立子的刚性分类，判定其是否必然分裂为低维Kähler--Einstein流形与复欧氏空间的黎曼乘积。

Method: 设$(M^{2m},g,f,J)$满足$\operatorname{Ric}+\nabla^2 f=\frac12 g$。在数量曲率$R$为常数的假设下，证明存在整数$k\in\{0,\ldots,m\}$使得$R\equiv k$；作为证明的关键步骤，建立黎曼刚性判据：对具常数量曲率的完备非稳态梯度Ricci孤立子，条件$\mathcal{L}_{\nabla f}\operatorname{Ric}=0$蕴含径向平坦性，进而得到刚性。

Result: 若标量曲率为常数，则孤立子刚性：$(M^{2m},g,J)\cong\bigl(N^{2k}\times\mathbb C^{m-k},g_N+g_{\mathrm{Euc}},J_N\oplus J_0\bigr)$，其中$(N^{2k},g_N,J_N)$为Kähler--Einstein且$\operatorname{Ric}_{g_N}=\frac12 g_N$，$R\equiv k\in\{0,\ldots,m\}$。

Conclusion: 常数量曲率的完备梯度Kähler--Ricci收缩孤立子必为Kähler--Einstein因子与平坦复空间的乘积形式。所建立的径向平坦性判据（$\mathcal{L}_{\nabla f}\operatorname{Ric}=0$）是推广刚性结果的一般性工具。

Abstract: Let $(M^{2m},g,f,J)$ be a complete gradient Kähler--Ricci shrinker satisfying \[ \operatorname{Ric}+\nabla^2 f=\frac12 g. \] We prove that if the scalar curvature is constant, then the soliton is rigid. More precisely, there exists an integer $k\in\{0,\ldots,m\}$ such that \[ R\equiv k \] and \[ (M^{2m},g,J)\cong \bigl(N^{2k}\times\mathbb C^{m-k},
  g_N+g_{\mathrm{Euc}},J_N\oplus J_0\bigr), \] where $(N^{2k},g_N,J_N)$ is Kähler--Einstein and \[ \operatorname{Ric}_{g_N}=\frac12 g_N. \] As part of the proof, we establish a Riemannian rigidity criterion: for a complete nonsteady gradient Ricci soliton with constant scalar curvature, the condition $\mathcal{L}_{\nabla f}\operatorname{Ric}=0$ implies radial flatness and hence rigidity.

</details>

Method: 通过直接计算得到Willmore能量的精确下界(4nπ²)^(n/2)，利用Möbius变换下的不变性分析极小元的刚性（唯一性），并构造Clifford n-环面的特定Möbius变换反例来检验Chen猜想在全平均曲率情形下的有效性。

Abstract: This paper establishes the sharp lower bound $(4nπ^2)^{n/2}$ for the Willmore energy $\mathcal{W}$ of flat $n$-tori in the Euclidean space. Up to Möbius transformations, the Clifford $n$-torus $\mathbb{S}^1\bigl(\sqrt{1/n}\,\bigr) \times \cdots \times \mathbb{S}^1\bigl(\sqrt{1/n}\,\bigr) \subset \mathbb{S}^{2n-1} \subset \mathbb{R}^{2n}$ is shown to be the unique minimizer attaining this bound. This also confirms Chen's conjecture for flat $n$-tori. However, when $n \geq3 $, we show that Chen's conjecture fails on the total mean curvature of general immersed $n$-tori: certain Möbius transformations of the Clifford $n$-torus strictly decrease the total mean curvature.

</details>


### [83] [Willmore Energy Estimates for Klein Bottles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36519)
*Ruijie Ni,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文给出了 $\mathbb R^N$ 中 Klein 瓶的 Willmore 能量估计，部分证实 Kusner 猜想，并证实 flat Klein 瓶情形下 Hirsch-Mäder-Baumdicker 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿用 Li-Yau 与 Montiel-Ros 关于 $2$-tori 的思想，研究 Klein 瓶的 Willmore 能量下界问题，并回应 Kusner 与 Hirsch-Mäder-Baumdicker 的相关猜想。

Method: 对 flat Klein 瓶 $\mathbb{K}^2_b$（$0.350\lesssim b\lesssim 0.755$）上的共形分枝浸入建立 Willmore 能量估计；对 flat 浸入推导下界；并在 $\mathbb R^5$ 中构造 flat Klein 瓶的光滑族。

Result: 证明任意共形分枝浸入 $\varphi:\mathbb{K}^2_b\to\mathbb R^N$ 满足 $\mathcal W(\varphi)>6\pi$，部分证实 Kusner 猜想；对 flat 浸入 $\psi$ 得到 $\mathcal W(\psi)$ 下界，证实 Hirsch-Mäder-Baumdicker 猜想；构造出 Willmore 能量介于 $6.912\pi$ 与 $8\pi$ 之间的 $\mathbb R^5$ 中 flat Klein 瓶光滑族。

Conclusion: 结果推广了 tori 情形的方法，给出 Klein 瓶 Willmore 能量的新下界与显式构造，部分解决 Kusner 猜想并完全解决 flat Klein 瓶的 Hirsch-Mäder-Baumdicker 猜想。

Abstract: In this note, we provide an estimate for the Willmore energy of Klein bottles in $\mathbb R^N$, building on the ideas of Li-Yau and Montiel-Ros for $2$-tori. In particular, we prove that the Willmore energy $\mathcal{W}(φ)> 6π$ for any conformal branched immersion $φ:\mathbb{K}^2_b\rightarrow \mathbb R^N$, where $\mathbb{K}^2_b$ is a flat Klein bottle with $0.350\lesssim b \lesssim 0.755$. This confirms partially a conjecture of Kusner. Moreover, for a flat immersion $ψ:\mathbb{K}^2_b\rightarrow \mathbb R^N$, we derive a lower bound of $\mathcal{W}(ψ)$, which confirms a conjecture of Hirsch-Mäder-Baumdicker for flat Klein bottles in $\mathbb R^N$. We also construct a smooth family of flat Klein bottles in $\mathbb R^5$, with Willmore energy between $6.912π$ and $8π$.

</details>


### [84] [A comparison theorem for the planar strip isoperimetric problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36564)
*Skilyn Leon,Rosa Pavlak,Evelyn Pulla,Xi Sisi Shen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Cañete-Miranda Jr-Vittone 关于水平带内外不同平面密度的等周问题的猜想：任意四段弧区域都存在同加权面积但加权周长严格更小的三段弧竞争区域，且对每个 λ>1 给出周长差的显式正下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平面密度在水平带内为 1、带外为常数 λ>1 的等周问题，并解决 Cañete-Miranda Jr-Vittone 提出的猜想，即四段弧区域并非最优构型，可被三段弧区域严格改进。

Method: 针对水平带内密度为 1、带外密度为常数 λ>1 的加权等周设定，对任意四段弧区域构造具有相同加权面积的三段弧竞争者，通过几何比较论证其加权周长严格更小，并给出周长差的显式正下界。

Result: 证明了对每个 λ>1，四段弧区域均可被三段弧竞争者以相同加权面积和严格更小的加权周长替代，同时得到周长差的显式正下界，从而完整解决该猜想。

Conclusion: 该猜想成立：在所述密度模型下四段弧区域永远不是最优的，三段弧构型严格更优；结果对所有 λ>1 一致成立，并给出可量化的周长改进下界。

Abstract: We consider the isoperimetric problem with planar density equal to 1 on a horizontal strip and to a constant $λ>1$ outside the strip. We resolve a conjecture made by Cañete-Miranda Jr-Vittone by showing that every four-arc region admits a three-arc competitor with the same weighted area and strictly smaller weighted perimeter. The argument applies to every $λ>1$ and gives an explicit positive lower bound for the perimeter difference.

</details>


### [85] [Tensile minimal surfaces and thread boundary problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36717)
*Romane Boutillier,Laurent Hauswirth,Magdalena Rodríguez*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 受张拉建筑启发，本文用Plateau共轭方法构造了两类以常曲率渐近弧为边界的嵌入极小曲面：拉伸悬链面（极小环面）与拉伸k-noid。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜在均匀张力下呈极小曲面，其柔性不可伸长的边界沿常测地曲率的渐近线。自1960年代斯图加特轻量结构研究所以来该构型被实验研究，近年渐近线与测地线网格壳亦有实现。作者以此为动机，构造以有限多条常曲率渐近弧为边界的嵌入极小曲面。

Method: 对满足“沿自由边界与单位球正交”的部分自由边界问题的极小圆盘解，应用Plateau共轭方法进行构造。

Result: 对任意整数m≥3，证明存在单参数族的嵌入极小环面（拉伸悬链面），其每个边界分量由m条在尖点相接的常曲率渐近弧组成；参数变化时该族从平面构型退化为m个极小圆盘之并。对任意整数k≥3，证明存在拓扑为球面挖去k个圆盘的嵌入极小曲面（拉伸k-noid），其k个边界分量各由四条以尖点相连的渐近弧组成。

Conclusion: 两族曲面关于水平面及若干竖直对称面均对称；嵌入性由证明共轭所得基本片段是位于对称面所围区域内的图来得到。

Abstract: Minimal surfaces bounded by cables or threads arise naturally in tensile architecture: a membrane under uniform tension takes the shape of a minimal surface, and its flexible, inextensible boundary lies along an asymptotic line of constant geodesic curvature. Such configurations have been studied experimentally since the 1960s at the Institute for Lightweight Structures in Stuttgart and more recently realized in gridshells built along networks of asymptotic and geodesic curves. Motivated by this architectural context, we construct two new families of embedded minimal surfaces bounded by finitely many asymptotic arcs of constant curvature, using the Plateau-conjugate method applied to the solution of a partially-free boundary problem for minimal disks that meet the unit sphere orthogonally along the free boundary component.
  For every integer $m\geq 3$ , we prove the existence of a one-parameter family of embedded minimal annuli, called tensile catenoids, whose boundary components each consist of $m$ asymptotic arcs of constant curvature that meet at cusps. As the parameter varies, the family degenerates from a planar configuration to a union of $m$ minimal disks.
  For every integer $k\geq 3$, we prove the existence of an embedded minimal surface with the topology of a sphere minus $k$ disks, called a tensile $k$-noid, each of whose $k$ boundary components consists of four asymptotic arcs joined by cusps.
  Both families are symmetric with respect to a horizontal plane and possess several vertical planes of symmetry. Embeddedness follows from showing that the fundamental piece obtained by conjugation is a graph contained in the region delimited by the planes of symmetry.

</details>


### [86] [The capillary $L_{p}$ dual Christoffel-Minkowski problem for $1<p<q\leq k+1$ with $1\leq k\leq n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37258)
*Guanghan Li,Mengliang Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究一类毛细型 $L_p$ Christoffel–Minkowski 问题，该问题涉及由第 $k$ 毛细面积测度与第 $q$ 对偶曲率测度组成的 $L_p$ 几何测度。通过建立梯度估计，证明了在参数范围 $1<p<q\leq k+1$（$1\leq k\leq n$）内存在偶、光滑、严格凸解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 $L_p$ Christoffel–Minkowski 理论推广到毛细（capillary）情形，并处理同时包含毛细面积测度与对偶曲率测度的更一般 $L_p$ 几何测度处方问题。核心难点在于该问题对应的完全非线性偏微分方程的解的先验估计，尤其是梯度估计，从而为存在性提供关键先验界。

Method: 对由第 $k$ 毛细面积测度与第 $q$ 对偶曲率测度构成的 $L_p$ 几何测度所对应的完全非线性方程建立梯度估计；结合该梯度估计与连续性方法（连续性方法/先验估计框架）证明解的存在性。

Result: 在参数范围 $1<p<q\leq k+1$，$1\leq k\leq n$ 内，证明了偶、光滑、严格凸解的存在性。

Conclusion: 该问题在所给参数范围内可解，梯度估计是证明存在性的关键技术工具，结果推广了毛细 $L_p$ Christoffel–Minkowski 型问题的可解性理论。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a capillary $L_p$ Christoffel--Minkowski-type problem involving the prescription of a class of $L_p$ geometric measures formed by the $k$-th capillary area measure and the $q$-th dual curvature measure. By establishing a gradient estimate, we prove the existence of an even, smooth, strictly convex solution in the parameter range $1<p<q\leq k+1$, where $1\leq k\leq n$.

</details>


### [87] [New Inhomogeneous Nearly $\mathrm{G}_2$-metric on the Berger Space and a Sine Cone Desingularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37265)
*Simon Salamon,Ragini Singhal*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究SO(4)余齐一作用下不变的近并行G₂结构，在Berger空间SO(5)/SO(3)上构造首个非齐次近并行G₂结构（锥具有完整Spin(7) holonomy），并通过严格的计算机辅助打靶法证明其存在性；同时给出S³×S³上正弦锥有限商由C₇ AC气泡粘合的局部去奇异化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近G₂流形是锥度量具有Spin(7)（或其子群）特殊holonomy的七维正Einstein流形。已知例子稀少，尤其缺乏非齐次例子；同时希望理解近并行G₂流形收缩时奇异轨道附近的去奇异化行为，并将其与Foscolo–Haskins–Nordström构造的渐近锥气泡联系起来。

Method: 研究在SO(4)余齐一作用下不变的近并行G₂结构；通过计算机辅助的打靶（shooting）论证建立解的存在性。另对S³×S³上正弦锥的有限商做局部去奇异化，粘入C₇的渐近锥（AC）气泡，并分析收缩极限与爆破极限。

Result: 在Berger空间SO(5)/SO(3)上构造出首个非齐次近并行G₂结构，其锥具有完整Spin(7) holonomy；用严格计算机辅助打靶法证明解存在。并实现正弦锥有限商的局部去奇异化：当奇异轨道收缩时，远离奇异轨道去奇异化族收敛到正弦锥商，而自然爆破收敛到C₇的AC无挠G₂度量。

Conclusion: Berger空间上存在非齐次近并行G₂结构（锥holonomy完整为Spin(7)），扩展了近G₂流形的例子类；同时给出了正弦锥商经C₇ AC气泡的局部去奇异化机制，阐明收缩极限与爆破极限的对应关系。

Abstract: Nearly $\mathrm{G}_2$ manifolds are positive Einstein seven-dimensional manifolds whose associated cone metric has special holonomy equal to, or contained in, $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$. We study nearly parallel $\mathrm{G}_2$-structures invariant under a cohomogeneity-one action of $\mathrm{SO}(4)$, and construct the first inhomogeneous nearly parallel $\mathrm{G}_2$-structure on the Berger space $\mathrm{SO}(5)/\mathrm{SO}(3)$. The cone over this metric has full holonomy $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$. The existence of the solution is established by a rigorous computer-assisted shooting argument.
  We also establish a local desingularisation of a finite quotient of the sine cone over $S^3\times S^3$ by gluing in the asymptotically conical $C_7$ bubble constructed by Foscolo--Haskins--Nordström. We show that as the singular orbit of a family of nearly parallel $\mathrm{G}_2$ manifolds collapses, away from the singular orbit the desingularising family converges to the sine cone quotient, while the natural blow-up converges to the AC torsion-free $\mathrm{G}_2$-metric of $C_7$.

</details>


### [88] [Stable ample vector bundles without semipositive Kobayashi curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37309)
*Kefeng Liu,Xueyuan Wan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在Hirzebruch曲面\(\mathbb{F}_e\)上构造斜率稳定的秩二丰沛向量丛，使其上的强伪凸Finsler度量不可能具有半正Kobayashi曲率，从而否定Kobayashi问题，并给出Fulton问题与Hartshorne–Schneider猜想的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kobayashi问题、Fulton问题以及Hartshorne–Schneider猜想均关注正性（凸性）与度量/曲率之间的对应关系。此前缺乏反例，作者旨在通过显式向量丛构造检验这些问题的强伪凸Finsler表述是否成立。

Method: 在每个Hirzebruch曲面\(\mathbb{F}_e\)上构造斜率稳定的秩二丰沛向量丛，并分析其全空间上不存在具有半正Kobayashi曲率的光滑强伪凸Finsler度量。

Result: 所构造的向量丛不存在满足条件的Finsler度量，构成Kobayashi问题的否定回答；同一族丛同时反证了Fulton问题的光滑强伪凸Finsler表述及Hartshorne–Schneider关于补集凸性的猜想。当\(e=1\)时，反例建立在非乘积的单连通Fano基上。

Conclusion: 正性与强伪凸Finsler曲率之间一般不存在所期望的蕴含关系，相关经典问题的强伪凸Finsler表述不成立。

Abstract: We construct slope-stable ample rank-two vector bundles on every Hirzebruch surface $\mathbb{F}_e$ that admit no smooth strongly pseudoconvex Finsler metric with semipositive Kobayashi curvature. These examples give a negative answer to Kobayashi's problem. The same bundles give counterexamples to the smooth strongly pseudoconvex Finsler formulation of Fulton's problem and to the Hartshorne--Schneider conjecture on the convexity of complements. For $e=1$, these counterexamples have a simply connected Fano base that is not a product.

</details>


### [89] [Smoothing polyhedral spaces via Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37549)
*Richard H. Bamler,Esther Cabezas-Rivas*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Petrunin光滑化猜想在所有维数下成立：无边界、非负Alexandrov曲率的紧欧氏多面体空间是几何非负曲率光滑Riemannian orbifold的Gromov-Hausdorff极限，且逼近度量来自单一orbifold Ricci流的正时刻切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Petrunin光滑化猜想关注奇异空间（多面体空间）能否被光滑几何对象逼近。已有结果通常限于特定维数或仅得到极限而缺乏统一的流结构，本文旨在全维数解决该猜想并强化逼近的构造性。

Method: 建立Ricci流的短时存在性与正则化理论：在双边体积界和广义线段不等式下，用保持曲率锥偏差的小尺度尺度不变积分控制替代逐点下曲率控制，从而允许任意大的逐点违反，并给出与初始上曲率界无关的存在时间与正时刻曲率估计。

Result: 证明每个无边界非负Alexandrov曲率的紧欧氏多面体空间都是几何非负曲率光滑Riemannian orbifold的Gromov-Hausdorff极限；更强地，逼近度量是单一orbifold Ricci流（初值为该多面体空间）的正时刻切片。此外得到小积分曲率缺陷流形的刚性结论。

Conclusion: 全维数解决了Petrunin光滑化猜想，通过新的Ricci流短时正则化框架实现统一逼近，并将其应用于小积分曲率缺陷的刚性。

Abstract: We prove Petrunin's smoothing conjecture in all dimensions: every compact Euclidean polyhedral space without boundary and with nonnegative Alexandrov curvature is a Gromov-Hausdorff limit of smooth Riemannian orbifolds with geometrically nonnegative curvature. More strongly, the approximating metrics are positive-time slices of a single orbifold Ricci flow whose metric initial condition is the given polyhedral space. The proof rests on a new short-time existence and regularization theory for Ricci flow that, under two-sided volume bounds and a generalized segment inequality, replaces pointwise lower curvature control by small scale-invariant integral control of the defect from a preserved curvature cone. Although it allows arbitrarily large pointwise violations, this theory yields an existence time and positive-time curvature estimates independent of the initial upper curvature bound. As a further application, it gives rigidity consequences for manifolds with small integral curvature defect.

</details>


### [90] [A geometric rigidity estimate for codimension-1 immersions into spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37665)
*Siran Li,Isaac Newell*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为球面中的余一维浸入建立了定量稳定性估计：在参考浸入的形算子给定的条件下，任何Sobolev浸入在相差一个环境旋转的意义下接近参考浸入，其浸入与Gauss映射的W^{1,p}距离由拉伸-弯曲能量控制，且无需对φ的基本形式作先验假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何刚性问题的定量稳定性：经典等距浸入刚性结果通常只给出定性结论，且往往需要先验的基本形式有界假设。本文旨在去除这些限制，建立以拉伸-弯曲能量控制的定量稳定性估计，适用于余一维球面浸入。

Method: 证明通过沿法向测地线延拓浸入，将问题约化为球面上的等维几何刚性估计；随后用有限局部化与拼接（patching）论证处理参考浸入法向延拓可能非单射的情形。

Result: 在每个相对紧的强Lipschitz区域上，对任意1<p<∞，任何Sobolev浸入φ在模去环境旋转后接近θ，浸入与Gauss映射的W^{1,p}距离可由φ的L^p拉伸-弯曲能量控制；无需对φ的基本形式施加先验界。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了球面余一维浸入的定量稳定性估计，去除了基本形式先验界的要求，方法上把稳定性问题化归为等维几何刚性并结合局部化拼接技术。

Abstract: We establish a quantitative stability estimate for codimension-one immersions into a round sphere. Let $(M,g)$ be an oriented Riemannian manifold and suppose that a prescribed shape operator is realised by a smooth isometric immersion $θ:(M,g)\to\mathbb S^{n+1}$. We prove that, on every relatively compact strongly Lipschitz domain and for every $1<p<\infty$, any Sobolev immersion $φ$ is close to $θ$ modulo an ambient rotation, with the $W^{1,p}$-distances between both the immersions and their Gauss maps controlled by the $L^p$ stretching-plus-bending energies of $φ$. No {\it a priori} bounds on the fundamental forms of $φ$ are required. The proof proceeds by extending the immersions along normal geodesics and reducing the problem to an equidimensional geometric rigidity estimate on the sphere. A finite localisation and patching argument handles the possible non-injectivity of the normal extension of the reference immersion.

</details>


### [91] [Isoparametric Submanifolds in Products of Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37739)
*Chen Jinxuan,Liu xiaobo,Yang wanxu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文对单连通空间形式之积中的等参子流形（任意余维数）进行了分类，但不包括 $\mathbb{S}^n \times \mathbb{S}^m$ 与 $\mathbb{H}^n \times \mathbb{H}^m$ 中的等参超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等参子流形是子流形几何中的核心研究对象，其在空间形式中的分类已有较完整的结果。将研究范围推广到空间形式的乘积空间，是自然的推广方向，但乘积空间的曲率结构更复杂，分类难度显著提高，因此需要建立新的分类框架。

Method: 通过研究单连通空间形式乘积空间中的等参子流形，利用其主曲率、叶状结构与等参性条件，结合相应的几何与代数工具，对任意余维数的情形进行系统分类。

Result: 给出了单连通空间形式之积中等参子流形（任意余维数）的分类，例外情形为 $\mathbb{S}^n \times \mathbb{S}^m$ 和 $\mathbb{H}^n \times \mathbb{H}^m$ 中的等参超曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作在相当一般的设定下完成了乘积空间中等参子流形的分类，推动了等参子流形理论在非固定曲率空间中的发展，并指出了尚未解决的重要情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we obtain a classification of isoparametric submanifolds with arbitrary codimensions in products of simply-connected space forms, except for isoparametric hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{S}^n \times \mathbb{S}^m$ and $\mathbb{H}^n \times\mathbb{H}^m$.

</details>


### [92] [On filtration formula and nodal sets in min-max theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37769)
*Talant Talipov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文否定了Marques-Neves关于Laplace-Beltrami特征函数节集扫描生成Gromov体积谱渐近最优的猜想，并在任意维数、即使正曲率实解析度量下给出反例；随后引入"节(p,N)-宽度"这一较宽松的节集扫描概念，证明了ω_p(M,g)=lim_{N→∞}ν_p^N(M,g)的滤过公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2014年Marques-Neves猜想：由φ_0,…,φ_p节集生成的扫描在p→∞时对体积谱ω_p(M,g)渐近最优。作者旨在检验这一猜想的正确性，并寻找能够真正刻画体积谱的节集型变分框架。

Method: 1) 构造反例，证明Marques-Neves猜想在任意维数、包括正曲率实解析度量下均不成立。2) 引入较宽松的节集扫描概念：取E_N(M,g)=span{φ_0,…,φ_N}，令a_N∈H^1(P(E_N(g));Z_2)为生成元，定义扫描族N_p^N(g)={Ψ: X为有限复形, Ψ∈C(X,P(E_N(g))), Ψ^*(a_N^p)≠0}；定义节(p,N)-宽度ν_p^N(M,g)=inf_{Ψ∈N_p^N(g)} sup_{x∈dmn(Ψ)} H_g^n({Ψ_x=0})。3) 证明滤过公式ω_p(M,g)=lim_{N→∞}ν_p^N(M,g)。

Result: Marques-Neves猜想被证伪，反例覆盖所有维数及正曲率实解析度量。同时证明了对每个p≥1，体积谱可由节(p,N)-宽度的极限ω_p(M,g)=lim_{N→∞}ν_p^N(M,g)恢复，即较宽松的节集扫描概念可刻画体积谱。

Conclusion: 刚性过强的原猜想不成立，但通过放宽节集扫描的定义（引入高阶Z_2上同调条件与非零上同调类a_N^p），可得到体积谱的滤过刻画。文末还提出若干关于节几何与min-max理论的问题。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n+1},g)$ be a closed smooth Riemannian manifold, and let \[ 0=λ_0\leqλ_1\leq\ldots \] be the spectrum of the Laplace-Beltrami operator, with corresponding real eigenfunctions $\{φ_j\}_{j\geq0}$. Let $\{ω_p(M,g)\}_{p\geq1}$ denote Gromov's volume spectrum. In 2014, Marques-Neves conjectured that the sweepout generated by the nodal sets of $φ_0,\ldots,φ_p$ is asymptotically optimal for $ω_p(M,g)$ as $p\to\infty$. We show that this conjecture is false in every dimension, even for positively curved real-analytic metrics.
  Nevertheless, we show that a less rigid notion of nodal sweepouts recovers the volume spectrum. Let \[ E_N(M,g):=\operatorname{span}\{φ_0,\ldots,φ_N\}, \qquad a_N\in H^1\bigl(\mathbb P(E_N(g));\mathbb Z_2\bigr) \] be the generator, and for $p\geq1$ define \[ \mathcal N_p^N(g) := \left\{ Ψ\mid X\text{ is a finite complex},\ Ψ\in C\bigl(X,\mathbb P(E_N(g))\bigr),\ Ψ^*(a_N^p)\neq0 \right\}. \] We define the nodal $(p,N)$-width by \[ ν_p^N(M,g) := \inf_{Ψ\in\mathcal N_p^N(g)} \sup_{x\in\operatorname{dmn}(Ψ)} \mathcal H_g^{n}\bigl(\{Ψ_x=0\}\bigr). \] We prove the following filtration formula \[ ω_p(M,g) = \lim_{N\to\infty}ν_p^N(M,g) \] for every $p\geq1$. We state several questions on nodal geometry and min-max theory.

</details>


### [93] [Harmonic maps from $\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}$ into $\mathbb{CP}^n$ with least Morse index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37945)
*Qun Chen,Guofang Wang,Mingwei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了从奇数维球面到复射影空间的非常值调和映射，证明其Morse指标等于2n+2当且仅当它是Hopf映射与等距变换及一次全纯映射的复合，并给出零化度的精确值3n²+5n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究调和映射的Morse指标与零化度是几何分析的核心问题。经典工作（Urakawa、Rivière）在低维情形刻画了指标最小的映射，本文旨在将其推广到一般维数，精确分类临界点附近的最优调和映射结构。

Method: 以Hopf纤维化π:S^{2n+1}→CP^n为基础，结合Morse指标与零化度的变分理论，构造并分析复合映射h∘π∘Ξ（Ξ为等距变换，h为一次全纯映射）的稳定性与简并性，进而证明指标下界唯一实现该结构。

Result: 证明Morse指标等于2n+2当且仅当映射可写成h∘π∘Ξ的形式；当n=1时，该结论加强并统一了Urakawa的经典结果与Rivière的近期结果；同时证明此类映射的零化度为3n²+5n。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一般维数下最小指标调和映射的完整分类，并精确计算其零化度，推广了Hopf映射的变分稳定性理论，为高维调和映射的指标研究提供了新工具与判据。

Abstract: Let $u:\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}\to\mathbb{CP}^n$ be a smooth nonconstant harmonic map. We prove that its Morse index is equal to $ 2n+2$ if and only if \begin{equation*}
  u = h \circ π\circ Ξ, \end{equation*} where $Ξ:\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}\to\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}$ is an isometric transformation, $π:\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}\to\mathbb{CP}^n$ is the Hopf map and $h:\mathbb{CP}^n\to\mathbb{CP}^n$ is a holomorphic map with degree one. When $n=1$, it refines a classical result of Urakawa and a recent result of Rivière. Moreover, we prove that $h\circπ\circ Ξ$ has nullity $3n^2+5n$.

</details>


### [94] [Spin and scalar curvature on punctured four manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37948)
*Aditya Kumar,Boyu Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 闭自旋四维流形若签名非零，即使挖去一点也不存在完备的一致正标量曲率度量；挖孔的K3曲面即为一例。该结论在高维不成立。关键新观察是Gromov的μ-气泡可作为Dirac算子APS边值问题的便利边界类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究正标量曲率度量在四维闭自旋流形上的存在性限制，尤其是挖孔（非紧完备）情形的障碍，并厘清该现象与高维情形的维度差异。

Method: 结合Gromov的μ-气泡构造与Dirac算子的APS（Atiyah-Patodi-Singer）边值问题，将μ-气泡作为便于处理的边界，从而导出障碍性结果。

Result: 证明签名非零的闭自旋四维流形在挖去一点后不存在完备一致正标量曲率度量；特别地，挖孔K3曲面不具备此类度量。并指出该命题在高维为假。

Conclusion: 给出了四维（挖孔）正标量曲率度量的新障碍，凸显四维与高维的本质差异；同时提出μ-气泡作为APS边值问题边界的新工具，可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We show that a closed spin four-manifold with nonzero signature, even after being punctured, does not admit a complete metric with uniformly positive scalar curvature. In particular, the punctured $K3$ surface does not admit such a metric. This is known to be false in higher dimensions. A key new observation, which could be of independent interest, is that Gromov's $μ$-bubbles are a convenient class of boundaries for the APS boundary value problem for the Dirac operator.

</details>


### [95] [Opers on transversely holomorphic foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38040)
*Indranil Biswas,Sorin Dumitrescu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究余维2光滑叶状结构的横截复结构、横截复射影结构与横截oper，证明了与横截复射影结构自然相伴的横截全纯CP^1丛的唯一性定理，并证明与横截PGL(r,C)-oper相伴的横截全纯滤过CP^{r-1}丛的类似唯一性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在余维2光滑叶状结构上建立横截复结构、横截复射影结构与横截opers之间的对应关系，并刻画其相关丛的唯一性。

Method: 研究余维2光滑叶状结构上的横截复结构、横截复射影结构以及横截oper，构造与之自然相伴的横截全纯丛（CP^1丛与滤过CP^{r-1}丛），并证明相应的唯一性定理。

Result: 对横截复射影结构自然相伴的横截全纯CP^1丛证明了唯一性定理；对横截PGL(r,C)-oper自然相伴的横截全纯滤过CP^{r-1}丛也证明了类似的唯一性定理。

Conclusion: 余维2光滑叶状结构上的横截复射影结构与横截opers可通过其自然相伴的横截全纯丛（及其滤过结构）唯一确定，为叶状结构的横截几何研究提供了唯一性刻画。

Abstract: For real codimension two smooth foliations, we study the transversely complex structures, transversely complex projective structures and transverse opers associated to the foliation. We prove a uniqueness theorem for the transversely holomorphic ${\mathbb C}{\mathbb P}^1$--bundle naturally associated with a transversely complex projective structures. A similar uniqueness theorem is proved for the transversely holomorphic filtered ${\mathbb C}{\mathbb P}^{r-1}$--bundle naturally associated to a transverse ${\rm PGL}(r,{\mathbb C})$--oper.

</details>


### [96] [Positive sectional curvature on the exotic $8$-sphere and order-three homotopy $10$-spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38126)
*Fernando Galaz-García*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明奇异8维球面以及两个代表三阶元素的定向同伦10维球面均存在严格正截面曲率的黎曼度量，方法是将Sperança的特殊S³-S³丛模型与He-Liu-Yau的相容圆盘构造相结合，并通过Reiser-Wraith粘合定理完成构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正截面曲率度量的存在性问题上，奇异球面与同伦球面（尤其是高维、非标准微分结构的情形）是长期关注的核心对象。此前Sperança给出了特殊S³-S³丛模型，但如何在这些奇异微分结构上实际构造出严格正截面曲率度量仍待解决。

Method: 作者证明相关丛具有等变极正规形式，其转移函数沿子午线为常数且共轭等变；指出He-Liu-Yau构造中依赖于表示的部分只需对无穷小作用场的一致界；并验证Sperança的8维与10维例子满足该界。随后将南北半球度量通过具有匹配边界度量和相容第二基本形式的Reiser-Wraith粘合定理进行拼接。

Result: 奇异8维球面、以及两个代表三阶元素的定向同伦10维球面均被构造出严格正截面曲率的黎曼度量；同时给出的极正规形式条件与一致界条件为构造提供了可验证的技术框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将Sperança丛模型与He-Liu-Yau相容圆盘构造有效结合，借助Reiser-Wraith粘合定理，成功在若干奇异与同伦球面上得到正截面曲率度量，扩展了高维正曲率度量的存在性结果。

Abstract: We prove that the exotic smooth $8$-sphere and both oriented homotopy $10$-spheres representing elements of order three admit Riemannian metrics with strictly positive sectional curvature. The construction combines Sperança's special $S^3$-$S^3$ bundle models with the compatible-disk construction of He, Liu and Yau. We show that the relevant bundles admit equivariant polar normal forms, with transition functions that are constant along meridians and conjugation-equivariant. We also show that the representation-dependent part of the He--Liu--Yau construction requires only a uniform bound on the infinitesimal action fields. Sperança's $8$- and $10$-dimensional examples satisfy this bound. The resulting northern and southern metrics have matching boundary metrics and compatible second fundamental forms, so the Reiser--Wraith gluing theorem gives the required positively curved metrics.

</details>


### [97] [Positive Sectional Curvature on All Smooth 7-spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29426)
*Yang-Hui He,Ziran Liu,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在每一个光滑同伦七维球面上构造具有严格正截面曲率的光滑黎曼度量，覆盖全部28个定向微分同胚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正截面曲率度量在拓扑与几何之间建立了强约束，但高维（尤其是七维）情形下已知例子稀少；同伦七维球面共有28个相异微分结构，其是否均能承载正曲率度量长期缺乏统一构造。

Method: 对每个光滑结构，分别构造两个七维圆盘上的正曲率度量，使其在给定粘合映射诱导的边界度量下相互吻合，再利用粘合定理将两个圆盘拼接，得到闭流形上的光滑正曲率度量。

Result: 所构造的粘合方法对全部28个定向微分同胚类均适用，从而在每个光滑同伦七维球面上获得严格正截面曲率的光滑黎曼度量。

Conclusion: 所有光滑同伦七维球面均可赋予严格正截面曲率度量，证明正曲率并非区分这28个微分结构的障碍。

Abstract: We construct a smooth Riemannian metric with strictly positive sectional curvature on every smooth homotopy seven-sphere. For each smooth structure, we construct positively curved metrics on two seven-dimensional disks whose induced boundary metrics agree under the prescribed attaching map. Under this identification, a gluing theorem then yields the required smooth metric on the closed manifold. The construction applies to all 28 oriented diffeomorphism classes.

</details>

### [98] [Counterexamples and gap phenomena for derived delooping levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35849)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文回答了Guo与Igusa关于derived delooping level与sub-derived delooping level比较、其与opposite big finitistic dimension关系及差异幅度的问题，证明ddell(A)≤sub-ddell(A)，给出高维差异反例，并构造使差异无界的单项代数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guo与Igusa引入derived delooping level与sub-derived delooping level作为delooping level的细化，并提出三个问题：两者的比较关系、derived delooping level是否总等于opposite big finitistic dimension、若不等差异能有多大。本文旨在系统回答这些问题。

Method: 对任意Artin代数A证明ddell(A)≤sub-ddell(A)；构造显式八维代数C说明高阶级别不满足对比；计算Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata单项族的所有k-derived与k-sub-derived delooping level；构造十一维单项代数Λ与十维非单项变体，以及满足Findim(B_r^op)=1且ddell(B_r)=2r+1的单项代数族B_r。

Result: 对普通次数问题给出肯定回答；给出八维代数C满足k-sub-ddell(C)=0<1=k-ddell(C)（k≥2）；证明Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata族满足k-ddell(A_{n,s})=Findim(A_{n,s}^op)，故不构成文献中暗示的反例；构造十一维单项代数Λ满足Findim(Λ^op)=1<2=ddell(Λ)=dell(Λ)，并给出十维非单项变体；构造单项代数族B_r满足Findim(B_r^op)=1而ddell(B_r)=2r+1。

Conclusion: ddell与sub-ddell在普通次数下满足ddell(A)≤sub-ddell(A)，但高阶级别可以严格不等；derived delooping level并不总等于opposite big finitistic dimension；差值ddell(A)-Findim(A^op)即使在opposite big finitistic dimension固定的单项代数中也可无界。

Abstract: Guo and Igusa introduced the derived and sub-derived delooping levels as refinements of the delooping level and asked how these two invariants compare, whether the derived delooping level always coincides with the opposite big finitistic dimension, and, if not, how large their difference can be. We answer these questions. First, we prove that every Artin algebra $A$ satisfies $\mathrm{ddell}(A)\leq\mathrm{sub\mbox{-}ddell}(A)$. This gives a positive answer to the ordinary-degree comparison problem, whereas a single explicit eight-dimensional algebra $C$ satisfies $k\mbox{-}\mathrm{sub\mbox{-}ddell}(C)=0<1=k\mbox{-}\mathrm{ddell}(C)$ for every $k\geq 2$. We then compute, for every $k\geq 1$, all $k$-derived and $k$-sub-derived delooping levels of the monomial family of Barrios--Lanzilotta--Mata. In particular, that family satisfies $k\mbox{-}\mathrm{ddell}(A_{n,s})=\mathrm{Findim}(A_{n,s}^{\mathrm{op}})$ for every $k\geq 1$, so it does not provide the counterexample suggested in the later literature. By contrast, we construct an eleven-dimensional monomial algebra $Λ$ over an arbitrary field such that $\mathrm{Findim}(Λ^{\mathrm{op}})=1<2=\mathrm{ddell}(Λ)=\mathrm{dell}(Λ)$. A ten-dimensional non-monomial variant is also given. Finally, we construct a family of finite-dimensional monomial algebras $B_r$ satisfying $\mathrm{Findim}(B_r^{\mathrm{op}})=1$ and $\mathrm{ddell}(B_r)=2r+1$. Thus the difference $\mathrm{ddell}(A)-\mathrm{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})$ is unbounded even among monomial algebras with fixed opposite big finitistic dimension.

</details>


### [99] [Relative Suspension and Higher Delooping for Support $τ$-Tilting Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36444)
*Mingfei Xu,Xiaojin Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为支持τ-倾斜模生成的正合范畴构造相对悬挂函子，用伴随的单位刻画高阶delooping，证明其与普通高阶delooping相差一个指标平移，并应用于带局部系数与自同构扭转的循环Nakayama代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gélinas在Adv. Math.(2022)中给出了（高阶）delooping的刻画，本文希望将其推广到相对情形，即在支持τ-倾斜模生成的正合范畴上建立相对悬挂与相对高阶delooping理论，并厘清相对不变量与普通不变量之间的关系。

Method: 对由支持τ-倾斜模生成的正合范畴构造相对悬挂（relative suspension）；利用所得伴随的单位（units）刻画相对高阶delooping；通过构造具体模给出相对level与普通k-delooping level的指标平移比较；证明张量诱导保持相对构造与诱导模上的高阶delooping level；并将其应用于带任意局部系数和自同构扭转的循环Nakayama代数。

Result: 证明与普通高阶delooping的比较需要指标平移1：对任意k,d≥1，存在模其普通k-delooping level为零而相对level为d；同时证明张量诱导保持相对构造及诱导模上的高阶delooping level。对带任意局部系数与自同构扭转的循环Nakayama代数，其左、右小的与大的finitistic dimension，以及普通与导出的delooping level，均等于底层Nakayama代数的finitistic dimension。

Conclusion: 本文把Gélinas的delooping刻画推广到相对框架，揭示相对与普通高阶delooping之间存在指标平移的现象，并通过张量诱导与循环Nakayama代数的应用展示了该理论的适用性与计算能力。

Abstract: We construct relative suspension for the exact category generated by a support $τ$-tilting module and characterize higher delooping by the units of the resulting adjunction, which are the generalizations of Gélinas's results in Adv. Math.(2022). We prove that comparison with ordinary higher delooping requires a shift of one in the index. More precisely, for every $k,d\ge1$, there is a module whose ordinary $k$-delooping level is zero and whose relative level is $d$. We also prove that tensor induction preserves the relative constructions and higher delooping levels on induced modules. As an application, we study cyclic Nakayama algebras with arbitrary local coefficients and automorphism twists. Their left and right little and big finitistic dimensions and ordinary and derived delooping levels all equal the finitistic dimension of the underlying Nakayama algebra.

</details>


### [100] [Complexes of modules over hierarchical groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36823)
*Ioannis Emmanouil,Olympia Talelli*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Kropholler的LH运算与Talelli的Φ运算迭代生成的群类$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$，描述了其群代数上投射模无环复形的余核结构，并证明了关于投射、平坦与内射模无环复形的收缩性结论，应用于完全分解、周期上同调及Gorenstein同调维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于将群类$\mathfrak{F}$通过Kropholler的LH运算与Talelli的Φ运算扩张为群类$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$，并在这一更广的群类上刻画群代数上无环复形的性质，从而推广已有的同调代数结果，并应用于完全分解、周期上同调以及Gorenstein与普通同调维数之间的关系。

Method: 方法上，作者以交换环为系数，考察$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-群代数上投射模无环复形的余核结构；对$\mathbb{Q}G$-模的投射（相应地，平坦）无环复形证明其可收缩（相应地，纯无环）；对无挠群$G$证明$\mathbb{Z}G$-模有相同结论；并给出内射模无环复形的类似结果。

Result: 主要结果包括：若$G$为$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-群，则任何投射$\mathbb{Q}G$-模无环复形可收缩，任何平坦$\mathbb{Q}G$-模无环复形纯无环；若$G$无挠，则投射或平坦$\mathbb{Z}G$-模无环复形具有相同结论；内射模无环复形也有类似结果。

Conclusion: 结论表明，在$\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-群上无环复形具有良好的收缩或纯无环性质，并由此得到关于允许完全分解的模、若干步后具有周期上同调的群以及群代数上Gorenstein维数与普通同调维数之间关系的若干应用。

Abstract: Let $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$ be the group class obtained from the class $\mathfrak{F}$ of finite groups by iterated applications of Kropholler's operation ${\scriptstyle{\bf LH}}$ and Talelli's operation $Φ$. We describe the structure of the cokenels of acyclic complexes of projective modules over the group algebra of $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-groups with coefficients in a commutative ring. If $G$ is an $\overline{\mathfrak{F}}$-group, we show that any acyclic complex of projective (respectively, flat) $\mathbb{Q}G$-modules is contractible (respectively, pure acyclic). If $G$ is torsion-free, the same conclusions hold for acyclic complexes of projective or flat $\mathbb{Z}G$-modules. Analogous results are valid for acyclic complexes of injective modules. We present some applications regarding modules that admit complete resolutions, groups with periodic cohomology after some steps and the relation between Gorenstein and ordinary homological dimensions for modules over group algebras.

</details>


### [101] [Dirac operators for Cartan motion groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37245)
*Spyridon Afentoulidis-Almpanis,Eyal Subag*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究Cartan运动群的代数Dirac算子，建立其基本性质（含Casimir型元素的平方公式），定义Dirac上同调，并证明Vogan猜想的强形式：Dirac系列中的模由其Dirac上同调完全决定，同时显式确定了Cartan运动群的Dirac系列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将实约化Lie群的Dirac上同调与Vogan猜想理论推广到其对应的Cartan运动群，并揭示两者通过代数形变族Dirac理论的联系（零纤维）。

Method: 构造Cartan运动群的代数Dirac算子；证明涉及自然Casimir型元素的平方公式；定义Dirac上同调；证明显式Vogan猜想强形式；并将该理论实现为对应实约化群Dirac形变族理论的零纤维。

Result: 得到平方公式与Dirac上同调定义；证明Dirac系列模由其Dirac上同调完全决定；显式确定Cartan运动群的Dirac系列；给出其作为形变族零纤维的构造。

Conclusion: Cartan运动群具备完整的Dirac理论与Vogan型刚性结论，且可由实约化群Dirac形变理论统一刻画。

Abstract: We introduce and study the algebraic Dirac operator for the Cartan motion group associated with a real reductive Lie group. We establish its fundamental properties, including a square formula involving a natural Casimir-type element, define the corresponding notion of Dirac cohomology, and prove a strong form of Vogan's conjecture: a module in the Dirac series is completely determined by its Dirac cohomology. We explicitly determine the Dirac series of a Cartan motion group.
  We explain how the Dirac theory of a Cartan motion group can be obtained as the zero fiber of the recently introduced Dirac theory for the algebraic deformation family of the corresponding real reductive group.

</details>


### [102] [Absolutely irreducible modules via James triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37430)
*Nora Krauß*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 将James关于对称群绝对不可约模的显式构造方法推广到一般有限群，利用适当的子群数据构造模块，使其关联的商模为绝对不可约或零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对不可约KG-模的构造是表示论中的基本问题；James已用μ-表的子群数据对对称群Sn给出显式解，本文旨在探究该方法能否推广到对称群之外的一般有限群。

Method: 从有限群中辨识出合适的子群数据（类似James构造中与μ-表相关的数据），并据此构造模，进而分析其关联因子模的性质。

Result: 利用所识别的子群数据构造出相应模块，并证明其关联的因子模为绝对不可约或为零。

Conclusion: James的构造思路可超出对称群范围：通过选取合适的子群数据，可在一般有限群上构造出因子模绝对不可约或退化为零的模，为绝对不可约模的显式构造提供了推广。

Abstract: The construction of absolutely irreducible $KG$-modules for a field $K$ and a finite group $G$ is a basic problem in representation theory. For the symmetric group $\operatorname{S}_n$, Gordon James gave an explicit solution using subgroup data associated to a $μ$-tableau $t$ for partitions $μ\vdash n$. Here, we want to investigate in which way James's method can be extended beyond symmetric groups. We identify suitable subgroup data from a finite group and use it to construct a module such that an associated factor module is absolutely irreducible or zero.

</details>


### [103] [A Galois-equivariant Dade-Glauberman-Nagao correspondence with central defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37558)
*Shi Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在中心亏格块的广义 Dade-Glauberman-Nagao 对应之上，构造了零高度普通不可约特征之间的对应，并证明其等变性、块关系及射影表示层面的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中心亏格块的情形下，需要对广义 Dade-Glauberman-Nagao 对应进一步建立零高度普通不可约特征之间的精细对应，且要求该对应在群自同构与 Galois 自同构下等变，并满足 H-三元组之间的块关系，从而为 Alperin-McKay-Navarro 约化提供适用形式。

Method: 构造基于中心亏格子群群代数的重数代数、残差半线性实现的规范化整提升，以及分次角同构；对每一对对应特征，使一对关联射影表示实现匹配的因子集、相等的中心标量、同步混合比较函数，并对每个中间群给出相容的块对应。

Result: 建立了零高度普通不可约特征之间的对应，该对应在群自同构与 Galois 自同构下等变，满足 H-三元组间的块关系；对应特征的射影表示在因子集、中心标量、混合比较函数与中间群块对应上相容。

Conclusion: 该构造统一处理所有中心特征纤维（含非平凡中心特征），并得到适用于 Alperin-McKay-Navarro 约化中正规 p-截断的形式。

Abstract: We construct a correspondence between ordinary irreducible characters of height zero above the generalized Dade-Glauberman-Nagao correspondence for blocks with central defect. The correspondence is equivariant under group and Galois automorphisms and satisfies a block relation between $\mathcal{H}$-triples. For each pair of corresponding characters, one pair of associated projective representations realizes matching factor sets, equal central scalars, synchronized mixed comparison functions and compatible block correspondences for every intermediate group. The construction uses the multiplicity algebra over the group algebra of the central defect subgroup, a normalized integral lift of a residual semilinear realization, and a graded corner isomorphism. It treats all central-character fibers, including those with nontrivial central character. We also obtain a form suited to normal $p$-sections in an Alperin-McKay-Navarro reduction.

</details>


### [104] [Asymptotics for number of indecomposable components of tensor powers of the natural $\mathrm{SL}_2$-module in odd characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37706)
*Aranya Lahiri,Nai-Heng Sheu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 设 $K$ 为奇特征 $p$ 的代数闭域，$G=\mathrm{SL}_2(K)$，$V$ 为自然表示。记 $b_k$ 为 $V^{\otimes k}$ 的 $G$-不可分解因子的个数（计重数）。则 $b_k$ 渐近于 $\omega(k)k^{-\delta_p}2^k$，其中 $\delta_p=1-\log_{p^2}\big((p+1)/2\big)$，$\omega(t)$ 为光滑、严格正、乘法 $p^2$-周期的函数。此外，$t^{-\delta}\omega(t)$ 作为一正权 $3/2$ theta 函数的缩放副本经重整化卷积后的极限密度出现，该 theta 函数由 $(0,p)$ 上 Dirichlet 热核的边界热流得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $\mathrm{SL}_2(K)$（$p$ 为奇特征）自然表示张量幂的不可分解因子计数 $b_k$ 的渐近行为，并揭示其与模形式（theta 函数、Dirichlet 热核边界热流）之间的深刻联系。

Method: 利用不可分解因子的计数 $b_k$；引入乘法 $p^2$-周期的光滑正函数 $\omega(t)$；并通过重整化卷积与缩放的权 $3/2$ theta 函数（源自 $(0,p)$ 上 Dirichlet 热核的边界热流）的极限密度分析进行刻画。

Result: 得到渐近公式 $b_k \sim \omega(k)k^{-\delta_p}2^k$，其中 $\delta_p=1-\log_{p^2}\big((p+1)/2\big)$，$\omega(t)$ 光滑、严格正且乘法 $p^2$-周期；并证明 $t^{-\delta}\omega(t)$ 是某正权 $3/2$ theta 函数缩放副本重整化卷积的极限密度。

Conclusion: $V^{\otimes k}$ 的不可分解因子计数具有含周期振荡因子的幂律-指数渐近形式，且其密度函数可由来源于 Dirichlet 热核边界热流的 $3/2$ 权 theta 函数刻画，建立了模表示论与模形式/热核之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Let $K$ be an algebraically closed field of odd characteristic $p$, let $G= {\rm SL}_2(K)$, and let $V$ be the natural representation of $G$. Let $b_k$ denote the number of $G$-indecomposable factors of $V^{\otimes k}$, counted with multiplicity, and let $δ_p=1-\log_{p^2}\!\bigl(\tfrac{p+1}{2}\bigr)$. Then there exists a smooth, strictly positive, multiplicatively $p^2$-periodic function $ω(t)$ such that $b_k$ is asymptotic to $ω(k)k^{-δ_p}2^k$. We also show that $t^{-δ}ω(t)$ arises as the limiting density of renormalized convolutions of rescaled copies of a positive weight $3/2$ theta function, obtained from the boundary heat flux of the Dirichlet heat kernel on $(0,p)$.

</details>

### [105] [The derived Picard group of Fukaya categories of closed surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37403)
*Dongjian Wu,Nantao Zhang*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文确定了闭可定向曲面 $\\Sigma_g$ ($g\\geq2$) 的分裂闭、严格无阻碍、二周期 Fukaya 范畴 $\\mathscr F_g$ 的导出 Picard 群，证明了 arXiv:2006.09689 猜想的增强形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 Fukaya 范畴的导出 Picard 群，并验证关于该群结构的猜想（arXiv:2006.09689 的增强形式）。

Method: 在复 Novikov 域 $\\Lambda$ 上研究分裂闭、严格无阻碍、二周期的 Fukaya 范畴 $\\mathscr F_g$，计算其导出 Picard 群 $\\operatorname{DPic}_\\Lambda(\\mathscr F_g)$。

Result: 得到 $\\operatorname{DPic}_\\Lambda(\\mathscr F_g)\\cong (H^1(\\Sigma_g;\\Lambda^\\times)\\rtimes\\pi_0\\operatorname{Diff}^+(\\Sigma_g))\\times\\mathbb Z/2\\mathbb Z$。

Conclusion: 证明了 arXiv:2006.09689 猜想的增强形式；作为推论，导出 Picard 群本身可确定曲面的亏格。

Abstract: Let $Σ_g$ be a closed oriented surface of genus $g\geq2$, and let $\mathscr F_g$ be its split-closed, strictly unobstructed, two-periodic Fukaya category over the complex Novikov field $Λ$. We determine the derived Picard group \[
  \operatorname{DPic}_Λ(\mathscr F_g)
  \cong
  \bigl(H^1(Σ_g;Λ^\times)
  \rtimesπ_0\operatorname{Diff}^+(Σ_g)\bigr)
  \times\mathbb Z/2\mathbb Z. \] This proves the enhanced form of the conjecture of arXiv:2006.09689. As a corollary, we obtain that the derived Picard group itself determines the genus of the surface.

</details>


### [106] [The Ekeland--Hofer--Zehnder Capacity of rotated $L_p$-Ellipsoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37794)
*Vardan Oganesyan*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文给出了旋转 $L_p$-椭球的 EHZ 容量与柱容量的显式公式，并提供了一个估计 EHZ 容量的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHZ 容量与柱容量是辛几何中的核心不变量，但通常难以计算显式值；$L_p$-椭球作为一类非平凡的凸体，其容量计算具有代表性，因此作者希望给出可计算的显式结果。

Method: 通过对旋转 $L_p$-椭球进行解析计算，推导 EHZ 容量与柱容量的显式表达式；并在此基础上设计用于估计 EHZ 容量的数值算法。

Result: 得到了旋转 $L_p$-椭球的 EHZ 容量与柱容量的显式公式，并给出了可估计 EHZ 容量的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为特定凸体的辛容量提供了显式、可计算的结果，并给出了实用的容量估计方法。

Abstract: We find explicit formulas for the EHZ and cylindrical capacities of rotated $L_{p}$-ellipsoids. In addition, we provide an algorithm to estimate the EHZ capacity.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [One-dimensional quantum Gibbs states in constant circuit depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35973)
*Saúl Pilatowsky-Cameo,Georgios Styliaris,Ainesh Bakshi,Daniel Malz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 任意自旋链的吉布斯态在常数温度下可精确分解为有限长度量子比特块上乘积态的混合，从而可由常数深度局域幺正电路制备；热纠缠在空间结构上受到强限制，并对量子热化与费米子系统给出类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画热平衡态（吉布斯态）的纠缠结构是量子多体物理的核心问题。既有认知多集中于零温基态或特定模型，对一般自旋链在常数温度下热纠缠的空间范围、可制备复杂度及其热化含义缺乏统一且紧致的结论，本文旨在给出此类强限制与精确分解。

Method: 通过将吉布斯态精确表示为连续量子比特块上长度有界的乘积态之混合（凸组合），并推广至一维局域费米子哈密顿量，构造高斯态上常数深度局域费米子电路的混合分解；据此导出常数深度局域幺正电路制备方案与高效经典预处理。

Result: 证明纠缠深度与纠缠宽度（多体纠缠度量）均被常数界所限；可局域化纠缠在超过常数阈值距离后严格为零。对具非简并谱隙的平移不变自旋链哈密顿量，任意常数有效温度下最大熵系综中典型低复杂度纯态经幺正演化后与吉布斯态局域不可区分。费米子情形：任意一维局域费米子哈密顿量的吉布斯态精确为作用在高斯态上的常数深度局域费米子电路之混合。

Conclusion: 热平衡态在空间纠缠上本质是"浅层"的：其多体纠缠范围与可局域化纠缠均受常数限制，并可由常数深度电路高效制备。这为热化现象提供了低复杂度初态视角的解释，并将结论统一扩展至费米子系统。

Abstract: We prove that the Gibbs state of any spin chain can be exactly decomposed into a mixture of product states over contiguous blocks of qubits with bounded length, at any constant temperature. As a consequence, any such Gibbs state can be prepared by local unitary circuits of constant depth, with efficient classical preprocessing. We place strong limits on the spatial structure of thermal entanglement: both the entanglement depth and entanglement width (measures of multiparticle entanglement) are bounded by constants, while the localizable entanglement (the bipartite entanglement that can be generated with the aid of local measurements and classical communication) vanishes exactly beyond a constant threshold distance. Our result also has implications for quantum thermalization: for any translation-invariant spin-chain Hamiltonian with nondegenerate spectral gaps, typical low-complexity pure states drawn from a maximally entropic ensemble at any constant effective temperature become locally indistinguishable from the Gibbs state upon unitary evolution. Additionally, we obtain an analogous decomposition for fermionic systems: the Gibbs state of any 1D local fermionic Hamiltonian is exactly a mixture of constant-depth local fermionic circuits acting on Gaussian states.

</details>


### [108] [Characterizing quantum precision enhancement for multiple currents in open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35982)
*Khalak Mahadeviya,Sheikh Parvez Mandal,Mahasweta Pandit,Javier Prior,Mark T. Mitchison,Saulo V. Moreira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了非平衡稳态下多电流动力学不确定性关系（MKUR），并以此为基准区分量子器件中由相干演化导致的非经典电流涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典KUR用动力学活度约束单个电流的涨落，但无法刻画多电流之间的关联。为量化量子相干性对联合电流精度的提升，需要一个包含电流相关性的推广界限，并与经典对应体系对比。

Method: 推导多电流KUR（MKUR），给出经典随机过程在非平衡稳态下多电流联合精度的限制；构造具有相同平均电流与能级结构及非相干资源的经典模拟器，将量子体系的联合电流涨落与之比较以判定是否违反MKUR。

Result: MKUR的违反意味着量子相干演化使联合电流涨落低于同结构经典马尔可夫体系的下限；由于包含电流间关联，MKUR的违反可在单电流KUR仍满足的参数区间识别非经典涨落。以相干驱动二能级系统和受寄生环境耦合的三能级热机模型为例验证了两种界限的角色互补性。

Conclusion: MKUR为多电流精度提供了经典基准，可作为判定量子器件非经典涨落的判别准则，且与单电流KUR互补，在多电流及关联重要时尤为有效。

Abstract: The kinetic uncertainty relation (KUR) constrains fluctuations of individual currents in classical nonequilibrium systems in terms of the dynamical activity, which quantifies the average number of transitions per unit of time. Here, we derive a multi-current KUR (MKUR) establishing the precision limit for multiple currents generated in classical stochastic processes in the nonequilibrium steady state. We then apply our theory to identify quantum devices whose correlated current fluctuations surpass this classical limit by comparing the quantum system to a classical emulator with the same average currents in the steady state. In this way, violations of the emulator's MKUR have a clear physical meaning, i.e., that the quantum-coherent evolution reduces joint current fluctuations below what is possible for a classical Markovian system with the same energy-level structure and incoherent resources. Since the MKUR incorporates correlations between currents, its violation pinpoints nonclassical fluctuations in parameter regimes where the single-current KUR is satisfied. We illustrate the essential and complementary role of these bounds for characterizing joint current precision with two examples: a coherently driven two-level system and an experimentally motivated model of a three-level heat engine subject to parasitic environmental couplings.

</details>


### [109] [Deciding the Attainability of the Multiparameter Quantum Fisher Information is NP-Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35975)
*Lorcan O. Conlon,Laura Shou,V Vijendran,Kishor Bharti,Aritra Das,Simon K. Yung,Jacob M. Taylor,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Jun Suzuki,Syed M. Assad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，判定量子Fisher信息给出的多参数估计精度极限能否通过单体测量精确达到，这一问题是NP困难的，即使限制在实常数秩量子模型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QFI给出多参数同时估计的精度下界，但最优测量之间的不相容性可能导致该极限不可达。纯态和满秩态的可达性条件已知，但一般的可达性条件长期未解决，甚至被Horodecki等人列为量子信息论五大开放问题之一。

Method: 通过归约证明：以判定给定二分密度矩阵是否可分离这一NP困难问题作为源问题，将单体测量下QFI精确可达性判定归约到该问题，论证即便对实、常数秩的受限量子模型该判定仍是NP困难的。

Result: 证明单体测量下QFI可达性的精确判定是NP困难的，且该困难性在实常数秩模型的受限集合中依然成立。

Conclusion: QFI可达性一般条件难以获得并非数学技巧不足，而是根本性的计算障碍：除非P=NP，否则不存在一般性的高效可计算的充要判据。

Abstract: The quantum Fisher information (QFI) sets a fundamental bound on the attainable precision when estimating multiple parameters simultaneously. Incompatibility among the individually optimal measurements can, in some cases, imply that the precision limit set by the QFI is not attainable. In certain special cases, including pure states and full-rank states, the exact conditions for when the precision limit set by the QFI can be saturated are known. However, general conditions for the attainability of the QFI with measurements on individual copies of the quantum state have long been sought. Indeed, this was recently stated as one of the five problems in quantum information theory highlighted by [P. Horodecki et al, PRX Quantum 3, 010101 (2022)]. In this work we prove that exact attainability with individual measurements is NP-hard to decide, even for a restricted set of real, constant-rank quantum models.
