The source problem for our proof is the NP-hard problem of deciding whether a given bipartite density matrix is separable or not. Our result shows that the longstanding difficulty in obtaining general conditions for QFI attainability reflects a fundamental computational obstruction, rather than merely a limitation of existing mathematical techniques: unless P=NP, no efficiently computable necessary-and-sufficient criterion can exist in general.

</details>


### [110] [Digital Quantum Algorithms for Generating and Utilizing Spin Squeezed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36004)
*Mingru Yang,Ruby Wei,Chao Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在数字量子计算机上高效生成与利用自旋压缩态（SSS）的算法，包括深度为 O(log 1/ξ) 的自适应局域线路协议制备压缩参数为 ξ 的 SSS，并证明该深度对置换对称 SSS 最优，同时可确定性制备 Dicke 态，噪声模拟预测 55 比特下超越标准量子极限 4.2 dB 的度量增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋压缩态传统上被视为量子度量的模拟资源，难以直接应用于数字量子设备；本文旨在为近期数字量子平台提供可高效制备并利用 SSS 的算法，从而在含噪中规模量子设备上实现量子度量优势。

Method: 提出自适应局域线路协议，以 O(log 1/ξ) 深度制备压缩参数为 ξ 的 SSS；通过推导纠缠熵的匹配下界证明该深度对置换对称 SSS 最优；并利用压缩范式确定性制备 Dicke 态；在真实噪声模型下对制备协议进行经典模拟。

Result: 自适应协议相对非自适应方法实现指数级深度改进；证明 O(log 1/ξ) 深度最优；Dicke 态制备资源渐近少于既有方法；在包含辅助比特的 55 个超导量子比特上，噪声模拟预测超越标准量子极限 4.2 dB 的度量增益。

Conclusion: 该工作为在近期数字量子设备上开展量子度量铺平道路，表明数字量子计算平台可有效生成并利用自旋压缩态这一关键度量资源。

Abstract: Spin squeezed states (SSSs) are conventionally viewed as analog resources for quantum metrology. Here we develop algorithms to efficiently generate and exploit SSSs on a digital quantum computer. We introduce an adaptive local-circuit protocol that prepares SSSs with squeezing parameter $ξ$ in depth $\mathrm{O}\left(\log{1/ξ}\right)$, yielding an exponential improvement over non-adaptive approaches. We prove that this depth is optimal for permutation symmetric SSSs by deriving a matching lower bound on entanglement entropy. We further leverage the squeezing paradigm to deterministically prepare the Dicke states using asymptotically fewer resources than prior methods. Remarkably, classical simulations of our preparation protocols under realistic noise models predicts a metrological gain of 4.2 dB beyond the standard quantum limit with 55 superconducting qubits in total including ancillae. Our work paves the way for performing quantum metrology on near-term digital quantum devices.

</details>


### [111] [Chern-number estimation: nearly optimal quantum algorithm and provable quantum speedup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35978)
*Soichiro Imamura,Shintaro Ae,Kazuki Sakamoto,Ryu Hayakawa,Chusei Kiumi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个近乎最优的量子算法，用于估计二维参数环面上光滑哈密顿量族的陈数，并给出了匹配的最坏情况oracle下界，证明了其最优性（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陈数是刻画拓扑物相的核心不变量，将量子态几何与鲁棒物理可观测量联系起来。确定陈数的高效方法对理解量子物质至关重要，因此需要研究量子计算机估计这一量子化拓扑不变量的最优效率。

Method: 算法在单个参考点制备两个基态副本并进行输运与复用，利用广义量子信号处理相干地累积几何相位，适用于具有唯一、有隙基态的光滑哈密顿量族，包括相互作用多体系统。

Result: 算法实现哈密顿量oracle演化时间 $\tilde{O}(L_xL_y/Δ_{\min}^3)$；证明了匹配的最坏情况oracle下界（即使陈数被限定为零或一），确立了至多对数因子的最优性。对于逆多项式能隙且提供引导态的局域哈密顿量族，精确计算陈数属于 $\mathsf{FBQP}$ 且是 $\mathsf{BQP}$-hard，仅判定陈数为零还是一已是 $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete。

Conclusion: 结果确立了量子计算机估计量子化拓扑不变量的高效性，并为在刻画拓扑物相方面实现量子优势提供了证据。

Abstract: Topological phases of matter are characterized by global properties of quantum states beyond conventional local order parameters. The Chern number is a central invariant in this characterization, linking the geometry of quantum states to robust physical observables and making its determination key to understanding quantum matter. We construct a nearly optimal quantum algorithm for estimating the Chern number of a smooth Hamiltonian family over a two-dimensional parameter torus with a unique, gapped ground state, including interacting many-body systems. The algorithm transports and reuses two ground-state copies prepared at a single reference point and uses generalized quantum signal processing to coherently accumulate geometric phases. It achieves a Hamiltonian-oracle evolution time of $\tilde{O}(L_xL_y/Δ_{\min}^3)$, where $L_x,L_y$ bound the parameter derivatives of the Hamiltonian and $Δ_{\min}$ is a known spectral-gap lower bound. We prove a matching worst-case oracle lower bound, even when the Chern number is promised to be zero or one, establishing optimality up to polylogarithmic factors. For local Hamiltonian families with an inverse-polynomial gap and a supplied guiding state, exact Chern-number computation is in $\mathsf{FBQP}$ and is $\mathsf{BQP}$-hard, and merely deciding whether the Chern number is zero or one is already $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete. These results establish how efficiently quantum computers can estimate a quantized topological invariant and provide evidence for quantum advantage in characterizing topological phases.

</details>


### [112] [Fast Hamiltonian engineering from cut polytope geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36006)
*Thomas Joachim Friese,Özgün Kum,Aram W. Harrow,Martin Kliesch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对Hamiltonian engineering (HE) 问题提出统一框架：将其重构为复k-割多胞体问题并证明NP完全，随后松弛到椭球体并采用Krivine型舍入，得到O(√m)近似比的模拟量子运行时间，在三类基准上达到近优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamiltonian engineering用于在原生纠缠系统哈密顿量与受限控制下模拟目标哈密顿量的量子动力学，应用涵盖量子门设计到模拟量子模拟。观察到许多系统只需控制脉冲与系统哈密顿量之间的特定对易关系即可将HE表述为线性规划，作者希望为2-local qubit、qudit及费米子系统建立统一且有理论保证的框架。

Method: 将时间最优HE重构为复k-割多胞体问题（k为对易关系中不同相位数目），证明对每个有限k均NP完全；将多胞体松弛为椭球体并应用Krivine型舍入，得到由系统与目标哈密顿量启发的脉冲用于线性规划；推导保证线性规划可靠可行的informed脉冲数量；混入均匀采样脉冲，从O(m)个脉冲（m为相互作用项数）获得接近松弛值的解；结合最优量子运行时间的上下界。

Result: 得到O(√m)近似比。在全连接Ising模型、qudit的手性时钟模型及费米Harper-Hofstadter模型基准上，凡最优可计算处均达到近优量子运行时间，费米情形运行时间随晶格尺寸饱和，并以O(m)个脉冲优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为模拟量子模拟器的自动编程建立了具有可证明保证的统一方法。

Abstract: Hamiltonian engineering (HE) simulates quantum dynamics under a target Hamiltonian using a native entangling system Hamiltonian and restricted control, with applications from quantum gate design to analog quantum simulation. Observing that, for many systems, formulating HE as a linear program only requires specific commutation relations between the control pulses and the system Hamiltonian, we provide a unified framework for broad classes of $2$-local qubit, qudit, and fermionic systems. We reformulate time-optimal HE as a complex $k$-cut polytope problem, with $k$ the number of distinct phases in these commutation relations, and prove it NP-complete for every finite $k$. We therefore relax the polytope to the elliptope and apply a Krivine-type rounding, yielding pulses informed by the system and target Hamiltonians, which we then use in a linear program. We derive how many informed pulses are necessary and sufficient for the linear program to be reliably feasible. Additionally mixing in uniformly sampled pulses guarantees a solution close to the relaxation value from $\mathrm{O}(m)$ pulses, with $m$ the number of interaction terms. Together with upper and lower bounds on the optimal quantum run time, this yields an $\mathrm{O}(\sqrt{m})$ approximation ratio. In benchmarks on a fully connected Ising model, a chiral clock model for qudits, and the fermionic Harper-Hofstadter model, our approach attains near-optimal quantum run times wherever the optimum is computable, reaches run times that saturate with the lattice size in the fermionic case, and outperforms state-of-the-art methods with $\mathrm{O}(m)$ pulses. This establishes a unified approach with provable guarantees to the automatic programming of analog quantum simulators.

</details>


### [113] [Entanglement spectrum and magic in higher-dimensional free fermionic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36008)
*Beatrice Magni,Daniele Iannotti,Riccardo Travaglino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过纠缠谱研究高维自由费米子系统中的费米子非局域魔法，揭示了其边界律的对数违反、与纠缠容量的界限关系及在平衡与非平衡情形下的普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高维自由费米子系统中非局域魔法的结构，需理解其与纠缠谱、边界律及纠缠哈密顿量低能性质的联系，并刻画Kitaev模型不同相及淬火动力学中的区别。

Method: 对条形几何中平移不变的Gaussian态，利用维度约化将问题映射为独立低维动量扇区；证明第二Rényi费米子非局域魔法受纠缠容量界定，并引入纠缠-温度变形探测纠缠哈密顿量低能结构；将准粒子描述推广至高维淬火。

Result: 二维下得到类似Gioev-Klich-Widom标度的乘法对数边界律违反；Kitaev模型物质-Majorana扇区中该响应在Abelian相指数抑制、非Abelian相代数衰减且与容量之比趋于普适常数；随机Gaussian线路在二维呈扩散传播。

Conclusion: 纠缠谱视角为高维自由费米子非局域魔法提供统一框架，揭示了其与纠缠容量、低能纠缠结构及相区性质的深刻关联，并可推广至非平衡动力学。

Abstract: We study fermionic non-local magic in higher-dimensional free-fermion systems through the lens of the entanglement spectrum. For translationally invariant Gaussian states in strip geometries, dimensional reduction maps the problem to independent lower-dimensional momentum sectors, leading in two dimensions to a multiplicative logarithmic violation of the boundary law analogous to Gioev-Klich-Widom scaling. We further show that the second Rényi fermionic non-local magic is bounded by the capacity of entanglement and introduce an entanglement-temperature deformation that probes the low-energy structure of the entanglement Hamiltonian. Applied to the matter-Majorana sector of the Kitaev model, this response is exponentially suppressed in the Abelian phase and algebraic in the non-Abelian phase, where its ratio with the capacity approaches a universal constant. Out of equilibrium, we extend the quasi-particle description to fermionic non-local magic in higher-dimensional quenches and show that random Gaussian circuits exhibit diffusive spreading in two spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [114] [Asymptotically Optimal Mixed-State Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35986)
*Jiani Fei,Jinzhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了具有简单（非退化）满秩谱的混合态在渐近意义下通用克隆的最优全局保真度：给出了已知谱时的最优根保真度闭式解 F_orb(γ,p)，以及在谱未知情形下谱无关克隆器的一致最优（极小极大）保真度 F_univ(γ,p)。此外，还分别求出平坦投影态 ρ=P/r 的最优保真度 γ^{-r(d-r)/2}，并给出 purify–clone–trace (PCT) 的精确渐近保真度，证明所构造克隆器在任意固定增益下严格优于 PCT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子克隆是量子信息理论的核心问题。已有工作多关注纯态或特定混合态的克隆，而对于一般混合态、尤其是具有简单满秩谱的混合态，其渐近最优克隆保真度以及谱不确定性带来的代价尚缺乏精确刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，同时厘清谱不确定性与本征基不确定性对克隆性能的分别贡献，并与常用协议 PCT 进行严格比较。

Method: 构造克隆信道：将 Schur–Weyl 标签的经典处理与协变的 Cartan 扇区信道相结合。逆命题（上界）通过局部渐近正态性（LAN）以及针对任意信道的 Gaussian 保真度界导出。对平坦投影态单独分析并求最优保真度。另外解析估计 purify–clone–trace 协议的精确渐近保真度，并与本文克隆器逐点比较。

Result: 在固定维数 d 下，输入数 n 与输出数 m_n 满足 m_n/n→γ>1。对已知简单满秩谱 p_1>⋯>p_d>0，最优根保真度为 F_orb(γ,p)=∏_{i<j} (√γ+√(q_{ij}(γ−1+q_{ij})))/(γ+q_{ij})，其中 q_{ij}=p_j/p_i。谱未知时，谱无关克隆器在简单满秩腔的紧子集上一致达到 F_univ(γ,p)=((2√γ)/(1+γ))^{(d−1)/2} F_orb(γ,p)，且为极小极大最优；两因子分别刻画谱不确定性与本征基不确定性。对平坦投影态 ρ=P/r（P 为秩 r 正交投影），最优保真度为 γ^{−r(d−r)/2}。同时得到 PCT 的精确渐近保真度，并证明对简单满秩谱及 1<r<d 的秩 r 投影态，所构造克隆器在每个固定增益下严格优于 PCT。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了简单满秩谱混合态渐近克隆的最优全局保真度，并将谱未知情形下的极小极大最优值分解为谱与本征基两部分代价。对平坦投影态给出了独立的最优结果，并证明所提克隆器全面超越 PCT 协议，为混合态量子克隆提供了精确的渐近理论边界。

Abstract: We determine asymptotic global fidelities of cloning generic mixed states with simple (non-degenerate) full-rank spectra. In fixed dimension $d$, let $n$ inputs and $m_n$ outputs satisfy $m_n/n\toγ>1$. For a known simple full-rank spectrum $p_1>\cdots>p_d>0$, we prove that the optimal root fidelity between the entire output and the product target state is $$
  F_\rm{orb}(γ,p)
  =\prod_{i<j}
  \frac{\sqrtγ+\sqrt{q_{ij}(γ-1+q_{ij})}}
  {γ+q_{ij}},
  \qquad q_{ij}=\frac{p_j}{p_i}. $$ When the spectrum is unknown, one spectrum-independent cloner attains $$
  F_\rm{univ}(γ,p)
  =\left(\frac{2\sqrtγ}{1+γ}\right)^{(d-1)/2}
  F_\rm{orb}(γ,p), $$ uniformly on compact subsets of the simple full-rank chamber and is minimax optimal. The two factors quantify spectral and eigenbasis uncertainty. Our channels combine classical processing of Schur--Weyl labels with a covariant Cartan-sector channel. Local asymptotic normality and a Gaussian fidelity bound against arbitrary channels yield the converses. For flat projector states $ρ=P/r$, with $P$ a rank-$r$ orthogonal projector, we separately prove the optimum $γ^{-r(d-r)/2}$. Furthermore, we estimate the exact asymptotic fidelity of purify--clone--trace (PCT), and show that our cloners strictly outperform PCT at every fixed gain for simple full-rank spectra and rank-$r$ projector states with $1<r<d$.

</details>


### [115] [Out-of-Time-Ordered Correlators Beyond Lindblad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36029)
*Elisa Vallini,Felix Fritzsch,Pieter W. Claeys*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从微观模型出发，指出Lindblad方法在OTOC层面不成立，会缺失由副本间关联引起的额外项，并提出扩展形式以更忠实刻画信息扰乱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC是量子混沌与信息扰乱的重要探针，但开放系统中常用Lindblad方程描述OTOC缺乏微观依据：在时序关联函数层面导出Lindblad的机制，并不能保证在OTOC层面同样适用。

Method: 构建一个子系统耦合随机浴的微观模型，推导其OTOC动力学，并与标准Lindblad方法对比，识别出由不同副本间关联诱导的额外贡献，进而提出扩展的动力学形式。

Result: 结果显示，微观模型给出的OTOC动力学比标准Lindblad方法多出一项；该额外项保留了底层幺正动力学的长时自由性，并更准确地捕捉信息扰乱。作为应用，可用耗散边界替代部分大闭合系统，从而在超出可直接模拟尺度的系统中数值研究OTOC。

Conclusion: 标准Lindblad方法不足以描述开放系统的OTOC；纳入副本间关联的扩展形式更忠实，并为研究大尺寸闭合系统中的算符展宽与高阶关联提供了可行数值途径。

Abstract: Out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) provide a powerful probe of quantum chaos and scrambling. While the Lindblad formalism is often used to describe OTOCs in open systems, microscopic mechanisms that give rise to the Lindblad equation at the level of time-ordered correlation functions do not necessarily justify this approach at the level of OTOCs. Starting from a microscopic model of a subsystem coupled to a random bath, we show that the resulting OTOC dynamics contains an additional contribution compared to the standard Lindblad approach. This contribution, induced by correlations between different replicas, allows key properties of the underlying unitary dynamics such as long-time freeness to be retained and information scrambling to be captured more faithfully. As an application, we show that this extended formalism can be used to numerically study OTOCs in large closed systems by replacing part of the system with a dissipative boundary, offering a way to study operator spreading and higher-order correlations beyond directly accessible system sizes.

</details>


### [116] [Out-of-time-order Correlators in Volcano potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35990)
*Aritra Banerjee,Reetik Jangir,Rishant Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析与数值研究了Volcano势（含Pöschl-Teller特例）中的乱序时序关联函数（OTOC），涵盖正负曲率参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC是量子信息扰乱与量子混沌的重要诊断工具，但短时指数增长是否必然标志全局量子混沌尚需澄清；Volcano势参数化地连接有限深势阱与限制壁，为研究此问题提供理想平台。

Method: 结合数值对角化与半经典分析，系统考察Volcano势中同时包含正、负曲率的参数区间内的OTOC行为。

Result: 在该模型中，OTOC的短时指数增长并不一定意味着全局量子混沌，而是与激发态对负曲率区域的采样相关联。

Conclusion: 短时OTOC指数增长不能简单等同于全局量子混沌，其来源可能与负曲率区域的激发态采样有关，为OTOC作为混沌诊断量提供了重要修正。

Abstract: The out-of-time-order correlator (OTOC) has emerged as an important diagnostic for quantum information scrambling and a key signature of quantum chaos. In this work, we discuss out-of-time-order correlators, analytically and numerically, for the so-called Volcano potentials. This family of potentials parametrically interpolates between bounded wells and confining walls, and includes Pöschl-Teller as a specific case. Through a combination of numerical diagonalization and semiclassical analysis, we investigate various regimes of the potential parameter regimes containing regions of both positive and negative curvatures. Moreover, we demonstrate in this case that short-time exponential growth of the OTOC does not necessarily signal global quantum chaos; instead, it correlates with the sampling of negative curvature regions by excited states.

</details>


### [117] [Collectivity limits quantum entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36113)
*Donghoon Kim,Tomotaka Kuwahara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了“集体性”作为一种独立于空间局域性的多体纠缠约束机制：对于Kac归一化幂律相互作用 r^{-α} 的Γapped哈密顿量，基态纠缠在非局域区仍受对数或亚扩展型限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，空间局域性被认为是限制多体纠缠的根本机制：区域与其补集之间的关联必须通过小的空间界面传递。然而当相互作用强烈非局域时，这一图像失效，是否存在普适的纠缠约束尚不清楚。本文旨在寻找局域性之外的纠缠限制机制。

Method: 针对D维晶格上Kac归一化幂律相互作用 r^{-α} 的一般有能隙哈密顿量，作者严格证明：对任意二分，α<D/2时基态纠缠随系统尺寸至多对数增长，D/2<α<D时为亚扩展增长，其根源是单个格点周围集体涨落被抑制。对具有余维一边界的空间规则二分，通过重整化群构造将集体抑制传播到更大长度尺度。

Result: 对空间规则二分，纠缠上界在 D/2<α<(D+1)/2 时改进为polylogarithmic标度；在 (D+1)/2<α<D 时仍参数化地强于任意二分上界。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了“集体性”是与空间局域性并列的根本性多体纠缠约束机制，即使在强非局域相互作用下也成立。

Abstract: Understanding what limits many-body quantum entanglement is a central problem in physics. Spatial locality has long provided a fundamental mechanism: correlations between a region and its complement must be mediated through a small spatial interface, thereby constraining their entanglement. Here we show that universal constraints on entanglement can persist even when interactions are strongly nonlocal, through collectivity: many weak interactions suppress collective quantum fluctuations while retaining a finite overall interaction scale. We establish this mechanism rigorously for generic gapped Hamiltonians with Kac-normalized power-law interactions $r^{-α}$ on a $D$-dimensional lattice. For arbitrary bipartitions, we prove that the ground-state entanglement scales at most logarithmically with system size for $α<D/2$ and subextensively for $D/2<α<D$, due to suppressed collective fluctuations around individual sites. For spatially regular bipartitions with codimension-one boundaries, we show that collective suppression can be propagated to successively larger length scales through a renormalization-group construction. As a result, we prove that the entanglement bound improves to polylogarithmic scaling for $D/2<α<(D+1)/2$, and remains parametrically stronger than the arbitrary-bipartition bound for $(D+1)/2<α<D$. Together, these results reveal collectivity as a fundamental mechanism for constraining many-body entanglement alongside spatial locality.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum States and Spectra of Small Cylindrical and Toroidal Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36711)
*Caelan Brooks,Kunal K. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了柱面和环面拓扑小格子在标量规范势下的谱与量子态，揭示规范依赖性、边缘态间隙及局域-离域转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限格子中假定的规范等价性在周期边界条件下通常失效，需明确其恢复条件并理解有限尺寸格子的规范相关谱特性。

Method: 对柱面与环面拓扑的小格子施加引入格点间耦合位置依赖相位的标量规范势，分析规范选择、边界条件、耦合强度及附加轴向场等参数，考察避免交叉、持续简并、本征态、谱间隙与局域化行为。

Result: 发现避免交叉与持续简并呈规范依赖出现；小格子出现与边缘态相关的显著谱线间隙，在热力学极限下被抑制；环面格子具有柱面格子多数特征，并出现由场参数与格点数关系决定的局域到离域转变。

Conclusion: 有限格子的谱与量子态强烈依赖规范与拓扑，规范等价性需特定条件恢复；尺寸效应与拓扑结构共同决定边缘态间隙和局域-离域转变。

Abstract: We examine the spectrum and quantum states of small lattices with cylindrical and toroidal topology subject to a scalar gauge potential that introduces a position dependent phase in the inter-site coupling. Equivalency of gauges assumed in infinite lattices is generally lost due to the periodic boundary conditions, and conditions that restore it are identified. We trace the impact of various system parameters including gauge choice, boundary conditions and inter-site coupling strengths, and an additional axial field. We find gauge dependent appearance of avoided crossings and persistent degeneracies, and we show their impact on the associated eigenstates. Smaller lattices develop prominent gaps in spectral lines associated with edge states, which are suppressed in the thermodynamic limit. Toroidal lattices have counterparts of most of the features observed in cylindrical lattices, but notably they display a transition from localization to delocalization determined by the relation between the field parameter and the number of lattice sites.

</details>


### [119] [L-band single-photon generation from a cavity-coupled silicon C-center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37244)
*Carolina Crosta,Kyu-Young Kim,Purbita Purkayastha,Abhijit Biswas,Jasvith Raj Basani,Amirehsan Alizadehherfati,Amirehsan Boreiri,Chang-Min Lee,Fabio Pezzoli,Edo Waks*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次在硅中隔离出单个C色心，并将其耦合至纳米光子晶体腔，实现电信L波段单光子发射，Purcell因子下界达33，g⁽²⁾(0)=0.23。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单光子源单片集成到硅光子学中，是实现可扩展量子通信架构的理想方案。硅色心中，C色心的零声子发射位于电信L波段，恰对应光纤传输损耗最低的波段，具有独特优势；然而在固态量子光学中隔离单个C色心一直是尚未攻克的难题。

Method: 通过将单个硅C色心耦合到纳米光子晶体腔，增强其零声子线的自发辐射速率；并在体材料条件下对比测量激发态寿命，结合二阶自相关测量表征单光子特性。

Result: 成功隔离单个硅C色心；观察到激发态寿命较体材料缩短五倍，对应Purcell因子下界为33；二阶自相关测量证实单光子发射，未做背景扣除时g⁽²⁾(0)=0.23。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了硅中天然的电信L波段量子光源，为超低损耗、长距离量子应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Monolithic integration of single-photon sources into silicon photonics offers a compelling solution for realizing scalable quantum telecommunication architectures. Among the candidate silicon color centers, the C-center features a zero-phonon emission in the telecom L-band, corresponding to the spectral window of minimum transmission loss in optical fibers. Despite this advantage, isolating single C-centers has remained an unrealized challenge in solid-state quantum optics. In this work, we report the isolation of a single silicon C-center. We enhance the spontaneous emission rate of the zero-phonon line by coupling the single emitter to a nanophotonic crystal cavity, observing a fivefold reduction in the excited-state lifetime compared to bulk conditions, which corresponds to a Purcell factor lower bound of 33. Second-order autocorrelation measurements confirm single-photon emission from the investigated defect, obtaining an optical purity of $g^{(2)}(0) = 0.23$ without background correction. Our results establish a native telecom L-band quantum light source in silicon, laying the foundation for ultra-low-loss and long-distance quantum applications.

</details>


### [120] [Is rapid mixing stable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36016)
*Jordi A. Montañà-López,Barbara Roos,Sebastian Stengele,Ángela Capel,Rahul Trivedi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Lindblad耗散动力学的快速混合稳定性，构造反例表明快速混合在局部扰动下一般不具鲁棒性，甚至任意弱的局部哈密顿扰动也能破坏快速混合；同时给出若干保证稳定快速混合的充分条件，并证明快速混合不蕴含正的MLSI常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理上重要的Lindbladians（如一般开放量子系统模型与工程化态制备过程）具有快速混合特性，即弛豫到稳态的时间只随系统尺寸多项式对数增长。基于混合时间很短这一直觉，人们预期局部扰动不会显著增加混合时间。本文质疑这一预期，考察快速混合在扰动下是否仍然成立。

Method: 采用理论分析与构造性方法：构建反例检验快速混合的稳定性，包括扰动不改变稳态的情形；分析任意弱局部哈密顿扰动对快速混合的影响；推导保证稳定快速混合的一类充分条件，覆盖准自由费米子系统、对易Lindbladian及局部自旋系统；并考察修正对数Sobolev不等式（MLSI）与稳定快速混合的关系。

Result: 快速混合一般不具稳定性，即使扰动不改变稳态也可被破坏；任意弱的局部哈密顿扰动即可破坏快速混合。给出多类保证稳定快速混合的充分条件，包括准自由费米子系统、对易Lindbladians和局部自旋系统；识别出MLSI可导致稳定快速混合的条件。此外证明快速混合并不蕴含正的MLSI常数。

Conclusion: 快速混合并非普遍鲁棒的动力学性质，局部扰动可能破坏该性质，因此不能仅凭短混合时间推断扰动下的稳定性；但在特定结构条件下（如准自由费米子、对易Lindbladians、局部自旋系统）快速混合可保持稳定。快速混合与MLSI常数正性之间存在本质差距。

Abstract: Several physically interesting Lindbladians, such as those describing generic open quantum system models as well as engineered state preparation processes, are often rapidly mixing, that is, they relax to steady states in time scaling polylogarithmically with the system size. Owing to the short mixing times, it is physically expected that local perturbations do not substantially increase the mixing time. In this work we question this expectation and ask whether rapid mixing survives. We provide counterexamples showing that rapid mixing is not stable in general, even when the perturbation does not change the steady state. We also show that arbitrarily weak local Hamiltonian perturbations can destroy rapid mixing. Complementing these results, we develop an array of sufficient conditions which imply stable rapid mixing, including several classes of quasifree fermionic systems, commuting Lindbladians, and local spin systems. We also identify conditions under which modified logarithmic Sobolev inequalities (MLSIs), a stronger form of convergence, lead to stable rapid mixing. As a further implication of our results, we show that rapid mixing does not imply a positive MLSI constant.

</details>


### [121] [Independent density and coherence skin effects in adaptive fermion circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36017)
*Karim Chahine,Markus Siegl,Michael Buchhold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究自适应费米子电路中的非互易动力学，揭示密度与相干性可独立调控的趋肤效应：稳态密度倾斜仅由测量强度与反馈位移决定，而体相干衰减由受条件门控的相对占据相位控制的Hatano-Nelson生成元描述，可引发点隙拓扑转变并反转相干局域化，实现密度与相干性在相反边缘局域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应电路通过测量与结果条件反馈实现非互易动力学而无需后选择，但其中密度与相干性两类自由度的趋肤效应能否独立调控尚不清楚。

Method: 采用受条件U(2)门控的自适应费米子电路，解析推导稳态密度分布及体相干性的有效Hatano-Nelson生成元，利用相对占据相位调控点隙拓扑，并提出基于系综平均可观测量的关联函数比值探针。

Result: 稳态密度呈指数单粒子分布且任意填充下构型权重倾斜；相干衰减遵循有效Hatano-Nelson生成元，相对相位可驱动点隙拓扑转变并在固定密度下反转相干局域化，使密度与相干性局域于相反边缘；导出两种指数并给出探测方案。

Conclusion: 自适应费米子电路支持密度与相干性趋肤效应的独立调控，二者可由不同机制分别决定并局域于相反边缘，为无需后选择的非互易量子动力学提供新途径。

Abstract: Adaptive circuits use measurements and outcome-conditioned feedback to implement nonreciprocal dynamics without postselection. We show that fermion circuits host independently tunable density and coherence skin effects. The stationary state has an exact density tilt set only by measurement strength and feedback displacement: conditioned-gate details cancel, yielding an exponential one-particle profile and, at any filling, a tilted ensemble of configuration weights. Decaying coherences instead obey, in the bulk, an effective Hatano-Nelson generator controlled by the conditioned gates through a relative occupation phase. Tuning this phase drives a point-gap topological transition and reverses coherence localization at fixed density, allowing density and coherence to localize at opposite edges. We derive both exponents for conditioned $U(2)$ gates and propose a correlator-ratio probe using ensemble-averaged observables.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Estimation under Decoherence in Neutrino Oscillations: Quantum Resources, Flavor Accessibility, and Multiparameter Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36038)
*Jilali Loulijat,Abdallah Slaoui,Mohamed Gouighri,Berihu Teklu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以量子信息几何为框架，区分传播基退相干与有效味模退相干两种统计族，给出两味模态的纠缠与失谐度量精确解，并把退相干率纳入量子/经典费歇尔信息与SLD不相容性分析，形成状态层面的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡为开放系统动力学、量子资源与参数估计提供了干涉平台，但文献中传播基退相干（用存活因子η_p在味投影前阻尼干涉）与有效味模退相干（仅压低相干项而保持布居）常被混为一谈；同时需要区分动力学信息损失与测量不可及性，并评估多参数联合估计的可行性。

Method: 对两味模态解析导出 concurrence、entanglement of formation、local quantum uncertainty，并将单侧投影失谐（discord）化为一参数优化；在有效味模退相干下计算(P,χ,η_m)的量子费歇尔信息矩阵（QFIM）；对传播基退相干分别计算基匹配QFI与味FI；再以单色三味基准评估(θ23,δ_CP,ξ31,g)的混合态QFIM、味FIM与SLD不相容性，选取DUNE-like、T2HK-like与ESSnuSB-inspired参数点。

Result: 当0<P<1且0<η_m<1时，(P,χ,η_m)的QFIM为对角，H_mode_χχ=C_ηm^2，H_mode_ξmξm=C_ηm^2/(1-η_m^2)，其中ξ_m=−lnη_m；在Γ0=10^-23 GeV下，固定坐标比值F_flav_δδ/H_δδ在三个基准点约为0.010、0.013、0.105，且三点均显示强θ23–ξ31不相容性；由于单能量味测量仅提供两个独立概率，其四参数FIM秩亏，故上述对角比值是条件性诊断而非联合四参数灵敏度。

Conclusion: 研究建立了中微子振荡的状态层面信息几何基准，明确了有效味模退相干下QFIM的解析结构与传播基退相干的QFI/FI分离意义；同时强调单能量味测量的FIM秩亏，提示需多能量或额外通道才能实现四参数联合估计，所得结果属状态层面基准而非事件层面灵敏度预报。

Abstract: Neutrino oscillations offer an interferometric setting for open-system dynamics, quantum resources, and parameter estimation. We distinguish two often-conflated statistical families: propagation-basis dephasing uses a survival factor $η_p$ to damp interference before flavor projection, changing flavor probabilities; effective flavor-mode dephasing suppresses only off-diagonal coherence of a single-particle, two-mode state while fixing its populations. For the two-flavor mode state, we derive exact concurrence, entanglement of formation, and local quantum uncertainty, and reduce one-sided projective discord to a one-parameter optimization. For $0<P<1$ and $0<η_m<1$, the QFIM in $(P,χ,η_m)$ is diagonal, with $\mathcal{H}^{\rm mode}_{χχ}=C_{η_m}^2$ and $\mathcal{H}^{\rm mode}_{ξ_mξ_m}=C_{η_m}^2/(1-η_m^2)$, where $ξ_m=-\lnη_m$. For propagation-basis dephasing, basis-matched QFI and flavor FI separate dynamical information loss from measurement inaccessibility for the mixing angle, mass-squared splitting, and dephasing rate. A monochromatic three-flavor benchmark evaluates the mixed-state QFIM, flavor FIM, and SLD incompatibility for $(θ_{23},δ_{\rm CP},ξ_{31},g)$ at DUNE-like, T2HK-like, and ESSnuSB-inspired points. At $Γ_0=10^{-23}\,\mathrm{GeV}$, the fixed-coordinate ratio $\mathcal{F}^{\rm flav}_{δδ}/\mathcal{H}_{δδ}$ is approximately $0.010$, $0.013$, and $0.105$, respectively; all three points show strong $θ_{23}$--$ξ_{31}$ incompatibility. A single-energy flavor measurement supplies only two independent probabilities, so its four-parameter FIM is rank deficient: these diagonal ratios are conditional diagnostics, not joint four-parameter sensitivities. These are state-level information-geometric benchmarks, not event-level sensitivity forecasts.

</details>


### [123] [Local autonomous inference machines for quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38139)
*Siddhant Midha,Dmitry A. Abanin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种局域、分布式的量子LDPC码译码框架：以置信传播（BP）为基础，通过严格局域的校验子处理、局域反馈与异步自治动力学实现高度并行译码，并在多个传统BP失效的场景中（2D/3D环面码点状扇区、3D环面码膜状扇区及二元自行车码）观察到阈值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的译码通常依赖全局或集中式处理，缺乏可扩展、低开销、易硬件实现的方案；同时朴素BP在诸多量子码上并不呈现阈值，亟需一种既能保留局域性又能获得阈值行为的译码机制。

Method: 构建于BP之上的局域分布式译码架构，包含严格局域的校验子处理、局域反馈、自治运行和高度并行化。分两个层次：一是证明任何在迭代BP下具有阈值的码，都可提升为保持该阈值的自治局域测量-反馈动力学；二是针对朴素BP无阈值的码，利用BP提供的局域边际信息，令活跃校验子缺陷通过异步反馈进行移动、配对与湮灭。

Result: 数值实验显示，该构造在二维和三维环面码的点状扇区中呈现阈值行为，而传统BP译码在这些场景下失效；进一步借助BP的图通用性，在三维环面码的膜状扇区以及一族二元自行车（bivariate-bicycle）量子LDPC码上研究了该推断动力学。

Conclusion: 局域分布式BP框架能够在保持自治与并行性的同时实现阈值译码，并可推广至多种量子LDPC码结构，为可扩展量子纠错译码提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce a local and distributed framework for decoding quantum LDPC codes. Built atop belief propagation (BP), the architecture combines strictly local processing of syndrome information, local feedback, autonomous operation, and highly parallelized decoding. We establish the framework at two complementary levels. First, we show that any code exhibiting a threshold under iterative BP decoding can be promoted to an autonomous local measurement-and-feedback dynamics while preserving that threshold. We then turn to codes for which naive BP does not itself exhibit threshold behavior. Our key observation is that BP can nevertheless provide sufficiently accurate \emph{local} marginal information: active syndrome defects use these local beliefs to \emph{move}, pair, and annihilate through asynchronous feedback. We demonstrate numerically that this construction exhibits threshold behavior in the point-like sectors of the two- and three-dimensional toric codes, where conventional BP decoding fails. Exploiting the graph-generality of BP, we then study the inference dynamics on the membrane-like sector of the three-dimensional toric code and in a family of bivariate-bicycle quantum LDPC codes.

</details>


### [124] [From Projected Subspaces to Full-Space Implementations: Representation-Equivalence Audits for Open-Shell ADAPT-VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36060)
*Lucia Malíčková,Petr Klenovský*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对称性投影空间中的变分态不一定等于对未投影母体生成元取指数所制备的态；该等价性要求目标空间不变性 $QAP=0$。作者将其形式化为开壳层 ADAPT-VQE 的表征等价性审计，并在 Cu(II) 与 12/18/24 量子比特基准上量化了该失效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对称性投影（如自旋投影）ADAPT-VQE 中，常用做法是"以投影空间算得的振幅直接驱动母体（未投影）生成元的指数化电路"。但这一参数迁移隐含了一个未被检验的假设：投影空间最优态等于未投影母体序列表征出的态。作者指出该假设仅在目标空间不变性 $QAP=0$ 时成立，因此需要一套显式审计框架来识别并量化这种表征/参数迁移失效。

Method: 1) 理论推导：对正交投影算符 $P$、$Q=I-P$ 与反厄米生成元 $A$，证明对全部振幅的精确等价要求 $QAP=0$，即目标空间不变性；将其表述为开壳层 ADAPT-VQE 的表征等价性审计。2) 数值验证：在规范 CAS(15e,9o) 型-1 Cu(II) 哈密顿量上统计违反该条件的裸 Hartree–Fock 派生单/双/三重激发；比较投影-双重态 ADAPT 与"同振幅全空间序列"的表现，并在硬约束 $p_D\ge0.999$ 下对同序 121 个母体生成元重优化。3) 对照实验：12 量子比特 NO 基准重复机制；构造自旋保持的广义全空间方案消除表征歧义，并在 18/24 量子比特上测试。4) 电路验证：对 18 量子比特阳性对照，仅精修 4 个 Suzuki-2 因子并用固定编译器种子重转译，检查预声明的电路等价门限（CX 门数与深度）。

Result: 在 Cu(II) 基准中，323 个裸 HF 派生单/双/三重激发里有 274 个违反 $QAP=0$。投影-双重态 ADAPT 以 121 个算符达到 1.5719 m$E_h$ 误差；而同振幅全空间序列误差达 464.730 m$E_h$，与投影态保真度仅 0.07343，四重态权重 54.45\%。在 $p_D\ge0.999$ 硬约束下重优化同序 121 个母体生成元，最佳收敛误差降至 8.482 m$E_h$，说明灾难性"同角失效"主要是表征/参数迁移失效，但固定序列的全空间重优化无法完全修复。12 量子比特 NO 基准呈现较温和的同类机制。自旋保持广义全空间构造消除表征歧义，在 18 与 24 量子比特分别达到 1.493 和 1.181 m$E_h$。18 量子比特阳性对照仅精修 4 个 Suzuki-2 因子并固定种子重转译即通过预声明电路等价门限，CX 门 19,660、深度 23,016。

Conclusion: 对称性投影 ADAPT 中的振幅迁移并非天然有效，必须以 $QAP=0$ 作为审计条件；不满足时会出现表征/参数迁移失效，且固定序列的全空间重优化难以完全补救。自旋保持的广义全空间构造可消除表征歧义。电路合成与最终能量测量应作为两个独立的验证层。本文贡献在于一套验证框架，而非新的自旋适配 ADAPT 算法。

Abstract: A variational state optimized in a symmetry-projected space need not be the state prepared by exponentiating the corresponding unprojected parent generators. For an orthogonal target-space projector $P$, $Q=I-P$, and an anti-Hermitian generator $A$, exact equivalence for all amplitudes requires target-space invariance, $QAP=0$. We formulate this condition as a representation-equivalence audit for open-shell ADAPT-VQE. For a canonical CAS(15e,9o) type-1 Cu(II) Hamiltonian, 274 of 323 bare Hartree--Fock-origin single, double, and triple excitations violate this condition. Projected-doublet ADAPT reaches 1.5719 m$E_h$ with 121 operators, whereas the same amplitudes in the corresponding full-space sequence give 464.730 m$E_h$ error, 0.07343 fidelity with the projected state, and 54.45\% quartet weight. Reoptimizing the same 121 parent generators in the same order under the hard constraint $p_D\ge0.999$ reduces the best converged error to 8.482 m$E_h$, showing that the catastrophic same-angle failure is primarily a representation/parameter-transfer failure but is not completely repaired by full-space reoptimization of the fixed sequence. An independent 12-qubit NO benchmark exhibits the same mechanism more mildly. A spin-preserving generalized full-space construction removes the representation ambiguity and reaches 1.493 and 1.181 m$E_h$ at 18 and 24 qubits. For the 18-qubit positive control, selectively refining only four Suzuki-2 factors and re-transpiling with a fixed compiler seed passes the predeclared circuit-equivalence gate at 19,660 controlled-X (CX) gates and depth 23,016. Circuit synthesis and final-energy measurement therefore constitute separate validation layers. The contribution is a validation framework, not a new spin-adapted ADAPT algorithm.

</details>


### [125] [Witness-based quantification of non-Markovianity without complete Choi state reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36080)
*Pritam Roy,Saheli Mukherjee,Bivas Mallick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于见证者（witness）的非马尔可夫性量化框架，避免完整重建Choi态，并证明正半定见证者可为RHP度量提供可认证下界，形成完整层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源理论与操作型非马尔可夫性量化方法通常需要完整掌握中间动力学映射（即完整重建Choi态），实验要求苛刻，因此需要更易实验实现的量化方法。

Method: 首先证明任意非马尔可夫性见证者给出几何资源度量的可认证下界，并给出该下界紧致的充要条件；针对非正半定见证者难以测量的现实问题，聚焦正半定见证者（可作为POVM元实现），证明每个正见证者给出RHP度量的可认证下界，建立见证量化、RHP度量与几何距离度量间的完整层级；并将框架应用于振幅阻尼与一般Pauli动力学以求解最优正见证者。

Result: 在振幅阻尼与一般Pauli动力学中，最优正见证者简化为简单的Bell宇称投影算符；相应见证值可由少量实验可得的关联测量直接获得。

Conclusion: 该框架无需完整Choi态重建，为开放量子系统中非马尔可夫性量化提供了高效且实验可行的途径。

Abstract: Quantifying non-Markovianity is a fundamental problem in the theory of open quantum systems, as it is essential for exploiting environmental memory as a resource in quantum technologies. Existing resource-theoretic and operational quantifiers, however, generally require complete knowledge of the intermediate dynamical map and are therefore experimentally demanding. Here, we develop a witness-based framework for the quantification of non-Markovianity that bypasses complete reconstruction of the Choi state associated with the intermediate dynamical map. We first show that every non-Markovianity witness provides a certifiable lower bound on the geometric resource measure of non-Markovianity and identify the necessary and sufficient conditions under which this bound is tight. Motivated by the fact that non-positive-semidefinite witnesses are generally difficult to measure in real experimental scenario, we then focus on positive-semidefinite witnesses, which admit a natural implementation as positive operator valued measure (POVM) elements. We prove that every positive witness yields a certified lower bound on the Rivas--Huelga--Plenio (RHP) measure, thereby establishing a complete hierarchy connecting witness-based quantification, the RHP measure, and the geometric distance measure. Applying our framework to amplitude-damping and general Pauli dynamics, we find that the optimal positive witnesses reduce to simple Bell-parity projectors. Furthermore, we show that the resulting witness values can be obtained directly from a small number of experimentally accessible correlation measurements, thereby eliminating the need for complete Choi-state reconstruction and providing an efficient and experimentally feasible approach for the quantification of non-Markovianity in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [126] [Near-Optimal Parallel Unitary Process Tomography in Diamond Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36127)
*Noam Scully,Sisi Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在并行电路架构下，酉过程层析可以达到接近最优的查询复杂度，从而在忽略对数因子的意义下弥合了并行与顺序电路之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子技术的发展，理解不同电路类型执行酉过程层析所需的计算资源十分重要。在钻石距离度量误差时，顺序电路已知能达到最优查询复杂度，但并行电路能否同样达到最优尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者引入了一种明确、新颖的并行过程，证明使用并行电路亦可实现具有近最优查询复杂度的酉过程层析。

Result: 证明了并行电路能够实现近最优查询复杂度的酉过程层析，将顺序与并行电路之间的差距缩小至仅相差一个对数因子。

Conclusion: 并行与顺序策略在酉过程层析任务上能力几乎相当，表明并行电路架构在此任务中同样高效。

Abstract: Unitary process tomography is the act of learning how an unknown unitary gate acts on a quantum system. It is an essential procedure for realizing a functioning quantum computer. As quantum technologies develop, it is important to understand the computational resources needed to conduct unitary process tomography using a variety of types of circuits. In particular, when measuring error in diamond distance, it is known how to achieve optimal query complexity using a sequential circuit architecture, but it had not been known whether a parallel circuit architecture could achieve the same optimal query complexity. In this work, we prove that unitary process tomography with near-optimal query complexity is indeed possible using a parallel circuit by introducing an explicit, novel parallel procedure. This closes the gap between sequential and parallel circuits up to a logarithmic factor, showing that parallel and sequential strategies are nearly equally capable for this task.

</details>


### [127] [From Classical to Quantum Channels: Achieving Positive Covert Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36162)
*Hassan ZivariFard,Xiaodong Wang,Alexei Ashikhmin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究离散无记忆经典、经典-量子及量子信道中正速率隐蔽通信的可行性条件，证明输入概率单纯形维度超过输出概率单纯形（或输出态仿射维度）时可实现正的隐蔽速率，且MAC、无辜符号选择和混合无辜态均可放宽隐蔽性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐蔽通信要求传输行为对监测者不可检测。已有研究多关注隐蔽容量在何种信道下为零，而本文旨在系统刻画在离散无记忆信道（经典、经典-量子、量子）中实现正隐蔽速率的充要维度条件，并探讨多址信道、无辜符号选择及量子混合态对隐蔽性约束的影响。

Method: 针对经典点对点DMC，比较输入概率单纯形与输出概率单纯形的维度（等价于输入字母表基数大于输出字母表基数），分析可实现正隐蔽速率的信道类别；研究允许最优选择无辜符号对可达DMC类别的影响；分析经典多址信道MAC中额外发送机扩大可用输入对集合对隐蔽性约束的作用；将结果推广到经典-量子DMC，比较输入概率单纯形维度与信道输出可诱导输出态集合的仿射维度；对量子信道，分析无辜态为混合态时非平凡输入系综满足隐蔽性约束的条件。

Result: 经典点对点信道中，若输入概率单纯形维度大于输出概率单纯形维度（输入字母表基数大于输出字母表基数），则特定类DMC可实现正隐蔽速率；适当选择无辜符号可扩大可达正隐蔽速率的DMC类别。MAC中额外发送机扩大输入对集合，使满足隐蔽性约束的条件比点对点信道更宽松。经典-量子DMC中，若输入概率单纯形维度超过输出态集合仿射维度，则特定类信道可实现正隐蔽速率。量子信道中，若无辜态为混合态，非平凡输入系综总能满足隐蔽性约束；只要合法接收方能区分该系综中至少两个态，即可实现正隐蔽速率。

Conclusion: 信道输入与输出概率单纯形（或输出态集合仿射维度）之间的维度关系是决定正隐蔽速率可行性的关键因素；多址接入、无辜符号优化及混合无辜态均可放宽隐蔽性约束，从而扩大可实现正隐蔽速率的信道范围，为隐蔽通信可行性提供了统一的维度判据。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the conditions under which covert communication at positive rates is feasible over discrete memoryless classical, classical-quantum, and quantum channels. For classical point-to-point channels, we show that if the dimension of the channel input probability simplex exceeds that of the channel output probability simplex, equivalently, if the input alphabet has larger cardinality than the output alphabet, then positive covert rates are achievable for certain classes of Discrete Memoryless Channels (DMCs). We further show that allowing the innocent symbol (i.e., the symbol transmitted in the no-communication mode) to be chosen appropriately can enlarge the class of DMCs for which positive covert rates are achievable. We also study covert communication over classical Multiple-Access Channels (MACs), where the additional transmitter effectively enlarges the set of available channel input pairs, and we show that the conditions required to satisfy the covertness constraint are less restrictive for MACs than for point-to-point channels. We extend these results to classical-quantum DMCs by showing that if the dimension of the input probability simplex exceeds the affine dimension of the set of output states that can be induced at the channel output, then positive covert rates are achievable for certain classes of classical-quantum DMCs. Finally, for quantum channels, we show that if the innocent state (i.e., the state transmitted in the no-communication mode) is mixed, then the covertness constraint can always be satisfied by a non-trivial input ensemble. Consequently, positive covert rates are achievable whenever the legitimate receiver can distinguish at least two states in a suitable such ensemble.

</details>


### [128] [Near-optimal quantum simulation of lattice Lindbladian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36203)
*Rahul Trivedi,Xiehang Yu,Daniel Malz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用几何局域量子线路以深度 O(t·polylog(Nt/ε)) 近似几何局域 Lindbladian 在格点 qudit 上的时间演化，性能可比肩格点哈密顿量模拟的 HHKL 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟研究多集中于闭系哈密顿量，而开放系统由 Lindbladian 描述更为一般，其在几何局域格点上的动力学模拟缺乏与 HHKL 相当的高效算法。

Method: 设计几何局域量子线路算法，对作用于 qudit 格点的几何局域 Lindbladian，构造演化时间 t、diamond norm 误差 ε 的近似线路，深度为 O(t·polylog(Nt/ε))。

Result: 在 N 个 qudit 上实现了对几何局域 Lindbladian 时间演化的近似，线路深度为 O(t·polylog(Nt/ε))，误差以 diamond norm 度量，效率与 HHKL 对格点哈密顿量模拟相当。

Conclusion: 将 HHKL 式的高效模拟从闭系哈密顿量推广至几何局域开放系统 Lindbladian，达到相近性能。

Abstract: We consider the problem of simulating dynamics under geometrically local Lindbladians acting on a lattice of qudits. We present an algorithm to approximate the time evolution under these Lindbladians for evolution time $t$ and on $N$ qudits to diamond norm error $\varepsilon$ using geometrically local circuits of depth $O(t \ \polylog(Nt/\varepsilon))$. Our results thus achieve a performance comparable to that of the Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low (HHKL) algorithm for lattice Hamiltonian simulation.

</details>


### [129] [Bivariate Bicycle Codes over Group Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36213)
*Chaobin Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双变量双循环群代数（BBGA）码的加权移位构造：一个二元移位为右乘、另一个为左乘，结合律保证二者可交换（即使种子群非阿贝尔），从而双变量多项式（含混合单项式）定义CSS码。给出显式稀疏界，并用$D_3$恢复已知$[[144,12,12]]$、$[[144,14,14]]$码；构造并计算机验证$A_4\times C_6$上的$[[144,16,12]]$码（每个校验权为8），通过穷举低权枚举与内在稳定子支撑不变量证明其在量子比特置换+局部Clifford下与任何阿贝尔BB/两块群代数实现不等价；另有$\mathbb{F}_2[C_3]$上的非均匀加权移位$[[18,4,3]]$码（校验权6和8），排除同长度所有普通二元2BGA实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通双变量双循环（BB）码与两块群代数（2BGA）码通常依赖阿贝尔结构实现左右移位可交换。本文动机在于引入加权移位形式，使非阿贝尔种子群也能构造CSS码，从而突破普通2BGA类的限制，并系统区分非阿贝尔种子群所产生的新码与可由阿贝尔实现描述的码。

Method: 构造加权移位BBGA码：一个二元移位取右乘、另一个取左乘，由结合律确保两移位可交换；以移位中的双变量多项式（包括混合单项式）定义CSS码。推导显式稀疏界。对$D_3$用一对加权置换移位生成正则$C_6\times C_{12}$作用。对$A_4\times C_6$构造$[[144,16,12]]$码并进行计算机验证。采用穷举低权枚举与内在稳定子支撑不变量判定等价性。另在$\mathbb{F}_2[C_3]$上作非均匀加权移位构造$[[18,4,3]]$码，并精确枚举最小稳定子支撑以排除普通二元2BGA实现。

Result: $D_3$情形下，一对加权置换移位生成正则$C_6\times C_{12}$作用，并恢复已知$[[144,12,12]]$、$[[144,14,14]]$ BB码。构造出计算机验证的$A_4\times C_6$上$[[144,16,12]]$码，每个校验权为8。通过低权枚举与内在稳定子支撑不变量证明该码在量子比特置换结合局部Clifford操作下，不等价于任何普通阿贝尔BB或两块群代数实现。$\mathbb{F}_2[C_3]$上非均匀加权移位给出$[[18,4,3]]$码，校验权为6和8；精确枚举其最小稳定子支撑排除了同长度所有普通二元2BGA实现在相同等价意义下的可能。

Conclusion: 加权移位形式可推广到非阿贝尔种子群，且即使在阿贝尔种子群下也超出普通2BGA类；非阿贝尔情形能产生在置换+局部Clifford等价下本质上不等价于阿贝尔实现的新码。因此无限制加权移位构造严格扩展了普通2BGA码类。

Abstract: We study a weighted-shift formulation of bivariate bicycle group-algebra (BBGA) codes over finite group algebras. One binary shift acts by right multiplication and the other by left multiplication; associativity ensures that these shifts commute even for a nonabelian seed group. Bivariate polynomials in the shifts, including mixed monomials, therefore define CSS codes. We give explicit sparsity bounds and distinguish nonabelian seed groups from codes that are intrinsically inequivalent to abelian realizations. For $D_3$, a pair of weighted permutation shifts generates a regular $C_6\times C_{12}$ action and recovers the known $[[144,12,12]]$ and $[[144,14,14]]$ bivariate bicycle codes. We also present a computer-verified $[[144,16,12]]$ code over $A_4\times C_6$, with every check of weight eight. This example belongs to the established nonabelian two-block group-algebra family. Exhaustive low-weight enumeration and an intrinsic stabilizer-support invariant certify that it is inequivalent to any ordinary abelian BB or two-block group-algebra realization, even under qubit permutations combined with local Clifford operations. Finally, a nonuniform weighted-shift construction over $\mathbb{F}_2[C_3]$ yields a $[[18,4,3]]$ code with check weights six and eight. An exact enumeration of its minimum stabilizer supports rules out every ordinary binary 2BGA realization at that length, under the same equivalence. This establishes that the unrestricted weighted-shift formulation extends beyond the ordinary 2BGA class, even with an abelian seed group.

</details>


### [130] [Quantifying Teleportation Overhead in Distributed Unitary Coupled-Cluster Ansätze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36266)
*Grier M. Jones,Hassan Tariq Shafi,Zixuan Wang,Thomas Trenty,Zachary Vernec,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了分布式量子计算（DQC）中用于氢链量子化学的UCC类拟设的分布式资源需求，基于隐形传态方法比较朴素分布与TeleSABRE算法，发现自旋块化Jordan-Wigner排序下的UpCCD具有最优扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单芯片量子处理器难以扩展到实际量子化学应用所需规模，分布式量子计算被视为突破该架构限制的途径，因此需量化其分布式资源开销。

Method: 针对氢链，估计UCCSD、UpCCD与UpCCGSD电路的分布式资源需求；采用基于隐形传态的DQC方案，在固定中点或四分点划分下，定量比较朴素分布方法与TeleSABRE算法的输出，并统计跨分区非局域双量子门所需的Bell对及经典通信开销；同时对比Jordan-Wigner与Bravyi-Kitaev两种映射。

Result: 在Jordan-Wigner与Bravyi-Kitaev两种映射下，采用自旋块化Jordan-Wigner排序的UpCCD展现出最优的扩展性，而UCCSD的分布式资源需求显著更大。

Conclusion: 在隐形传态式分布式量子计算中，UpCCD配合自旋块化Jordan-Wigner排序是氢链量子化学最具资源效率的选择。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing (DQC) has been proposed as a way to scale quantum algorithms for practical applications beyond monolithic quantum processor architectures. Among these applications, quantum chemistry is widely regarded as one of the most promising use cases for quantum computing. In this work, we estimate the distributed-resource requirements of unitary coupled-cluster (UCC) ansätze for quantum chemistry, focusing on unitary coupled-cluster singles and doubles (UCCSD), unitary pair coupled-cluster doubles (UpCCD), and unitary pair coupled-cluster with generalized singles and doubles (UpCCGSD) circuits for hydrogen chains. We focus on a teleportation-based approach to DQC, quantitatively comparing a naive distribution method to the output of the TeleSABRE algorithm. For both approaches, we estimate the cost of handling nonlocal two-qubit gates across a fixed midpoint or quarter-point partition, reporting Bell-pair/classical-communication costs in teleportation. Across Jordan-Wigner and Bravyi-Kitaev, we find that UpCCD with spin-blocked Jordan-Wigner ordering gives the most favorable scaling, while UCCSD incurs substantially larger distributed-resource requirements.

</details>


### [131] [Puncturing for Adaptive Entanglement-Assisted Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36273)
*Nicolai Peder Bülow Pedersen,Jakob Kaltoft Søndergaard,Jaron Skovsted Gundersen,René Bødker Christensen,Petar Popovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典/无辅助稳定子的打孔构造推广到纠缠辅助场景，通过"视为无辅助码—打孔接收端量子比特—恢复纠缠辅助表示"三步流程，在保持传输与逻辑量子比特数不变的前提下减少一个Bell对需求，并给出距离损失的界与保距条件；随机码实验显示该方案可在维持原距离的同时降低纠缠开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠辅助稳定子码虽能改善可达参数，但需消耗预共享Bell对；当Bell对供给是间歇的，需要单一母码能适应不同纠缠预算。因此需要一种可调节纠缠消耗、同时尽量不牺牲纠错能力的构造方法。

Method: 将现有针对标准/无辅助稳定子的打孔构造扩展到纠缠辅助情形，采用三步流程：先把码解释为无辅助稳定子码，再对接收端量子比特进行打孔，最后恢复纠缠辅助表示。该流程把所需Bell对数减少1，同时保持传输量子比特数与逻辑量子比特数不变；并推导距离损失上界及保持原码距离的条件。

Result: 对5至8个传输量子比特的随机生成纠缠辅助稳定子进行分析：其中14.75%的码距离大于对应的无辅助表示；在该子集中，81.4%至少存在一种打孔选择可保持原纠缠辅助码的距离。

Conclusion: 打孔可在降低纠缠（Bell对）需求的同时维持码距离，说明通过母码适配不同纠缠预算在纠缠辅助稳定子码中是可行的。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes are essential for reliable quantum communication. The achievable parameters can be improved by using entanglement-assisted stabilizers at the cost of consuming pre-shared Bell pairs. If the availability of such Bell pairs is intermittent, the ability to adapt the code to available resources is beneficial. That is, to pick a single mother code that can be adapted to different entanglement budgets. To achieve this, we extend an existing puncturing construction for standard/unassisted stabilizers to the entanglement-assisted setting through a three-step procedure: Interpreting the code as an unassisted stabilizer, puncturing a receiver-side qubit, and recovering an entanglement-assisted representation. This procedure reduces the number of required Bell pairs by one while preserving the number of transmitted and logical qubits. We derive a bound on the resulting distance loss and establish conditions for preserving the original code distance. Finally, we analyse distinct, randomly generated, entanglement-assisted stabilizers with five to eight transmitted qubits. Of these codes, 14.75% have a greater distance than their associated unassisted representations. Among this subset, 81.4% admit at least one puncturing choice that preserves the original entanglement-assisted distance. These results illustrate how puncturing can reduce entanglement requirements while maintaining code distance.

</details>


### [132] [Conversable Quantum Fire in the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36335)
*Ofer Casper,Barak Nehoran,Or Sattath*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型下基于一次性签名构造了"量子火"（quantum fire），首次实现可证明不可电报性，并引入"无密钥不可电报性"与"可对话性"两个新概念，给出了量子火的密码学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往所有具有可证明不可电报性（untelegraphability）的量子火构造都依赖于预言机（oracle），缺乏标准模型下的实例。作者希望在标准模型中构造量子火，并进一步刻画单向与双向经典通信在传输量子态能力上的本质差异，同时探索量子火的密码学应用价值。

Method: 基于一次性签名（one-shot signatures）构造量子火。核心思路是：量子火由可高效函数克隆但无法通过单向经典通信传输的量子态构成。作者引入并实现两个新性质：(a) 无密钥不可电报性（keyless untelegraphability），是原有不可电报性的强化；(b) 可对话性（conversability），即火焰虽不能通过单向经典通信传输，却可通过经典交互传输。构造了两个变体：变体一基于一次性签名（可由次指数级iO、次指数安全单向函数与LWE实例化），支持多项式数量的火焰和超对数级最小熵；变体二在更强假设（指数级不可伪造的一次性签名，可相对经典预言机实例化）下，支持指数级数量火焰和线性最小熵。

Result: 构造出标准模型下的可对话量子火，并给出两个变体：变体一支持多项式数量火焰与超对数级最小熵，基于次指数级iO、次指数安全单向函数与LWE；变体二支持指数数量火焰与线性最小熵，基于指数级不可伪造的一次性签名（可相对经典预言机实例化）。利用无密钥不可电报性、可对话性与可克隆性三者的结合，给出量子火的密码学应用：无密钥不可电报性迫使有效火焰序列号的分布具有高最小熵，而可对话性与可克隆性使得对应火焰仅用经典通信即可被克隆和转移。

Conclusion: 本文首次在标准模型下实现了具有可证明不可电报性的量子火，并提出了无密钥不可电报性与可对话性两个强化/新性质，揭示了单向经典通信与双向经典交互在传输量子火能力上的定性差异，进而给出了量子火的早期密码学应用之一。

Abstract: Quantum fire consists of quantum states that can be efficiently functionally cloned but cannot be transmitted using one-way classical communication. Whereas all previous quantum-fire constructions with proven untelegraphability are relative to an oracle, ours, based on one-shot signatures, is in the standard model.
  Our construction achieves two further notions that we introduce: (a) keyless untelegraphability, a strengthening of untelegraphability; and (b) conversability, meaning that a flame, though not telegraphable via one-way classical communication, can be transmitted via classical interaction. Hence one-way and two-way classical communication differ qualitatively in their power to transmit this quantum fire.
  We use these two novel properties of quantum fire, along with its clonability, to give one of the first cryptographic applications of quantum fire. Keyless untelegraphability forces the distribution of serial numbers of valid flames to have high min-entropy, while conversability and clonability allow the corresponding flame to be cloned and transferred using only classical communication.
  We present two variants of our conversable quantum-fire construction. The first, based on one-shot signatures (instantiable from subexponential iO, subexponentially secure one-way functions, and LWE), supports polynomially many flames, and super-logarithmic min-entropy of the serial-number distribution. The second, under the stronger assumption of exponentially unforgeable one-shot signatures (instantiable relative to a classical oracle), supports exponentially many flames, and linear min-entropy.

</details>


### [133] [Separability rigidity of Gaussian states under positive domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36336)
*Evgeny Shchukin,Peter van Loock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明有限模高斯态的"可分性刚性"定理：若高斯态 $\\rho_G$ 支配某个跨固定划分 $\\mathcal I$ 可分的非零正算子 $T$，则 $\\rho_G$ 本身跨 $\\mathcal I$ 可分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高斯态的可分性与纠缠结构，特别是支配关系（domination）对可分性的约束。目标是厘清有限模高斯态在凸混合、全不可分与真正多体纠缠之间的关系，并探究可分性蕴含是否可逆。

Method: 提出并证明一个可分性刚性定理：设有高斯态 $\\rho_G$ 与固定模划分 $\\mathcal I$，若存在非零正算子 $T$（$0\\neq T\\preccurlyeq\\rho_G$，可分且可非高斯）被 $\\rho_G$ 支配，则 $\\rho_G$ 跨 $\\mathcal I$ 可分。并据此分析凸类、全不可分与真正多体纠缠的等价性、纠缠激活问题，并构造反例检验逆命题。

Result: （1）有限多个可分划分的凸混合不产生新的高斯态：属于该凸类的高斯态必在某一固定划分下可分；（2）对有限模高斯态，全不可分与真正多体纠缠等价；（3）双可分高斯态的相同拷贝无法激活真正多体纠缠；（4）逆命题不成立：可构造一个全可分高斯态支配某真正多体纠缠态的正倍数。

Conclusion: 高斯态的支配关系对其可分结构具有强刚性约束，凸混合不能制造新的高斯纠缠，且全不可分与真正多体纠缠在高斯情形下重合；同时可分性蕴含不可逆，说明支配条件的充分性而非必要性。

Abstract: We prove a separability rigidity theorem for finite-mode Gaussian states. Let $ρ_G$ be Gaussian and let $\mathcal I$ be a fixed partition of the modes. If $ρ_G$ dominates a nonzero positive operator $T$ that is separable across $\mathcal I$, $0\neq T\preccurlyeq ρ_G$, then $ρ_G$ itself is separable across $\mathcal I$. The dominated operator is arbitrary and need not be Gaussian. As consequences, convex mixing among finitely many separability partitions creates no new Gaussian states: a Gaussian state belonging to such a convex class is already separable across one fixed partition. In particular, full inseparability and genuine multipartite entanglement coincide for finite-mode Gaussian states, and identical copies of a biseparable Gaussian state cannot activate genuine multipartite entanglement. We also show that the implication cannot be reversed by constructing a fully separable Gaussian state that dominates a positive multiple of a genuine multipartite entangled state.

</details>


### [134] [Near-Optimal Quantum Algorithm and Complexity Analysis for Riccati Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37207)
*Jingyao Wang,Yanqiao Wang,Bowen Li,Jin-Peng Liu,Zhengfeng Ji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为连续/离散代数Riccati方程、微分Riccati方程及有限Riccati递推设计了量子算法，通过量子加权Riesz方法构造解矩阵的块编码，并给出近最优查询复杂度与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riccati方程广泛出现在线性二次控制、量子化学与最优控制中，其经典求解代价高；作者希望借助量子计算在访问与归一化假设下实现查询复杂度最优的求解，并刻画问题的内在复杂度结构。

Method: 提出量子加权Riesz方法，统一处理四类Riccati问题：利用加权Riesz分支算子构造解图的投影算子并恢复解矩阵；针对CAREs/DAREs、DREs与有限时域问题分析查询复杂度；对指定输入预言机族建立乘积查询下界；并通过显式电路生成族证明特定值承诺问题的BQP完全性。

Result: CAREs和DAREs的查询复杂度在忽略对数因子下为Θ(Rα)，其中R为广义奇异性因子（反映谱分离），α为输出归一化；DREs与有限时域问题额外乘以初始化条件因子κ_init。建立乘积查询下界，说明必须同时依赖谱分离与初始化/输出尺度；并证明单控制离散时间代数与零终端微分Riccati方程的选定值承诺问题在电路生成族上BQP完全。

Conclusion: 该工作为多类Riccati方程提供了统一的量子求解框架与复杂度刻画，兼具近最优查询复杂度、匹配下界与BQP完全性结果，并在线性二次控制的经典反馈评估、量子化学中稳定随机相位近似Riccati方程的查询复杂度比较以及加热边界控制中给出应用与数值验证。

Abstract: We develop quantum algorithms that construct block-encodings of solution matrices for continuous- and discrete-time algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs and DAREs), differential Riccati equations (DREs), and finite Riccati recursions. The Quantum Weighted Riesz Method, unifying four kinds of Riccati problems, constructs projectors onto the solution graphs using weighted Riesz branching operators and recovers the solution matrices. Under the stated access and normalization assumptions, our algorithms for CAREs and DAREs have near-optimal query complexity $Θ(\mathcal Rα)$ up to logarithmic factors, with the generalized singularity factor $\mathcal R$ that reflects spectral separation, and the output normalization $α$. For DREs and finite-horizon problems, the query complexity has an additional initialization conditioning factor $κ_{\rm init}$. We establish product query lower bounds for specified input-oracle families, demonstrating necessary joint dependence on spectral separation and initialization or output scale. We also prove that selected-value promise problems are BQP-complete on explicit circuit-generated families of single-control discrete-time algebraic and zero-terminal differential Riccati equations. Applications include classical feedback evaluation for linear-quadratic control, a query-complexity comparison for stable random-phase approximation Riccati equations in quantum chemistry, and heated boundary control with numerical validation.

</details>


### [135] [Dynamical resource theory of time-reversal symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36408)
*Jisho Miyazaki,Kohdai Kuroiwa,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个操作性框架，用于量化量子信道（包括含噪过程）的固有时间反演破缺（T-violation），并将其分解为运动学T-violation与非幺正性两部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立系统的时间反演对称性由哈密顿量直接决定，但在含噪量子过程中，如何量化T-violation的幅度长期存在争议；同时，"时间反演对称性破缺"与"不可逆性"常被混淆。

Method: 将操作定义的时间反演变换纳入动力学资源理论（dynamical resource theory），把T-violation形式化为一种资源，并对T-violation进行分解分析，进而研究运动学T-violation的资源理论结构。

Result: T-violation可分解为两部分：源于反幺正运动反转的运动学T-violation，以及纯由热力学驱动的非幺正性（nonunitality）。运动学T-violation的资源理论既非quality-like也非quantity-like，并证明即使在经典信道中也存在普适的"黄金单元"（universal golden unit）。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了时间反演对称性破缺与不可逆性之间的长期混淆，并为量化含噪量子过程中的固有T-violation提供了操作上有意义的理论工具。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry and its violation (T-violation) are fundamental across diverse physics domains, from particle physics to fluctuation theorems and reciprocity. While time-reversal symmetry for isolated systems is simply determined by the Hamiltonian, quantifying the magnitude of T-violation, especially in noisy quantum processes, has been a subject of ongoing debate. Here, we propose an operationally meaningful framework to quantify the intrinsic T-violation of quantum channels. We integrate operationally defined time-reversal transformations into dynamical resource theory to formulate T-violation as a resource. T-violation decomposes into two distinct components: kinematic T-violation originating from antiunitary motion-reversal, and nonunitality driven purely by thermodynamics. This decomposition resolves the longstanding confusion between broken time-reversal symmetry and non-invertibility. Finally, we analyze the resulting resource theory of kinematic T-violation, showing that it is neither quality-like nor quantity-like, and proving the existence of a universal golden unit even within classical channels.

</details>


### [136] [Consistent histories and the ultrametric on infinite tensor products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37375)
*Andrew Lesniewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为累积量子记录的"历史"构造了一种渐近几何：当归一化Dowker–Halliwell干涉因子分解为记录的累积保真度时，其衰减由累积平方Bures距离控制。无限注册极限下，Kakutani–von Neumann二分给出"弱等价记录序列"与"正交超选扇区"的尖锐替代；Bures缺陷的收敛指数定义了一个伪超度量，商掉零距离退化后得到完备超度量，并与规范不变的扇区度量$\tilde d$一致。其值"一致性速率"$\delta$有三种等价解释（扇区分离度量、干涉对数增长指数、Helstrom最小误差概率指数），并由经典分离轮廓决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致性历史（consistent histories）框架仅在干涉项严格消失时才能赋予历史序列经典概率，但有限模型中干涉项一般不严格为零，其"小"本身无法在历史空间上诱导任何渐近几何。因此需要为累积量子记录的历史构建一种渐近度量结构，以刻画干涉衰减、记录序列等价性与扇区分裂的定量规律。

Method: 1）设定每条历史携带逐渐累积的量子记录，考察两条历史间的归一化Dowker–Halliwell干涉；2）在其分解为记录累积保真度时，用累积平方Bures距离控制干涉衰减；3）取无限注册极限，借助Kakutani–von Neumann二分分析Bures缺陷的可和性——可和⇒记录序列弱等价，不可和⇒正交超选扇区且归一化干涉消失；4）用Bures缺陷的收敛指数定义历史空间上的伪超度量，对零距离退化取商得到完备超度量，并与规范不变扇区度量$\tilde d$比较；5）通过Helstrom公式将该指数与最小误差概率的衰减指数联系；6）分析经典分离轮廓（长时间平均缺陷、边界轮廓$d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$）对速率$\delta$的影响。

Result: - 累积平方Bures距离支配归一化Dowker–Halliwell干涉的衰减；其收敛指数定义了一个伪超度量，商掉零距离后为完备超度量，且与规范不变扇区度量$\tilde d$一致。
- 该完备超度量的构造不要求结果空间紧致，也不要求记录族的连续性。
- 一致性速率$\delta$具三种等价解释：（i）扇区分离度量；（ii）归一化干涉的负对数的多项式增长指数；（iii）由Helstrom公式给出的、区分累积记录的最小误差概率的负对数对应的指数。
- 经典分离轮廓决定速率：正的长时平均缺陷给出最大值$\delta=1$；边界轮廓$d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$在零多项式速率下产生不同超选扇区，即$\delta=0$。

Conclusion: 该工作为累积量子记录的历史建立了一个由Bures缺陷收敛指数驱动的渐近几何，将干涉衰减、记录序列弱等价/正交超选扇区二分、扇区分离度量、统计判别误差以及经典分离轮廓统一在同一"一致性速率"$\delta$之下，从而在不依赖严格干涉消失、不要求紧致结果空间或记录连续性的一般情形下，为一致性历史提供定量的扇区结构刻画。

Abstract: The consistent histories formulation of quantum mechanics assigns classical probabilities to sequences of coarse-grained events when the corresponding interference terms vanish. In finite models these terms generically need not vanish exactly, and their smallness alone supplies no asymptotic geometry on the space of histories. We construct such a geometry for histories that accumulate quantum records. When the normalized Dowker - Halliwell interference between two histories factorizes as the cumulative fidelity of their records, its decay is controlled by the accumulated squared Bures distance. In the infinite registration limit, the Kakutani - von Neumann dichotomy gives a sharp sector alternative: summable Bures defect yields weakly equivalent record sequences, whereas nonsummable defect produces orthogonal superselection sectors and vanishing normalized interference. The convergence exponent of the Bures defect defines a pseudo-ultrametric on histories. After quotienting its zero distance degeneracy, it becomes a complete ultrametric, requiring neither compactness of the outcome space nor continuity of the record family, and coincides with the gauge invariant sector metric $\tilde d$ introduced in \cite{LesTP}. Its value, the \emph{consistency rate}, has three equivalent interpretations: it is the sector separation metric, the polynomial growth exponent of the negative logarithm of normalized interference, and, by the Helstrom formula, the corresponding exponent of the negative logarithm of the minimum error probability for discriminating the cumulative records. Classical separation profiles determine the rate: positive long time mean defect gives the maximal value $δ=1$, while the borderline profile $d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$ produces distinct superselection sectors at zero polynomial rate, $δ=0$.

</details>


### [137] [Transverse momentum as the counter-diabatic generator in bent waveguide couplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36431)
*Yu-kai Lee,Shuo-Yen Tseng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出弯曲模式演化耦合器的横向动量算符 $\hat{p}_x$ 即是其反绝热（CD）协议的生成元，并据此给出弯曲轴斜率的闭式表达，无需数值搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲耦合器已被证明可实现CD协议，但其背后的物理生成元尚不明确；同时斜率的确定原本依赖数值搜索，缺乏解析形式。

Method: 在实超模基下分析横向动量算符 $\hat{p}_x$（对角元为零、非对角元纯虚，双模下正比于 $\sigma_y$），证明该算符即两波导耦合器的CD项生成元；将$\hat{p}_x$提供的项与非绝热耦合相等，推导轴斜率闭式解，并用交换子恒等式将其改写为耦合模变量 $\dot{x}_o=\dot{\theta}/(\gamma\,\delta\beta^2)$；通过光束传播仿真验证。

Result: 闭式表达式与数值偏移搜索吻合至0.3%，且无需搜索；仿真中弯曲耦合器与经幺正变换实现的直耦合器分别达到0.994与1.000的超模保真度，而未处理耦合器仅为0.886；两者带宽与制造容差一致，内部轨迹恰好相差相应的参考系变换。

Conclusion: 弯曲耦合器的CD生成元必然是横向动量算符，而非人为设计；该结果给出无需搜索的解析设计方法，并首次将弯曲实现与幺正变换实现的CD协议进行对比，二者性能等价。

Abstract: Bending the axis of a mode-evolution coupler suppresses the nonadiabatic coupling between its supermodes, and bent couplers of this kind were recently shown to realize the counter-diabatic (CD) protocol. Here we identify the operator responsible. Rigid lateral displacement of a waveguide structure is generated by the transverse momentum $\hat p_x$, whose diagonal matrix elements vanish in a real supermode basis and whose off-diagonal element is purely imaginary; in a two-mode system such an operator is proportional to $\sy$. The CD term of a two-waveguide coupler is itself proportional to $\sy$, while the detuning and the coupling, the two parameters set by the waveguide widths and spacing, lie in the $σ_z$--$σ_x$ plane and cannot produce it. The transverse momentum is therefore the CD generator of the bent coupler, by necessity rather than by design. Equating the term it supplies to the nonadiabatic coupling gives the axis slope in closed form as a ratio of two matrix elements of the unperturbed supermodes at a single cross section, which a commutator identity recasts in coupled-mode variables as $\dot{\xo}=\dotθ/(γ\dbeta^{2})$. The expression agrees with the numerical offset search to $0.3\%$ and requires no search. Beam propagation simulations confirm the design and compare it, for the first time, with the CD protocol realized by unitary transformation in a straight coupler built from the same reference structure. The two devices reach $0.994$ and $1.000$ supermode fidelity where the untreated coupler reaches $0.886$, and agree in bandwidth and fabrication tolerance, while their internal trajectories differ by exactly the frame transformation that relates them.

</details>


### [138] [Degree Balance as a Fine-Grained Complexity Boundary for Quantum SAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36464)
*Atsuya Hasegawa,Jonas Kamminga,François Le Gall,Suguru Tamaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究局部哈密顿量问题（QMA完全），在无阻挫（frustration-free）与近似正则两个温和假设下，给出了指数级更快的经典与量子算法，并通过(Q)SETH下界证明了算法对正则性依赖近乎最优，建立了量子可满足性的细粒度复杂性二分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部哈密顿量问题最坏情况下仅有O(2^n)经典和O(2^{n/2})量子暴力算法，由于基态高度纠缠，经典CSP策略难以直接迁移，因此探索在何种条件下能突破暴力界限十分必要。

Method: 提出两个温和假设：(1)YES实例上哈密顿量无阻挫；(2)近似正则，即每个量子比特受到的约束数大致相同。在此条件下设计指数级加速的经典与量子算法；并利用(Q)SETH构造量子5-SAT的下界，分析算法对正则性的依赖。

Result: 在假设成立时可指数级加速求解；下界表明量子5-SAT在(Q)SETH下无非平凡最坏情形加速，即使仅O(√n)个量子比特参与O(√n)个约束仍保持(Q)SETH难。若高自由度子集规模或其度数缩小n^δ倍，算法可在O(2^{(1-ε)n})时间内求解。

Conclusion: 上下界共同给出了量子可满足性的细粒度复杂性二分，说明算法对正则性的依赖近乎最优。

Abstract: The local Hamiltonian problem is the canonical $\mathsf{QMA}$-complete problem, and $O(2^n)$ time classical algorithms and $O(2^{n/2})$ time quantum algorithms are known to solve the problem in the worst case. It is not clear how to improve these brute force strategies for a broad class of the problem because ground states are highly entangled in general, and we cannot directly apply known strategies for classical CSPs.
  In this work, we present exponentially faster classical and quantum algorithms under two mild assumptions: (1) the Hamiltonian is frustration-free on YES instances, and (2) it is approximately regular, meaning that every qubit is acted upon by approximately the same number of constraints.
  We complement these upper bounds by showing that, assuming (Q)SETH, quantum 5-SAT admits no non-trivial worst-case speedup. Our lower bound further demonstrates that the dependence of our algorithms on regularity is in some sense nearly optimal. Specifically, quantum 5-SAT remains (Q)SETH-hard even for Hamiltonians in which all but $O(\sqrt{n})$ qubits participate in only constantly many constraints, while the remaining $O(\sqrt{n})$ qubits each participate in $O(\sqrt{n})$ constraints. By contrast, if either the size of this high-degree subset or the degrees of its qubits is reduced by a factor of $n^δ$, for any $δ>0$, our algorithm solves the problem in time $O(2^{(1- \varepsilon)n})$ for some $\varepsilon>0$.
  Together, our upper and lower bounds establish a fine-grained complexity dichotomy for quantum satisfiability.

</details>


### [139] [Resonance Breaking in Noisy Shor's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37636)
*Zhengwei Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析噪声量子电路中的共振破坏现象，论证在任意小错误率的单层去极化噪声下，Shor算法的指数级量子优势被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Shor算法在噪声环境下的鲁棒性，探讨任意小错误率的单层去极化噪声是否足以瓦解其指数级量子优势。

Method: 1. 将n量子比特串上的测量分布表示为Pauli路径积分中4^n个波函数的叠加；2. 分析中间层存在速率λ的独立去极化噪声时，不同Hamming权重d对应的测量率上界；3. 设计多项式时间O(n^d)的经典因数分解算法以替代低频贡献。

Result: 对任意Hamming权重d，高频项测量率上界为2(1-λ)^d，低频项上界为O(n^d)/2^{n/2}；当n和d趋于无穷时测量率趋近于零；无噪声情况下达到共振峰的比特串保证了分解大数的恒定成功测量率；共振破坏摧毁了指数级量子优势。

Conclusion: 单层任意小错误率的去极化噪声即可通过共振破坏现象破坏Shor算法的指数级量子优势，且可用多项式时间经典算法替代低频贡献。

Abstract: We argue that the exponential quantum advantage in Shor's algorithm is broken under one-layer depolarizing noise of arbitrary small error rate, by analyzing a resonance breaking phenomenon in noisy quantum circuits. First, we express the distribution of measurements on $n$-qubit strings as the superposition of the $4^n$ wave functions in the Pauli path integral. In the noiseless case, the bit-strings achieving resonant peaks guaranteed a constant rate of successful measurements to factor a large number. Secondly, when the middle layer of the quantum circuit has independent depolarizing noise of rate $λ$, for any Hamming weight $d$, we obtain corresponding measurement rate bounded by $2(1-λ)^d$ for higher frequency terms and by $O(n^d)/2^{n/2}$ for low frequency terms. The rate approaches to zero as $n$ and $d$ approaches to infinity. Resonance breaking destroys the exponential quantum advantage. Furthermore, we design a classical factoring algorithm in polynomial time $O(n^d)$ to substitute the low frequency contribution.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Computing for Network Security Classification: Near-Term Classification and Long-Term Memory Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36479)
*Yuqing Li,Poonam Bala Nehru,Yunpeng Zhang,Danindu Gammanpilage,Xin Jin,Zeguan Wu,Junyu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过两个互补实验系统探讨量子计算对网络安全分类的近期与长期贡献：近期评估量子核SVM并对比经典SVM基线，长期使用量子预言机草图(QOS)研究流式样本分类的内存优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算已在多个网络安全场景中被探索，但其对网络安全分类在近期与长期的作用缺乏系统讨论，尤其是近期量子核方法能否实用、长期量子优势究竟体现在速度还是内存，均尚未厘清。

Method: 实验一：在KDD Cup 1999、CICIDS2017和BoT-IoT数据集上评估近期量子核支持向量机(SVM)，与经典SVM基线对比。实验二：采用量子预言机草图(QOS)，在线处理流式经典样本，增量构建近似量子预言机，为下游量子算法提供相干查询访问而无需保存整个数据集。

Result: 近期实验中，量子核表现具竞争力，在部分设置下可持平或优于经典基线，但经典RBF核在其他设置下仍更强。长期实验中，在QOS启发的机器规模估计下，达到相当精度所需的有效内存规模代理显著小于显式稀疏/QRAM式存储；但与简单流式代理相比结果更微妙，因为激进的特性过滤会使流式维度变小。

Conclusion: 近期量子核方法应被视为依赖数据集的实用替代方案，而非普遍优越的替代；量子计算对网络安全分类的长期价值可能在于内存高效的数据访问，而非即时的运行时加速。两个实验共同展示了近期分类性能与长期内存效率两个维度的量子贡献。

Abstract: Quantum computing has already been explored in several network-security applications. However, how quantum computing may contribute to network-security classification in both the near term and the longer term has not been systematically discussed. This paper studies this question through two complementary experiments. First, we evaluate near-term quantum-kernel support vector machines (SVMs) on practical network-security classification tasks and compare them with classical SVM baselines on KDD Cup 1999, CICIDS2017, and BoT-IoT. Across these runs, quantum kernels are competitive. They can match or improve classical baselines in some settings, while classical RBF kernels remain stronger in others. This suggests that near-term quantum-kernel methods should be evaluated as practical, dataset-dependent alternatives to classical kernels rather than as uniformly superior replacements. Second, we use quantum oracle sketching (QOS) to study a longer-term memory advantage for classification with streaming classical samples. In QOS, samples are processed online and used to incrementally construct an approximate quantum oracle, which provides coherent query access for downstream quantum algorithms without retaining the entire dataset. Under the QOS-inspired machine-size estimate, comparable accuracy corresponds to a substantially smaller effective memory-size proxy than explicit sparse/QRAM-style storage. Compared with a simple streaming proxy, the result is more nuanced because aggressive feature filtering can make the streaming dimension small. This suggests that the long-term value of quantum computing for network-security classification may lie in memory-efficient data access rather than immediate runtime speedup. Together, these experiments show how quantum computing may contribute to network-security classification from near-term classification performance and longer-term memory efficiency.

</details>


### [141] [Improved Quantum Algorithms for Black-Box Abelian Group Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36480)
*Junrong Luo,Yinan Li,Francois Le Gall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分解有限阿贝尔黑盒群为循环因子的量子算法，通过适配Regev因式分解算法中的量子采样与经典格约化方法，计算不变因子分解及对应循环生成元，并在电路规模、量子时间与空间上优于Cheung-Mosca算法及其公共模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分解有限阿贝尔黑盒群为循环因子是量子计算中的基础问题，已有Cheung-Mosca类算法在量子电路门数、量子时间与量子比特数上开销较大，需要更高效的算法。

Method: 算法适配Regev因式分解算法中的量子采样与经典格约化方法；证明所用整数关系格以高概率具有欧氏范数不超过exp(O(sqrt(n)))的整数基；在保持格约化维度为O(sqrt(n))的同时纳入全部O(n)个采样生成元。

Result: 对阶至多2^n、具有唯一编码且群运算可逆代价T_op=Omega(sqrt(n))的群G，算法使用O(sqrt(n))个量子电路，每个电路含O~(n T_op)个门且执行至多O(n)次。相比Cheung-Mosca算法及其公共模实现：电路门数总和由O~(n^2 T_op)降至O~(n^{3/2} T_op)，总量子时间由O~(n^3 T_op)降至O~(n^{5/2} T_op)，量子空间由O(n^2)降至O(n)量子比特；经典计算使用多项式位运算与群运算查询。

Conclusion: 该算法以高概率给出有限阿贝尔黑盒群的不变因子分解及循环生成元，并显著降低量子资源开销，改进依赖于整数关系格的短基性质与保留Regev算法的电路规模优势。

Abstract: Decomposing finite Abelian black-box groups into cyclic factors is a basic problem in quantum computation. We give a quantum algorithm for decomposing finite Abelian black-box groups by adapting the quantum sampling and classical lattice-reduction method of Regev's factoring algorithm. The algorithm computes an invariant-factor decomposition and corresponding cyclic generators with high probability. For a group G of order at most 2^n with unique encodings and reversible group-operation cost T_op = Omega(sqrt(n)), our algorithm uses O(sqrt(n)) quantum circuits, each with O~(n T_op) gates and executed at most O(n) times.
  For comparison, we consider the Cheung-Mosca algorithm, which decomposes the Sylow p-subgroups separately. We also consider the common-modulus implementation of the extended Cheung-Mosca algorithm. Our algorithm reduces the sum of the circuit gate counts from O~(n^2 T_op) to O~(n^(3/2) T_op). The total quantum time bound decreases from O~(n^3 T_op) to O~(n^(5/2) T_op), and the quantum space bound decreases from O(n^2) to O(n) qubits. The classical computation uses polynomially many bit operations and group-operation queries.
  These improvements rely on two technical ingredients. We prove that the integer relation lattices used in our algorithm admit, with high probability, integral bases whose vectors have Euclidean norm at most exp(O(sqrt(n))). We preserve the circuit-size advantage of Regev's algorithm for group decomposition by incorporating all O(n) sampled generators while keeping the lattice-reduction dimension at O(sqrt(n)).

</details>


### [142] [Improved estimate of local Markovianity for quantum Gibbs states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38007)
*Tai Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文改进了量子Gibbs态在任意固定正温度下的局部Markov性质，证明有限程相互作用下条件互信息（CMI）随距离指数衰减，前置因子仅指数依赖于边界大小，优于此前Chen-Rouzé的指数体积依赖结果。方法为静态证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Chen与Rouzé建立的局部Markov界中前置因子对体积（边界大小）的指数依赖，获得更优的局部Markov性质，并推广至多种衰减型相互作用。

Method: 采用静态证明，与Chen-Rouzé的动力学方法不同。将Gibbs态与通过移除边界相互作用得到的产品态进行比较，把CMI界归约为相对熵损失，并利用局域性估计控制该项。

Result: 有限程、有界相互作用度：CMI随分离指数衰减，前置因子指数依赖于边界大小；指数与拉伸指数衰减相互作用：拉伸指数CMI衰减，前置因子依赖边界；幂律相互作用：反对数或代数衰减，区域大小依赖为多项式。

Conclusion: 在任意固定正温度下获得改进的局部Markov性质，统一刻画多种相互作用衰减下的CMI衰减行为，且证明为静态方法。

Abstract: We establish improved locally Markovian properties of quantum Gibbs states at every fixed positive temperature. For finite-range interactions of bounded interaction degree, the conditional mutual information (CMI) decays exponentially with separation, with a prefactor exponential in the boundary size. This improves the exponential volume dependence of the local Markov bounds established by Chen and Rouzé \cite{Chen2025}. For exponentially and stretched-exponentially decaying interactions, we obtain stretched-exponential CMI decay with a boundary-dependent prefactor. For power-law interactions, we prove inverse-logarithmic or algebraic decay with polynomial region-size dependence. Our proof is static, in contrast to the dynamical approach of Chen and Rouzé. We compare the Gibbs state with a product state obtained by removing boundary interactions, reducing the CMI bound to a relative-entropy loss that we control using locality estimates.

</details>


### [143] [Affine spin-pencil factorization of generalized driven anisotropic Rabi--Stark Hamiltonians in an inhomogeneous orthosymplectic superalgebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36511)
*B. M. Rodriguez-Lara,F. H. Maldonado-Villamizar,A. Kafuri,A. Moroz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用非均匀正交感超代数中的仿射因子构造广义驱动各向异性Rabi–Stark哈密顿量的超对称分解，得到Witten指数为2及一系列极限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理驱动各向异性Rabi–Stark类模型，并揭示其超对称代数结构与可解性条件。

Method: 引入仿射因子Â=Ĉ+âĎ（Ĉ、Ď为实可逆自旋矩阵），在非均匀正交感超代数中构造超对称分解；利用六个实铅笔参数约束九个哈密顿量频率与一个能量偏移（至整体频率标度）；通过正规乘积Â†Â及其反正规伙伴ÂÂ†定义Witten指数；对相异铅笔根且基线斜率有限情形，用相干规范构造Bargmann层级与例外有限多项式态的端点条件。

Result: 正规乘积Â†Â具有精确零能基态二重态，Witten指数为2；自旋分级组织出四类极限情形：两类基线全部精确的驱动振子族，以及受约束的各向异性Rabi与Rabi–Stark面，二者在退化量子比特各向同性Rabi模型处相交；一个对齐的内部示例同时包含直接与纵向驱动、Stark形变和强度依赖耦合，并保留该分解。

Conclusion: 该超级对称因子分解为驱动各向异性Rabi–Stark模型提供了统一框架，统一了驱动振子族与受约束Rabi/Rabi–Stark面，并支持包含多种驱动与耦合形变的可解内部示例。

Abstract: We construct a supersymmetric factorization for generalized driven anisotropic Rabi--Stark Hamiltonians using an affine factor $\hat{A}=\hat{C}+\hat{a}\hat{D}$ in an inhomogeneous orthosymplectic superalgebra, with two real invertible spin matrices $\hat{C}$ and $\hat{D}$. The six real pencil parameters constrain nine Hamiltonian frequencies and one energy offset up to an overall frequency scale. The normal product $\hat{A}^{\dagger}\hat{A}$ has an exact zero-energy ground doublet and, with its antinormal partner $\hat{A}\hat{A}^{\dagger}$, gives a Witten index of two. For distinct pencil roots with finite baseline slopes, coherent gauges define a Bargmann hierarchy and endpoint conditions for exceptional finite-polynomial states. The spin grading organizes four limiting cases, comprising two driven-oscillator families with every baseline exact and the constrained anisotropic-Rabi and Rabi--Stark sheets, which meet at the degenerate-qubit isotropic Rabi model. An aligned interior example adds direct and longitudinal drives, Stark deformation, and intensity-dependent coupling while retaining the factorization.

</details>


### [144] [Exponential Advantage of Quantum over Classical References in Leakage Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36541)
*Zheng An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在编码子空间无经典描述时如何检测泄漏，提出利用同一编码器制备的独立量子参考态，通过相干测量实现泄漏检测，并给出精确检测律与最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泄漏检测依赖已知编码子空间的投影算符，但当编码子空间无法经典指定时投影算符不可用，因此需要一种无需经典描述编码的泄漏检测方法。

Method: 使用同一编码器制备的独立量子参考态；对参考态与单条消息进行相干测量，使二维编码子空间内所有状态（包括与远端系统的纠缠）保持不变；推导M个理想参考态下的精确检测律，并在零虚警条件下证明最优性；比较相干测量与先测量所有参考态的策略。

Result: 对正交泄漏，漏检概率渐近为4/M，且与环境的维度d无关；先测量所有参考态即使在集体测量下，在相同零虚警要求下任意有限预算检测率均为零；对固定检测目标，最优的“先测量”成本在容差ε足够小时为Θ(d/ε)，而相干预算可与维度无关；一个逻辑量子比特编码到三个物理量子比特时，七个相干参考态可实现至少50%正交泄漏检测，而相同1%正常容差下任何“先测量”接收器至少需要588个。

Conclusion: 量子参考态支持无需经典重构的泄漏检查，并相对“先测量”方案具有随物理量子比特数指数增长的样本优势。

Abstract: Leakage from a known encoding subspace can be detected by projection. However, the corresponding projector is unavailable when the encoding subspace is not classically specified. Here we show how independent quantum references prepared by the same encoder enable leakage detection without a classical description of the encoding. A coherent measurement on the references and a single message leaves every state within the two-dimensional encoding subspace, including its entanglement with a remote system, exactly unchanged. We derive the exact detection law for $M$ ideal references and prove optimality among tests with zero false alarm for every encoding. For orthogonal leakage, the miss probability is asymptotic to $4/M$, independent of the ambient dimension $d$. Measuring all references first, even collectively, gives zero detection at every finite budget under the same zero-false-alarm requirement. For a fixed detection target between zero and one, the optimal measurement-first cost is $Θ(d/ε)$ at sufficiently small tolerance $ε$ on normal false alarm and conditional disturbance; a dimension-independent coherent budget suffices. For one logical qubit encoded in three physical qubits, seven coherent references achieve at least $50\%$ orthogonal-leakage detection, whereas any measurement-first receiver needs at least $588$ under the same $1\%$ normal tolerances. Quantum references thus support leakage checks without classical reconstruction, with a sample advantage exponential in the number of physical qubits.

</details>


### [145] [Factorization dynamics between quantum Fisher information and quantum coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36571)
*Xinzhi Zhao,Xinglei Yu,Liangsheng Li,Wenting Zhou,Chengjie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论与实验研究量子Fisher信息(QFI)与量子相干性之间的因式分解关系,在qubit与qutrit系统中验证该定律,并给出无需末态信息的参数估计方差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QFI刻画量子态对参数变化的敏感度,是量子度量学的核心;量子相干性是量子信息处理的关键资源。尽管量子度量学各方向已有大量研究,但QFI动力学与量子相干性动力学之间的相互作用关系尚未被探索,作者希望揭示二者的内在联系。

Method: 在理论上探讨QFI与量子相干性之间的因式分解关系;实验上制备qubit与qutrit系统的纯态,并施加幺正演化,通过测量验证该因式分解定律;进一步推导出不依赖末态信息的估计参数方差下界。

Result: 实验结果证实了因式分解定律,为QFI与量子相干性之间的关系提供了有力证据;并成功建立一个无需末态信息即可给出的估计参数方差下界。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了QFI与量子相干性之间的深层联系,为量子参数估计提供新见解,并在量子传感与量子度量学中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum Fisher information (QFI) quantifies the sensitivity of a quantum state to a parameter change and plays a key role in quantum metrology. Meanwhile, quantum coherence is a crucial resource for quantum information processing. However, despite extensive studies on various aspects of quantum metrology, the interplay between the dynamics of the QFI and quantum coherence remains unexplored.
