Here, we explore the factorization relationship between the QFI and quantum coherence, both theoretically and experimentally. We prepare pure states in qubit and qutrit systems and apply unitary evolution to investigate this relationship. Our results confirm the factorization law, offering strong evidence for this relationship. Furthermore, we establish a lower bound on the variance of the estimated parameter that does not require the final state information. This approach reveals a deep connection between the QFI and quantum coherence, providing insights into quantum parameter estimation. These findings have potential applications in quantum sensing and quantum metrology.

</details>


### [146] [Shared Phase Arithmetic for Parallel Quantum Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36574)
*Anbang Wu,Liqiang Lu,Pu Pang,Jianwei Yin,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出并行相位回授（PPK）框架，将相位函数表示为可逆计算的值并相干累加至相位梯度态，从而将相位函数的表示成本与应用成本分离，并给出T门计数与误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对角量子门层可由计算基态上的整数值函数刻画，因此其实现可分解为对该函数的可逆求值与相位转换两步。作者希望借此视角区分"表示相位函数"与"施加相位"的开销，寻找共享相位算术能否降低摊销旋转成本的条件。

Method: 提出PPK：相干计算若干旋转参数的加权和，一次性加到相位梯度态上，再反计算。构造适配一般权重、旋转对以及二进支持不相交的权重。采用仅Clifford编码，b比特相位分辨率下每批次≤4(b−1)个T门（不计初始化）。首个相位参考用Gridsynth独立合成，后续相位梯度态由支持不相交构造生成，各需O(b)个T门。

Result: 证明了构造的正确性并给出显式误差界，建立了上述T门计数；明确了共享相位算术在何时能降低摊销旋转成本、以及编码开销何时会限制该优势。

Conclusion: PPK给出相位函数表示与应用成本的清晰分离，其门数界可作为判断批量旋转是否值得采用共享相位算术的定量依据。

Abstract: A layer of diagonal quantum gates can be specified by an integer-valued function on computational basis states. This description suggests implementing the layer by evaluating that function reversibly and translating its value into a phase using a shared quantum register. We develop this viewpoint for parallel phase kickback (PPK): the weighted sum of several rotation parameters is computed coherently, added once to a phase-gradient state, and then uncomputed. The construction separates the cost of representing a phase function from the cost of applying it. We prove correctness, give explicit error bounds, and analyze logical gate counts for general weights, pairs of rotations, and weights with disjoint binary support. With Clifford-only encoding, a batch uses at most $4(b-1)$ T gates at $b$-bit phase resolution, excluding initialization. The first phase reference is prepared with independent rotations synthesized by Gridsynth; additional phase-gradient states then follow from the disjoint-support construction with $O(b)$ T gates each. The resulting bounds identify when shared phase arithmetic reduces amortized rotation cost and when encoding overhead limits that advantage.

</details>


### [147] [Root-system structure of sloppiness in passive Gaussian metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36579)
*A. Kafuri,B. M. Rodriguez-Lara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究被动变换下n模压缩探针的量子Fisher矩阵秩亏导致的局部不可辨识性，利用Sp(2n,R)/U(n)的C_n限制根系统刻画该"松弛性"，并求解E-最优探针设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动变换下n模压缩探针在量子Fisher矩阵降秩时会出现局部不可辨识（sloppiness）现象，需要一个统一的代数结构来精确刻画哪些方向不可辨识，并在此约束下优化探针设计。

Method: 针对纯零均值高斯探针，采用Sp(2n,R)/U(n)的C_n限制根系统进行分析：根2r_j标记局部相位旋转，r_j±r_k标记两束分离器正交分量，其消失对应Fisher零方向；并通过Fisher权重量化接近各奇异壁的程度，在固定正平均光子数下求解E-最优设计（两种生成元归一化）。

Result: 在正则相位与束分离器角度约定下，基本Weyl腔内的唯一最优解为等差数列，在大资源极限下趋近连续奇数对偶Weyl方向；采用不变生成元范数时，最小特征值与被动参考系无关，最优解恰好落在该射线上。均匀压缩最小化局域相位A-最优代价，但使每个模式对的某一束分离器正交分量不可辨识。每个可辨识模式对的两个正交分量饱和量子几何不相容界，最大相容被动子模型在正则谱下含n(n+1)/2个参数。

Conclusion: 该分类清晰区分了局部可辨识性、灵敏度优化与同时可达性三个问题层次，为多模压缩探针的被动变换设计提供了代数化框架。

Abstract: Passive transformations of an $n$-mode squeezed probe become locally unidentifiable when the quantum Fisher matrix loses rank. For pure zero-mean Gaussian probes, we show that the $C_{n}$ restricted-root system of $\mathrm{Sp}(2n,\mathbb{R})/\mathrm{U}(n)$ governs this exact sloppiness. For squeezing magnitudes $r_{1},\ldots,r_{n}$ in the canonical basis, the roots $2r_{j}$ and $r_{j}\pm r_{k}$ label the local phase rotations and two beam-splitter quadratures, and their vanishing identifies every Fisher-null direction. The associated Fisher weights quantify the approach to each singular wall. At fixed positive mean photon number, we solve the E-optimal probe-design problem of maximizing the smallest passive Fisher eigenvalue in two explicit generator normalizations. In the canonical phase and beam-splitter angle convention, the unique optimum in the fundamental Weyl chamber is an arithmetic progression approaching the consecutive-odd-integer dual-Weyl direction at large resource. With an invariant generator norm, the smallest eigenvalue is independent of the passive frame and the optimum lies exactly on that ray at every resource. Uniform squeezing instead minimizes the local-phase A-optimal cost while leaving one beam-splitter quadrature unidentifiable for every mode pair. Finally, the two quadratures of each identifiable mode pair saturate the quantum-geometric incompatibility bound, and the largest compatible passive submodel has $n(n+1)/2$ parameters at a regular spectrum. The resulting classification separates local identifiability, sensitivity optimization, and simultaneous attainability.

</details>


### [148] [Synchronized Quantum Devices in Lossy Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36627)
*Ravi Singh Adhikari,Aman Gupta,Xiaoyu Ai,Robert Malaney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并实验验证一种偏振辅助协议，利用自发参量下转换产生的纠缠光子对，在星地高损耗量子信道下延长量子设备网络同步的可靠获取范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠光子对的时间强耦合可用于改进量子设备间的网络同步，而天基量子互联网中同步对运行至关重要；但星地信道存在高损耗，需要一种能在光子稀缺条件下仍保持同步的机制。

Method: 设计偏振辅助的同步协议，并进行实验演示；通过蒙特卡洛仿真评估其在不同光子稀缺条件下的性能。

Result: 偏振辅助将可靠同步获取扩展到更高信道损耗，星地距离提升最高达40%；仿真表明性能改善在广泛的光子稀缺条件下成立。

Conclusion: 结果既为偏振辅助网络同步提供概念验证，也表明其在显著延长量子互联网骨干设备保持高度同步的适用范围方面具有实际意义。

Abstract: Due to their tight temporal coupling, entangled photon pairs produced by spontaneous parametric down-conversion offer a new route to improved network synchronization among quantum-enabled devices. In the emerging space-based quantum internet, such synchronization will be pivotal to operational success. Here, we propose and experimentally demonstrate a new polarization-assisted protocol that allows quantum-enabled devices to remain synchronized under the high loss conditions anticipated for satellite-to-ground quantum channels. Our experimental results show that polarization assistance extends reliable synchronization acquisition to higher channel losses and an increase of up to 40$\%$ in satellite-to-ground distance. Supporting Monte Carlo simulations show that this performance improvement extends over a wide range of photon-starved conditions. Collectively, these results not only provide a proof of concept for polarization-assisted network synchronization but also demonstrate its pragmatic importance in significantly extending the range under which quantum-enabled devices forming the backbone of the quantum internet can remain highly synchronized.

</details>


### [149] [Optimized Randomized Hamiltonian Simulation via Average-Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36694)
*Hayata Morisaki,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个随机化哈密顿量模拟框架，通过按任意概率采样哈密顿项并顺序实现短时演化，以采样生成元方差刻画主阶信道误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化哈密顿量模拟通过采样量子电路近似目标动力学，常可简化电路实现。qDRIFT 按算符范数比例采样，最小化最坏情况误差上界，但对典型输入态未必最优，因此需要面向平均误差的采样优化。

Method: 构建随机采样哈密顿项并顺序实现短时演化的框架，以采样生成元方差度量误差；分别最小化最坏情况误差上界与 Haar 平均误差上界，后者导出与各项 Hilbert-Schmidt 范数成比例的采样概率；并结合对易 Pauli 分组。

Result: 最小化最坏情况上界可恢复 qDRIFT；基于 Hilbert-Schmidt 范数的采样可给出比常规 qDRIFT 更小的平均误差上界。对 Pauli 串哈密顿量，在固定 R_z 深度下误差上界更小。Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev 模型误差上界改进因子随量子比特数线性增长；108 量子比特 FeMoco 哈密顿量误差上界约降低 5.65 倍。

Conclusion: 平均误差优化可作为随机化哈密顿量模拟的实用设计原则。

Abstract: Hamiltonian simulation is a central application of quantum computing. Randomized Hamiltonian simulation approximates the target dynamics by sampling quantum circuits and often allows simpler circuit implementations. We develop a framework for randomized Hamiltonian simulation in which Hamiltonian terms are sampled with arbitrary probabilities and the corresponding short-time evolutions are implemented sequentially. The leading channel error relative to ideal time evolution is governed by the variance of the sampled generators. Minimizing the variance-based upper bound on the worst-case error recovers qDRIFT, a leading randomized Hamiltonian simulation algorithm that samples terms in proportion to their operator norms. For typical input states, however, this sampling distribution need not be optimal. Minimizing Haar-averaged error bounds instead yields sampling probabilities proportional to the Hilbert-Schmidt norms of the terms. This choice can provide smaller average-error bounds than conventional qDRIFT. For Hamiltonians expressed as sums of Pauli strings, we combine this framework with commuting Pauli grouping to obtain smaller error bounds than conventional qDRIFT at fixed $R_z$ depth. Numerical benchmarks for the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model show error-bound improvement factors consistent with linear scaling in the number of qubits. For the 108-qubit FeMoco Hamiltonian, the error bounds are reduced by a factor of about 5.65. These results establish average-error optimization as a practical design principle for randomized Hamiltonian simulation.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum Fidelity Landscape-Guided Prior Calibration for Single-Circuit QGAN Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36702)
*Xue Yang,Rigui Zhou,Dax Enshan Koh,Siong Thye Goh,Yitao Tang,ShiZheng Jia,Young-Wook Cho,Hongyu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出单量子线路的端到端像素级QGAN，通过引入量子保真度景观（QFL）理论视角解释其训练行为，并据此设计BasicQGAN框架，在对抗训练前校准先验QFL，实现稳定生成且所需量子比特和可训练参数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QGAN多依赖分块（patch-based）分解策略，削弱生成图像的全局一致性并增加量子资源开销，因此需要探索更简单的像素级端到端生成方法，并从理论上理解朴素端到端QGAN的训练行为。

Method: 提出量子保真度景观（QFL），定义为量子态集合诱导且在量子生成过程的共享酉变换下保持不变的成对保真度结构；在固定Lipschitz读出下，该不变量对解码样本分离度施加单侧界，从而驱动在对抗训练前校准先验诱导的QFL。据此提出BasicQGAN，在对抗优化前将先验诱导的QFL与数据诱导的QFL对齐。

Result: 在小规模灰度图像数据集上，BasicQGAN实现了稳定有效的端到端像素级图像生成，且所需量子比特和可训练参数少于代表性的分块量子生成器；不同初始量子态集合的实验表明，经QFL校准的集合具有更好的生成性能。

Conclusion: QFL为理解朴素端到端QGAN的训练行为提供了新理论视角，先验诱导QFL的校准对稳定端到端像素级量子图像生成至关重要，BasicQGAN以更低资源开销验证了该理论洞见的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum Generative Adversarial Networks (QGANs) have emerged as representative generative models in the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era and have attracted increasing attention in quantum machine learning. However, most existing QGAN methods rely on patch-based decomposition strategies, which weaken the global consistency of generated images and increase quantum resource overhead. In this work, we investigate a simpler approach: pixel-level, end-to-end image generation using a single-quantum-circuit QGAN. By analyzing the structural matching relationship between the quantum prior and the target data distribution in Hilbert space, we provide a new theoretical perspective for understanding the training behavior of naive end-to-end QGANs. Specifically, we introduce the Quantum Fidelity Landscape (QFL), defined as the pairwise-fidelity structure induced by an ensemble of quantum states and preserved under shared unitary transformations of the quantum generation process. We show that, under a fixed Lipschitz readout, this invariant imposes a one-sided bound on decoded sample separation, motivating calibration of the prior-induced QFL before adversarial training. To validate this theoretical insight, we propose BasicQGAN, a QGAN framework incorporating quantum prior calibration. Before adversarial optimization, BasicQGAN aligns the prior-induced QFL with the data-induced QFL. Experimental results on small-scale grayscale image datasets show that BasicQGAN achieves stable and effective end-to-end pixel-level image generation while requiring fewer qubits and trainable parameters than representative patch-based quantum generators. Furthermore, experiments with different initial quantum-state ensembles show that QFL-calibrated ensembles achieve better generative performance.

</details>


### [151] [A Divide-and-Conquer Quantum-Selected Configuration Interaction for Evaluating $π$-$π$ Stacking Interaction Energies in the Benzene Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36708)
*Ryotaro Tajima,Rei Sato,Yosuke Iyama,Ryo Kiguchi,Yoshitake Kitanishi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Deep QSCI 方法，将 Deep VQE 的分而治之思想与 QSCI 结合，用 20 量子比特单体计算高效构建苯二聚体相互作用势曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述 π-π 堆叠需刻画小能标电子相关，直接变分量子计算苯二聚体 CAS(28e,20o) 需 40 量子比特且线路深，资源开销大。

Method: 对单个单体做 QSCI，并施加粒子数守恒的单电子激发构建约化局域基；结合单体间相互作用哈密顿量构建二聚体有效哈密顿量并对角化。因两单体结构相同，QSCI 结果可复用，量子寄存器由 40 降至 20 比特。

Result: 6-31G** 下给出 4.0 Å 处 -0.91 kcal/mol 的吸引极小值，接近 counterpoise 校正的 CCSD(T) 值 -1.14 kcal/mol；cc-pVDZ 下产物态相互作用能与对角化能降出现大幅抵消。

Conclusion: Deep QSCI 为构建模型相互作用曲线提供资源高效途径，无需在每个间距重复单体量子计算；但轨道一致性、约化空间跨基组收敛性与电荷转移仍是定量精度的关键问题。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation of $π$--$π$ stacking interactions requires a description of electron correlation on a small energy scale. Direct variational quantum calculations of the benzene dimer with CAS(28e,20o) require 40 qubits and deep circuits. Here, we propose Divide-and-Conquer Quantum-Selected Configuration Interaction (Deep QSCI), combining the divide-and-conquer idea of Deep VQE with QSCI, and apply it to the sandwich benzene dimer. We perform QSCI for one monomer and construct a reduced local basis by applying particle-number-conserving one-electron excitations to its ground state. Using this basis and the intermonomer interaction Hamiltonian, we build and diagonalize an effective Hamiltonian of the dimer. Since the monomers have the same structure, the QSCI result is reused for both monomers and across intermolecular distances, reducing the required quantum register from 40 to 20 qubits for the monomer CAS(14e,10o). With 6-31G**, the model gives an attractive minimum of -0.91 kcal/mol at 4.0 $Å$, compared with the counterpoise-corrected CCSD(T) value of -1.14 kcal/mol. With cc-pVDZ, a large cancellation appears between the product-state interaction energy and the energy lowering by diagonalization. This may reflect insufficient consistency of the active orbitals and reduced local bases between monomers. Orbital consistency, reduced-space convergence across basis sets, and charge transfer remain key issues for quantitative accuracy. Deep QSCI thus provides a resource-efficient approach to constructing model interaction curves without repeating monomer quantum calculations at each separation.

</details>


### [152] [Block-Wise Variational Quantum Algorithms for PDEs with Interface Penalty Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36710)
*Hangran Jie,Yuntao Cui,Sunho Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出分块式变分量子算法（block-wise VQA）框架求解空间异质复杂度的偏微分方程，通过域分解、动态资源配置、统一跳跃惩罚与自适应重分块，降低近似误差与峰值电路宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局VQA框架在均匀网格上依赖单一高表达能力拟设，当解存在局部奇异或薄边界层等空间异质特征时结构效率低下：局部非光滑特征会拖累整体量子表示的收敛，导致电路深度冗余并加剧贫瘠高原下的梯度消失风险。

Method: 将计算域分解为局部量子子问题，依据可计算的难度指标动态分配网格预算与拟设族；人工块界面通过状态值、导数与物理通量的统一跳跃惩罚耦合；采用自适应重分块追踪移动的粗糙区域以维持局部精度并抑制全局量子比特开销；误差分析严格分离空间离散、拟设表达能力、优化收敛、有限采样噪声、重分块传递误差与界面耦合贡献。

Result: 在代表性椭圆、对流-扩散与Burgers问题上的可复现残差驱动仿真表明，相较全局VQA方法，所提框架具有更低的近似误差与更小的峰值局部电路宽度。

Conclusion: 结果验证了分块式量子资源局部化的有效性，表明无需单一全局高表达拟设即可在近期量子设备上获得高保真PDE解。

Abstract: Global variational quantum algorithm (VQA) frameworks for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) often rely on a single expressive ansatz over a uniform grid, which becomes structurally inefficient when solutions exhibit spatially heterogeneous complexity such as localized singularities or thin boundary layers. A localized nonsmooth feature can degrade the convergence of the entire global quantum representation, imposing unnecessary circuit depth and amplifying the risk of vanishing gradients in barren plateaus.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a block-wise VQA framework for PDEs characterized by spatially heterogeneous solution complexity. The computational domain is decomposed into locally represented quantum subproblems, where grid budgets and ansatz families are dynamically assigned based on a computable difficulty indicator. Artificial block interfaces are coupled through unified jump penalties for state values, derivatives, and physical fluxes, while adaptive reblocking tracks moving rough regions to maintain local accuracy without excessive global qubit overhead. The error analysis rigorously separates spatial discretization, ansatz expressivity, optimization convergence, finite-shot sampling noise, transfer errors from reblocking, and interface-coupling contributions. Reproducible residual-based simulations on representative elliptic, advection--diffusion, and Burgers problems demonstrate lower approximation errors and reduced peak local circuit width compared to global VQA approaches. These results substantiate block-wise quantum resource localization, confirming that high-fidelity PDE solutions can be achieved on near-term quantum devices without requiring a single globally expressive ansatz.

</details>


### [153] [Ordering controlled by the measurement-feedback interval in quantum Boltzmann samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36836)
*Peng Wang,Yu-Xuan Zhang,Hai-Tao Ding,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 测量-反馈间隔本身即可在固定耦合与固定局域耗散规则下的量子玻尔兹曼采样器中诱导有序。零环境退相位时，重复投影测量移除弛豫过程中再生的横向相干，增强局域纵向响应，从而在低于平衡Curie-Weiss阈值时通过缩短读出间隔驱动连续相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子采样/量子退火中测量过程本身（而非仅耦合与耗散）对稳态有序的贡献，说明即使程序化玻尔兹曼分布本身无序，仅靠缩短测量-反馈间隔也能诱导有序，并揭示其与非平衡相互作用的对应关系。

Method: 在Curie-Weiss极限下解析推导相邻测量记录间的精确有限间隔马尔可夫核；在零退相位条件下，用重复投影测量刻画横向相干被移除的效应，并结合精确有限尺寸稳态计算分析双峰磁化分布与平均场临界标度。

Result: 得到有序阈值及连续相变判据：在耦合低于平衡Curie-Weiss阈值时，缩短读出间隔可使系统连续转变为有序稳态。稳态响应与状态方程精确映射为带退相位的持续相互作用模型，但在匹配静态响应下线性弛豫速率仍有差异；有限尺寸计算呈现双峰磁化分布与平均场临界标度。

Conclusion: 测量-反馈间隔是一个可独立调控有序的物理参数，能在固定耦合与固定局域耗散下产生稳态相变；该机制提供了将测量诱导有序映射为有效相互作用模型的框架，并指出静态响应与动力学弛豫速率的可区分性。

Abstract: We show that the measurement--feedback interval alone can induce ordering in a quantum Boltzmann sampler with fixed programmed couplings and a fixed local dissipative rule. At zero environmental dephasing, repeated projective measurements remove transverse coherence regenerated during relaxation, thereby enhancing the local longitudinal response. We derive the exact finite-interval Markov kernel for successive measurement records and obtain the ordering threshold in the Curie--Weiss limit. For couplings below the equilibrium Curie--Weiss threshold, shortening the readout interval drives a continuous transition to an ordered stationary state even though the programmed Boltzmann distribution remains disordered. The stationary response and equation of state map exactly onto those of a continuously interacting model with dephasing, while the linear relaxation rates remain distinct at matched static response. Exact finite-size stationary calculations reveal the transition through bimodal magnetization distributions and mean-field critical scaling.

</details>


### [154] [Exact Trotterization in the Degenerate Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36861)
*I. Lizuain,A. Rodriguez-Prieto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在可精确求解的退化极限下，为量子Rabi模型的Trotter化误差给出精确刻画：一阶Trotter算符有闭式表达，其相空间轨迹为对精确轨迹做缩放与刚性旋转后的圆，由此得到态保真度的闭式，并证明该几何误差可通过将Trotter步耦合复数化而精确校正且不增加电路成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强耦合区量子Rabi模型的数字量子模拟需要精确刻画Trotter化误差，但既有结果仅为定性界，缺乏可作基准的精确描述。

Method: 在可精确求解的退化极限下解析推导一阶Trotter算符的闭式表达，分析其生成的相空间轨迹几何（相对精确轨迹的缩放与刚性旋转），并用位移Fock态可区分性统一描述保真度；进一步引入复数Trotter步耦合进行误差校正。

Result: 一阶Trotter轨迹恰为经过缩放和刚性旋转的圆，而非近似；态保真度有闭式，其结构与深强耦合区决定revival的物理存活概率同构，二者同为位移Fock态可区分性的几何问题；该几何误差可被复数化耦合精确消除，且无额外电路开销。

Conclusion: 该结果在可精确求解的退化极限下为一般Rabi模型的Trotter化误差提供了精确基准，并给出零成本的误差校正方案。

Abstract: Digital quantum simulation of the quantum Rabi model in the deep strong coupling regime motivates the need for an exact characterization of Trotterization errors, so far known only through qualitative bounds. Here we derive such a characterization in the exactly solvable degenerate limit, valid for arbitrary coupling strength. The first-order Trotter operator admits a closed expression, showing that the generated phase-space trajectory lies exactly on a circle that is rescaled and rigidly rotated with respect to the exact one, rather than merely approximating it. This geometric renormalization yields a closed form for the state fidelity, structurally identical to the physical survival probability governing revivals in the deep strong coupling regime: both are the same geometric question -- the distinguishability of a displaced Fock state -- applied to two different pairs of points in phase space. We further show that this geometric error is exactly correctable by promoting the Trotter-step coupling to a complex amplitude, at no additional circuit cost. These results, obtained in the exactly solvable degenerate limit, provide a benchmark for Trotterization errors in the general Rabi model.

</details>


### [155] [Closing a single-copy gap to the Holevo bound with two-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36874)
*Changhao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定了三参数以上建模中"有限拷贝间隙普遍持续"的猜想：构造出一个三参数秩二模型，其Holevo界单拷贝不可达但两拷贝恰好可达；同时证明任意参数数的满秩模型仍存在有限拷贝持续间隙，并在超导量子比特上实验实现了两拷贝集体测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Holevo Cramér-Rao界给出量子多参数估计的渐近可达精度，但其在有限量子态拷贝数下的可达性在一般情况下尚未解决。两参数模型已显示任何有限拷贝数下单拷贝最优值与Holevo界之间存在间隙，而更多参数的情形此前仍属空白，因此需要判定这种有限拷贝间隙是否在所有参数数下普遍持续。

Method: 1. 通过构造性反例，建立一个三参数秩二模型，分析其单拷贝与两拷贝的集体测量可达性；2. 对任意参数数的满秩模型进行理论证明，讨论有限拷贝间隙的持续性；3. 在超导量子比特平台上实验实现该模型的两拷贝集体测量，并引入可控退极化噪声，对比单拷贝界与分离测量界（由态层析确定）。

Result: 所构造的三参数秩二模型其Holevo界单拷贝不可达，但两拷贝可精确达到，从而否定了三参数以上有限拷贝间隙普遍持续的猜想。同时证明满秩模型无论参数数目多少，有限拷贝间隙仍然持续存在。实验上观测到两拷贝估计误差低于单拷贝界与分离测量界，且该精度优势在有限噪声范围内保持。

Conclusion: 最终量子精度的可达性取决于多参数估计模型的结构（如秩与参数数）以及可用于集体测量的资源，纠正了此前关于有限拷贝间隙持续性的普适性认识，并给出可由当前量子硬件验证的实例。

Abstract: The Holevo Cramér-Rao bound sets the asymptotically attainable precision of quantum multiparameter estimation, but its attainability with finite quantum state copies remains unresolved in general. While a gap between the single-copy optimum and the Holevo bound persists at any finite copy number for two-parameter estimation models, the higher-parameter case has remained open. Here we disprove universal finite-copy gap persistence beyond two parameters by constructing a three-parameter rank-two model whose Holevo bound is unattainable with one copy but attained exactly with two. We further prove finite-copy persistence for full-rank models with any number of parameters. We then experimentally implement the two-copy collective measurement for the constructed model using superconducting qubits. We observe a two-copy estimation error below the single-copy and separate-measurement bounds determined from state tomography. By introducing controlled depolarization, we show that this precision advantage persists over a finite noise range. Our results connect the attainability of ultimate quantum precision to the structure of the multiparameter estimation model and the resources available for collective measurements.

</details>


### [156] [Digital Twin Modeling of Quantum Dynamical Systems: Dissipative Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36901)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Shital Chauhan,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出耗散量子储备池计算（DQRC）框架，用固定的小型开放量子系统作为储备池、仅训练经典读出层，从输入—输出数据中学习强驱动多体量子系统的非线性、记忆依赖动力学，并成功构建高次谐波产生（HHG）的数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动多体量子系统的响应建模困难：动力学非线性、依赖历史、且随系统规模增大模拟成本高昂。HHG是典型例子，其辐射对驱动高度敏感并编码长程时间相关性，传统模拟与既有机器学习模型（如时间卷积网络、KAN）在紧凑性与物理可实现性上仍有不足。

Method: 提出耗散量子储备池计算（DQRC）：以小型开放量子系统作为储备池，储备池保持固定不训练，仅训练经典读出层，直接从数据学习输入—输出映射，构建目标系统的数字孪生。以最小单量子比特储备池复现远大于其规模的Ising自旋链的HHG响应。

Result: 最小单量子比特储备池可复现更大Ising自旋链的HHG响应；在代表性基准上达到甚至部分指标超越此前报道的时间卷积模型与Kolmogorov–Arnold网络模型，且所用系统更简单、物理可实现。单一固定储备池还能泛化到广泛驱动范围，表明其学到的是共享的物理响应结构而非记忆轨迹。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 耗散量子储备池可作为紧凑、物理根基扎实的数字孪生，用于建模非线性、记忆依赖的量子动力学，为强驱动多体量子系统的高效数据驱动建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: Modeling the response of driven many-body quantum systems from input--output data is difficult: the dynamics are nonlinear, history dependent, and expensive to simulate as system size grows. A paradigmatic case is High-Harmonic Generation~(HHG), where a strong field drives a medium to emit radiation that is highly sensitive to the drive and encodes long-range temporal correlations. We introduce a dissipative quantum reservoir computing~(DQRC) framework that builds a digital twin of such a system, learning its input--output map directly from data while the reservoir---itself a small open quantum system---stays fixed and only a classical readout is trained. We show that a minimal single-qubit reservoir reproduces the HHG response of a substantially larger Ising spin chain, and on a representative benchmark matches and on several metrics surpasses previously reported temporal convolutional and Kolmogorov--Arnold-network models, while using a simpler, physically realizable system. A single fixed reservoir further generalizes across a broad range of drives, indicating that it learns a shared physical response structure rather than memorizing trajectories. These results establish dissipative quantum reservoirs as compact, physically grounded digital twins for nonlinear, memory-dependent quantum dynamics.
  Code is available at \href{https://github.com/AI-and-Quantum-Computing/DQuRC}{https://github.com/AI-and-Quantum-Computing/DQuRC}.

</details>


### [157] [Device-independent quantification of steerability in tripartite scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36907)
*Xin-Hong Wang,Miao-Tzu Lin,Shin-Liang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将组装矩矩阵（AMM）框架从两方扩展至三方量子导引，覆盖2-steer-1与1-steer-2两种方案，构建半定规划从观测关联或Bell不等式违背中获得导引鲁棒性下界，并设备无关地证明测量不可兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子导引的设备无关（DI）量化此前仅在两方情形下通过组装矩矩阵（AMM）框架得到发展，三方及以上情形基本未被探索，因此需要将AMM框架推广至三方导引场景。

Method: 将AMM框架扩展至三方导引，分别处理两方联合导引第三方的2-steer-1方案与单方导引其余两方的1-steer-2方案；对每种方案构造半定规划，输入为观测到的关联数据或仅Bell不等式的违背情况，输出为底层组装导引鲁棒性的下界。

Result: 所构造的半定规划能够在两种三方导引方案下给出组装导引鲁棒性的下界；同时作为副产物，这些下界还能以设备无关方式证明导引方所执行测量的不可兼容性。

Conclusion: AMM框架可成功推广到三方导引，为多体量子导引提供设备无关的鲁棒性量化工具，并附带实现测量不可兼容性的DI认证。

Abstract: Quantum steering captures the ability of one party, through local measurements on a shared entangled state, to affect the conditional states held by distant parties in a way that admits no local explanation. Its device-independent (DI) quantification -- requiring no characterization of any state or measurement -- has been developed in the bipartite setting, notably through the framework of assemblage moment matrices (AMMs) [Phys. Rev. Lett. 116, 240401 (2016)], but has remained largely unexplored beyond it. Here, we extend the AMM framework to tripartite steering, covering both the scenario in which two parties jointly steer a third one (2-steer-1) and that in which a single party steers the remaining two (1-steer-2). For each scheme, we construct a semidefinite program that, from the observed correlations -- or merely from the violation of a Bell inequality -- yields lower bounds on the steering robustness of the underlying assemblage. As a by-product, our bounds also certify, in a DI manner, the incompatibility of the measurements performed by the steering parties.

</details>


### [158] [Exact quantum algorithm for searching of multiple sets intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36908)
*Sheng-Ao Mao,Lin Zhang,Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Long的相位匹配方法，提出了一种精确搜索多个集合交集的Grover型量子算法，推导并证明了精确搜索该交集的相位匹配条件，给出了相应的量子线路实现，并通过数值模拟验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法在无结构搜索中具有二次加速优势，但其成功概率通常低于100%；Long通过引入相位匹配条件提出了零理论失败率的改进版本。然而在实际应用中，目标集常由多个约束集合的交集确定，而现有基于Grover的方法大多仅适用于两个集合的交集，且搜索并不总能成功。因此，亟需一种能够精确搜索多个集合交集的算法。

Method: 在Long方法的基础上，提出了一种用于精确搜索多集合交集的算法；推导并证明了实现精确交集搜索所需的相位匹配条件；设计并给出了实现该精确交集搜索的量子线路。

Result: 推导并证明了针对多集合交集精确搜索的相位匹配条件，给出了可实现的量子线路，并通过数值模拟对理论结果进行了验证支持。

Conclusion: 该工作将Long的精确搜索方法从单目标推广到多集合交集场景，弥补了现有Grover型方法仅适用于两集合且成功率不足的局限，为多约束条件下的精确量子搜索提供了理论与线路层面的可行方案。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm achieves a quadratic speedup for unstructured search when a global oracle for the target set is available. However, its success probability is generally below $100\%$. To overcome this issue, Long presented a modified version of Grover's search algorithm by introducing a phase-matching condition, which can search for the target set with zero theoretical failure rate. Nevertheless, in many applications, the target set is determined by the intersection of multiple constrained sets. Most existing Grover-based methods are limited to the intersection of only two sets and are not always successful in searching for the target set. In this work, based on Long's method, we propose an algorithm for exactly searching the intersection of multiple sets. We derive and prove a phase-matching condition for searching the intersection exactly. Furthermore, we present a quantum circuit that implements exact intersection search. Finally, we support our theoretical results with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [159] [Universal Inductive Inference of Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36912)
*Taiga Hiroka,Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Yuki Shirakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"通用量子归纳推理"框架，将Solomonoff归纳推理推广到可含任意时间关联与纠缠的量子源，给出信息论学习算法及匹配下界，并构造非i.i.d.量子态层析算法，最后探讨其计算困难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solomonoff通用归纳推理为经典可计算随机源提供了最一般的预测保证，但尚缺乏量子系统对应的通用归纳理论。作者希望建立可处理任意时间关联与纠缠的量子源学习与预测框架，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将量子源建模为多体量子态，其描述由未知的s比特程序在给定时间界内生成。学习者在已知过去测量结果及测量后量子系统的条件下，输出一个联合态以预测下一次测量结果与测量后系统，同时保持与过去系统的关联。要求输出态以至少1-δ的概率在迹距离下ε接近目标态。作者给出信息论推理算法，并证明匹配下界；此外构造非i.i.d.态层析算法输出给定过去测量结果下下一系统条件态的经典描述；并基于量子密码假设研究计算困难性。

Result: 建立了具有轮复杂度O(sε^{-2}δ^{-1})的信息论推理算法，并在相关参数区间证明匹配下界（即使对经典源也成立）。非i.i.d.态层析算法的轮复杂度为O(s·min{2^s,2^n}·ε^{-2}δ^{-1})，其中n为每轮接收的量子比特数。进一步给出了通用量子归纳推理在量子密码假设下的计算困难性分析。

Conclusion: 该工作将通用归纳推理从经典领域推广至量子领域，给出了可证明的样本/轮复杂度保证与下界，并揭示了量子态层析与通用量子归纳推理的信息论可行性边界及计算困难性。

Abstract: Solomonoff's universal inductive inference [Inf. Control. 1964] is one of the most general frameworks for learning from sequential data and predicting future observations. It provides prediction guarantees for any computable stochastic source. We ask whether an analogous universal theory of induction can be developed for quantum systems. To this end, we introduce universal quantum inductive inference, a framework for learning and predicting quantum sources that may exhibit arbitrary correlations and entanglement across time. Given the outcomes of past measurements and the corresponding post-measurement quantum systems, the learner produces a joint state that predicts the next measurement outcome and post-measurement system while preserving correlations with the past systems. We model a quantum source as a multipartite quantum state whose description is generated by an unknown $s$-bit program within a given time bound. The learner is required to produce a state that is $ε$-close in trace distance to the target with probability at least $1-δ$. We establish an information-theoretic inference algorithm with round complexity $O(sε^{-2}δ^{-1})$. We further prove a matching lower bound in the relevant parameter regime, even for classical sources. As a second result, we construct a non-i.i.d. state tomography algorithm that outputs a classical description of the conditional state of the next system given past measurement outcomes. Our information-theoretic tomography algorithm achieves round complexity $O(s\min\{2^s,2^n\}ε^{-2}δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of qubits received in each round. Finally, we investigate the computational hardness of universal quantum inductive inference under quantum cryptographic assumptions.

</details>


### [160] [Towards Scalable Quantum Unit Commitment in Power Systems: Encoding and Complexity Reduction Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36999)
*Marc Carrillo-MuÑoz,Sergio MartÍnez-Hermida,Sara Barja-MartÍnez,Antonio E. SaldaÑa GonzÁlez,MÒnica AragÜÉs-PeÑalba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子机组组合（UC）优化框架，结合Pauli相关编码（PCE）进行量子比特约简预处理，并利用基于采样的量子对角化（SQD）作为混合后处理精化方法。UC问题被二值化并转化为QUBO，再映射为Ising表示，在Qibo框架内进行绝热量子演化。研究表明PCE降低有效问题规模，SQD提升可行解与最优解的概率集中度，二者结合在多个可扩展算例中表现最佳，以更低的等效计算成本获得更可靠的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着发电机数量增加，UC问题的量子求解面临量子比特可扩展性、计算开销与解可行性等挑战，需要有效的降维与后处理手段来提升量子优化的实用性与可靠性。

Method: 将UC问题二值化建模并转化为QUBO，再映射为适用于绝热量子演化的Ising表示，在Qibo框架中实现；采用Pauli相关编码（PCE）作为量子比特约简预处理，采用基于采样的量子对角化（SQD）作为混合后处理精化方法，并分析二者对量子比特规模、计算量、可行性与最优解恢复概率的影响。

Result: PCE减少了编码问题的有效规模；SQD提高了概率在可行解与最优解上的集中度；二者联合应用在多个可扩展算例中综合表现最佳，相较未约简的建模方式，以更低的等效计算成本获得更可靠的解。

Conclusion: PCE与SQD的组合为量子UC求解提供了一种兼顾可扩展性与可靠性的有效途径，代码已开源（论文发表前链接暂不可用，可按审稿需求在限定时段开放）。

Abstract: This paper develops a Quantum optimisation Unit Commitment (UC) framework that benefits from Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE) as a qubit reduction preprocessing technique and Sample-based Quantum Diagonalisation (SQD) as a hybrid post-processing refinement method. The UC problem is formulated in binary form, then transformed into a QUBO problem and mapped onto an Ising representation suitable for adiabatic quantum evolution within the Qibo framework. This study analyses how PCE and SQD techniques affect qubit scalability, computational effort, feasibility, and probability of recovering the optimal solution as the number of generators increases. The results show that PCE reduces the effective size of the encoded problem, SQD improves the concentration of probability on feasible and optimal solutions, and their combined application provides the best overall behaviour in several scalable case studies, achieving more reliable solutions with lower effective computational cost than the unreduced formulation. The repository is available open source in Github (link is not yet available until work is published. However, if requested by the reviewers, we can make it open for a scific time window, if requested).

</details>


### [161] [Quantum Query Lower Bounds for Triangle-Listing and Spanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37091)
*Yu Chen,Ananta Mukherjee,Mingyang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在一般图量子查询模型下，首次给出三角形列举的量子查询下界，以及显式乘性spanner构造的量子查询下界，均通过归约到中间的多块搜索问题得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角形列举此前缺乏非平凡量子下界（Jiang和Peng提出的问题），而乘性spanner构造的量子查询复杂度也缺少紧致刻画。作者希望借助量子查询记录技术，为这两个关系型图问题建立可靠的量子查询下界。

Method: 扩展Zhandry（CRYPTO 2019）与Hamoudi-Magniez（ToCT 2023）的量子查询记录框架，处理双向oracle的记录架构，提出通用分块可靠性框架，对局部接受投影算子族给出其与有界权重记录子空间最大重叠的精确算子范数刻画。再通过中间多块搜索问题做归约。

Result: 构造出一族含Θ(n)个三角形的n顶点图，列举任意常数比例三角形需Ω(n^{3/2-o(1)})量子查询，这是首个非平凡三角形列举量子下界；对每个固定k≥7，构造乘性k-spanner需Ω(n^{1+1/(2μ_k)})量子查询，其中μ_k=k/3+O(1)；k∈{7,8}时为Ω(n^{5/4})，与大k时指数1+3/(2k)均超过Lazebnik-Ustimenko-Woldar高围长稠密图所暗示的1+4/(3k)。

Conclusion: 本文建立了三角形列举与显式乘性spanner构造的量子查询下界，并提供了可复用的双向oracle记录与分块可靠性分析工具，回答了三角形列举量子下界这一开放问题。

Abstract: This paper gives quantum query lower bounds for two relational graph problems, triangle listing and explicit multiplicative spanner construction, in the general graph query model, where quantum adjacency, degree and neighborhood queries are all available in arbitrary superposition. Both results are obtained by reductions via intermediate multi-block search problems. We extend the quantum query recording framework by Zhandry (CRYPTO 2019) and Hamoudi and Magniez (ToCT 2023) to handle a recording architecture for bidirectional oracles and give a generic blockwise soundness framework, which for arbitrary families of local accepting projectors, gives an exact operator-norm characterization of their maximum overlap with the subspace of bounded weight records. Using the intermediate search problems, we exhibit a family of $n$-vertex graphs with $Θ(n)$ triangles on which listing any constant fraction of the triangles requires $Ω(n^{3/2-o(1)})$ quantum queries. This is the first nontrivial quantum lower bound for triangle listing, a question raised by Jiang and Peng (ICML 2026). Further, we show that, for every fixed $k\ge 7$, constructing a multiplicative $k$-spanner requires $Ω(n^{1+\frac{1}{2μ_k}})$ quantum queries, where $μ_k=k/3+O(1)$. For $k\in\{7,8\}$ the bound is $Ω(n^{5/4})$, which matches what would be implied by an unproven instance of the Erdős girth conjecture, and for large $k$ the exponent $1+\frac{3}{2k}$ exceeds the $1+\frac{4}{3k}$ implied by the provable high girth dense graphs due to Lazebnik, Ustimenko and Woldar, 1995.

</details>


### [162] [A quantum double-bracket algorithm for imaginary-time evolution with exponentially shorter depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37095)
*Ioannis Kolotouros,Raul Garcia-Patron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出概率型双重括号量子虚时演化算法(PDBQITE),以单辅助比特和含受控实时演化与中电路测量的电路,实现电路深度指数级降低,代价是总成功概率随迭代次数指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子虚时演化(ITE)是制备目标哈密顿量基态的常用技术,但已有方案(如DB-QITE)电路深度较大,难以在近期量子设备上实现,因此需要降低电路深度。

Method: 在双重括号量子虚时演化(DB-QITE)框架基础上引入概率化变体PDBQITE;每次迭代仅需一个辅助量子比特,电路由一次受控实时演化和一次中电路测量构成;并推导了与系统规模无关的总成功概率解析下界。

Result: 推导出与系统规模无关的总成功概率下界;数值模拟显示单步成功概率接近1,可执行多次迭代;在分子哈密顿量基态制备中优于近期概率算法PITE;混合QAOA-PDBQITE方案在相同电路深度下于随机正则图的MaxCut问题上取得比QAOA更高的近似比。

Conclusion: PDBQITE用成功概率的指数衰减换取电路深度的指数降低,且单步成功概率接近1使其实际可行,在基态制备与组合优化问题上均表现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Imaginary-time evolution (ITE) is a widely used technique for preparing the ground state of a target Hamiltonian. Building on the recently proposed framework of Double-Bracket quantum ITE (DB-QITE), we introduce a probabilistic variant (PDBQITE) that achieves an exponential reduction in circuit depth. Our algorithm requires only a single ancilla qubit and a quantum circuit that consists of one controlled real-time evolution and one mid-circuit measurement per iteration. The trade-off is an exponential decay of the total success probability with the number of iterations, for which we derive an analytical lower bound that is independent of system size. Our numerical simulations show that the per-step success probability remains close to unity, making it realistic to execute many iterations, thus providing a substantial improvement over prior constructions. We show that PDBQITE outperforms the near-term probabilistic algorithm PITE for ground-state preparation of molecular Hamiltonians, and that a hybrid QAOA-PDBQITE scheme achieves higher approximation ratios than QAOA at the same circuit depth on random regular graphs for the MaxCut problem.

</details>


### [163] [Minimal building blocks for molecular quantum circuits with exact spin symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37101)
*Mengwei Liu,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定在固定粒子数N、总自旋S与自旋投影M_S的完整子空间内，生成所有实态空间旋转所需的额外操作：内部区域重复单重态对转移即可，极大自旋边界需额外生成元，最少空间轨道数内部为2、边界为3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保持粒子数与自旋的量子线路虽能锁定目标分子电子态，却无法保证可达该量子数下的所有态，因此需明确还需哪些操作才能在每个固定(N, S, M_S)子空间内生成全部实态空间旋转。

Method: 在实数轨道、无额外空间对称性限制的自旋无关分子计算框架下，考虑保持粒子数、完整自旋对称性与实振幅的连续可调操作；分析其与连接所有空间轨道的自旋无关轨道旋转共同生成目标子空间全部旋转的条件，并给出构造与基准测试。

Result: 在电子数与空穴数决定的最大自旋极限以下，任意两个固定空间轨道间重复的单重态对转移即足够；非平凡最大自旋边界上对转移消失，需额外生成元，当其在目标子空间的作用不是轨道旋转生成元的线性组合时恰好充分。一个额外生成元所需最少空间轨道数内部为2、非平凡边界为3，即便允许超过两电子的项亦然，且两个最小值均可用单、双电子项达到。三轨道最优操作按第三个轨道占据数旋转两个轨道，其酉算符精确分解为八个对易的Pauli旋转。分子基准显示该操作消除了观测到的边界能量误差平台，稀疏内部构造在部分固定轨道比较中以更少编译CNOT门达到指定能量精度。

Conclusion: 保持粒子数与自旋不足以保证固定(N, S, M_S)子空间内全部实态可达；需补充对转移或特定一/双电子生成元，其最小轨道需求由是否处于最大自旋边界决定，所得操作可显著改善边界能量误差并降低编译成本。

Abstract: Preserving particle number and spin helps quantum circuits target molecular electronic states, but does not guarantee access to every state with the required quantum numbers. We determine which additional operations, combined with spin-independent orbital rotations connecting all spatial orbitals, generate every real state-space rotation within each complete subspace of fixed particle number $N$, total spin $S$, and spin projection $M_S$. We consider spin-free molecular calculations in real orbitals, without an additional spatial-symmetry restriction, and continuously tunable operations that preserve particle number, full spin symmetry, and real amplitudes. Below the maximal-spin limits set by the electron and hole numbers, repeated singlet-pair transfer between any two fixed spatial orbitals is sufficient. On nontrivial maximal-spin boundaries, pair transfer vanishes, and an additional generator built from one- and two-electron terms is sufficient exactly when its action in the target subspace is not a linear combination of orbital-rotation generators. The minimum number of spatial orbitals needed by one additional generator is two in the interior and three on nontrivial boundaries, even when terms involving more than two electrons are allowed. Both minima are attained using only one- and two-electron terms. The three-orbital optimum rotates two orbitals according to the occupation of a third, and its unitary factors exactly into eight commuting Pauli rotations. Molecular benchmarks show that this operation removes the observed boundary energy-error plateaus, while sparse interior constructions attain the prescribed energy accuracy with fewer compiled CNOT gates in selected fixed-orbital comparisons.

</details>


### [164] [SQUARE: Structured Quantum Representation Adapters as Compact Quadratic Feature Maps for Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37134)
*Emily Jimin Roh,Hyojun Ahn,Hoyeong Lee,Soohyun Park,Sung Whan Yoon,Vaneet Aggarwal,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SQUARE是一种结构化量子表示适配器，将低维瓶颈向量振幅编码进参数化量子线路并测量，其基概率特征恰为瓶颈坐标的归一化二次型，可用少量共享参数刻画O(d²)个坐标对的交互，在受控交互任务上优于多个经典对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型常被用作固定特征提取器以支持下游的重排序、打分与偏好建模。问题在于：如何在固定的低维瓶颈中，用一个紧凑模块表示特征间的交互？常见的线性与低秩适配器在适配模块本身仍是线性的，而显式二阶方法则通过直接参数化或预定义分解引入成对交互，因此需要一种结构化且参数高效的二阶交互表示方案。

Method: 提出SQUARE（Structured QUAntum REpresentation）适配器：对瓶颈向量做振幅编码，施加参数化量子线路，并测量所得量子态。理论分析表明，每个基概率特征恰为瓶颈坐标的归一化二次型，额外的Pauli-Z读出是这些概率的带符号线性组合，因此该测量映射能以少量共享线路参数刻画O(d²)个坐标对的交互，属经典归一化二次特征类内的结构化参数化。所有线路实验均在模拟下完成，学得的特征映射可在PyTorch中批量精确计算，无需量子硬件。

Result: 在八个GLUE衍生受控交互任务、五个共享随机种子上进行同流程的分离式评测：SQUARE平均测试准确率0.7565，对比仿射归一化二次预测器0.7355、参数匹配的Givens混合模型0.7271、MLP 0.6817以及冻结线路对照0.6155。在监督减少时，相对最强经典对照仍有稳定增益，且在多个冻结语言模型骨干上呈现一致的定性趋势。

Conclusion: SQUARE通过量子线路的测量映射在固定低维瓶颈内实现结构化二阶交互参数化，参数高效且在受控交互任务与减少监督条件下稳定优于经典对照，同时完全可用经典模拟与批量PyTorch精确评估，无需量子硬件。

Abstract: Frozen language models (LMs) are increasingly used as fixed feature extractors for downstream reranking, scoring, and preference modeling, raising a practical question: how should a compact module represent interactions among features in a fixed low-dimensional bottleneck? Common linear and low-rank adapters remain linear at the adaptation module itself, whereas explicit second-order alternatives introduce pairwise interactions through direct parameterization or predefined factorizations. We propose SQUARE, a Structured QUAntum REpresentation adapter that amplitude-encodes the bottleneck vector, applies a parameterized quantum circuit, and measures the resulting state. We show that each basis-probability feature is exactly a normalized quadratic form in the bottleneck coordinates, while the additional Pauli-$Z$ readouts are signed linear combinations of these probabilities. The measured map can therefore parameterize interactions over $O(d^2)$ coordinate pairs through a small set of shared circuit parameters, where $d$ is the bottleneck dimension. It provides a structured parameterization within, rather than beyond, the classical normalized-quadratic feature class. In a disjoint same-pipeline evaluation over eight GLUE-derived controlled interaction tasks and five shared seeds, SQUARE achieves an average test accuracy of $0.7565$, compared with $0.7355$ for an affine normalized-quadratic predictor, $0.7271$ for the evaluated parameter-matched Givens mixing model, $0.6817$ for an MLP, and $0.6155$ for a frozen-circuit control. Under reduced supervision, it also shows consistent gains over the strongest evaluated classical comparator, with the same qualitative pattern across multiple frozen LM backbones. All circuit experiments use simulation, while the learned feature map can be evaluated exactly in batched PyTorch without quantum hardware.

</details>


### [165] [Arbitrary high-dimensional entanglement purification with the superposition of paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37136)
*Zi-Han Zheng,Wen-Qiang Liu,Chen-Ming Bai,Hai-Rui Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于路径叠加的可扩展高维纠缠纯化协议（HDEPP），可纯化qudit翻转与相位翻转错误，并给出基于超纠缠的光子频率自由度光学实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维量子纠缠在量子信息处理任务中相较二维纠缠具有显著优势，但实际传输中会受到qudit翻转和相位翻转等噪声影响，因此需要可扩展、高效的高维纠缠纯化方案来支撑高维量子通信与量子网络。

Method: 利用路径叠加实现任意高维纠缠纯化协议；通过选取合适的单qudit门分别纯化qudit翻转错误与相位翻转错误；并设计基于超纠缠的光学方案，对光子的频率自由度进行纯化。

Result: HDEPP在更高维系统中反而更鲁棒，且纯化保真度随维度增加而提高；所需操作资源更少，原理上可通过多轮迭代达到单位保真度；同时给出了可实验实现的光学纯化方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维量子通信与量子网络的发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-dimensional quantum entanglement offers significant advantages over its two-dimensional counterparts in various quantum information processing tasks. We propose a scalable method for implementing an arbitrary high-dimensional entanglement purification protocol (HDEPP) using path superposition. By selecting appropriate single-qudit gates, qudit-flip errors and phase-flip errors can be purified. Counterintuitively, the HDEPP becomes more robust for higher-dimensional systems and exhibits enhanced purification fidelity as the dimension increases. Our HDEPP not only requires fewer operational resources but also in principle can achieve unity fidelity through multiple rounds of iteration. Furthermore, we develop feasible optical schemes to purify the frequency degree of freedom of photons suffering from qudit-flip and phase-flip errors using hyperentanglement. This work paves the way toward high-dimensional quantum communication and quantum networks.

</details>


### [166] [Margenau-Hill distribution as a Necessary and Sufficient Signature of Measurement Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37144)
*Partha Patra,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在测量不相容性与Margenau-Hill(MH)准概率分布之间建立了操作性联系，证明二分测量下MH分布的正性充要等价于联合可测性，并提出了干涉测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量不相容性与准概率分布负性均为非经典性的标志，但二者的关系此前多停留在定性层面，缺乏明确的操作性联系。

Method: 首先导出任意有限维度d下任意一对二分可观测量的联合可测性(测量相容性)条件；随后证明对于维度d中任意一对非锐二分测量，MH分布的正性等价于联合可测性；进一步为n个二分非锐可观测量引入MH类准概率分布，并在可观测量互反交换时导出联合可测性的充分条件；最后提出受量子开关架构启发的干涉方案来直接重构MH准概率。

Result: 证得MH分布正性与联合可测性在二分非锐测量下互为充要条件；给出互反交换情形下联合可测性的充分条件；所提干涉方案可直接重构MH准概率，从而实现对不相容性的检验。

Conclusion: 该工作将测量不相容性与MH准概率分布的负性以充要条件形式定量关联，并提供了可实验实施的干涉测量方案，为检验测量不相容性提供了新的操作工具。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility and the negativity of quasi-probability distributions both demonstrate the signature of nonclassicality. However, their relations largely remained qualitative, and no explicit operational connection has been established. We provide an operational link between measurement incompatibility and the Margenau-Hill(MH) quasi-probability distribution associated with two dichotomic observables. { We first derive the joint measurability (measurement compatibility) condition for any pair of dichotomic observables in arbitrary finite dimension $d$.} We then rigorously prove that for any pair of unsharp dichotomic measurements in dimension $d$, the positivity of the MH distribution is equivalent to joint measurability \emph{i.e.}, the MH distribution is positive \emph{if and only if} the measurements are jointly measurable. We further introduce the MH-like quasi-probability distribution for $n$ dichotomic unsharp observables in arbitrary finite dimension and derive a sufficient condition of joint-measurability when the observables are mutually anticommuting. Finally, we propose an interferometric setup, inspired by quantum-switch architectures, that directly reconstructs MH quasi-probability which in turn provides a test of incompatibility.

</details>


### [167] [A quantum thermal machine surpassing the classical thermodynamic limit on precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37149)
*Simon Sundelin,Ludvig Nordqvist,Khalak Mahadeviya,Vyom Kulkarni,Mohammed Ali Aamir,Mark T. Mitchison,Simone Gasparinetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验在超导量子热机中观测到稳态量子输运突破经典热力学不确定性关系（TUR）：测得 Q=1.71±0.17，违反经典下界 Q≥2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑制涨落通常需要增加耗散，TUR 将电流涨落与熵产生定量关联。经典稳态 TUR 及其有限时间推广已成为非平衡热力学基石，但"经典 TUR 能否在可控量子器件中被违反"仍是未决的实验问题，难点在于需分辨极小的稳态电流及其涨落。

Method: 构建超导量子热机，耦合微波波导作为冷浴，并以经典噪声源提供等效无穷高温热浴；在稳态下测量量子输运电流及其涨落，提取 TUR 比值 Q。

Result: 观测到 TUR 比值 Q = 1.71 ± 0.17，低于经典界限 Q ≥ 2，即稳态量子输运精度超越经典 TUR 所允许的上限。

Conclusion: 结果展示了经典与量子热力学之间的根本区别，为在更低能量代价下实现更高精度的量子热器件开辟了道路。

Abstract: Precision in nonequilibrium processes comes at a thermodynamic cost: suppressing fluctuations generally requires increased dissipation. Thermodynamic uncertainty relations (TURs) make this trade-off quantitative by linking current fluctuations to entropy production in classical stochastic dynamics. In the decade since its discovery, the canonical steady-state TUR and its finite-time generalizations have become a cornerstone of non-equilibrium thermodynamics, constraining the performance of molecular machines and allowing heat dissipation to be inferred from observable fluctuations. Whether the canonical TUR can be violated in a controlled quantum device remains an outstanding experimental question, in part because doing so requires resolving extremely small steady-state currents as well as their fluctuations. Here we experimentally show that steady-state quantum transport can surpass the precision permitted by the canonical TUR. We observe this violation in a superconducting quantum thermal machine coupled to a microwave waveguide acting as a cold bath and to a classical noise source providing an effective infinite-temperature bath. We observe a TUR ratio $Q = 1.71 \pm 0.17$, in violation of the classical bound $Q \geq 2$. Our results demonstrate a fundamental distinction between classical and quantum thermodynamics, paving the way for quantum thermal devices that achieve enhanced precision at reduced energy cost.

</details>


### [168] [Non-Canonical Quantum Otto Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37194)
*Wei Wu,Jun-Hong An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究非正则热化效应对有限时间量子奥托热机效率与功率统计特性的影响，发现通过调控工作物质与热库复合系统的能谱结构，可在量子临界点附近同时提升平均效率与功率并降低二者方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间量子热机因热涨落与量子涨落而具有随机性，其效率与功率存在权衡关系，难以同时实现高功率与高效率。常规研究假设正则热化，仅在弱耦合条件下成立，因此需要考察非正则热化（强耦合）对热机统计特性的影响。

Method: 针对有限时间量子奥托循环，引入非正则热化效应，通过量子储库工程调控由工作物质与热库组成的复合系统的能谱结构，分析效率与功率的平均值及方差的响应。

Result: 能谱结构可高度调控效率与功率的均值与方差；在由能谱中束缚态形成所诱导的量子临界点附近，平均效率与平均功率可同时被提升，且二者的方差均下降。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对量子储库工程在热力学中作用的理解，为实现高效且稳定的量子能源器件奠定了基础。

Abstract: The fast-developing quantum technology is propelling thermal machine, which lies at the heart of thermodynamics, into a new golden era. Being stochastic because of the non-negligible thermal and quantum fluctuations, the efficiency and power of a finite-time quantum heat engine exhibit a trade-off, preventing the simultaneous achievement of high power and high efficiency. Conventional studies treat the statistics of a heat engine by assuming canonical thermalization, which is valid only under weak-coupling conditions. We here investigate the noncanonical effect on the statistics of the efficiency and power for a finite-time quantum Otto cycle. It is revealed that their average values and variances are highly controllable by engineering the energy spectrum structure of the composite system consisting of the working substance and the bath. Near the quantum critical point induced by the formation of a bound state in the energy spectrum, the average efficiency and power can be boosted simultaneously, meanwhile, both of their variances are decreased. Deepening our understanding of the usefulness of quantum reservoir engineering in thermodynamics, our result lays the foundation for the realization of efficient and stable quantum energy devices.

</details>


### [169] [Encoding universal quantum computation into quantized Berry phases: Hardness results and classical algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37199)
*Kazuki Sakamoto,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画了量子化Berry相估计的计算复杂度，证明在反多项式谱隙下判定Berry相为0或π是BQP完全的，并在2D格点Heisenberg/XY模型上拓展该硬度，同时给出常谱隙下几何局域哈密顿量的经典多项式算法，指出谱隙而非精度是经典可解性的关键资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已确立Berry相估计在反多项式精度下具有超多项式量子优势，但对称性量子化、可在常数精度下区分的Berry相是否仍保持该硬度尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，刻画量子化Berry相估计的计算复杂度。

Method: 首先构造一种编码，将任意量子计算的输出映射为恰好为0或π的Berry相，从而证明在反多项式谱隙下判定Berry相为0或π是BQP完全的；随后将该硬度推广到2D方格子上的物理哈密顿量（含Heisenberg与XY相互作用）；最后针对固定维度格点上常谱隙的几何局域哈密顿量，设计经典多项式时间算法。

Result: 证明判定Berry相为0或π在反多项式谱隙下为BQP完全（蕴含BPP≠BQP时仍具超多项式量子优势）；该硬度可扩展至2D方格子的Heisenberg与XY模型；对固定维度格点上常谱隙的几何局域哈密顿量给出经典多项式时间算法，表明谱隙是经典可处理性的关键资源。

Conclusion: 本文确立了在对称性与谱隙保持形变下不变的量子化拓扑不变量计算具有超多项式量子优势，为量子物理中实现实用量子优势提供了新步骤。

Abstract: The Berry phase is a fundamental geometric quantity for characterizing the geometry and topology of quantum many-body systems. Previous work established a super-polynomial quantum advantage in Berry phase estimation at inverse-polynomial precision, given an ansatz state approximating the initial ground state. However, whether this hardness persists for Berry phases quantized by symmetry, which can be distinguished at constant precision, remained open. In this work, we characterize the computational complexity of quantized Berry phase estimation. First, we prove that deciding whether the Berry phase is exactly $0$ or $π$ is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete when the spectral gap is inverse-polynomially small. This result implies that distinguishing the quantized Berry phase still has a super-polynomial quantum advantage, assuming $\mathsf{BPP}\neq \mathsf{BQP}$. To prove this result, we introduce an encoding that maps the output of any quantum computation to a Berry phase that is exactly $0$ or $π$. Second, we extend this hardness to physically motivated Hamiltonians on a 2D square lattice, including Heisenberg and XY interactions. Third, we provide a classical polynomial-time algorithm for geometrically local Hamiltonians on fixed-dimensional lattices with constant spectral gaps. Therefore we identify that the spectral gap is a key resource for the classical tractability, rather than precision. These results establish a super-polynomial quantum advantage for computing a quantized topological invariant that is unchanged under symmetry- and gap-preserving deformations, and provide new steps towards practical quantum advantage in quantum physics.

</details>


### [170] [Optimal Entanglement Routing in Quantum Repeater Chains: Beyond Fixed Operation Order and Purification Schedule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37206)
*Aikaterini Mandilara,Antonia Tsili,Dimitris Syvridis,Konstantinos Christodoulopoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文放宽了纠缠路由中操作顺序（固定为先纯化后交换PtS）和每条链路纯化调度（限于pumping）两大限制，针对有限链路容量的线性中继链，在两种量子噪声模型下建立容量感知优化框架（PtS下的精确MILP，含pumping或一般树形纯化；任意操作顺序下的精确DP），并从理论上证明pumping在对称Pauli噪声下的可行性上界及交换后纯化不增益，最终指出纯化调度是决定可行性的主导因素，与操作顺序互为替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠路由方法将决策空间沿两个维度受限：操作顺序多固定为纯化后再交换（PtS），且每条链路的纯化调度多限于pumping。作者希望放松这两项限制，在有限链路容量的线性中继链上研究满足保真度阈值约束下端到端期望吞吐量的最大化问题，从而厘清操作顺序与纯化调度对可行性的相对重要性。

Method: 针对带有限链路容量的线性中继链，在两种量子噪声模型下构建统一的容量感知优化框架：其一为PtS下精确的混合整数线性规划（MILP），可实例化为pumping或一般树形纯化调度；其二为可覆盖任意操作顺序的精确动态规划（DP）。同时给出理论分析：在对称Pauli噪声下研究pumping的收敛保真度、一般树形调度的容量相关界，以及交换后纯化对吞吐量的影响。

Result: 理论结果：(1) 对称Pauli噪声下pumping收敛到严格低于1的保真度，给出与容量无关的可行性上界及所有基于pumping的PtS方法的最大链长，而一般树形调度给出依赖容量的上界；(2) 交换后纯化从不提高吞吐量，故自由操作顺序只能提升可行性。数值评估验证了上述界：中等保真度阈值下，树形调度可服务超过90%的pumping无法满足的请求；在pumping类内，自由操作顺序可恢复其中大部分优势；但一旦链路采用树形调度，基于树的MILP与顺序精确DP所服务的请求集合完全相同。

Conclusion: 操作顺序与纯化调度是可相互替代的因素，在对称Pauli噪声下纯化调度是主导可行性（feasibility）的关键因素。

Abstract: Entanglement routing establishes entangled pairs between distant nodes of a quantum network by purifying and swapping pairs generated on elementary links. Existing methods typically restrict the decision space along two axes: the operation order, often fixed to purify-then-swap (PtS), and the purification schedule of each link, often restricted to pumping. Focusing on linear repeater chains with finite link capacities, we relax both restrictions and study the maximization of the expected end-to-end throughput subject to a fidelity threshold under two quantum-noise models. We develop a unified capacity-aware optimization framework comprising an exact mixed-integer linear program (MILP) under PtS, instantiated with either pumping or general tree purification schedules, and an exact dynamic program (DP) over arbitrary operation orders. Under symmetric Pauli noise, we prove that pumping converges to a fidelity strictly below unity, imposing a capacity-independent feasibility bound and a maximum chain length on every pumping-based PtS method, whereas general tree schedules yield a capacity-dependent bound. We further prove that post-swap purification never increases the throughput, so a free operation order can only increase feasibility. Numerical evaluations confirm the bounds: tree schedules serve over 90\% of the requests that pumping cannot at moderate fidelity thresholds; within the pumping class, a free operation order recovers much of this advantage; but once links use tree schedules, the tree-based MILP and the order-exact DP serve identical request sets throughout. Operation order and purification schedule are thus substitutes, with the purification schedule the dominant factor governing feasibility under symmetric Pauli noise.

</details>


### [171] [Encoding and Node Choices in Transversal Fault-Tolerant Distributed Quantum Computations: An Initial Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37210)
*Seng W. Loke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 比较不同BB码编码与节点选择对分布式逻辑门（非局域CNOT、GCZ）的影响，指出仅以减少ebit消耗为准则并非最优，需兼顾码距。自对偶[[120,8,12]] BB码上的横向物理GCZ可实现8个并发逻辑GCZ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算中需在多个节点间执行非局域逻辑操作，编码方案与节点分配会影响ebit消耗与逻辑错误率；已有实践中常以最小化ebit消耗作为选择编码的准则，本文质疑该单一准则的合理性。

Method: 对比多种Bivariate-Bicycle (BB) 码编码方式及节点选择方案，以分布式非局域CNOT和分布式逻辑量子比特上的全局门（GCZ）为示例任务，分析物理量子比特数、ebit需求、码距与并发操作能力之间的关系，并考察横向Hadamard引起的逻辑置换。

Result: 部分编码所需物理量子比特更多、分布式计算所需ebit更多；但仅减少ebit消耗未必能降低分布式计算的逻辑错误率，使用更多物理量子比特换取更大码距可能更优。此外，编码选择可支持特定并发操作：在自对偶[[120,8,12]] BB码上，横向物理GCZ在考虑横向Hadamard导致的逻辑置换后，可实现8个并发逻辑GCZ操作。

Conclusion: 为分布式量子计算选择BB码编码时，应综合权衡ebit消耗与码距（逻辑错误率），而非仅最小化ebit；编码选择还可用于实现并发逻辑操作，如[[120,8,12]]码上的8路并发GCZ。

Abstract: We compare and study different Bivariate-Bicycle (BB) encodings and node choices for distributed quantum operations such as transversal non-local CNOTs. We observe that while some encodings have more physical qubits requiring more ebits for a distributed computation, reducing ebit consumption alone might not be the best criterion for selecting an encoding for the logical qubits, if the goal is to reduce the logical error rate of the distributed computation; for example, one should also consider encodings with a larger distance, which using more physical qubits can provide. We consider distributed, or non-local, CNOTs and computation of the global gate (GCZ) over distributed logical qubits as examples. The choice of encoding enables particular concurrent operations; e.g., we show that a transversal physical GCZ on a self-dual $[[120,8,12]] BB$ code can realize eight concurrent logical GCZ operations after accounting for the logical permutation induced by transversal Hadamard.

</details>


### [172] [Ultra-high-distance quantum memories from amplified qLDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37231)
*Zijian Liang,Boren Gu,Yu-An Chen,Jens Eisert,Zongyuan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"距离放大器"（distance amplifiers），通过将基码与放大器进行张量积并适配综合征提取电路，系统性地同时提升量子LDPC码的码距与认证电路级保护；借助故障响应证书给出电路距离的严格下界，并证明在flagged [[4,2,2]]、超图积与旋转表面码三类放大器下电路距离呈乘积关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大码距本身不足以保证量子存储的良好保护，因为综合征提取过程中的故障会传播为关联数据错误；随着电路规模增大，认证电路距离愈发困难。因此需要一种可模块化、可认证的方法来构建高距离且具电路级保护的量子存储。

Method: 提出距离放大器：将基码与放大器作张量积，并相应改造二者的综合征提取电路以适配放大码。为认证所得存储，发展故障响应证书（fault-response certificate），给出电路距离的严格下界。通过合适的门排序与flag量子比特抑制关联错误，建立放大电路距离等于各组成电路距离乘积的显式条件，并在flagged [[4,2,2]]、超图积和旋转表面码三类放大器上证明该乘法关系。证书可递归扩展，使电路距离在每一步放大中精确相乘；同时给出单个逻辑量子比特在放大过程中的显式追踪方法。

Result: 证明了三类放大器下电路距离的乘积关系，且证书递归扩展使每步放大中电路距离精确相乘。以flagged [[4,2,2]]放大器对[[18,4,4]]种子码连续放大四次为例，得到[[13320,64,64]]码，电路距离d_circ ≥ 48，校验权重w ≤ 14；并实现了逻辑量子比特在放大过程中的显式追踪。

Conclusion: 该框架使高距离量子存储能够由更小的构建模块搭建并认证，为可扩展的容错量子计算提供了一条系统化路径。

Abstract: A large code distance does not by itself guarantee a well-protected quantum memory, as faults during syndrome extraction can propagate into correlated data errors. Certifying circuit distance becomes demanding as circuits grow. In this work, we introduce distance amplifiers, a modular approach to increasing both code distance and certified circuit-level protection in quantum low-density parity-check codes. The construction tensors a base code with an amplifier, adapting their syndrome-extraction circuits to the amplified code. To certify the resulting memory, we develop a fault-response certificate that yields rigorous lower bounds on circuit distance. Suitable gate ordering and flag qubits control correlated errors, allowing us to establish explicit conditions under which the amplified circuit distance equals the product of the constituent circuit distances. We prove this multiplicative relation for three types of amplifiers: the flagged $[[4,2,2]]$, hypergraph-product, and rotated surface codes. The certificate extends recursively, so the circuit distance multiplies exactly at every amplification step. For example, four successive applications of the flagged $[[4,2,2]]$ amplifier to a $[[18,4,4]]$ seed yield a $[[13320,64,64]]$ code with the circuit distance $d_{\mathrm{circ}} \geq 48$ and check weights $w \leq 14$. We further show how individual logical qubits can be tracked explicitly through amplification. By enabling high-distance quantum memories to be built and certified from smaller building blocks, our framework offers a systematic route toward scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [173] [Approximate majorization and high-order capacity of quantum depolarizing channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37240)
*Zhiwei Song,Xin Wang,Marco Tomamichel,Kun Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究退极化信道在多次相干使用下纠缠是否能带来信息处理优势，提出退极化信道输出的majorization关系猜想，并证明其近似版本，进而给出量子退极化信道经典容量的紧致三阶展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息论的核心问题之一是：当允许多次信道使用间的相干操作时，纠缠能否带来信息处理优势。对于退极化信道，King关于最小输出熵可加性的证明排除了渐近主阶上的经典通信优势，但更高阶上是否存在优势仍未知。

Method: 作者对退极化信道的输出提出一个majorization（主序）关系猜想，并证明其近似版本。证明结合了平移对数行列式的精确排序与乘积态输出谱的反集中界，改进了King基于Schatten范数的分析。

Result: 作为直接应用，作者得到量子退极化信道经典容量的紧致三阶展开。由此可知，相比于乘积编码策略，纠缠码字与联合解码最多可将消息规模提升O(log log n)比特。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对有限分组长度下纠缠作用的理解，表明纠缠在退极化信道高阶级上带来的优势极为有限，最多为O(log log n)比特。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum information theory is to determine whether entanglement provides an advantage for information processing when coherent operations across multiple channel uses are allowed. For depolarizing channels, King's proof of the additivity of the minimum output entropy rules out such an advantage for classical communication at leading asymptotic order, while leaving open the possibility of an advantage for higher orders. In this work, we conjecture a majorization relation for the outputs of depolarizing channels and prove an approximate version of this conjecture. Our proof combines an exact ordering of shifted log-determinants with an anti-concentration bound for the spectrum of the product-state output, refining King's analysis based on Schatten norms. As a direct application, we obtain a tight third-order expansion for the classical capacity of quantum depolarizing channels. Consequently, entangled codewords and collective decoding can improve the message size over the product-coding strategy by at most $O(\log \log n)$ bits, sharpening our understanding of the role of entanglement in the finite-blocklength regime.

</details>


### [174] [Directional transfer from coherence to longitudinal polarization and algebraic storage in a transmon with Markovian dephasing and a glassy environment: A non-Markovian fractional renewal model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37270)
*Ruha Uğraş Erdoğan,Ferit Acar Savacı*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个完全正且保迹的非马尔可夫transmon模型，其纵向退相干为马尔可夫型，横向为受玻璃态双能级系统环境启发的更新事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃态双能级系统环境中的慢缺陷重构会导致非马尔可夫、更新型的退相干行为，需要建立能刻画此类记忆效应的transmon微观模型。

Method: 通过微观哈密顿量识别耦合子空间，采用归一化Prabhakar等待时间律唯象粗粒化慢缺陷重构；由前向时序更新历史导出精确的映射级Volterra方程、非对易核-预解主方程及等价的漂移共轭Caputo型表示；在无能量弛豫下分析Pauli空间动力学分解，并用分支割分析与独立逆拉普拉斯重构比较数值精度。

Result: 无能量弛豫时Pauli空间分解为两个一维子空间与耦合的(σ_y,σ_z)子空间，支持相干与纵向极化间的定向转移，σ_x极化对更新不敏感；重尾等待时间(0<α<1)下纵向极化出现通用t^{-α}存储尾，反向转移为O(t^{-1-α})；恒定速率Poisson更新给出指数衰减；有限T_1的独立零温弛豫在速率低于两倍纯退相干速率时指数截断尾部；Prabhakar更新在指定读出处的正向转移幅度大于尺度匹配Poisson与不均匀Poisson参考，但两种Poisson参考给出更大的有限窗口峰值。

Conclusion: 无序启发的更新记忆按状态和方向选择性地重塑transmon退相干，非马尔可夫更新机制可产生区别于Poisson参考的长时存储尾与定向相干-极化转移。

Abstract: We develop a completely positive and trace-preserving non-Markovian transmon model with longitudinal Markovian dephasing and transverse renewal events motivated by a glassy two-level-system environment. A microscopic Hamiltonian identifies coupling subspaces; a normalized Prabhakar waiting-time law phenomenologically coarse grains slow defect reconfigurations. Forward chronological renewal histories yield an exact map-level Volterra equation, a noncommuting kernel--resolvent master equation, and an equivalent drift-conjugated Caputo-type representation with its required initial-value term. Without energy relaxation, Pauli-space dynamics splits into two one-dimensional subspaces and a coupled $(σ_y,σ_z)$ subspace supporting directional transfer between coherence and longitudinal polarization; $σ_x$ polarization is renewal-insensitive. For heavy-tailed waiting times with $0<α<1$, branch-cut analysis gives a generic $t^{-α}$ storage tail in longitudinal polarization generated from initial coherence and $O(t^{-1-α})$ reverse transfer; constant-rate Poisson renewal yields pole-generated exponential decay. Independent zero-temperature relaxation at finite $T_1$ exponentially cuts off these tails when its rate is below twice the pure-dephasing rate. We compare independent inverse-Laplace reconstructions of the waiting-time density, memory kernel, and selected qubit-response components to assess numerical accuracy. For the studied parameters, Prabhakar renewal gives larger forward-transfer magnitudes at the prescribed readout than both a scale-matched constant-rate Poisson reference and an inhomogeneous Poisson reference matching the full expected-count profile, including finite-$T_1$ tests. Both Poisson references yield larger evaluated finite-window peaks. Disorder-motivated renewal memory reshapes transmon decoherence selectively by state and direction.

</details>


### [175] [Quantum Leakage Resilience of Shamir Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37276)
*Rishabh Batra,Fuyuki Kitagawa,Ryo Nishimaki,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文开创性地研究素数域上未经修改的Shamir秘密共享的量子泄漏韧性。核心结论：当阈值率 t/n > τ*≈0.73339 且有一定余量时，抗单量子比特局部泄漏的区分优势为 2^{-Ω(n)}；允许线性数量设备与敌手共享纠缠时仍安全；但若任意大规模纠缠（如 t 个设备共享 GHZ 态）存在，则经典单比特泄漏也不再安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Shamir 秘密共享在单比特局部泄漏模型下已被广泛研究，但其量子泄漏模型（每方本地泄漏信道输出一个量子比特）尚未被系统考察。本文旨在填补这一空白，刻画量子泄漏与纠缠对 Shamir 方案安全性的影响边界。

Method: 研究素数域上未经修改的 Shamir 秘密共享，定义量子局部泄漏模型：每方本地信道输入其份额、输出一个泄漏量子比特。分三种情形分析：(1) 无预共享纠缠；(2) 允许任意固定最大尺寸的不相交纠缠块（块间及与敌手无纠缠），每块泄漏固定数量量子比特；(3) 指定设备集可与敌手共享纠缠。负向方面构造 GHZ 纠缠攻击。

Result: (1) 无纠缠时，若 t/n=τ 超过 τ*≈0.73339 且余量固定，区分优势为 2^{-Ω(n)}。(2) 允许不相交纠缠块时，阈值率足够接近 1 即安全。(3) 即便 αn（小 α>0）个设备彼此及与敌手共享纠缠，足够高的阈值率下仍安全。负向结果：恰好 t 个泄漏设备共享 GHZ 态时，经典单比特泄漏即不安全，各设备仅输出一经典比特，其联合奇偶性以常数优势区分任意秘密对。

Conclusion: 对高于 τ* 的固定阈值率，可与彼此及敌手任意纠缠而仍保持安全的设备数量为 n 的线性，最优支持分数仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We initiate the study of quantum leakage resilience of unmodified Shamir secret sharing over prime fields. A well-studied leakage model for Shamir's secret sharing classically is single-bit local leakage from each share. We consider its quantum analogue where, for each party, a local leakage channel takes as input the party's share and outputs a leaked qubit. Without preshared entanglement, we show that the distinguishing advantage is $2^{-Ω(n)}$ when the threshold rate $t/n=τ$ exceeds $τ_\star\approx0.73339$ by a fixed positive margin.
  More generally, we allow disjoint entangled blocks of any fixed maximum size where there is no entanglement between different blocks or with the adversary, and each block emits at most a fixed number of qubits. Security holds when the threshold rate is high enough (sufficiently close to one). We then allow a specified set of devices to share entanglement with the adversary. We show that security holds even when a linear number of devices ($αn$ for small $α>0$) share entanglement with each other and with the adversary for a large enough threshold rate.
  As a complementary negative result, we also show that even classical single-bit leakage makes Shamir scheme insecure if we allow arbitrarily large entanglement between the leakage devices. A GHZ state shared by exactly $t$ leakage devices makes even classical one-bit leakage insecure, without any entanglement with the adversary. In this attack, each participating device emits only one classical bit, and their joint parity distinguishes any chosen pair of secrets with a constant advantage. Thus, for fixed threshold rates above $τ_\star$, the maximum number of devices that may share arbitrary entanglement with one another and with the adversary while preserving security is linear in $n$ up to constant factors, although the optimal support fraction remains open.

</details>


### [176] [Parametric DRAG for leakage-suppressed exchange gates in superconducting qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37296)
*Yiwen Li,Xinsheng Tan,Yang Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 PDRAG 脉冲方案，在保持目标交换相互作用的同时抑制弱非简谐 transmon 中快激发转移的两条泄漏通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程交换相互作用对超导电路中的量子门和量子多体模拟至关重要，但快速激发转移在弱非简谐 transmon 中会开启两条泄漏路径，降低门保真度并将模拟动力学驱离编码状态空间。

Method: 引入参数化绝热门导数移除（PDRAG），其共轭结构决定一阶与二阶导数修正，通过一个实频率指令实现；在 g/2π = 100 MHz 的 Duffing 模型中进行标定与仿真，验证 12.5–35 ns 脉冲作用下的泄漏抑制；并将导数系数迁移至电荷哈密顿量，经幅度与载波重标定后评估迁移效果。

Result: 标定后的 PDRAG 相对基础脉冲实现几何平均泄漏降低 5.27×10^5；在 13 ns 时，标定的全交换脉冲端点泄漏为 1.98×10^-7；导数系数迁移至电荷哈密顿量后，经重标定在 12.5–22 ns 区间平均降低 893 倍。

Conclusion: Floquet 回归干涉与电荷诱导的能隙移动解释了标定与迁移增益，将紧凑脉冲设计与泄漏抑制的可编程相互作用相连接，为快速高保真交换门与多体模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Programmable exchange interactions support quantum gates and many-body simulation in superconducting circuits. Fast excitation transfer, however, opens two leakage pathways in weakly anharmonic transmons, degrading gates and driving simulated dynamics outside the encoded state space. We introduce parametric derivative removal by adiabatic gate (PDRAG) to suppress both pathways while preserving the target exchange. Their conjugate structure determines first- and second-derivative corrections implemented through one real frequency command. At $g/2π=100$ MHz, calibrated PDRAG achieves a geometric-mean leakage reduction of $5.27\times10^5$ relative to the base pulse across 12.5--35 ns in Duffing simulations. At 13 ns, endpoint leakage reaches $1.98\times10^{-7}$ for the calibrated full-exchange pulse. Transferring the derivative coefficients to a charge Hamiltonian gives a mean 893-fold reduction over 12.5--22 ns after amplitude and carrier recalibration. Floquet return interference and charge-induced gap shifts explain the calibration and transfer gains, connecting compact pulse design to leakage-suppressed programmable interactions.

</details>


### [177] [A magnetic-field insensitive gate set for trapped-ion nuclear spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37318)
*Jonathan Paul Home,Jeremy Flannery,Matteo Mazzanti,Jan Apolin,Shreyans Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套在高磁场（如Penning阱所需条件）下操控囚禁离子核自旋量子比特的完整操作方案，并设计了一种利用电子自旋实现但保持核自旋编码保护的双量子比特相位门技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高磁场环境下，核自旋与电子自旋因外场哈密顿量主导而基本解耦，核自旋磁矩小使其对外磁场不敏感（相比电子自旋更抗干扰），但同时也导致核自旋难以在快时间尺度上被操控，因此需要一种兼顾抗磁干扰与快速操控的方案。

Method: 提出完整的高磁场囚禁离子核自旋量子比特操控操作集；针对速度问题，设计一种双量子比特相位门技术，该技术借助电子自旋进行操控，但通过保留核自旋编码使量子比特免受磁场影响；该方法适用于较低磁场下的磁不敏感量子比特，并兼容微波与激光场梯度。

Result: 所提相位门方案能够利用电子自旋实现快速操作，同时通过核自旋编码保持对磁场的抗干扰性；方案适用范围包括低磁场下的磁不敏感量子比特，并可用于微波梯度与激光场梯度两种驱动方式。

Conclusion: 该工作为高磁场（如Penning阱）中囚禁离子核自旋量子比特提供了一套完整的操控与纠缠方案，在保证磁场鲁棒性的同时解决了核自旋操控速度慢的问题，具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: We outline a complete set of operations for manipulating nuclear spin qubits of trapped-ions stored in high magnetic fields such as required for Penning trapping, where the nuclear and electron spins are largely decoupled due to the dominance of the external field Hamiltonian. The reduced nuclear magnetic moment results in insensitivity to external magnetic fields compared to the use of an electron spin, but also makes the nuclear spin hard to manipulate on fast timescales. To maintain speed, we propose a two-qubit phase gate technique which utilizes the electron spin, but for which the qubit remains protected from magnetic fields through retaining the nuclear encoding. This method works for magnetic-field insensitive qubits at lower fields as well as for both microwave and laser field gradients.

</details>


### [178] [A Memory-Magic Exchange Law in Streaming Clifford+T Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37368)
*Jinze Yang,Yangyang Li,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究容错量子计算中"相位存储 vs 相位执行"的权衡：以每放弃一位经典存储所承诺的T门数α作为交换率，对无辅助位、按坐标的Clifford+T编译进行分析。通过Ramanujan界、行列式方法与高度二分法，得到α的下界估计α≥2、α≥11/5及α≥17/7，并在典型情形与猜想下论证α=3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 到达容错处理器的相位以可加片段形式出现，需要决定是跨轮保留（消耗经典存储）还是到达即执行（消耗尚未确定相位前就承诺的魔法态）。量化这两者之间的兑换关系（承诺T门数与放弃存储位数之比α）是资源优化的核心问题，但此前缺乏严格的下界与可达结果。

Method: 针对无辅助位的按坐标Clifford+T编译，利用Clifford+T格的Ramanujan界给出α≥2并附显式常数（谱方法平方根障碍）；提出初等行列式方法，依据四元数分子在两个实数嵌入下均为球面格点的事实，计数任意旋转陪集附近的词，得到渐近无条件下界α≥11/5，并通过所得球面截面的高度二分法提升至α≥17/7；进一步对Clifford框架陪集分析体积律，引入Ross-Selinger典型代价假设、等分布猜想与分数直通族，并考察带干净辅助位与相位梯度催化剂时的查表批处理。

Result: Ramanujan界给出α≥2（显式常数）；行列式方法给出渐近无条件α≥11/5，高度二分法提升至α≥17/7。在Clifford框架陪集上体积律成立到次指数因子：几乎全部z旋转需T计数(3-o(1))log₂(1/ε)，承诺片段接近Clifford框架z旋转的流程（含逐旋转流水线）有α≥3-o(1)；分数直通族在Ross-Selinger典型代价假设下达此值。在T计数至22的穷举支持的等分布猜想下，一般情况下α=3，存储应以整旋转为单位舍弃。上述界在相位相消为恒等时仍成立；侧信息通过条件熵进入；概率混合使代价减半但不改变速率。

Conclusion: 交换率α刻画了按坐标Clifford+T编译中存储与魔法态之间的基本权衡，下界从α≥2经α≥11/5提升到α≥17/7，并在典型代价与等分布假设下达到α=3，此时应以整旋转为单位舍弃存储。带干净辅助位与相位梯度催化剂时，跨坐标批处理查表将速率推至O(1/loglog(1/ε))，说明常数速率律是坐标式合成所特有的。

Abstract: A phase that reaches a fault-tolerant processor in additive pieces can be remembered until the last piece arrives, or executed on arrival: the first option costs classical memory carried across rounds, the second costs magic states committed before the phase is known. We determine the exchange rate $α$, committed $T$ gates per bit of memory forgone, for ancilla-free coordinatewise Clifford+$T$ compilation. The Ramanujan bound for the Clifford+$T$ lattice gives $α\ge2$ with explicit constants, the square-root barrier of the spectral method. An elementary determinant method, using that quaternion numerators are lattice points on spheres in both real embeddings of $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$, counts words near an arbitrary rotation coset below that barrier and gives $α\ge11/5$ asymptotically and unconditionally, and a height dichotomy for the resulting sphere sections raises this to $α\ge17/7$. At Clifford-framed cosets the volume law holds up to subexponential factors: all but a vanishing fraction of $z$-rotations need $T$-count $(3-o(1))\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$, and processes whose committed pieces are close to Clifford-framed $z$-rotations, including per-rotation pipelines, have $α\ge3-o(1)$, which a fractional-passthrough family attains under the Ross-Selinger typical-cost hypothesis. Under an equidistribution conjecture supported by exhaustive enumeration to $T$-count 22, $α=3$ in general and memory should be shed in whole rotations. The bounds hold even when the phases cancel to the identity; side information enters through a conditional entropy; probabilistic mixing halves the costs but not the rate. With clean ancillas and a phase-gradient catalyst, table lookups batched across coordinates drive the rate to $O(1/\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$, so the constant-rate law is specific to coordinatewise synthesis.

</details>


### [179] [Succinct Arguments for QMA in the Quantum Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37429)
*Alessandro Chiesa,Zihan Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了首个在量子随机预言机模型（QROM）下、不依赖额外密码学假设或未证明猜想的QMA简洁论证，表明理想哈希函数（非结构化困难性）不仅足以支撑NP的简洁论证，也足以支撑QMA的简洁论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下，NP的简洁论证可仅由非结构化困难性（如哈希函数）通过commit-and-open范式编译PCP/IOP得到；而已有的QMA简洁论证却依赖"结构化"密码原语或量子PCP猜想。作者希望在QMA场景中也实现仅基于非结构化困难性的简洁论证。

Method: 提出一个保效率的变换，将量子交互式预言机证明（QIOP，量子PCP的交互式推广）通过自然的量子commit-and-open范式编译为同一语言的量子论证。该变换适用于具备"公开查询可靠性"（public-query soundness，本文形式化的概念）的任意QIOP，而已知的QMA的QIOP满足该性质。关键组件是在QROM中形式化并构造了具有局部打开性质的量子态可提取向量承诺。

Result: 得到首个在QROM下无额外假设的QMA简洁论证；给出从非结构化困难性出发的QMA简洁论证；并构造出量子态的可提取向量承诺（含局部打开，在QROM中），该组件可能具有独立价值。

Conclusion: 理想哈希函数足以支撑QMA的简洁论证，将commit-and-open范式及非结构化困难性基础从NP推广到QMA；所提出的QIOP到量子论证的编译变换及量子可提取向量承诺为后续研究提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Succinct arguments are a fundamental cryptographic primitive for verifying computational claims with small communication. In the classical setting, succinct arguments for NP can be constructed from unstructured hardness alone (e.g., hash functions) by compiling probabilistically checkable proofs (PCPs) or interactive oracle proofs (IOPs) for NP via the commit-and-open paradigm. In contrast, known succinct arguments for QMA rely on ``structured'' cryptographic primitives, or on the quantum PCP conjecture.
  We construct the first succinct argument for QMA in the quantum random oracle model (QROM) without relying on additional cryptographic assumptions or unproven conjectures. This yields succinct arguments for QMA from unstructured hardness alone, showing that ideal hash functions not only suffice for succinct arguments for NP but also for QMA.
  Underlying our result is an efficiency-preserving transformation that compiles quantum interactive oracle proofs (QIOPs), a recently introduced interactive generalization of quantum PCPs, into quantum arguments for the same language, via a natural quantum commit-and-open paradigm. Our transformation applies to every QIOP with public-query soundness, a notion that we formalize to capture a natural requirement of the commit-and-open paradigm and is satisfied by a known QIOP for QMA. As a key ingredient in our transformation, we formalize and construct extractable vector commitments for quantum states with local openings in the QROM, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [180] [Experimental certification of multipartite Bell correlations using only few-body symmetric correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37442)
*Jiacheng Sun,Mengyao Hu,Paweł Cieśliński,Jinfu Chen,Fei Shi,Bowen Wang,Hongyu Yang,Jizhou Wu,Jordi Tura,Valerio Scarani,Dian Wu,Jian Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明多体贝尔关联可用固定最大关联阶数的少体置换不变（PI）关联来认证，并在六光子和八光子后选择Dicke态上实验验证了少体PI贝尔关联见证，四体关联项的加入显著提升了噪声容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝尔不等式依赖多体关联，随着系统规模增大，实验上估计这些关联变得越来越困难，因此需要一种无需测量全体关联、实验上可行的多体贝尔关联认证方法。

Method: 针对六光子和八光子后选择Dicke态，设计并实验测试少体置换不变（PI）贝尔关联见证，其最大关联阶数固定；在原有二体见证基础上引入少量四体关联器，每个参与方仍使用相同的两种测量设置。

Result: 加入四体关联后噪声容忍度显著提升：六量子比特时选定见证可容忍白噪声比例 p_c≈0.301，而相同每方两设置下最优二体PI不等式仅为 p_c≈0.048。六光子和八光子分别以32和8.9个标准差的显著性观测到见证界被违反，对应后选择偏振态与半激发Dicke态的保真度分别为0.933(4)和0.916(11)。六光子与八光子情形使用相同的对称二体、四体关联器，仅系数不同。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需测量全体关联即可在更大系统中认证多体贝尔关联，提供了一种实验上可扩展的可行途径，且相同少体关联器可通过调整系数适配不同规模系统。

Abstract: Certifying Bell correlations in increasingly larger multipartite quantum systems is challenging because conventional Bell inequalities rely on many-body correlations, which become experimentally demanding to estimate as system size grows. Here we demonstrate that multipartite Bell correlations can be certified using few-body permutation-invariant (PI) correlations with a fixed maximal correlation order. We experimentally test few-body PI Bell correlation witnesses tailored for six- and eight-photon postselected Dicke states.
