The addition of a few four-body correlators is found to significantly enhance the noise tolerance compared with previously studied two-body witnesses: for instance, for six qubits, the selected witness tolerates a white-noise fraction of $p_c\approx0.301$, compared with $p_c\approx0.048$ for the best two-body PI inequality with the same two settings per party. For six and eight photons respectively, we observe violations of the witness bounds by $32$ and $8.9$ standard deviations, arising from postselected polarisation states with fidelities of $0.933(4)$ and $0.916(11)$ to the corresponding half-excitation Dicke states. The witnesses for the six- and eight-photon cases use the same symmetric two- and four-body correlators, just with different coefficients. These results demonstrate an experimentally accessible approach for certifying multipartite Bell correlations in larger systems without requiring full-body correlation measurements.

</details>


### [181] [An Operator-Norm Approach to Security with Quantum Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37467)
*Minki Hhan,Sunghyuk Jo,Qipeng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种算子范数方法，用于量子随机预言机与随机置换模型下的非均匀安全性，统一处理搜索成功率与区分优势的归约，并给出Yao盒、PRG、加盐决策游戏及加盐求逆等问题的更紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均匀安全性刻画了攻击者可在挑战前获得关于预言机的有限建议，是最现实的攻击模型，已有大量研究。但既有归约在决策游戏中仍只针对成功概率，导致界较松，需要一种能同时处理成功概率与区分优势的统一方法。

Method: 引入算子范数方法处理量子随机预言机与随机置换模型下的非均匀安全性；通过定义博弈的一种性质，将离线阶段已有查询与后续在线查询的贡献分离，从而获得更紧的界。

Result: 得到搜索成功率与区分优势的统一归约；给出Yao盒（含与不含加盐）的紧界、伪随机生成器的改进界；证明决策游戏的最优通用加盐定理；对特定加盐构造给出更强界，包括加盐置换求逆（至多对数因子内紧）和加盐随机函数求逆（至多对数因子及经典函数求逆已有差距内紧）。

Conclusion: 算子范数方法为量子随机预言机与随机置换模型下的非均匀安全性提供了统一且更紧的分析框架，推广了归约适用范围并改进了多类密码学问题的安全性界。

Abstract: Non-uniform security allows an adversary to receive bounded advice about an oracle before attempting a fresh challenge. This captures the most realistic attacks and has already been studied extensively in prior work. In this work, we introduce an operator-norm approach for non-uniform security in the quantum random oracle and random permutation models. This new approach yields a unified reduction for both search success probability and distinguishing advantage. Previously, the reduction only worked with success probability even in the decision games, yielding a worse bound.
  Our framework enables tight bounds for Yao's box, both with and without salting, and improved bounds for pseudorandom generators. We also prove an optimal generic salting theorem for decision games. By defining a property of a game, which separates the contributions of the existing queries in the offline stage and subsequent online queries, we obtain stronger bounds for specific salted constructions. These include salted permutation inversion, tight up to logarithmic factors, and salted random-function inversion, tight up to logarithmic factors and the gap already present in classical function inversion.

</details>


### [182] [Asymptotic Pseudospectra in Dissipative Floquet Quantum Systems: Geometric Structures and Observable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37523)
*Yuncheng Xie,Haozhe Shi,Zhuocheng Ma,Weibin Chu,Xin-Gao Gong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究周期驱动开放量子系统中伪谱的全局几何结构，按残差的标度律将单位圆盘划分为指数、代数和有界精度区域，并用两个可解模型揭示几何结构驱动的物理可观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动开放量子系统中非正态性使Floquet谱在热力学极限下不足以刻画动力学，需要伪谱；但既有研究多关注孤立伪谱的局域动力学，其全局连接及对可观测量产生的物理后果尚不明确。

Method: 按伪谱残差的渐近尺度标度律，将单位圆盘划分为指数精度、代数精度和有界精度三类不同区域，据此刻画伪谱的集体行为与几何结构；并构造两个严格可解模型（耗散位移链、手性XY模型）进行验证。

Result: 在耗散位移链中，参数调制驱动几何相变，可通过自旋波观测量检测；在手性XY模型中，利用该几何结构解释了连续参数调制下两个关联信号交换保持顺序这一可测现象的起源。

Conclusion: 研究为理解耗散Floquet量子系统建立了新的理论范式，并预言了可由量子计算实验测量的几何驱动观测量。

Abstract: In periodically driven open quantum systems, nonnormality renders the Floquet spectrum insufficient as the system approaches the thermodynamic limit, so that pseudospectra are needed to characterize the dynamics accurately. While conventional approaches mainly focus on the local dynamics of isolated pseudospectra, their global connections and the resulting physical consequences for observables have remained largely unexplored. Here, we uncover this collective behavior by classifying the unit disk into distinct domains of exponential, algebraic, and bounded accuracy according to the asymptotic size-scaling laws of pseudospectral residuals, yielding an underlying geometric structure. We demonstrate the physical implications of this structure through two exactly solvable models. First, in a dissipative shift chain, parameter tuning drives geometric transitions that are detectable via spin-wave observables. Second, in a chiral XY model, we use this geometric structure to explain the origin of a measurable phenomenon: two correlation signals exchange their retention order under continuous parameter tuning. Our findings not only establish a new theoretical paradigm for understanding dissipative Floquet quantum systems, but also predict geometry-driven observables for quantum computing experiments.

</details>


### [183] [Digital quantum state preparation by nucleation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37527)
*Jean Paul Sadia,Morten Hjorth-Jensen,Dean Lee,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受成核现象启发的格点哈密顿量量子态制备方法：先在小尺寸格点上制备精确基态，再通过Trotter化的绝热演化逐级扩大格点规模，并给出演化时间与Trotter步数的充分条件；进一步引入变分优化的"优化成核"方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备对量子算法至关重要，改进的制备方法可将资源需求降低数个数量级，因此需要更高效的格点哈密顿量基态制备方案。

Method: 受成核启发：在量子电路中先制备小尺寸格点哈密顿量的精确基态，随后通过一系列Trotter化绝热演化"生长阶段"逐步扩大格点至目标模型；利用绝热定理与Trotter误差界推导总演化时间与Trotter步数的充分条件；再提出优化成核，在成核结构之上叠加变分优化。

Result: 给出了成核法达到目标保真度所需总演化时间与Trotter步数的充分条件；在二维Ising模型上，优化成核优于当前数字量子计算机上领先的态制备方法。

Conclusion: 成核及其优化变体为数字量子计算机上格点哈密顿量的基态制备提供了具理论保证且经验上更高效的新途径。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is crucial for quantum algorithms, and improved state preparation methods can reduce resource requirements by orders of magnitude. In this work, we introduce a state preparation method for lattice Hamiltonians on digital quantum computers inspired by nucleation. In our method, the exact ground state of a small lattice Hamiltonian is prepared in a quantum circuit, then a series of growth stages are performed via Trotterized adiabatic evolution to increase the size of the lattice to the desired model. Using the adiabatic theorem and Trotter error bounds, we provide sufficient conditions on the total evolution time and number of Trotter steps required for nucleation to prepare an initial state with desired fidelity. In addition, we introduce optimized nucleation which considers variational optimization on top of the nucleation structure. For a two-dimensional Ising model, we show that optimized nucleation can outperform a current leading method for state preparation on digital quantum computers.

</details>


### [184] [Semiclassical spin-bath calculation of the nitrogen-isotope effect on NV$^-$ ensemble coherence in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37596)
*Chikara Shinei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用半经典自旋浴模型研究了氮同位素对NV色心系综T2/T2*比值的影响，发现P1对的超精细简并比例在14N中为1/4、15N中为5/16，导致T2(14N)/T2(15N)=1.118，可部分解释实验观测到的比值差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心系综的T2/T2*比值在自然丰度（14N）金刚石中约为16且与氮浓度无关，但15N掺杂系综磁力计在单量子约定下仅给出8.05±0.18。作者希望验证氮核同位素本身是否能单独造成如此显著的降低，并定量刻画其贡献。

Method: 采用半经典自旋浴模型，引入Jahn-Teller分辨的P1超精细张量，统计可自由flip-flop的超精细简并P1对比例；并在[N]=0.1–100 ppm范围内进行T2和T2*的模拟。

Result: 超精细简并、可flip-flop的P1对比例：14N（I=1）为1/4，15N（I=1/2）为5/16。模拟显示该机制缩短T2但精确保持T2*不变，得到T2(14N)/T2(15N)=1.118±0.011，与[N]无关且高于1达11σ。实验测得对比度为2.07±0.25，比理论高3.8σ。

Conclusion: 氮核同位素通过改变共振flip-flop通道数量确实会缩短T2并影响T2/T2*比值，但其贡献是真实而部分的；实测对比度更大，说明还有其他机制参与，该结果为多体计算提供了定量基准。

Abstract: The ratio $T_2$/$T_2^*$ of the Hahn-echo and Ramsey times of nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) ensembles is independent of the nitrogen concentration [N] and is $\approx$ 16 in diamond of natural isotopic abundance ($^{14}$N), yet a recent $^{15}$N-doped ensemble magnetometer gives only 8.05 $\pm$ 0.18 in the single-quantum convention. We ask whether the nitrogen nuclear isotope alone can cause such a reduction. Using a semiclassical spin-bath model with the Jahn-Teller-resolved P1 hyperfine tensor, we show that the fraction of P1 pairs that are hyperfine-degenerate, and hence free to flip-flop, is 1/4 for $^{14}$N (I = 1) but 5/16 for $^{15}$N (I = 1/2). Simulations over [N] = 0.1--100 ppm confirm that this shortens $T_2$ while leaving $T_2^*$ exactly unchanged: $T_2$($^{14}$N)/$T_2$($^{15}$N) = 1.118 $\pm$ 0.011, independent of [N] and 11$σ$ above unity. The measured contrast, 2.07 $\pm$ 0.25, is 3.8$σ$ larger, identifying resonant-channel counting as a real but partial contribution and setting a quantitative benchmark for many-body calculations.

</details>


### [185] [Optimal interferometric certification of multi-photon indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37620)
*Marco Robbio,Michał Oszmaniec,Nicolas J. Cerf,Ernesto Galvão,Leonardo Novo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种高效、实验友好的协议，用于估计或界定N光子态相对于最接近完全不可区分态的保真度F_ind，且均以可证明最优的O(1/ε)样本量实现认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子不可区分性是光子量子技术的关键资源，但传统表征方法资源消耗大，且以往方法需对部分不可区分模型作先验假设，限制了严格性与操作意义。

Method: 第一协议针对制备可分离态的源，采用单个傅里叶干涉仪结合光子数分辨探测，给出F_ind的紧致双侧界；第二协议结合随机化线性光学干涉仪实现与光子计数，可直接估计任意N光子态的F_ind。二者均基于Hong-Ou-Mandel检验的多光子推广。

Result: 两种协议均能以可证明最优的O(1/ε)样本量认证F_ind = 1 - O(ε)；第一协议给出紧致双侧界，第二协议适用于任意N光子态。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需对部分不可区分模型作先验假设，使多光子不可区分性的严格且具操作意义的认证在当前光子技术条件下成为可能。

Abstract: Multiphoton indistinguishability is a key resource for photonic quantum technologies, yet its characterization typically relies on resource-intensive methods. In this work, we develop two efficient and experimentally friendly protocols to estimate or bound the fidelity $F_{\mathrm{ind}}$ of an $N$-photon state to the closest perfectly indistinguishable state. The first protocol applies to sources preparing separable states, uses a single Fourier interferometer together with photon-number-resolving detection, and yields tight two-sided bounds on $F_{\mathrm{ind}}$. The second protocol combines randomized implementations of linear-optical interferometers with photon counting, enabling direct estimation of $F_{\mathrm{ind}}$ for arbitrary $N$-photon states. Both protocols can certify $F_{\mathrm{ind}} = 1 - \mathcal{O}(ε)$ using provably optimal $\mathcal{O}(1/ε)$ samples, in contrast to previous approaches which required prior assumptions on the model of partial distinguishability. Our methods, based on a multiphoton generalization of the Hong-Ou-Mandel test, bring the rigorous and operationally meaningful certification of multiphoton indistinguishability within reach of current photonic technologies.

</details>


### [186] [Operational theory for photonic circuits: generalizing linear optics beyond quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37628)
*Ismaël Septembre,Matthias Kleinmann,Martin Plávala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子光学中的正交算符与力学谐振子相空间坐标类比，在广义概率论框架下重构线性光学，发现Hong-Ou-Mandel效应和Mach-Zehnder干涉等光子学现象并非量子理论独有，也可在经典光及修正的量子化光理论中出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探究Hong-Ou-Mandel效应、Mach-Zehnder干涉等标志性光子学现象究竟是量子理论的专属特征，还是可在更广泛的广义概率论中普遍存在，从而为复杂光子装置与光子量子计算机的广义理论研究奠定基础。

Method: 通过将量子光学的正交算符类比于机械谐振子的相空间坐标，作者基于正交算符建立了用于分析光子电路的广义概率论框架，并在此框架下考察分束器输出光子符合计数与Mach-Zehnder干涉等具体场景。

Result: 分束器输出端光子符合计数消失的现象不仅在量子理论中出现，在经典光以及一种修正的量子化光版本中同样存在；Mach-Zehnder干涉方面，量子理论与这些替代理论之间未发现定性差异。

Conclusion: Hong-Ou-Mandel效应与Mach-Zehnder干涉并非量子理论的定性专属现象，该框架可作为研究复杂光子装置及光子量子计算机的广义理论的通用基础。

Abstract: We use the analogy between the quadrature operators in quantum optics and the phase-space coordinates in a mechanical harmonic oscillator to generalise the theory of linear optics beyond quantum theory. For this, we introduce a framework for analysing photonic circuits within generalised probabilistic theories based on quadrature operators. Using this framework, we ask whether phenomena like the Hong-Ou-Mandel effect, Mach-Zehnder interferometry are specific to quantum theory or also occur across a broader class of theories. We find the latter: the vanishing photon coincidences at the output of a beam splitter also occur for classical light and for a modified version of quantised light. Similarly, for Mach-Zehnder interferometry we find that there is no qualitative difference between quantum theory and these alternative theories. Our framework can serve as a foundation to study generalised theories of complex photonic set-ups and photonic quantum computers.

</details>


### [187] [Adaptive decoding of quantum LDPC codes through decoder disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37629)
*Maida Wang,Peter V. Coveney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于BP与OSD不一致性的自适应qLDPC解码器，仅在少数高风险实例上加深搜索，可恢复大部分性能增益并显著降低解码成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码的精确解码通常依赖昂贵的后处理搜索，但不同错误综合征（syndrome）的解码难度差异很大；若能识别少数真正需要深度搜索的实例，即可将计算资源集中在关键处，从而在保持解码性能的同时降低平均开销。

Method: 采用BP与ordered-statistics decoding（OSD）相结合的自适应解码框架，以BP硬判决与满足综合征的order-zero OSD解之间的不一致性作为解码器内部风险信号，据此决定哪些实例需要升级到更深的搜索。在[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码（电路级去极化噪声）上，仅对风险最高的20%实例进行扩展搜索，并与全量单自由变量扫描、BP综合征残差、综合征权重及随机选择等路由策略对比；同时在lifted-product构造的radial qLDPC码以及Quantinuum H2离子阱处理器的Z-memory实验（含X型和Z型校验）上验证。

Result: 在[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码上，仅升级风险最高的20%实例即可恢复全量单自由变量扫描所获逻辑错误率改善的85%–92%，同时相对对所有实例施加相同截断搜索，平均串行解码成本降低3.6倍；在相同预算下，基于不一致性的路由优于基于BP综合征残差、综合征权重和随机选择的策略。在结构不同的radial qLDPC码上，升级20%实例可恢复87%–94%的可用增益；Quantinuum H2处理器的Z-memory实验表明该信号在器件噪声下仍具预测性。

Conclusion: 解码器内部的不一致性信号能够有效指示哪些实例值得投入额外解码努力，使经典计算可集中于最可能受益的实例，为自适应、资源高效的qLDPC解码提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate decoding of quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes often relies on expensive post-processing search, although decoding difficulty varies strongly between syndromes. We find that the benefit of deeper post-processing search is highly concentrated in a small subset of decoding instances, and that these instances can be identified directly from the decoder itself. To this end, we introduce an adaptive decoder based on belief propagation (BP) and ordered-statistics decoding (OSD), using the disagreement between the BP hard decision and the syndrome-consistent order-zero OSD solution as an internal risk signal to determine where deeper search is required. On a $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate bicycle code under circuit-level depolarising noise, encoding $12$ logical qubits in $144$ data qubits with distance $12$, escalating only the highest-risk $20\%$ of instances recovers $85\%$ to $92\%$ of the improvement in logical error rate available from a full one-free-variable sweep, while reducing the mean serial decoding cost by a factor of $3.6$ relative to applying the same truncated search to every instance. At matched budget, disagreement-guided routing also outperforms routing based on the BP syndrome residual, syndrome weight and random selection. The same behaviour reappears on a structurally distinct radial qLDPC code obtained from a lifted-product construction, where escalating $20\%$ of instances recovers $87\%$ to $94\%$ of the available gain. A $Z$-memory experiment on the Quantinuum H2 trapped-ion processor, implementing both $X$-type and $Z$-type checks, further shows that the signal remains predictive under device noise. These results show that decoder-internal disagreement can expose where additional decoding effort is valuable, allowing classical computation to be concentrated on the instances most likely to benefit from it.

</details>


### [188] [Integrated balanced homodyne detector using CMOS capacitive-feedback TIA for quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37671)
*Sarah Bastiaens,Cedric Bruynsteen,Simone Cammarata,Axl Bomhals,Leandro da Silva,Michiel Van Osta,Johan Bauwelinck,Xin Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种低噪声高速平衡零拍探测器，将28 nm CMOS电容反馈跨阻放大器与imec iSiPP200硅光平台定制的光子集成电路相结合，实现7.6 GHz带宽、27 dB散粒噪声裕度等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子与相干光传感应用（如连续变量量子密钥分发、量子随机数生成、光学相干层析）对平衡零拍探测器提出低噪声与高速的双重要求，需要验证CMOS电容反馈跨阻放大器在此类场景中的适用性。

Method: 采用28 nm CMOS工艺实现电容反馈跨阻放大器，并与基于imec iSiPP200硅光子平台制作的定制光子集成电路进行混合集成，构成平衡零拍探测接收机。

Result: 接收机实现7.6 GHz的3 dB带宽、最高27 dB的散粒噪声裕度、13.7 GHz的散粒噪声受限带宽，以及高达54.8 dB的共模抑制比。

Conclusion: 结果表明CMOS电容反馈跨阻放大器适用于严苛的量子与相干光传感应用，包括连续变量量子密钥分发、量子随机数生成和光学相干层析。

Abstract: A low-noise, high-speed balanced homodyne detector is demonstrated, combining a capacitive-feedback transimpedance amplifier implemented in 28 nm CMOS with a custom photonic integrated circuit fabricated in imec's iSiPP200 silicon photonics platform. The receiver achieves a 3-dB bandwidth of 7.6 GHz, a maximum shot noise clearance of 27 dB, a shot noise-limited bandwidth of 13.7 GHz and a common-mode rejection ratio up to 54.8 dB. These results demonstrate the suitability of CMOS capacitive-feedback transimpedance amplifiers for demanding quantum and coherent sensing optical applications, including continuous-variable quantum key distribution, quantum random number generation, and optical coherence tomography.

</details>


### [189] [Constant-rate quantum codes with low-weight stabilizers and full logical Clifford actions via transversal and fold-transversal gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37699)
*Takaya Matsuura,Yohji Chin,Shohei Kiryu,Kosuke Fukui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类量子码，具有渐近恒定的编码率和次对数增长的稳定子权重，同时仅用横向门和折叠横向门即可实现完整的逻辑Clifford群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空间开销的容错量子计算不仅需要高码率量子纠错码，还需要空间高效逻辑操作。横向门与折叠横向门能限制错误传播且不需额外量子比特，但通常只能实现受限的逻辑操作。核心挑战是构造同时具备完整逻辑Clifford门集与优良码参数的量子码。

Method: 基于码空间为其Tanner图自同构群的绝对不可约Steinberg模的经典码；对这些经典码取超图积（hypergraph product）得到量子码，使其全部逻辑Clifford操作可由一组固定的横向门和折叠横向门合成。

Result: 所得量子码族具备渐近恒定编码率与次对数增长的稳定子权重，并支持完整逻辑Clifford群；稳定子权重的缓慢增长使小规模实例具有高纠错性能。

Conclusion: 该结果为使用横向门和折叠横向门实现低空间开销的容错量子计算提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Low-space-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computation requires not only high-rate quantum error-correcting codes but also space-efficient implementations of logical operations. Transversal and fold-transversal gates are promising since they limit error propagation and require no additional qubits. However, the logical operations they enable are typically restricted, and a central challenge is to construct codes that combine a complete set of logical Clifford gates with favorable code parameters. In this work, we introduce a family of quantum codes with an asymptotically constant encoding rate and sublogarithmically growing stabilizer weight, while supporting the entire logical Clifford group using only transversal and fold-transversal gates. Our construction is based on classical codes whose code spaces are absolutely irreducible Steinberg modules of their Tanner-graph automorphism groups. Taking hypergraph products of these classical codes yields quantum codes for which all logical Clifford operations can be synthesized from a fixed set of transversal and fold-transversal gates. Moreover, the slow growth of the stabilizer weight enables a high error-correcting performance in small instances. These results provide a path toward fault-tolerant quantum computation with low space overhead using transversal and fold-transversal gates.

</details>


### [190] [Optimal single-copy estimation of quantum state moments: why $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^3)$ and $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^4)$ are equally hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37781)
*Zhenhuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了估计量子态矩 Tr(ρ^t) 的最优样本复杂度，对任意固定整数 t≥2 为 Θ(d^{⌈log₂t⌉/(1+⌈log₂t⌉)})，并证明自适应测量相对非自适应协议的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子学习中的一个基本问题是在实验访问受限的情况下估计未知量子态的非线性性质。尽管纯度估计（t=2）的最优复杂度已知为 Θ(√d)，但更高阶矩的最优维度依赖关系此前尚不清楚。本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 上界方面，采用自适应态滤波（adaptive state filtering），将高阶矩估计约化到更小态空间中的低阶矩估计。下界方面，通过平滑插值直接比较矩匹配（moment-matched）的态集合，绕过最大混合态作为中间假设。

Result: 对每个固定整数 t≥2，在足够小的常数加性误差下，最优样本复杂度为 Θ(d^{⌈log₂t⌉/(1+⌈log₂t⌉)})（常数成功概率）。复杂度遵循二进制层级：所有 2^{h-1}<t≤2^h 的矩阶共享同一指数；三阶和四阶矩均需 Θ(d^{2/3}) 份拷贝。对任意非自适应单拷贝协议，建立了 Ω(d^{1-1/t}) 下界，与已知上界匹配，表明对每个 t≥4 自适应具有优势。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了估计量子态矩的最优样本复杂度随维度的依赖关系，揭示了矩阶之间的二进制层级结构，并首次证明自适应测量相对于非自适应协议对所有 t≥4 严格更优。所用平滑插值下界技术在超过 √d 的情形下也能获得匹配界。

Abstract: Estimating nonlinear properties of an unknown quantum state with restrictive experimental accessibility is a fundamental problem in quantum learning. We study the sample complexity of estimating the state moments $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^t)$, allowing arbitrary adaptive single-copy measurements. While purity estimation has optimal sample complexity $Θ(\sqrt d)$ at constant additive error, the optimal dimension dependence for higher moments has remained unclear. We resolve this question for every fixed integer $t\ge 2$: for any sufficiently small constant additive error, the optimal sample complexity is \[ Θ\!\left(d^{\frac{\lceil\log_2 t\rceil}{1+\lceil\log_2 t\rceil}}\right) \] with constant success probability. The complexity thus follows a dyadic hierarchy: for each integer $h\geq1$, all moment orders $2^{h-1}<t\leq2^h$ share the same exponent. In particular, the third and fourth moments both require $Θ(d^{2/3})$ copies. We also establish an $Ω(d^{1-1/t})$ lower bound for arbitrary nonadaptive single-copy protocols, matching the known upper bound and demonstrating an advantage from adaptivity for every $t\geq4$. Our upper bound uses adaptive state filtering to reduce higher-order moment estimation to lower-order moment estimation in a smaller state space. For the lower bound, we directly compare moment-matched ensembles through a smooth interpolation, bypassing the maximally mixed state as an intermediate hypothesis and yielding matching bounds beyond $\sqrt d$.

</details>


### [191] [Quantum Complexity of Ancilla-Free Unitary Embeddings for Nonlinear Dynamics via Generalized State-Dependent Double-Bracket Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37802)
*Yuki Ito,Hideaki Hakoshima,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将无辅助比特的双括号量子算法推广至态依赖哈密顿量情形，实现非线性动力学的全相干无辅助比特幺正嵌入，并将查询复杂度与动力学对初值的敏感性（收缩/非扩张/扩张）联系起来，给出相应上界，并在离散Gross-Pitaevskii方程上达到最优最坏查询复杂度Θ(e^{gT/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算模拟非线性动力学日益受关注，但由于幺正演化本质是线性的，此类模拟通常需要额外量子资源。核心问题在于：如何将非线性动力学嵌入到完全相干、无辅助比特的幺正电路中，以及动力学的复杂度如何决定所需的量子资源。

Method: 推广无辅助比特双括号量子算法用于虚时演化：将其态无关哈密顿量替换为态依赖的厄米算符。该框架递归调用初始态制备预言机及其逆，以完全相干、无辅助比特的幺正嵌入方式将目标解制备到任意给定精度。通过分析解之间距离的演化行为（收缩、非扩张、扩张）来刻画查询成本与非线性动力学复杂度（对初值的敏感性）的关系。

Result: 当解间距离至少指数收缩（收缩型）、不增长（非扩张型）、至多指数增长（扩张型）时，分别获得查询上界 exp(O(T))、exp(O(T²))、exp(exp(O(T)))，其中 T 为目标演化时间。对离散 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程，该无辅助比特双括号电路在给定单量子比特初态族上达到最优最坏查询复杂度 Θ(e^{gT/2})，g>0 为非线性强度。

Conclusion: 该工作将非线性动力学的复杂度与相干量子模拟的查询成本相联系，为设计具有最优查询复杂度的无辅助比特幺正嵌入奠定了基础。

Abstract: Simulating nonlinear dynamics with quantum computers has gained increasing attention. In general, such simulations require additional quantum resources because unitary quantum evolution is linear. A fundamental question is how nonlinear dynamics can be embedded into fully coherent, ancilla-free unitary circuits and how the complexity of the dynamics governs the required quantum resources. In this work, we generalize the ancilla-free double-bracket quantum algorithm for imaginary-time evolution by replacing its state-independent Hamiltonian with a state-dependent Hermitian operator. Our framework recursively calls an initial state preparation oracle and its inverse, and prepares the target solution to any prescribed accuracy using a fully coherent, ancilla-free unitary embedding. We relate the query cost to the complexity of the nonlinear dynamics, specifically their sensitivity to initial conditions. We obtain query upper bounds of $\exp(O(T))$, $\exp(O(T^2))$, and $\exp(\exp(O(T)))$ when the distance between solutions contracts at least exponentially (contractive), does not increase (nonexpansive), or grows at most exponentially (expansive), respectively, where $T$ is the target evolution time. For the discrete Gross--Pitaevskii equation, our ancilla-free double-bracket circuit achieves optimal worst-case query complexity $Θ(e^{gT/2})$ over a specified family of single-qubit initial states, where $g>0$ is the nonlinearity strength. These results connect the complexity of nonlinear dynamics to the query cost of coherent quantum simulation and provide a foundation for designing ancilla-free unitary embeddings with optimal query complexity.

</details>


### [192] [Measuring the spin of a spin-1/2 and getting a result of 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37822)
*Joseph McGowan,Nicholas Mantella,Noah Baker,Aephraim M. Steinberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了1988年Albert、Aharonov和Vaidman最初提出的弱Stern-Gerlach实验方案：将87Rb玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体置于磁场梯度中，通过低概率后选择，观测到约8 μm/s的弱磁动量踢被放大14倍的反常弱值，并证明增大动量踢会削弱测量的"弱性"、降低放大倍数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常弱值虽已在众多光学系统中被测量，但AAV最初提出的弱Stern-Gerlach实验方案本身从未被真正实现，本文旨在填补这一空白，以原始提出的方式完成该实验。

Method: 将87Rb玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体置于磁场梯度中，执行低概率的后选择过程，从而对微弱的磁动量踢（约8 μm/s）进行弱值测量并观察其放大效应；同时通过改变动量踢大小考察测量弱性与放大倍数之间的关系。

Result: 测得约8 μm/s的微弱磁动量踢被有效放大14倍；并发现增大动量踢会使测量的"弱性"下降，进而减小放大倍数。

Conclusion: 本文首次以AAV最初提出的形式实现了弱Stern-Gerlach实验，验证了反常弱值的放大机制及其受测量弱性制约的规律。

Abstract: The weak value of an operator is the average result of measuring that operator on a quantum system given specified initial and final states. The overlap between these states appears in the denominator of the weak value, and thus if that overlap is small, the weak value can be arbitrarily large. In 1988, Albert, Aharonov, and Vaidman proposed a weak Stern-Gerlach experiment which would be able to measure a so-called anomalous weak value, a measurement result which lies out- side of the eigenvalue spectrum of the operator being measured. Though anomalous weak values have been measured in numerous optical systems, here we present the first such realization of this experiment as originally proposed. By placing a Bose-Einstein condensate of 87Rb in a magnetic gradient and performing an unlikely postselection, we measure an effective magnification of a weak magnetic momentum kick (~ 8 $μ$m/s) by a factor of 14. We also demonstrate that increasing the momentum kick decreases the "weakness" of the measurement and reduces the amplification.

</details>


### [193] [Towards verifiable quantum advantage with random circuits: Observables that survive concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37890)
*Antonio A. Mele,Francesco A. Mele,Jarrod R. McClean,Thomas E. O'Brien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明随机局域电路中固定阶OTOC存在逆多项式级涨落，从而排除强集中作为OTOC可验证量子优势方案的障碍，并给出1D OTOC的经典模拟改进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有量子硬件上展示量子优势是量子计算的核心目标，随机量子电路是诸多领先方案的基础。但基于采样的演示难以验证，而基于可观测量的方案则面临集中效应可能抑制不同电路实例间差异的问题。近期实验提出以随机电路中OTOC估计作为可验证量子优势任务，但其电路间涨落是否随系统规模增大而被集中效应抹平，此前一直未决。

Method: 针对任意固定空间维度下广泛的局域随机电路，证明在系统尺度深度下固定阶OTOC具有逆多项式涨落；对一维Haar随机brickwork电路，进一步刻画涨落的门贡献结构；并据此构造经典算法。

Result: 证明了对任意固定空间维度的广泛局域随机电路，固定阶OTOC在系统尺度深度下存在逆多项式涨落；在一维Haar随机brickwork电路中，宏观多数量子门对涨落有贡献，但每一层中这些门被限制在亚线性宽度区域内；作为副产品，给出首个在1D中严格优于暴力经典模拟OTOC的经典算法。

Conclusion: 电路间涨落不会被集中效应消除，因此强集中不能作为OTOC类可验证量子优势方案的障碍；尽管经典计算硬度仍未解决，该结果为OTOC基础的可验证量子优势提案扫清了一个关键理论疑虑。

Abstract: Demonstrating quantum advantage on current quantum hardware is a central goal of quantum computing, and random quantum circuits underpin many leading proposals. Yet sampling-based demonstrations are often difficult to verify, while observable-based approaches face a different challenge: concentration can suppress differences between circuit instances. Recent experiments have put forward the estimation of out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) in random circuits as a promising task for verifiable quantum advantage, yet whether their circuit-to-circuit fluctuations survive concentration as system size grows has remained open. Here we show that they do. For broad classes of local random circuits in any fixed spatial dimension, we prove inverse-polynomial fluctuations of fixed-order OTOCs at system-scale depths. In one-dimensional Haar-random brickwork circuits, we further show that macroscopically many gates contribute to these fluctuations, yet in each layer they remain confined to a sublinear-width region. As a byproduct, we develop a classical algorithm exhibiting the first rigorous improvement over brute-force classical simulation of OTOCs in 1D. Although classical hardness remains open, our results rule out strong concentration as an obstruction to OTOC-based proposals for verifiable quantum advantage.

</details>


### [194] [A Neutral Atom-Based Hybrid Classical-Quantum Approach for the Entanglement Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37900)
*M. Yassine Naghmouchi,Quentin Ma,Agathe Blaise,René Veyland,Wesley Coelho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将保真度约束下的纠缠路由建模为不可拆分多商品流问题，并首次尝试用中性原子量子优化作为列生成中的定价预言机，在小规模基准上结合热启动与后处理取得低于1%的最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息网络中的端到端纠缠分发需在有限资源与保真度约束下进行路由。核心问题是：在最大化被接纳请求数量的目标下，如何求解保真度约束的不可拆分多商品流这一NP难问题，并探索量子优化能否在其中提供有效的路径生成能力。

Method: 提出经典—量子混合的列生成框架：经典受限主问题负责选择路由，定价问题负责生成满足保真度的路径。将NP难的约束最短路径定价问题形式化为QUBO，并采用硬件感知的寄存器嵌入与实例驱动的脉冲整形，在中性原子处理器仿真器上求解；再结合热启动与后处理。

Result: 在小规模基准实例上，该方法在所有测试规模下最优性差距均低于1%；而所选取的模拟退火路径生成基线差距最高达6%。

Conclusion: 生成的比特串可为列生成中的经典精化提供有用的候选路由。但该工作仅为概念验证，尚不能证明量子优势或可扩展性；仍需在更大规模实例、更强经典基线与真实量子硬件上进一步评估。

Abstract: Efficient end-to-end entanglement distribution in quantum information networks requires routing under limited resources and fidelity constraints. We study entanglement routing as a fidelity-constrained unsplittable multicommodity flow problem that maximizes the number of admitted requests. As a proof of concept, we integrate neutral-atom quantum optimization into a hybrid classical--quantum column-generation framework. A classical restricted master problem selects routes, while a pricing problem generates fidelity-feasible paths. We formulate this NP-hard constrained shortest-path pricing problem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and address it with hardware-aware register embedding and instance-driven pulse shaping. To our knowledge, this is the first study of neutral-atom quantum optimization as a pricing oracle for fidelity-constrained entanglement routing. On small benchmark instances evaluated with neutral-atom processor emulators, the method, combined with warm-start and post-processing, achieves an optimality gap below 1% across all tested sizes; the selected simulated-annealing route-generation baseline exhibits gaps of up to 6%. These results indicate that the generated bitstrings can provide useful candidate routes for classical refinement within column generation. This proof of concept does not establish quantum utility or scalability; further evaluation on larger instances, against stronger classical baselines, and on quantum hardware is needed.

</details>


### [195] [Optimal Quantum Junta Testing without Inverse Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37926)
*Jinge Bao,Minbo Gao,Penghui Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用测试量子junta与经典支撑集大小测试之间的强关联，分别给出了酉操作和信道的紧确查询复杂度，并揭示了正向查询与逆向访问之间的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子junta测试的查询复杂度：给定未知的n量子比特酉操作或量子信道，判断其是否至多非平凡作用于k个量子比特，或与所有此类操作都相差ε以上。此问题在酉操作与信道两个模型上此前尚不完整，尤其信道的复杂度存在较大间隙，因此需要给出紧确界并刻画其本质。

Method: 对于酉操作采用forward-only模型，测试器仅能访问U而无法访问U†或受控U；上界通过将量子junta测试归约到随机子集分布上的广义支撑集大小问题，并设计新的改进样本复杂度的测试器实现；下界则反向归约，通过phase-masked困难分布将经典支撑集大小测试归约到量子junta测试，并证明forward量子查询平均意义上可精确经典化。

Result: 当k足够大、n≥2k且ε范围较广时，酉操作与信道junta测试的查询复杂度均为Θ(k/(ε²log k))。对于信道，在常数ε下由此得到了最优的Θ(k/log k)查询复杂度，弥合了Bao和Yao之前Õ(k)上界与Ω̃(√k)下界之间的间隙。此外，在常数ε下，结果还给出了Θ(k/log k)次forward查询与Õ(√k)次含逆访问查询之间的分离。

Conclusion: 量子junta测试的经典核心是经典支撑集大小测试：上界与下界均通过两者间的双向归约获得，从而得到匹配的上下界。该归约框架不仅统一了酉操作与信道的情形，还量化了逆访问对查询复杂度的收益。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of testing quantum juntas. Given an unknown $n$-qubit unitary or quantum channel, the goal is to decide whether it acts nontrivially on at most $k$ qubits or is $\varepsilon$-far from every such operation. For unitaries, we consider the forward-only model, where the tester has access to $U$, but not to $U^\dagger$ or controlled-$U$. We give tight bounds for both unitary and channel junta testing. For sufficiently large $k$, $n \ge 2k$, and a broad range of $\varepsilon$, both problems have query complexity $Θ\left(k/(\varepsilon^2\log k)\right)$. Our upper bounds are achieved by simple nonadaptive testers using product inputs and single-qubit measurements. For junta channels, this closes the gap by Bao and Yao (COLT 23, TPAMI 2025) between an $\widetilde{O}(k)$ upper bound and a $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{k})$ lower bound at constant $\varepsilon$, establishing the optimal $Θ(k/\log k)$ query complexity. At the heart of both bounds is a strong connection between quantum junta testing and classical support-size testing. For the upper bounds, we reduce quantum junta testing to a generalized support-size problem for distributions over random subsets, and develop a new tester with improved sample complexity. For the lower bounds, we proceed in the reverse direction, reducing classical support-size testing to quantum junta testing through a phase-masked hard ensemble and showing that forward quantum queries can be exactly classicalized on average. Together, these reductions identify support-size testing as the classical core of quantum junta testing and yield matching lower bounds. At constant $\varepsilon$, they also give a separation between $Θ(k/\log k)$ forward queries and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{k})$ queries with inverse access.

</details>


### [196] [Rapid Mixing of Parallel Kac's Walk: From Spheres to Stiefel Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37999)
*Qian Chen,Minglong Qin,Fang Song,Penghui Yao,Mingnan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究并行 Kac 游走在 $k$ 个正交归一的量子态上的混合性质，证明其能以 $O((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon))$ 步在 Wasserstein 与全变差距离下逼近共同 Haar 随机酉作用的结果，从而将单态扩散性质推广到多态，并给出复 Stiefel 流形 $V_{d,k}$ 上的快速混合界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kac 游走是经典局部随机游走，其并行版本已被用于构造伪随机态扰乱器乃至伪随机酉。此前工作只证明了并行 Kac 游走对单个量子态具扩散性，本文旨在探究它同时作用于多个正交归一量子态时的行为，即能否将单态结果推广到多态，并刻画复 Stiefel 流形上的混合速度。

Method: 分析并行 Kac 游走同时作用于 $k$ 个正交归一量子态的联合分布；将有序的 $k$ 个正交归一态视为复 Stiefel 流形 $V_{d,k}=\{X\in\mathbb C^{d\times k}:X^\dagger X=I_k\}$ 上的点，在该流形上建立游走的混合分析，分别用 Wasserstein 距离和全变差距离度量其与共同 Haar 随机酉作用结果的接近程度。

Result: 对任意 $1\leq k<d$，经过 $O((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon))$ 步后，$k$ 个输出态的联合分布与对相同输入施加共同 Haar 随机酉所得分布 $\varepsilon$-接近，且在 Wasserstein 与全变差两种距离下均成立。等价地，并行 Kac 游走在 $V_{d,k}$ 上快速混合，两种混合时间均被 $O((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon))$ 界定。

Conclusion: 本文将并行 Kac 游走的扩散性质从单个量子态推广到多个正交归一量子态，并证明其在复 Stiefel 流形上快速混合；这推广了 Pillai、Smith、Vaikuntanathan 关于实 Stiefel 流形上标准 Kac 游走的 Wasserstein 混合结果。

Abstract: Kac's walk is a classical local random walk whose action on a single real unit vector in dimension $d$ mixes in total variation in $Θ(d\log d)$ sequential steps~\cite{PS17}. Lu, Qin, Song, Yao, and Zhao introduced a parallel version of Kac's walk that mixes a single quantum state in $O(\log d)$ rounds~\cite{LQSY+26}. After discretizing the randomness and replacing it by suitable pseudorandom primitives, this parallel walk gives rise to pseudorandom state scramblers, and was subsequently shown to yield pseudorandom unitaries~\cite{LQSY+25}.
  We study what happens when the parallel Kac's walk acts simultaneously on $k$ orthonormal quantum states. We prove that, for any $1\leq k < d$, after $O\!\left((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon)\right)$ steps, the joint distribution of the $k$ output states is $\varepsilon$-close, in both Wasserstein and total variation distance, to that obtained by applying a common Haar-random unitary to the same inputs. This generalizes the dispersing property of the parallel Kac's walk from a single quantum state to multiple orthonormal quantum states. Equivalently, viewing an ordered collection of $k$ orthonormal states as a point on the complex Stiefel manifold $V_{d,k}=\{X\in\mathbb C^{d\times k}:X^\dagger X=I_k\}$, we show that the parallel Kac's walk mixes rapidly on $V_{d,k}$, with both Wasserstein and total variation mixing times bounded by $O\!\left((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon)\right)$. This extends the Wasserstein mixing result of Pillai, Smith, and Vaikuntanathan for the standard Kac's walk on real Stiefel manifolds~\cite{PSV26}.

</details>


### [197] [Density Matrices of Pulsed Quantum Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38009)
*Joscelyn van der Veen,Daniel F. V. James*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 脉冲光的密度矩阵在考虑有限探测时间后给出等价测量结果，从而揭示量子化腔长度的物理意义并解释脉冲光物理谱的起源；通过推广脉冲高斯态密度矩阵，可在同一基下描述不同统计的脉冲光并关联光计数率与脉冲谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲光常以密度矩阵描述，其关联函数在平稳性与相干性上具有不同性质，缺乏统一框架；同时量子化腔长度与脉冲光物理谱的物理含义尚不清晰。

Method: 以密度矩阵刻画脉冲光，考察不同平稳性与相干性关联函数的密度矩阵，并计入有限探测时间的影响；将密度矩阵推广至脉冲高斯态，在同一基底下描述不同统计特性的脉冲光。

Result: 不同平稳性与相干性关联函数对应的密度矩阵在计入有限探测时间后给出等价的测量结果；据此赋予量子化腔长度物理意义，并解释脉冲光物理谱的来源；推广的脉冲高斯态密度矩阵可统一描述不同统计的脉冲光，并建立光计数率与脉冲谱之间的关系。

Conclusion: 有限探测时间是统一不同脉冲光密度矩阵描述的关键因素；该框架明确了量子化腔长度的物理意义与脉冲光物理谱的起源，并为在同一基下处理不同统计脉冲光、关联光计数率与脉冲谱提供了通用方法。

Abstract: Pulsed light is represented by density matrices whose correlation functions have different properties of stationarity and coherence. We show that these density matrices yield equivalent measurement results after accounting for finite detection times. This in turn reveals a physical meaning to the length of a quantization cavity and explains the origin of the physical spectrum for pulsed light. By generalizing the density matrices for pulsed Gaussian states of light, we can describe pulsed light of varying statistics in the same basis and relate the photocount rate to the pulse spectrum.

</details>


### [198] [Universal quantum coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38038)
*Jacopo Rizzo,Ludovico Lami,Jens Eisert,Lorenzo Leone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用Schur-Weyl对偶建立了一个通用的量子纠错原理，无需预先知道量子态或信道的具体信息，实现了无需层析的最优通用纠缠蒸馏与量子通信协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的最优纠缠蒸馏与噪声信道上的可靠量子通信方案通常依赖于对底层量子态或信道的先验知识（即需要先进行量子态层析）。这种依赖性限制了协议的实际可行性与通用性，因此需要一种无需先验知识、可通用的最优编码方案。

Method: 通过Schur-Weyl对偶，对未知双方混合态的多个相同副本进行局域Schur-Weyl分解，证明由此产生的不可约置换空间构成最大纠缠量子码空间，其错误结构完全由表示论决定。纠正这些对称性分辨的错误，从而构造通用协议，并引入与Petz-Rényi条件熵相关的Schur-Weyl信息谱来刻画有限块长下的可达速率。

Result: 所得协议在局域酉变换下不变，能精确恢复两个输入边缘态，达到相干信息速率，误差指数衰减并具有最优二阶修正。样本复杂度仅取决于相关谱秩而非Hilbert空间维数。对于全局秩为O(poly n)的n量子比特态，Petz-Rényi条件熵承诺即可实现最优且样本高效的纠缠蒸馏。

Conclusion: Schur-Weyl对偶被确立为最优、免层析通用量子编码的基本且普适机制，将纠缠蒸馏、量子纠错与量子通信统一于表示论框架之下。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation from noisy bipartite states and reliable quantum communication over noisy channels are fundamental tasks in quantum information theory, yet optimal coding schemes typically rely on prior knowledge of the underlying state or channel. Here, through Schur-Weyl duality, we establish a universal quantum error-correction principle that removes this dependence entirely. We show that the irreducible permutation spaces arising from the local Schur-Weyl decomposition of many identical copies of an unknown bipartite mixed state form maximally entangled quantum code spaces, with an error structure determined entirely by representation theory. Correcting these symmetry-resolved errors yields universal protocols for entanglement distillation and quantum communication that achieve coherent-information rates with exponentially vanishing error and optimal second-order correction. The protocols are invariant under local unitaries and allow both input marginals to be recovered exactly. At finite block length, their achievable rates are governed by an associated Schur-Weyl information spectrum, which we relate to Petz-Rényi conditional entropies of the underlying state. The resulting sample complexity depends only on the relevant spectral ranks, rather than on the respective Hilbert space dimensions. In particular, for $n$-qubit states of $O(\operatorname{poly} n)$ global rank, we show that a Petz-Rényi conditional-entropy promise suffices for optimal sample-efficient entanglement distillation. Altogether, these results identify Schur-Weyl duality as a fundamental and general mechanism for optimal, tomography-free universal quantum coding, placing entanglement distillation, quantum error correction and quantum communication within a unified representation-theoretic framework.

</details>


### [199] [A Tale of Two Walks: Kipnis, Marchioro and Presutti Meet Kac in a Quantum World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38044)
*Qian Chen,Jingcheng Liu,Minglong Qin,Leonard Schulman,Fang Song,Penghui Yao,Mingnan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了并行Kac游走与KMP过程之间的意外联系，证明对称子空间上的twirling信道可由分区上的经典马氏链编码，并借助KMP混合分析得到对数级Haar twirling所需重复次数，同时给出KMP混合时间的Aldous猜想类比及坐标hit-and-run的改进界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子信息中对称子空间上的Haar twirling近似问题，以及经典多粒子KMP过程的混合时间。核心动因是：twirling信道的分析通常困难，而KMP多粒子过程的混合时间对粒子数的均匀界尚不完善，需要通过新的结构性质建立二者联系以获得更优界。

Method: 1) 建立并行Kac游走在对称子空间上的twirling信道与分区上经典马氏链的精确对应，并提升为完全图上的并行KMP过程；2) 对一般图上的标准KMP过程，证明混合时间类比Aldous猜想；3) 提出核心技术贡献——并行与标准KMP过程的"条件乘积结构"：在适当辅助随机性条件下标记粒子独立演化；4) 结合精确耦合得到对粒子数均匀的混合界。

Result: 1) 证明O(log d + log(1/ε))次重复即可以误差ε近似(ℂ^d)^{⊗t}对称子空间上的Haar twirling，且对副本数t一致；2) 对一般图上的标准KMP过程，固定精度下t粒子混合时间至多为单粒子混合时间的常数倍乘以顶点数对数，对t一致；3) 将n维标准单纯形上坐标hit-and-run的总变差混合时间界从Õ(n^3)改进到Õ(n)，并消除对初始分布的依赖。所得界在粒子数上均匀，且在对数因子内是紧的。

Conclusion: 并行Kac游走与KMP过程之间存在深刻联系，使twirling信道分析可归约为KMP混合问题；条件乘积结构加精确耦合是获得粒子数均匀混合界的关键工具，这些界在并行Kac twirling信道上蕴含快速收敛，并改进了坐标hit-and-run的混合时间。

Abstract: We reveal an unexpected connection between the parallel Kac's walk and the Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti (KMP) process. The twirling channel induced by the parallel Kac's walk on the symmetric subspace is exactly encoded by a classical Markov chain on partitions, which lifts to a parallel KMP process on complete graphs. This correspondence reduces the analysis of the twirling channel to the mixing of the parallel KMP process. We prove that $O(\log d+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ repetitions suffice to approximate Haar twirling on the symmetric subspace of $(\mathbb C^d)^{\otimes t}$ to error $\varepsilon$, uniformly in the number of copies $t$.
  For the standard KMP process on general graphs, we prove a mixing-time analogue of Aldous's conjecture: at fixed accuracy, the mixing time of the $t$-particle process is at most a constant times the single-particle mixing time multiplied by the logarithm of the number of vertices, uniformly in $t$. As an application, we improve the total variation mixing-time bound for coordinate hit-and-run on the $n$-dimensional standard simplex from $\widetilde O(n^3)$ (Kook and Vempala, 2026) to $\widetilde O(n)$, while removing the dependence on the initial distribution.
  Our main technical contribution is conditional product structure for both parallel and standard KMP processes. Conditioned on suitable auxiliary randomness, the labeled particles evolve independently. Combining this structure with an exact coupling yields mixing bounds uniform in the number of particles for both unlabeled KMP models. These bounds are sharp up to logarithmic factors and imply rapid convergence of the parallel Kac twirling channel on the symmetric subspace.

</details>


### [200] [Classical Verification of Quantum Computation with Quasilinear Resources, from Compiled Nonlocal Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38060)
*Finn Holler,Anand Natarajan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于LWE假设与计算自测试框架，构造了首个总资源在电路模型中为准线性的BQP委托论证系统，参数为O(poly(λ, log g)·g)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单证明者、计算有界的设定下，如何高效（准线性资源）验证量子计算（BQP）问题，是量子委托计算的核心挑战。

Method: 构造新的计算自测试以认证证明者的量子态，实现单量子比特Clifford观测量张量积态的可验证随机远程态制备，并采用Kalai等（STOC 2023）的编译器将非局域博弈转为单证明者论证系统，对Coladangelo等（ToC 2024）自测试的修改版本进行编译。

Result: 在LWE假设下，委托含g个门的电路所需总资源为O(poly(λ, log g)·g)，且自测试具备常数鲁棒性（验证误差与制备量子比特数无关）。

Conclusion: 成功将多证明者设定中的结果复现于单证明者设定，建立了首个准线性资源的BQP论证系统。

Abstract: Computational self-testing gives a classical verifier command over the quantum register of a single computationally bounded prover. We use this framework to construct the first argument system for BQP with quasilinear total resource requirements in the circuit model. Our argument system is based on the learning with errors (LWE) assumption and requires total resources of $O(\mathrm{poly}(λ, \log g)\cdot g)$ for delegating a circuit with $g$ gates, where $λ$ is the LWE security parameter. This is achieved by constructing a new computational self-test for certifying the prover's quantum state and using it to dequantize the efficient verification protocol of Broadbent (ToC 2018). Specifically, this self-test enables the verifiable, random remote state preparation of tensor product states of the single-qubit Clifford observables $σ_X, σ_Y, σ_Z, (σ_Y-σ_X)/\sqrt{2}$ and $(σ_Y+σ_X)/\sqrt{2}$, with constant robustness: the verification error is independent of the number of prepared qubits. This approach was first proposed by Coladangelo et al. (ToC 2024) in the multi-prover setting. We replicate their result in the single-prover setting by applying the compiler proposed by Kalai et al. (STOC 2023)---which turns any nonlocal game into a single-prover argument system---to a modified version of their self-test.

</details>


### [201] [Optimal Quantum-Classical Separations for Exact Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38073)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Amin Shiraz Gilani,Nikhil S. Mande*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究成员查询下的精确学习，构造反例推翻经典-量子查询复杂度猜想 $\mathsf{R}=O(\mathsf{Q}^2+\mathsf{Q}\log N)$，并给出随机与确定性查询复杂度相对量子的三次多项式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确学习模型中确定性、随机化与量子查询复杂度的最优关系尚未被判定。已有两个典型量子加速（Grover 搜索、Bernstein-Vazirani）引出长期猜想 $\mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=O(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^2+\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)\log N)$，需要检验该猜想是否正确，并刻画经典与量子查询复杂度之间的最优关系。

Method: 在成员查询精确学习框架下，构造概念类 $\mathcal C$ 与 $\mathcal C'$，通过给出相应的随机化与确定性查询复杂度下界，与 Arunachalam 等（Quantum'21）的随机上界以及 Servedio-Gortler（SICOMP'04）的确定性上界进行对比，从而建立反例。

Result: 构造出满足 $\mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=\Omega\!\left(\frac{\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^3\log N}{\log \mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)}\right)$ 及 $\mathsf{D}(\mathcal C')=\Omega(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C')^3\log N)$ 的概念类。第一个下界与 Arunachalam 等的上界仅在常数因子内匹配；第二个下界与 Servedio-Gortler 的上界匹配。

Conclusion: 成功推翻原有猜想，表明 Arunachalam 等随机化上界中的加速本质上依赖随机性（对确定性算法不成立）。这些结果刻画了经典与量子查询复杂度之间的最优关系，并首次显示学习中的量子加速可以超越 Grover 与 Bernstein-Vazirani 两种范式。

Abstract: We study exact learning with membership queries for concept classes $\mathcal C\subseteq\{0,1\}^N$, focusing on the relationships among their deterministic, randomized, and quantum query complexities, denoted $\mathsf{D}(\mathcal C)$, $\mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)$, and $\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)$, respectively. The two canonical quantum speedups in this model are witnessed by Grover search and Bernstein-Vazirani, leading to the longstanding conjecture $$ \mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=O(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^2+\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)\log N). $$ We first refute this conjecture by constructing concept classes $\mathcal C$ and $\mathcal C'$ satisfying \[ \mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=Ω\!\left(\frac{\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^3\log N}{\log \mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)}\right) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \mathsf{D}(\mathcal C')=Ω(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C')^3\log N). \] The first bound matches the upper bound of Arunachalam et al.~[Quantum'21] up to constant factors, while the second matches the upper bound of Servedio and Gortler~[SICOMP'04]. In particular, this shows that the saving in the randomized upper bound of Arunachalam et al. fundamentally relies on randomness. Apart from characterizing the optimal relationship between classical and quantum query complexity, our results are the first to show that quantum speedups for learning can go beyond the Grover and Bernstein-Vazirani paradigms.

</details>


### [202] [Ramanujan quantum expanders from the Weil representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38075)
*Siddhartha Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对每个奇素数幂 $q$，取 $D=q+1$，构造了一族无限的 $D$ 度 Ramanujan 量子扩张器。方法是将 Morgenstern 在 $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$（$p$ 为 $q$ 的偶次幂）上的 Ramanujan Cayley 图，通过 $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ 的 Weil 表示的奇不可约子表示进行迁移。实现上，维度为 $N$ 的量子扩张器使用 $O(\log^2 N)$ 个基本门和 $O(\log N)$ 个辅助 qudit，门集为依赖 $q$ 的固定有限集。与 Iyer、Jain、Jordan 和 Somma（FOCS 2026）的先前工作相比，其优势在于：在量子电路被精确实现的假设下，可以精确满足 $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ 奇异值界，而无需任何加性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造高效、可显式实现的 Ramanujan 量子扩张器是量子信息与理论计算机科学中的重要问题，因为量子扩张器在量子纠错、量子密码和去随机化等领域有广泛应用。已有工作（Iyer 等，FOCS 2026）虽然给出了量子扩张器的实现，但仅在近似意义下满足 Ramanujan 界，存在加性误差。本文旨在消除该误差，给出精确满足 $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ 奇异值界的量子扩张器构造。

Method: 作者利用 Morgenstern 在 $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ 上构造的 Ramanujan Cayley 图（度数为 $D=q+1$，其中 $p$ 是 $q$ 的偶次幂）。通过 $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ 的 Weil 表示的奇不可约子表示，将这些经典图“迁移”到量子设置，从而得到量子扩张器。对于维度为 $N$ 的量子扩张器，使用 $O(\log^2 N)$ 个基本门与 $O(\log N)$ 个辅助 qudit 进行实现，基本门集为依赖 $q$ 的固定有限集合。

Result: 对每个奇素数幂 $q$，当 $D=q+1$ 时，成功构造出一族无限的 $D$ 度 Ramanujan 量子扩张器。维数为 $N$ 的实现仅需 $O(\log^2 N)$ 个基本门和 $O(\log N)$ 个辅助 qudit，且使用依赖 $q$ 的固定有限门集。在电路精确实现的假设下，所构造的量子扩张器精确满足 $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ 的奇异值界，不引入任何加性误差。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 Ramanujan 量子扩张器的一种显式、高效构造，其关键优势是在精确电路实现下可精确达到 $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ 奇异值界，克服了 Iyer 等（FOCS 2026）先前工作中的加性误差问题。该结果在门复杂度与辅助比特数上保持了对数级别的开销，为量子扩张器的精确实现提供了新途径。

Abstract: For every odd prime power $q$ and $D=q+1$, we construct an infinite family of Ramanujan quantum expanders of degree $D$. The construction transfers Morgenstern's Ramanujan Cayley graphs on $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{p})$ for $p$ which is an even power of $q$, through the odd irreducible subrepresentation of the Weil representation of $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{p})$. For a quantum expander of dimension $N$, our implementation uses $O(\log^2 N)$ elementary gates, and $O(\log N)$ ancilla qudits, using a fixed finite gate set depending on $q$. An advantage compared to the previous work of Iyer, Jain, Jordan, and Somma (FOCS 2026) is that assuming the quantum circuit is implemented exactly, we satisfy the $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ singular value bound exactly without any additive error.

</details>


### [203] [A commuting operator self-test for exact entanglement embezzlement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38083)
*Connor Paddock,Simon Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了基于纠缠挪用（embezzlement）的 Coladangelo 双人广义非局域博弈变体，其最优交换算子关联是一个交换算子自检验，这是交换算子框架下首个精确纠缠挪用协议的关联自检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交换算子框架下寻找精确纠缠挪用协议的自检验实例，并揭示量子张量积模型与交换算子模型之间的本质差异（即使局部空间无限维）。

Method: 构造 Coladangelo 双人广义非局域博弈的变体；利用 Harris 关于精确纠缠挪用算子的唯一抽象态刻画（涉及 Cuntz 代数）作为主要证明技术，分析最优交换算子关联。

Result: 该变体博弈的最优交换算子关联是唯一的，并构成交换算子自检验；由于唯一最优关联要求精确纠缠挪用，该关联在量子张量积模型下不可实现，即使允许局部空间无限维。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了交换算子自检验的一个例子：两方共享的 Hilbert 空间无法分解为与局部测量算子兼容的局部张量积；同时也首次实现了交换算子框架下精确纠缠挪用协议的关联自检验。

Abstract: We show that the optimal commuting operator correlation for a variant of Coladangelo's two-player generalized nonlocal game, based on the embezzlement of entanglement, is a commuting operator self-test. Our result gives the first correlation self-test for an exact entanglement embezzlement protocol in the commuting operator framework. Because the unique optimal correlation necessitates the exact embezzlement of entanglement, the correlation is unrealizable using quantum tensor-product models, even if the local spaces are allowed to be infinite dimensional. As such, our result provides an example of a commuting operator self-test where the Hilbert space shared by the two parties cannot be factored into a tensor-product of local spaces that is compatible with the local measurement operators. Our main proof technique relies on Harris' unique abstract state characterization of exact embezzlement of entanglement involving Cuntz algebras.

</details>


### [204] [Learning quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38085)
*Isaac Holt,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典的隐藏子群问题（HSP）推广到量子对象，提出并求解了非阿贝尔StateHSP、Anyonic态对称学习等问题，并给出高效量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典HSP（如Shor算法）擅长求经典对象的对称性，但量子对象的对称性学习尚缺乏系统理论。StateHSP作为HSP的量子推广，此前主要局限于阿贝尔群；同时StateHSP学习的是Bose对称性，而物理上纯态仅定义到全局相位，需要更自然的对称性刻画。

Method: 1）研究非阿贝尔StateHSP：在隐藏子群为正规子群、且背景群属于一大类非阿贝尔群时给出高效量子算法，将既有一般理论推广到非阿贝尔情形；2）提出基于投影表示的Anyonic态对称学习问题，通过归约到StateHSP求解，归约依赖于投影表示线性化与线性纠错码之间的新联系；3）将酉算子、哈密顿量及有限态集合等量子对象的对称学习问题归约到StateHSP。

Result: 获得了非阿贝尔StateHSP在正规子群情形及广泛非阿贝尔群类上的高效量子算法；给出Anyonic态对称学习的有效量子算法，并作为应用改进了适用于任意局域维度混合qudit态的稳定子群学习算法；同时对酉算子、哈密顿量和有限态集合的对称学习也给出了高效量子算法。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子对称学习理论从阿贝尔、Bose设定扩展到非阿贝尔群和更物理的Anyonic对称性，建立了投影表示线性化与纠错码的联系，并为多类量子对象提供了统一的归约框架与高效量子算法。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are powerful tools for finding symmetries of classical objects, most famously through Shor's algorithm and the framework of the Hidden Subgroup Problem (HSP). In this work, we study quantum algorithms for learning symmetries of quantum objects. Our starting point is the recently introduced State Hidden Subgroup Problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalisation of HSP in which the task is to learn the symmetry subgroup of a quantum state. We obtain positive results for non-abelian StateHSP, giving efficient quantum algorithms whenever the hidden subgroup is normal and for ambient groups belonging to a broad class of non-abelian groups, extending the previous general theory beyond the abelian setting.
  StateHSP learns \emph{Bose} symmetries, associated with linear representations, whereas physically equivalent pure states are defined only up to global phase. Motivated by this, we introduce a new \emph{Anyonic} state symmetry learning problem based on a more physically natural notion of symmetry than that considered by StateHSP. We give an efficient quantum algorithm by reducing the problem to StateHSP, where the reduction relies on a new connection between linearisations of projective representations and linear error-correcting codes. As an application, we obtain an improved algorithm for learning stabiliser groups that applies to mixed qudit states of arbitrary local dimension. Finally, we introduce symmetry learning problems for other quantum objects, including unitaries, Hamiltonians, and finite collections of states, and obtain efficient quantum algorithms by reducing them to instances of StateHSP.

</details>


### [205] [Optimal Ground-State Preparation with a Guiding State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38091)
*Stacey Jeffery,Rolando D. Somma,Freek Witteveen,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出量子基态制备的最优上界：在能量误差δ、谱隙至少3δ、引导态与基态重叠至少γ的条件下，可用O(log(1/ε)/γδ)次U=e^{iH}与A及其逆的调用获得基态的ε近似，且给出两种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作是作者此前两篇基态能量估计论文[JW26, SdW26]的后续，旨在将能量估计的结果扩展到量子基态制备问题，并证明其调用复杂度的最优性。

Method: 设定：哈密顿量H具有唯一基态|ψ_0⟩，本征值E_0，已知估计满足|Ẽ_0−E_0|≤δ，且E_0与其余本征值间隙至少3δ；存在酉A制备引导态A|0⟩，与|ψ_0⟩重叠至少γ。基于此给出两种算法：一是交织振幅放大与误差缩减（仿[HMdW03]风格）；二是利用transducer的复合。

Result: 两种算法均以O(log(1/ε)/γδ)次U=e^{iH}、A及其逆的调用，输出|ψ_0⟩的ε近似。结合作者先前基态能量估计工作，联合结果表明基态制备具有最优的O(log(1/ε)/γδ)上界。

Conclusion: 基态制备问题的调用复杂度上界为O(log(1/ε)/γδ)，且该上界为最优；两种不同技术路线（振幅放大+误差缩减、transducer复合）均达到该复杂度。

Abstract: Suppose a Hamiltonian $H$ has a unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$ with an eigenvalue $E_0$, and we have an estimate $\tilde{E}_0$ such that $|\tilde{E}_0-E_0|\leqδ$, and there is a gap of at least $3δ$ between $E_0$ and all other eigenvalues. Suppose we have a unitary $A$ available that can produce a "guiding state'" $A|0\rangle$ that has overlap at least $γ$ with $|ψ_0\rangle$. We show how to obtain an $\varepsilon$-approximation of $|ψ_0\rangle$ using $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ applications of $U=e^{iH}$ and $A$, and their inverses. We give two different algorithms, one based on interleaving amplitude amplification and error-reduction in the style of [HMdW03], and one using the composition of transducers. This paper is the state-preparation follow-up to our two recent ground-state-energy estimation papers [JW26, SdW26]. Combined, our results show an optimal $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ upper bound for ground state preparation.

</details>


### [206] [Resource Compatibility in Optimal Quantum Symmetry Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38134)
*Yimeng Cao,Ranyiliu Chen,Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优对称性检验是否必须使用与对称性自然关联的量子资源，通过对并行子群-对Haar检验推导表示论意义下的定量逆定理，给出以第一类错误为零为前提的第二类错误下界，并针对对角环面、正交子群与qutrit Clifford群刻画了资源需求随子群和查询次数的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答一个基础性问题：最优对称性检验能否在不使用与该对称性自然对应的量子资源（如相干性等）的情况下实现。作者希望把最优检验的资源需求与最优系统边缘态的结构约束联系起来，并区分“无资源最优性不可能”与“仅靠集合重叠不足以证明可达性”这两类情形。

Method: 针对并行子群-对Haar检验，作者为所有第一类错误为零的协议推导了一个定量的表示论逆定理：第二类错误的超额被两个非负罚项之和从下方界定——一是落在次优子群类型上的权重罚，二是同一类型内Schur谱偏离该类型极值谱的罚。在最优时两罚项均消失，从而对每个最优系统边缘态给出支撑与权重锁定；由此将锁定集与自由边缘态集的不相交性用于排除无资源最优性。随后作者分别分析维数d≥2的对角环面、维数d≥2的正交子群以及qutrit Clifford群，计算精确最优第二类错误与所需最小系统边缘态相干性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 表示论逆定理给出第二类错误超额的双罚项下界及最优时的支撑与权重锁定；(2) 锁定集与自由边缘态集不相交可排除无资源最优性，但仅重叠不能保证可达性；(3) 对d≥2的对角环面，无相干最优检验仅能在单次查询时实现，作者确定了所有非相干系统边缘态协议下的精确最优第二类错误，以及达到无约束最优所需的最小系统边缘态相干性；(4) 对d≥2的正交子群，存在实协议在任意查询次数下均达到最优；(5) 对qutrit Clifford群，Wigner正的最优协议在三次和四次查询时存在，但在五至九次查询时不存在，九次以上是否存在的问题仍开放。

Conclusion: 总体而言，这些结果将最优对称性检验中依赖于子群和查询次数的资源需求，与最优系统边缘态的结构约束联系起来，表明无资源最优性受表示论下界与锁定结构限制，并揭示了不同对称群在资源需求上的显著差异。

Abstract: Can a symmetry be tested optimally without using the quantum resource naturally associated with it? For parallel subgroup-versus-Haar testing, we derive a quantitative representation-theoretic converse for every protocol with zero type-I error: the excess type-II error is bounded below by the sum of two nonnegative penalties, one for weight on suboptimal subgroup types and one for deviations of within-type Schur profiles from their typewise extremal profiles. At optimality, both penalties vanish, yielding support and weight locking for every optimal system marginal. Disjointness of the resulting locked set and the free marginal set rules out resource-free optimality, whereas overlap alone does not establish attainability. For the diagonal torus in dimension $d\geq 2$, optimal testing without coherence is possible only with a single query. We determine both the exact optimal type-II error over all protocols with incoherent system marginals and the minimum system-marginal coherence required to attain the unrestricted optimum. For the orthogonal subgroup in dimension $d\geq 2$, a real protocol attains the optimum at every query number. For the qutrit Clifford group, Wigner-positive optimal protocols exist at three and four queries but not from five through nine; whether they exist beyond nine queries remains open. Together, these results relate the subgroup- and query-dependent resource requirements of optimal symmetry testing to structural constraints on optimal system marginals.

</details>


### [207] [The exact strong converse exponent for private communication over quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38144)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Christoph Hirche,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限维量子窃听信道下密钥传输与生成的精确强逆指数（以平方保真度联合度量可靠性）并引入正则化Rényi私密信息刻画该指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子窃听信道的私密容量已知，但速率高于私密容量时误差与泄露的指数衰减行为（强逆指数）尚未有精确刻画。

Method: 采用对理想密钥的平方保真度联合度量可靠性与保密性，定义新的Rényi私密信息，并以其正则化量刻画强逆指数；同时给出基于普通私密信息的精确积分表示与一致连续性界。

Result: 得到强逆指数的精确表达式；正则化Rényi私密信息随Rényi阶趋于1时收敛到私密容量，并给出一致连续性界。

Conclusion: 对任意量子窃听信道，在任意高于私密容量的速率下，保真度呈指数衰减，从而完整刻画了强逆行为。

Abstract: We determine the exact strong converse exponent for secret-key transmission and generation over general finite-dimensional quantum wiretap channels, measuring reliability and secrecy jointly by squared fidelity to an ideal secret key. We introduce a novel Renyi private information whose regularization characterizes this exponent. An exact integral representation in terms of ordinary private information gives a uniform continuity bound, showing that the regularized quantity converges to private capacity as the Renyi order approaches one. This establishes for any wiretap channel an exponential fidelity decay at every rate above private capacity.

</details>


### [208] [Query-optimal unitary channel tomography in diamond distance with parallel access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38145)
*Entong He,Zihao Li,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新协议，用 \mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon) 次并行查询即可在菱形距离下学习 d 维未知酉信道，匹配已知下界，从而弥合了并行与顺序策略在酉信道层析中的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子过程层析中，"能否用严格并行的查询、达到与顺序查询同等效率地学习酉信道（菱形距离意义下）"一直悬而未决。顺序查询可利用自适应性细化估计并调整策略，而并行查询无法调整；从高阶量子操作看，并行策略可被顺序策略模拟、反之一般不成立，因此直觉上顺序策略更强。作者旨在挑战这一直觉。

Method: 提出一种新的学习协议，仅使用并行查询来学习未知 d 维酉信道。协议目标是在菱形距离下达到 ε 精度，查询复杂度为 \mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)，与 [Haah, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Tang, FOCS '23] 给出的下界相匹配。

Result: 协议以 \mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon) 次并行查询在菱形距离下达到 ε 精度，与已知下界一致，从而闭合了并行与顺序策略在酉信道层析之间的差距。作为应用，它实现了边界区间量子信道层析的最优查询复杂度，并提升了费米子线性光学层析中已知最优协议的查询效率。

Conclusion: 并行查询在酉信道层析中并不弱于顺序查询：新协议在查询复杂度上达到最优并匹配下界，否定了"顺序策略本质上更强"的直觉，同时对相关层析任务（边界区间量子信道、费米子线性光学）带来改进。

Abstract: In the study of quantum process tomography, it has remained open how to learn a unitary channel in diamond distance with strictly parallel queries as efficiently as with sequential queries. Sequential queries allow one to exploit adaptivity to refine the estimate and adjust the learning strategy accordingly, whereas such adjustments are impossible for parallel queries. From the perspective of higher-order quantum operations, any parallel learning strategy can be simulated by a sequential one, whereas the converse does not hold in general, suggesting that sequential learning strategies are potentially more powerful. Contrary to this intuition, we present a new protocol for learning an unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channel to within $\varepsilon$ in diamond distance using $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ parallel queries, matching the lower bound presented in [Haah, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Tang, FOCS '23]. Our protocol thus closes the gap between parallel and sequential strategies for unitary channel tomography. As applications, it achieves optimal query complexity for boundary-regime quantum channel tomography and improves the query efficiency of the best-known protocol for tomography of fermionic linear optics.

</details>


### [209] [Optimal Tolerant Testing of Lindbladian Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38160)
*Jinge Bao,Yiyi Cai,Zhong-Xia Shang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究未知时不变Lindblad生成元的耗散强度检验问题，在无记忆前向演化访问模型下，给出了区分耗散强度是否超过容忍阈值的算法及其最优性下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化耗散对控制量子噪声与刻画开放系统动力学至关重要。实际实验中即使抑制噪声，残余噪声仍可能存在，因此需要判定耗散强度是否处于规定的容忍阈值内。已有耗散检验工作存在限制，如要求有界度（bounded-degree），限制了其适用范围。

Method: 针对强度有界、跳跃算子局部但允许任意重叠的未知时不变Lindblad生成元，仅使用无记忆前向演化访问。将任务形式化为以归一化Frobenius范数度量，区分耗散强度不超过ε1与至少ε2（0≤ε1<ε2）两类情形。提出相应检验算法，并给出下界证明。

Result: 算法在总演化时间O(ε2/(ε2−ε1)^2)内以常数成功概率解决所有非平凡阈值下的检验任务；并证明了匹配的下界Ω(ε2/(ε2−ε1)^2)，表明该复杂度即使对自适应协议也是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作将耗散检验推广到容忍（tolerant）设定，并去除了有界度要求，使框架适用于更广泛的实验相关场景，同时确立了该问题的最优样本/时间复杂度。

Abstract: Quantifying dissipation is essential for controlling quantum noise and characterizing open-system dynamics. In practical experimental settings, residual noise may still persist despite efforts to suppress it, making it important to determine whether the dissipative strength remains within a prescribed tolerance threshold. We study this question for an unknown, time-independent Lindblad generator with bounded strength, where the jump operators are local but with unrestricted overlap, using only memoryless forward-evolution access. The task is to distinguish dissipative strength of at most $\varepsilon_1$ from strength of at least $\varepsilon_2$, for $0 \leq \varepsilon_1 < \varepsilon_2$, measured in the canonical dissipator's normalized Frobenius norm. We give an algorithm that solves this task in total evolution time $O(\varepsilon_2/(\varepsilon_2-\varepsilon_1)^2)$ for all nontrivial thresholds, with constant success probability, and provide a matching lower bound $Ω(\varepsilon_2/(\varepsilon_2-\varepsilon_1)^2)$ that establishes optimality even for adaptive protocols. This extends dissipation testing to the tolerant setting and eliminates the bounded-degree requirement, making the framework applicable to a broader range of experimentally relevant settings.

</details>


### [210] [When Classical Correlations Certify Entanglement Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38168)
*Takeru Utsumi,Yota Tachibana,Yoshifumi Nakata,Takaya Matsuura,Ryuji Takagi,Francesco Buscemi,Masato Koashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明纠缠与可区分性的联系可推广到一般测量：只要POVM满足比互补性更宽松的不可约性条件，即可建立单向局域变换为最大纠缠态的失真度与态分辨失败概率之间的定量关系，并刻画两者同时消失的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与态可区分性是量子基础的两大核心主题，二者通常通过互补基测量建立联系，该联系支撑了量子纠错等任务。但在不假设互补性的情况下，该联系能否推广到一般测量此前基本未决，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 引入由测量诱导的不可约性（irreducibility）条件，该条件比互补性更为宽松；针对满足不可约性条件的POVM，建立单向局域变换为最大纠缠态的失真度与态分辨失败概率之间的定量关系，并刻画两类误差同时消失的条件。

Result: 成功将纠缠-可区分性联系推广至一般测量；得到失真度与分辨失败概率的定量关系及二者同时消失的判据；将结果应用于纠缠提纯与量子纠错，其误差可通过实验可及的局域测量估计经典输入-输出关联来认证；并在测量理论中导出连接信息增益-不可逆性与可观测噪声-扰动的新权衡关系。

Conclusion: 基于不可约性的纠缠-可区分性联系为纠缠提纯、量子纠错与测量权衡提供了统一框架，且不依赖互补性假设。

Abstract: Entanglement and state distinguishability have long been central topics in quantum foundations. While each has developed into a rich subject in its own right, they can be connected through measurements in complementary bases. This connection provides insights into quantum and classical correlations and underlies many information-processing tasks, most notably quantum error correction (QEC). However, it has remained largely open whether the connection between entanglement and distinguishability extends to general measurements without assuming complementarity. We answer this question in the affirmative: the key is irreducibility induced by measurements, a much more relaxed condition than complementarity. Specifically, for POVMs satisfying an irreducibility condition, we establish a quantitative relation between the infidelity of one-sided local transformation into a maximally entangled state and the failure probability in state discrimination. We then characterize when both errors can vanish simultaneously. Our results have direct applications to entanglement distillation and QEC. Their errors can be certified by estimating classical input-output correlations from experimentally accessible local measurements, without requiring complementarity. The results also extend to quantum measurement theory. We derive novel trade-off relations that bridge two historically distinct approaches: information gain--irreversibility and observable noise--disturbance. Our connection between entanglement and distinguishability via irreducibility thus offers a common framework for entanglement distillation, QEC, and measurement trade-offs.

</details>

### [211] [Meromorphic open-string vertex algebras and twisted modules from Courant algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36370)
*Qixuan Fang,Fei Qi*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文从带有广义度量与广义Levi-Civita联络的传递Courant algebroid出发，构造了1/2 ℤ-分次亚纯开弦顶点代数层，并将其实现为张量代数丛的平行截面，进而用顶点算子实现Dirac生成算子、形式伴随及广义Hodge Laplacian等几何微分算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机在于将几何微分算子的顶点算子实现从函数情形推广到带权旋量与微分形式，服务于二维超对称非线性sigma模型的研究纲领。

Method: 基于带有广义度量与选定广义Levi-Civita联络的传递Courant algebroid E，构造1/2 ℤ-分次亚纯开弦顶点代数层；利用传递anchored丛的全纯性原理，将其实现为由E的玻色-费米仿射化构成的张量代数丛的平行截面。在偶数秩E与适当Clifford模丛数据下，构造含零权规范带权旋量丛的规范扭曲模范畴。平行张量的结合代数通过E-联络的迭代作用于旋量截面，该联络结合协变微分与Clifford乘法，混合项包含玻色联络对费米张量的作用。由逆Courant配对与逆广义度量构造的特定态给出顶点算子分量。

Result: 由逆Courant配对与逆广义度量构造的特定态给出顶点算子分量，作用于嵌入旋量截面时表现为规范Dirac生成算子、其形式伴随及二者的反对易子（广义Hodge Laplacian）。在带三形式H的精确Courant情形与外代数Clifford模下，这些算子特化为d−H∧、其伴随与扭曲Hodge Laplacian；当H=0时退化为普通de Rham与Hodge算子。

Conclusion: 本文把几何微分算子的顶点算子实现从函数推广到带权旋量与微分形式，为二维超对称非线性sigma模型研究纲领提供了新的代数框架。

Abstract: We construct a sheaf of $\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}$-graded meromorphic open-string vertex algebras from a transitive Courant algebroid $E$ equipped with a generalized metric and a chosen generalized Levi-Civita connection on a smooth manifold. Using a holonomy principle for transitive anchored bundles, we realize these algebras as parallel sections of a tensor algebra bundle built from bosonic-fermionic affinizations of $E$. For even-rank $E$ with suitable Clifford module bundle data, we construct a sheaf of canonically twisted modules containing the canonical weighted spinor bundle in weight zero. The associative algebra of parallel tensors acts on spinor sections through iterates of an $E$-connection combining covariant differentiation and Clifford multiplication, with mixed terms incorporating the action of the bosonic connection on fermionic tensors. Distinguished states built from the inverse Courant pairing and inverse generalized metric give vertex-operator components, acting on embedded spinor sections as the canonical Dirac generating operator, its formal adjoint, and their anticommutator, the generalized Hodge Laplacian. In the exact Courant case with three-form $H$ and the exterior algebra Clifford module, the operators specialize to $d-H\wedge$, its adjoint, and the twisted Hodge Laplacian, reducing to the ordinary de Rham and Hodge operators when $H=0$. This extends the vertex-operator realization of geometric differential operators from functions to weighted spinors and differential forms, within the program of studying two-dimensional supersymmetric nonlinear sigma models.

</details>


### [212] [A Note on Universal Positivity of Rank-Two Quantum Greedy Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37452)
*Qiyue Tang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 证明了当 b∣c 或 c∣b 时秩二量子贪婪元具有普遍正性，解决了 Lee–Li–Rupel–Zelevinsky 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lee、Li、Rupel 与 Zelevinsky 提出了关于秩二量子贪婪元普遍正性的猜想，该问题此前未获解决。

Method: 在 b∣c 或 c∣b 的条件下，对秩二量子贪婪元的正性进行证明（摘要未给出具体技术手段）。

Result: 证明了当 b∣c 或 c∣b 时，秩二量子贪婪元的普遍正性成立。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了 Lee–Li–Rupel–Zelevinsky 提出的猜想，在满足整除条件的参数情形下给出了肯定回答。

Abstract: We prove universal positivity of rank-two quantum greedy elements when $b\mid c$ or $c\mid b$, resolving a conjecture of Lee, Li,Rupel and Zelevinsky.

</details>


### [213] [Structure and Representations of a Diagrammatic Non-Commutative Tangloid Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37524)
*Hadeel B. Albeladi,Sofia Lambropoulou*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入由扩展缠结胚范畴构造的tangloid代数$\mathcal{T}_n$及其子代数辫胚代数$\mathcal{B}_n$，并定义约化tangloid代数$δ\mathcal{T}_n$及其双线性配对，为knotoid、linkoid、braidoid等带内禀端点的图解结构提供范畴与代数框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具有内禀端点的图解结构（knotoid、linkoid、braidoid）及相关图解代数提供统一的范畴论与代数理论框架，受Turaev的knotoid理论启发。

Method: 从无向welded tangleoid范畴$UWTC$出发，去除welded关系、保留forbidden moves并添加额外关系得到无向tangloid范畴$UTC$，braidoid范畴作为其子范畴自然产生；通过添加占位态射$\emptyset$构造扩展tangloid范畴$\Ti$，使生成元扩展为固定$n$-盒的自同态，占位态射与交叉态射的作用产生对角态射$/$和$\backslash$并用于$\Ti$的定义关系；扩展braidoid范畴$\mathbf{Brd}_{\Ti}$作为$\Ti$的子范畴实现。对每个$n\geq 0$，线性化自同态代数$\operatorname{End}_{\Ti}(n)$定义tangloid代数$\mathcal{T}_n$（含图解生成元与关系的单位$\mathbb{C}$-代数），并定义braidoid子代数$\mathcal{B}_n$；再通过施加关于平凡knot和平凡knotoid分量的两个约化关系定义约化tangloid代数$δ\mathcal{T}_n$，并利用图解反射、复合与闭包在其上构造自然的图解双线性配对。

Result: 构造了tangloid代数$\mathcal{T}_n$、braidoid代数$\mathcal{B}_n$和约化tangloid代数$δ\mathcal{T}_n$，并在$δ\mathcal{T}_n$上建立了图解双线性配对。

Conclusion: 该范畴与代数框架为研究带内禀端点的图解结构（knotoid、linkoid、braidoid）及相关图解代数提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce the \emph{tangloid algebra} $\mathcal{T}_n$, a diagrammatic algebra arising from the extended tangloid category $\Ti$, and its subalgebra, the \emph{braidoid algebra} $\mathcal{B}_n$. The construction begins with the unoriented tangloid category $UTC$, inspired by Turaev's theory of knotoids, which is obtained from the unoriented welded tangleoid category $UWTC$ by removing the welded relation while retaining the forbidden moves and imposing some additional relations. Within this framework, the braidoid category arises naturally as a subcategory of $UTC$. We then introduce the extended tangloid category $\Ti$ by adjoining a placeholder morphism $\emptyset$, which allows the generators to be extended to endomorphisms of a fixed $n$-box. The interaction of the placeholder morphism with the crossing morphisms gives rise to diagonal morphisms, $/$ and $\backslash$, which are used in the defining relations of $\Ti$. The corresponding extended braidoid category $\mathbf{Brd}_{\Ti}$ is realised as a subcategory of $\Ti$. For each $n\geq 0$, we then define the tangloid algebra $\mathcal{T}_n$ by linearising the endomorphism algebra $\operatorname{End}_{\Ti}(n)$, as a unital $\mathbb{C}$--algebra presented by diagrammatic generators and relations. We also define the braidoid subalgebra $\mathcal{B}_n$, which provides an algebraic framework for the theory of braidoids. We further introduce the reduced tangloid algebra $δ\mathcal{T}_n$ by imposing two additional reduction relations for trivial knot and trivial knotoid components, and we construct a natural diagrammatic \textit{bilinear pairing} on $δ\mathcal{T}_n$ using diagrammatic reflection, composition, and closure. Our categorical and algebraic setting provides a theoretical framework for studying diagrammatic structures with intrinsic endpoints, namely knotoids, linkoids, and braidoids, and related diagram algebras.

</details>


### [214] [Higher-dimensional full vertex algebras and systems of conformally covariant correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37949)
*Yuto Moriwaki*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文为未必幺正的紧致d维共形场论（CFT）公理化了一类欧几里得关联函数系统，引入"共形d-顶点代数"（full vertex algebra的d维类比），证明每个共形协变关联函数系统均可由此代数得到，并构造了对应广义自由标量场的一族例子（d>2时同时包含幺正与非幺正情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形场论的代数刻画（如full vertex algebra）主要针对2维情形，且通常依赖幺正性假设。作者希望将公理化框架推广到任意d维、且不强求幺正的紧致CFT，为此需发展合适的代数结构以统一刻画关联函数。

Method: 1）公理化地定义一类紧致d维CFT的欧几里得关联函数系统（不假设幺正性）；2）引入共形d-顶点代数作为full vertex algebra在d维的类比；3）证明任一满足共形协变的关联函数系统都可构造出相应的共形d-顶点代数（构造性对应定理）。

Result: 建立了关联函数系统与共形d-顶点代数之间的对应；并显式构造了一族满足公理的例子，对应广义自由标量场；当d>2时该族同时含幺正与非幺正实例。

Conclusion: 该工作将顶点代数式的代数刻画从2维推广至任意d维、并放宽幺正性限制，为研究高维（含非幺正）CFT提供统一代数框架，并以广义自由标量场族验证了公理的可满足性与覆盖范围。

Abstract: We formulate a class of systems of Euclidean correlation functions for compact $d$-dimensional conformal field theories which are not necessarily unitary. We also introduce conformal $d$-vertex algebras, $d$-dimensional analogues of full vertex algebras, and prove that such an algebra is obtained from every system of conformally covariant correlation functions. Finally, for each $d\geq 2$, we construct a family of examples satisfying these axioms, corresponding to generalized free scalar fields. For $d>2$, this family contains both unitary and non-unitary examples.

</details>

### [215] [Five-dimensional thick branes in the scalar representation of $f(Q,B)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35862)
*F. C. E. Lima,F. M. Belchior,C. A. S. Almeida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在$f(Q,B)$引力（$Q$为非度量性标量，$B$为边界项，$R=Q-B$）的标量表示下研究余维一厚膜世界，通过Legendre变换引入辅助标量场$\varphi=f_Q$、$\psi=f_B$及相互作用势$U$，在重合规范与五维翘曲几何中推导完整膜方程，构建两个渐近AdS光滑厚膜模型，并分析张量扰动，证明无质量引力子的局域化与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在$f(Q,B)$引力框架下探讨厚膜世界方案，考察非度量性标量与边界项对膜世界几何及引力子局域化的影响，以拓展修改引力中膜世界模型的研究。

Method: 采用$f(Q,B)$引力的标量表示，通过Legendre变换将引力部分改写为两个辅助标量场$\varphi=f_Q$、$\psi=f_B$及相互作用势$U(\varphi,\psi)$的形式；在重合规范下假定具有四维Poincaré对称性的五维翘曲几何，推导由正则体标量场源化的完整膜方程；对两个具有局域标量剖面的渐近AdS光滑厚膜模型进行求解，并分析张量扰动。

Result: 推导出了完整的厚膜方程组，并成功构建了两个渐近AdS光滑厚膜模型，其标量场剖面具有局域化特征；对张量扰动的分析表明无质量引力子可实现局域化且满足稳定性要求。

Conclusion: $f(Q,B)$引力的标量表示可自洽地描述余维一厚膜世界，所得厚膜解与张量扰动分析显示无质量引力子局域化且稳定，表明该框架在厚膜模型构建中具有可行性。

Abstract: One examines a codimension-one thick braneworld in the scalar representation of $f(Q,B)$ gravity, where $Q$ is the nonmetricity scalar, and $B$ is the boundary term satisfying $R=Q-B$. We write the gravitational sector in terms of two auxiliary scalar fields, $\varphi=f_Q$ and $ψ=f_B$, and an interaction potential $U(\varphi,ψ)$ obtained by a Legendre transformation from the $f(Q,B)$ function. Working in the coincident gauge and assuming a five-dimensional warped geometry with four-dimensional Poincaré symmetry, we derive the complete set of brane equations sourced by a canonical bulk scalar field. To accomplish our purpose, we address two models of smooth asymptotically AdS thick brane with localized scalar profiles. Furthermore, we also analyze tensor perturbations and demonstrate the localization and stability of the massless graviton.

</details>


### [216] [Operational Meaning of the Cosmological Evolution of Dimensional Quantities: A Generalized Cosmological Time Framework for Cross-Epoch Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35903)
*Seokcheon Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Robertson-Walker时空中引入广义宇宙时(GCT)与时间速率因子N_G，厘清了时间参数、坐标失效自由度与meVSL现象学赋值之间的区别，并据此重新推导宇宙学时间膨胀公式。结果显示时间膨胀指数取决于跨红移比较所用内在时标的定义方式：(1+z)^(1-b/4)或(1+z)，且对meVSL相关标度的观测检验需完整模型预言而非孤立量纲量的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量纲常数的数值依赖于所选单位制，其孤立的时间变化本身并非单位不变的观测量。因此，需要在一个几何上允许时间重参数化的框架中，明确区分物理时间参数、坐标失效自由度与现象学的meVSL赋值，从而给出单位不变的、可观测的时间速率关系。

Method: 在Robertson-Walker时空的齐次各向同性几何与基本同余下，引入广义宇宙时GCT，保留共同宇宙时参数T_G并定义时间速率因子N_G=dτ/dT_G（固有时表示为单位速率成员）；把时间速率关系嵌入标准超曲面正交RW同余结构，将坐标失效自由度与保留的时间参数及meVSL赋值相区分；在meVSL中取N_G(a)=a^(b/4)，由同一b控制不同历元量纲量的相关标度；对零测地线关联区间推导Δτ_o=(1+z)Δτ_e与ΔT_G,o/ΔT_G,e=(1+z)N_G,e/N_G,o。

Result: 若跨红移比较所用的内在时标定义在T_G中，取当前归一化N_G,o=1，则时间膨胀关系为(1+z)^(1-b/4)；若定义在固有时中，则恢复通常的(1+z)定律。宇宙学时间膨胀为该框架提供了明确的计时应用，且meVSL的相关跨历元标度使观测检验必须依赖完整模型预言，而无法通过约束孤立的量纲量来完成。

Conclusion: 时间参数的选择与内在时标的定义共同决定了时间膨胀的可观测指数；坐标失效自由度应与物理时间参数及现象学赋值严格区分。对meVSL相关标度的检验须基于完整的模型预言，孤立量纲量的观测约束不足以判定该框架。

Abstract: The numerical value of a dimensional constant depends on the adopted units, so its isolated temporal variation is not by itself a unit-invariant observable. We reconsider this issue in Robertson--Walker spacetime, whose homogeneous and isotropic geometry admits temporal reparametrization freedom along the fundamental congruence. Generalized Cosmological Time retains a common cosmological time parameter T_G and defines the temporal-rate factor N_G = d tau/d T_G, with the proper-time representation as the unit-rate member. In this formulation, the temporal-rate relation is embedded in the standard hypersurface-orthogonal RW congruence structure, while coordinate lapse freedom is kept distinct from the retained temporal parameter and from the phenomenological meVSL assignment. In meVSL, N_G(a) = a^(b/4), with the same b governing correlated cross-epoch scalings of dimensional quantities. Cosmological time dilation provides the explicit timing application: Delta tau_o = (1+z) Delta tau_e, while the same null-linked intervals in T_G satisfy Delta T_G,o/Delta T_G,e = (1+z) N_G,e/N_G,o. If the intrinsic timescale used to compare sources across redshift is defined in T_G, the present normalization N_G,o = 1 gives (1+z)^(1-b/4); if defined in proper time, the usual (1+z) law is recovered. Observational tests of the correlated meVSL scalings require complete model predictions rather than constraints on isolated dimensional quantities.

</details>


### [217] [Low Frequency Gravitational Wave Detection with Gravito-Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35962)
*Reza Ebadi,Surjeet Rajendran*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用引力波的相对论特性（引力磁分量）来区分低频引力波信号与小行星带引力梯度噪声（GGN）的新型探测方案，并建议使用原子干涉仪作为纯净惯性参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用内太阳系检验质量探测低频（10 nHz–1 μHz）引力波时，会受到小行星带产生的牛顿引力梯度噪声（GGN）的严重限制；信号与噪声背景的简并问题亟需解决。

Method: 基于引力波的相对论特性：非相对论运动的小行星主要产生引力电场，其引力磁场分量受速度压低；而相对论引力波产生的引力电场与引力磁场分量量级相等。据此提出通过检验质量的洛伦兹加速度探测低频引力波的引力磁场分量，并在三星座编队中实现信号的提取；采用原子干涉仪作为纯净惯性参考。

Result: 低频引力波的预期天体物理源具有显著高于高频段典型值的特征应变，使得洛伦兹加速度的测量在计量学上可行；该测量能够在三星座星座中无歧义地从引力梯度噪声中提取引力波信号。

Conclusion: 引力磁场探测方案仍需要与非引力噪声良好隔离的检验质量，原子干涉仪可作为满足该要求的纯净惯性参考，从而实现低频引力波探测。

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves in the low-frequency band (10 nHz to 1 micro-Hz) using inner solar system proof masses is severely limited by Newtonian gravity gradient noise (GGN) sourced by the asteroid belt. In this paper, we propose a novel detection concept that exploits the relativistic nature of gravitational waves to break the degeneracy between the signal and the asteroid GGN background. The non-relativistic motion of asteroids dominantly sources gravito-electric fields, with velocity suppressed gravito-magnetic components. Relativistic gravitational waves produce gravito-electric and gravito-magnetic components of equal magnitude. We propose detecting the gravito-magnetic component of low frequency gravitational waves using the Lorentz acceleration of test bodies. The measurement of this Lorentz acceleration is metrologically possible due to the fact that expected astrophysical sources of low frequency gravitational waves have considerably higher characteristic strains than the strains typically expected at higher frequencies. We show that this measurement allows for unambiguous extraction of the gravitational wave signal from the gravity gradient noise in a three satellite constellation.
