The gravito-magnetic measurement still requires a proof mass that is well isolated from non-gravitational noise. We propose the use of atom interferometers to serve as such a pristine inertial reference.

</details>


### [218] [Black Hole Quasinormal Mode Resonances and Reconnections in Coupled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35971)
*Leah Jenks,Hayato Motohashi,Kazufumi Takahashi,Takuya Takahashi,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在扰动动力学陈-西蒙斯（dCS）理论的具体模型及一个极简玩具模型中研究了黑洞准正规模（QNM）的避免交叉、共振激发与模式重连现象，发现双螺旋端点极限频率与扰动强度无关，且激发因子呈现标量-引力模式共振的标度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QNM是探测黑洞内部结构及其所处环境的窗口；在超越广义相对论的耦合系统中，QNM动力学更为丰富（避免交叉、共振激发、模式重连），近期发现重连轨迹可在复频率平面上形成独特的双螺旋结构。作者希望在一个具体可计算的模型中考察该现象学，并判断其是否依赖于dCS理论的具体特征。

Method: 在扰动动力学陈-西蒙斯（dCS）理论的具体模型中，对不同扰动参数取值下的例外点（exceptional points）、避免交叉与重连进行刻画；计算激发因子（excitation factors）并分析其标度行为。为检验现象是否依赖dCS的特定细节，另构建一个由方形势垒耦合自由度组成的玩具模型进行对照分析。

Result: 在dCS模型中刻画了若干扰动参数下的例外点、避免交叉与重连，发现双螺旋端点的极限频率独立于扰动强度；激发因子遵循标量模与引力模之间共振所特有的标度关系。玩具模型同样能复现避免交叉与重连，表明这些现象在耦合系统中具有普遍性，且对系统参数高度敏感。

Conclusion: 双螺旋型的QNM重连现象学并非dCS理论所独有，而可在更一般的耦合自由度系统中出现，且对系统参数高度敏感；其中双螺旋端点的极限频率与扰动强度无关，激发因子显示出标量-引力模式共振的标度特征。

Abstract: Black hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) provide a window into the underlying structure of black holes and the environments in which they arise. QNMs have particularly rich dynamics in coupled systems beyond general relativity, in which they are known to undergo avoided crossings, resonant excitation, and mode reconnections. Recent work has shown that reconnecting QNM trajectories can trace out a distinctive double spiral structure in the complex frequency plane. In this work, we study this phenomenology in a concrete model of perturbed dynamical Chern-Simons (dCS) theory, as well as in a minimal toy model. In the former, we characterize the exceptional points, avoided crossings and reconnections for several values of the perturbation parameters, and find that the limiting frequency of the double spiral endpoints is independent of the perturbation strength. We compute the excitation factors and find that they follow the scaling characteristic of a resonance between the scalar and gravitational modes. To determine whether these phenomena depend on the specific features of dCS, we construct a toy model of coupled degrees of freedom with square potential barriers, and find that we can reproduce the avoided crossings and reconnections, indicating that these phenomena can occur more generally in coupled systems and are highly sensitive to the system parameters.

</details>


### [219] [Spectral instabilities in the time domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35993)
*Taillte May,Adrien Kuntz,Nicola Franchini,Valentin Boyanov,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文用一维双Pöschl-Teller模型研究谱不稳定性对时域波形的影响，通过求解多次散射发现回波前泛音频率不变，首次回波后出现线性长期修正，可解释为准简正模频移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱不稳定性（如微扰导致的准简正模频率移动）在天体物理黑洞铃宕信号中具有重要意义，但频域分析难以刻画其对时域波形的具体影响，尤其回波出现前后的时序结构。本文旨在通过可解析的模型揭示这一时域机制。

Method: 采用一维双Pöschl-Teller势模型，主势垒与一个弱的空间分离微扰势垒并存。解析追踪两级势垒之间的逐次散射过程，提取长期（secular）修正项，并与频域结果及数值时域演化进行对照验证。

Result: 长期修正项在两级势垒间完成一次因果往返后才首次出现。首次回波到达前，所有泛音保持未受微扰的频率；回波到达后，出现随时间线性增长的长期修正。在微扰成立时，该修正可解释为QNM频率移动，其系数与频域结果一致；更一般地，它产生有限时间的有效频率，既不等于未微扰也不等于完全微扰的QNM谱。数值时域演化证实了解析结果。

Conclusion: 谱不稳定性对时域波形的影响具有明确的时序结构：回波前无修正、回波后呈线性长期修正。该修正可退化为频域QNM频移，也可产生非微扰的有效频率，说明时域波形携带了频域分析无法完整捕捉的信息。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of a spectral instability on the time domain waveform using a one-dimensional double Pöschl-Teller model. By analytically following successive scatterings between the primary potential barrier and a weak, spatially separated perturbation, we identify a secular contribution that first appears after one causal round trip between the barriers. Before the first echo, all overtones remain at their unperturbed frequencies. After the first echo reaches the observer, there is a secular, linear-in-time correction. When this correction is perturbative, it can be interpreted as a shift of the quasinormal mode frequency. In that case, we show that its coefficient reproduces the frequency-domain result. More generally, it generates finite-time effective frequencies that need not coincide with either the unperturbed or fully perturbed quasi-normal mode spectrum. We confirm the results of our analytic calculation using numerical time-domain evolutions.

</details>


### [220] [Stochastic gravitational-wave background from subsolar neutron stars in collapsar disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36005)
*Valerio De Luca,Loris Del Grosso,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了坍缩星中黑洞周围中微子冷却吸积盘碎裂所形成的亚太阳质量中子星并合产生的随机引力波背景能量密度，并给出对坍缩星比例的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坍缩星吸积盘碎裂被认为是产生远低于标准核心坍缩下限质量中子星的通道，这些盘生天体的并合会在宇宙历史中叠加形成随机引力波背景，需要量化其可观测性以检验该情景。

Method: 针对亚太阳中子星与中心黑洞并合、以及双中子星并合两条通道，采用与长伽马射线暴观测率相联系的形成历史，计算随机引力波背景的能量密度，并与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第四次观测运行数据对比推导上限。

Result: 亚太阳中子星与中心黑洞并合是最响亮的通道，已被现有数据约束且处于下一代天文台探测范围内；双中子星通道最多暗两个数量级，其最宽双星仍可被下一代探测器触及。由此得到坍缩星盘产生此类并合比例的两个上限：基于各向同性背景未探测的稳健上限，以及通过专门搜索可分辨亚太阳事件可达到的更严格上限。

Conclusion: 该背景为盘生亚太阳中子星提供了群体层面的探针，下一代探测器有望使其清晰可见；坍缩星盘非轴对称形变的引力波还可作为独立特征信号，支持与未来分赫兹探测器的互相关研究。

Abstract: The fragmentation of the neutrino-cooled accretion disks that surround the black holes formed in collapsars has recently been proposed as an astrophysical channel for the production of neutron stars with masses well below the standard core-collapse floor. These disk-born objects merge either with one another or with the central black hole, and the superposition of all such mergers across cosmic history sources a stochastic gravitational-wave background. In this work we compute the energy density of this background for both merger channels, adopting a formation history tied to the observed rate of long gamma-ray bursts. We find that the merger of a subsolar neutron star with the central black hole is the loudest channel, already constrained by current data and within reach of next-generation observatories, while the binary neutron-star channel is at most two orders of magnitude fainter, with its widest binaries remaining accessible to next-generation detectors. Confronting these predictions with the fourth observing run of the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA Collaboration, we derive two upper bounds on the fraction of collapsars whose disks produce such mergers: a robust one from the non-detection of an isotropic background, and a tighter one that could be achieved by a dedicated search for resolvable subsolar events in current data. Gravitational waves from non-axisymmetric deformations of the collapsar disk itself would provide an additional independent signature for the collapsar scenario, enabling cross-correlation studies with future deci-Hz detectors. Overall, the background computed here provides a population-level probe of disk-born subsolar neutron stars, which next-generation detectors could bring firmly into view.

</details>


### [221] [Toward the CompactHedron: Bounds on Neutron Stars from Microscopic Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36025)
*Thomas Apostolidis,Alessandro Longo,Borna Salehian,Luca Santoni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在点粒子有效场论框架下研究潮汐Love数，通过因果性、正定性与被动性要求，推导出对Love数的非平庸限制，并将其应用于中子星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐Love数是刻画致密天体潮汐响应的关键量，但文献中通常依赖稳定性与热力学条件来约束它们。本文希望从因果性和正定性等基本原理出发，为致密天体参数提供独立于传统假设的限制。

Method: 采用点粒子有效场论，将Love数对应为曲率张量及其导数构成算符的Wilson系数；聚焦四极引力电扇区，要求潮汐响应是推迟的、在高频下衰减，并结合被动性假设，推导参数空间中的允许区域；随后对代表性多项式和表格化中子星状态方程计算到频率展开四阶的动态Love数。

Result: 因果性、正定性与被动性给出Love数的非平庸上下界，界定了有效理论参数空间的允许区域；该结果适用于任意局域物体。对中子星的应用表明，在低致密度区域，这些界转化为对恒星首个振荡模式的约束，且与传统稳定性及热力学条件概念上独立。

Conclusion: 本文为从基本原理系统推导致密天体约束奠定了框架，并讨论了耗散存在时界的修正方式。

Abstract: We investigate the implications of causality and positivity for the tidal Love numbers of gravitating objects. We work within the framework of point-particle effective field theory, where the Love numbers correspond to the Wilson coefficients of operators constructed from the curvature tensor and its derivatives. For simplicity, we focus on the quadrupolar gravito-electric sector, which provides the leading contribution to tidal deformability. We show that requiring the tidal response to be retarded and to decay at high frequencies, together with the assumption of passivity, leads to nontrivial bounds on the Love numbers. These bounds delineate an allowed region in the parameter space of the effective theory and apply to any localized object. In particular, we study their application to neutron stars. We compute the dynamical Love numbers through fourth order in the frequency expansion for a representative set of polytropic and tabulated neutron-star equations of state and show that, in the low-compactness regime, the bounds translate into constraints on the first oscillation mode of the star. Importantly, these constraints are conceptually independent of the stability and thermodynamic conditions commonly imposed in the literature. While our focus is primarily on conservative systems, we also discuss how the bounds are modified in the presence of dissipation. Our results lay the ground for a systematic framework to derive bounds on compact objects from fundamental principles.

</details>


### [222] [On the quasiblack-hole limit of rotating charged fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36102)
*Marcos L. W. Basso,Vilson T. Zanchin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究稳态轴对称带电完美流体（刚体或差动旋转、有无压强）的极端准黑洞极限，强调Weyl型构型。在正则性与光滑匹配条件下，电磁正则性与趋近均匀旋转迫使连通流体内部广义电磁势为常数，质量公式在可积条件下退化为极端Kerr-Newman Smarr关系，并分类讨论了多种物态方程与Weyl关系下的极限可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多针对静态或旋转尘埃分布的极端准黑洞极限，缺乏对更一般旋转带电完美流体（含压强、差动旋转）是否可容纳该极限的系统分析。本文旨在推广并明确该极限成立所需的条件。

Method: 在稳态轴对称带电完美流体框架下考察极端QBH极限：红移因子在整个流体内部趋零、边界成为准视界；要求物质与电磁场正则且与外部分光滑匹配。分别处理刚体旋转（线性Weyl尘埃、Kloster-Das与Guilfoyle含压流体、Islam假设）与差动旋转（洛伦兹力项恒为零、线性Weyl子类）情形，并在收敛性与可积性假设下推导约束。

Result: 在收敛假设下，电磁正则性加趋于均匀旋转推出连通流体内广义电磁势为常数（独立于Weyl假设）；附加可积条件下质量公式退化为极端Kerr-Newman Smarr关系。刚体旋转中线性Kloster-Das子类极限下变为无压，一般Guilfoyle情形允许非零压强；Islam假设在耦合参数、极限势与极限电荷密度均非零时阻碍正则极限。差动旋转中正则性要求控制角速度梯度。

Conclusion: 旋转Weyl型系统可具有与静态对应者相类的极端准黑洞极限，本文推广了旋转尘埃分布的分析，并给出更一般旋转带电流体与该极限相容的条件。

Abstract: We investigate the extremal quasiblack-hole (QBH) limit of stationary, axisymmetric charged perfect fluids in rigid or differential rotation, with and without pressure. We emphasize Weyl-type configurations, whose redshift factor is functionally related to the generalized electromagnetic potential. In this limit, the redshift factor vanishes throughout the fluid interior and the boundary becomes a quasihorizon. We require regular matter and electromagnetic fields and smooth matching to the exterior. Under suitable convergence assumptions, electromagnetic regularity and the approach to uniform rotation imply a constant generalized electromagnetic potential throughout the connected fluid interior, independently of the Weyl ansatz. With additional integrability conditions, the mass formula reduces to the extremal Kerr-Newman Smarr relation. For rigid rotation, we examine charged dust obeying a linear Weyl relation and fluids with pressure obeying the Kloster-Das or Guilfoyle relations. The linear Kloster-Das subclass becomes pressureless in the limit, whereas the general Guilfoyle case allows nonzero pressure. The Islam ansatz obstructs a regular limit when its coupling parameter, limiting potential, and limiting charge density are nonzero. For differential rotation, we analyze configurations with an identically vanishing Lorentz-force term and a linear Weyl subclass whose regularity requires control of angular-velocity gradients. Our results show that rotating Weyl-type systems admit extremal QBH limits much like their static counterparts, extending analyses of rotating dust distributions and identifying conditions for more general rotating charged fluids to be compatible with this limit.

</details>


### [223] [Zero expansion is not free: the sharp minimum of Eulerian negative energy in Natário warp drives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36211)
*Nelson Bolívar,Ivaylo Vasilev,Gabriel Abellán*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了Natário曲速引擎构造所需的Eulerian负能量，证明了在薄壁极限下其最小值为 $\mathcal{E}_{\min}\sim v^2R^4/(4\Delta^3)$，比此前估算高出两个量级 $(R/\Delta)^2$，并揭示了该变分问题等价于同心球间的经典Stokes流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Natário曲速引擎用无散度的shift替代了Alcubierre泡泡的收缩与膨胀，但其所需负能量的精确量值此前并不清楚，已有估算可能严重低估。作者希望精确确定该构造所需的Eulerian负能量下界与最小值。

Method: 作者在标准平坦切片上，取单位lapse、半径R的平坦内部，并在R+Δ处与速度v的均匀外部流精确匹配，针对Lobo-Visser体积积分 $\mathcal{E}_{\rm LV}=-\int_{\Sigma_t}\rho_{\rm E}\,d^3x$ ，证明了对所有允许shift的下界，并用闭式给出每个展弦比下的精确最小值；同时将完整变分问题等价为同心球间的经典Stokes流动，得到唯一极小元，并用精确通量恒等式与独立求积验证解析结果。

Result: 证明了薄壁极限下的精确最小值为 $\mathcal{E}_{\min}\sim v^2R^4/(4\Delta^3)$ ，常数精确为1/4，比早期应用于Natário和Alcubierre泡泡的估算 $v^2R^2/\Delta$ 高出 $(R/\Delta)^2$ 两个量级；不可压缩性迫使泡泡排开的通量经壁面回流，该回流电流的切向剪切主导了能量；唯一极小元在薄壁极限下趋于最小曲率三次函数。

Conclusion: Natário曲速引擎所需的Eulerian负能量远高于此前估算，其精确最小值由不可压缩回流引起的切向剪切主导，变分问题与同心球间Stokes流动等价，解析结果得到通量恒等式与独立数值求积的支持；所界定的量依赖于切片与观测者，不同于ADM质量。

Abstract: Natário's warp drive replaces the contraction and expansion of the Alcubierre bubble by a divergence-free shift. We determine exactly how much Eulerian negative energy this construction requires. For the Lobo-Visser volume integral $\mathcal{E}_{\rm LV}=-\int_{Σ_t}ρ_{\rm E}\,d^3x$ on the standard flat slice, with unit lapse, a flat interior of radius $R$, and exact matching to a uniform exterior stream of speed $v$ at $R+Δ$, we prove a lower bound for every admissible shift and find the exact minimum in closed form at every aspect ratio. As the wall thins, $\mathcal{E}_{\min}\sim v^2R^4/(4Δ^3)$ with sharp constant $1/4$, two powers of $R/Δ$ above the earlier estimate $v^2R^2/Δ$ applied to both Natário and Alcubierre bubbles. Incompressibility forces the flux displaced by the bubble to return through the wall, and the tangential shear of this return current dominates the energy. The full variational problem is equivalent to classical Stokes flow between concentric spheres. Its unique minimizer tends to a minimal-curvature cubic in the thin-wall limit. An exact flux identity and independent quadrature support the analytic result. The bounded quantity is slice and observer dependent and is distinct from the ADM mass.

</details>


### [224] [What Fraction of Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals That Merge After the LISA Mission Ends Are Detectable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36298)
*Daniel J. Oliver,Aaron D. Johnson,Jonathan E. Thompson,Curt J. Cutler*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了LISA任务结束后才坠入大质量黑洞的极端质量比旋近（EMRI）所占探测比例：在四年任务期、SNR>20条件下约为四分之一，十年延长期约为六分之一；各黑洞自旋与次天体质量下区间分别为25%–33%和13%–20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI可在毫赫兹频段辐射数年乃至数十年，因此相当一部分LISA可探测EMRI在任务结束时仍在旋近、任务结束后才坠入。这些"任务后"EMRI是全部探测对象的可观组成部分，但尚无系统量化结果，也缺乏适配的探测流程，本文据此展开研究。

Method: 基于天体物理motivated的EMRI族群抽样，对给定的"距坠入时间"反向积分完全相对论性的Kerr FastEMRIWaveforms（FEW）轨道，并在四年标称与十年延长观测窗口上计算完整LISA响应；以SNR阈值ρ=10、20、30为条件，将"任务后EMRI"的可探测比例表示为距坠入时间的显式函数，并考察其随参数（大质量黑洞自旋、次天体质量等）的分布。

Result: ρ>20、四年任务期下，任务后EMRI约占LISA可探测族群的1/4，十年延长期约1/6。窗口内可探测比例在ρ>20时随大质量黑洞自旋与次天体质量的变化可达约6倍；而任务后EMRI的份额在全部参数下相对稳定：四年任务期为25%–33%，十年任务期为13%–20%。

Conclusion: 任务后坠入的EMRI占全部探测的可观比例，其中坠入发生在任务结束后约1年以上的对象可能需要与任务期内EMRI不同的搜索算法，这为尽快发展适用于该类信号的探测流程提供了紧迫性。

Abstract: Stellar-mass compact objects spiraling into massive black holes in galactic nuclei (referred to as "extreme-mass-ratio inspirals", or EMRIs) will be an important class of sources for the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). A stellar-mass compact object can radiate in the millihertz band for years to decades before it plunges into its massive black hole, so a substantial fraction of the EMRIs that LISA can detect will still be inspiraling when the mission ends. We calculate what fraction of detectable EMRIs will plunge after the mission ends, and for those we calculate how they are distributed as a function of various parameters, such as their post-mission lifetime. To do this, we draw from astrophysically motivated populations, backward-integrate fully relativistic Kerr FastEMRIWaveforms trajectories for a given time to plunge, and compute the full LISA response over both the four-year nominal and ten-year extended observing windows. We calculate the detectable fraction as an explicit function of time to plunge at signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) thresholds of $ρ=10$, 20, and 30. We find that post-mission EMRIs are roughly one in four of the LISA-detectable population at $ρ>20$ over the four-year mission, and roughly one in six over the ten-year extended mission. The in-window detectable fraction varies by nearly a factor of six at $ρ>20$ across the massive black hole spins and secondary masses we ran, while the share stays between 25% and 33% for the four-year mission and between 13% and 20% for the ten-year one across all of them. The search for EMRIs that plunge more than $\sim 1$ year after the mission ends may require a different algorithm than the one used for EMRIs that plunge during the mission, and the fact that post-mission detections are a substantial fraction of all detections gives some urgency to developing a detection pipeline suitable for them.

</details>


### [225] [Computing gravitational radiation from highly eccentric bound binaries in the small-mass-ratio limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36327)
*Diego Andrés Rivera Orona,Scott A. Hughes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将频域Teukolsky方程方法推广至小质量比双星的高偏心率情形，通过对源项积分进行针对性处理，可轻松计算至e=0.99、并推进至e=0.999的引力波振幅及能量/角动量通量，同时发现经典Peters-Mathews公式在较宽分离下存在系统性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述束缚致密双星演化的强引力模型对理解引力辐射及其反作用至关重要。极高偏心率极限e→1难以建模：频域方法需大量谐波，时域方法因系统时间尺度长而收敛缓慢。近期天体物理分析强调该区域的重要性——许多系统可能在演化至可探测源的过程中经历e≈1，并可能以显著偏心率进入探测器灵敏频段。因此需要发展能有效处理高偏心率的计算方法。

Method: 将适用于小质量比极限的频域方法进行改进以有效处理高偏心率。核心是频域Teukolsky方程中源项的一个积分。作者考察使该积分在e→1时难以求值的特征，并研究规避这些困难的方法，提出一个简单的修正方案。

Result: 借助该修正，可轻松计算至e=0.99的引力波振幅及相应的能量和角动量通量；在极大耐心与毅力下推进至e=0.999，但如此极端情形数值精度似乎下降。此外发现广泛使用的Peters和Mathews经典公式存在系统性误差，该误差在相当宽的分离下依然存在，这对经历e≈1演化的致密双星形成模型具有意义。

Conclusion: 通过修正频域Teukolsky方程源项积分，所提方法能有效处理高偏心率小质量比双星的引力辐射计算，扩展了频域技术的适用范围；同时揭示经典Peters-Mathews公式在较宽分离下的系统误差，提示相关双星形成演化模型需重新审视其精度。

Abstract: Precise strong-gravity models describing the evolution of bound compact binary systems provide important information about the gravitational radiation such binaries generate and how they evolve due to the radiation's backreaction. The limit of very high eccentricity, $e \to 1$, is challenging to model: frequency-domain techniques require many harmonics to accurately describe radiation in this limit, and time-domain techniques can be slow to converge due to the systems' long timescales. Many recent astrophysical analyses have nonetheless emphasized the importance of understanding gravitational-wave emission in this regime, highlighting the fact that many systems may evolve through $e \simeq 1$ en route to becoming detectable sources, and may enter the sensitive band of detectors with substantial eccentricity. In this paper, we show how to adapt frequency-domain methods for computing gravitational radiation in the small-mass-ratio limit to effectively handle high eccentricity. The key piece of this analysis is an integral over the source of the frequency-domain Teukolsky equation. We examine features of this integral that make it difficult to evaluate as $e \to 1$, and study methods to circumvent these difficulties. We find a simple fix which allows us to compute gravitational-wave amplitudes and the associated fluxes of energy and angular momentum with ease up to eccentricities $e = 0.99$; with patience (and stamina), we have pushed to $e = 0.999$, though numerical accuracy appears to degrade for such extreme cases. Interestingly, we find that the well-known and often used classic formulas of Peters and Mathews are subject to a systematic error which persists at quite wide separation. This has implications for models of compact binary formation which evolve through $e \simeq 1$.

</details>


### [226] [Threshold Tails of Black hole Differential Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36650)
*Vedant Subhash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶微分可观测量如何影响黑洞波动方程的零频行为，并给出可观测量消除格林函数主导阈值项的判据，指出准正规模与晚期尾迹未必同步变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正规模与晚期尾迹通常被视为同一波动方程的两类特征，但一阶微分可观测量对其零频行为的影响机制尚不清晰，二者是否随可观测量同步改变缺乏统一判据。

Method: 通过分析格林函数在零频处的阈值结构，推导可观测量消除主导阈值项的简单条件；以 Schwarzschild Regge-Wheeler 模式为例，利用静态正则解确定相关算符，并考察变换在非零频与零频处的简并性。

Result: 该变换在非零频处非简并，但在零频处全局简并。因此非零准正规模问题保持不变，而定半径晚期尾迹的主导项增加一个额外的时间反幂。

Conclusion: 一阶微分可观测量对零频行为的敏感性使准正规模与晚期尾迹可能解耦；零频处变换的全局简并是准正规模不变而尾迹幂次改变的根本原因。

Abstract: We study how first-order differential observables affect the zero-frequency behavior of black-hole wave equations. Quasinormal modes and late-time tails do not always change in the same way. We give a simple condition for when an observable removes the leading threshold term of a Green function. For Schwarzschild Regge-Wheeler modes, the relevant operator is determined by the regular static solution. The transformation is nondegenerate at nonzero frequency but becomes globally degenerate at zero frequency. As a result, the nonzero quasinormal-mode problem is unchanged, while the leading fixed-radius late-time tail gains one extra inverse power of time.

</details>


### [227] [Correspondence between multileaf topology of closed geodesics and spatiotemporal autocorrelations of hotspot images in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36767)
*Fengting Xie,Qing-Hua Zhu,Xin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用光线追踪构造 Schwarzschild 黑洞周围沿束缚闭合测地线运动热点主图像的时空自相关，建立多叶轨道拓扑整数 $(z,w,v)$ 与相关带数 $N_{\rm band}$、复现点数 $N_{\rm rec}$ 的定量关系，从而实现从自相关中恢复闭合轨道拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论闭合轨道的多叶结构分类为强场动力学研究提供了框架，但如何在天文图像中识别这些结构仍是难题，因此需要一种从可观测图像特征反演轨道拓扑的定量方法。

Method: 采用光线追踪方法，计算绕 Schwarzschild 黑洞沿束缚闭合测地线运动的点状热点主图像的时空自相关；轨道以三个整数 $(z,w,v)$ 分类，其中 $z$ 为叶片数、$w$ 为每个径向周期内的额外回旋数、$v$ 为轨道叶片的追踪顺序。

Result: 对所考察的轨道族，数值结果建立了拓扑整数与自相关带数 $N_{\rm band}$、复现点数 $N_{\rm rec}$ 之间的对应关系：$z=N_{\rm rec}-1$，$w=\lfloor (N_{\rm band}+1)/(N_{\rm rec}-1)\rfloor-1$，$v=(N_{\rm band}+1)\bmod(N_{\rm rec}-1)$，其中 $\lfloor x\rfloor$ 为向下取整、$\bmod$ 为取余。

Conclusion: 这些关系提供了一种从热点图像自相关中定量恢复闭合轨道拓扑的方法，为在强场天文图像中识别多叶轨道结构提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The classification of relativistic closed orbits by their multileaf structures provides a framework for studying strong-field dynamics. Identifying these structures in astronomical images remains challenging. Using ray tracing, we construct the spatiotemporal autocorrelations of the primary image of a pointlike hotspot moving along bound closed geodesics around a Schwarzschild black hole. These orbits are classified by three integers $(z,w,v)$, where $z$ counts the leaves, $w$ counts the additional whirls during each radial period, and $v$ specifies the order in which the orbital leaves are traced. For the orbit families examined, our numerical results establish a correspondence between the topological integers $(z,w,v)$ and the numbers of correlation bands $N_{\rm band}$ and recurrence points $N_{\rm rec}$. The integers are recovered as $z=N_{\rm rec}-1$, $w=\left\lfloor (N_{\rm band}+1)/(N_{\rm rec}-1)\right\rfloor-1$, and $v=(N_{\rm band}+1)\bmod(N_{\rm rec}-1)$. Here, $\lfloor x\rfloor$ denotes the greatest integer not exceeding $x$, and $\bmod$ denotes the remainder operation. These relations provide a quantitative method for recovering closed-orbit topology from hotspot image autocorrelations.

</details>


### [228] [Holographic dark energy in Einstein-Cartan spin cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36853)
*Yongjun Yun,Jungjai Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Einstein-Cartan理论中，挠率标量修正了以Hubble半径为红外截断的非相互作用全息暗能量的尘埃状状态方程，使其能在低红移有效范围内实现晚期宇宙加速，而广义相对论中的对应模型无法做到这一点；同时推导出由挠率诱导的宇宙距离对偶关系偏离参数η。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广义相对论框架下，以Hubble半径为红外截断的非相互作用全息暗能量模型无法在低红移有效范围内实现晚期宇宙加速。作者希望借助引入挠率的Einstein-Cartan理论，考察挠率是否能改变这一局面，并进一步探讨挠率对宇宙距离对偶关系的影响。

Method: 在Einstein-Cartan理论框架下，采用以Hubble半径为红外截断的非相互作用全息暗能量作为背景，分析挠率标量对暗能量状态方程的修正作用；并在同一全息暗能量背景下，推导挠率诱导的宇宙距离对偶关系修正，即 d_L = d_A(1+z)^2(1+η)。

Result: 挠率标量将原本尘埃状的状态方程进行修正，使该全息暗能量模型能够在低红移有效范围内驱动晚期宇宙加速，这是广义相对论对应模型所不具备的。所得到的距离对偶关系偏离参数η在晚期宇宙中数值较小但不为零，体现了光传播路径上挠率效应的累积作用。

Conclusion: Einstein-Cartan理论中的挠率对全息暗能量状态方程及宇宙距离对偶关系均有可观测的修正作用：它使低红移晚期加速成为可能，并产生一个虽小但非零的距离对偶关系偏离参数η，可作为检验挠率效应的潜在观测探针。

Abstract: We show that, in Einstein-Cartan theory, the torsion scalar modifies the dust-like equation of state of non-interacting holographic dark energy with the Hubble radius as the infrared cutoff, enabling late-time cosmic acceleration within the low-redshift domain of validity, which is not realized in the corresponding model in general relativity. Using the same holographic dark energy background, we further derive the torsion-induced modification of the cosmic distance duality relation, $d_L=d_A(1+z)^2(1+η)$. In the late-time universe, the magnitude of the deviation parameter, $|η|$, is small but non-vanishing, reflecting the cumulative effect of torsion along the light-propagation path.

</details>


### [229] [Higher-curvature gravity in Weyl geometry: No-ghost and no-tachyon conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36880)
*Tomoya Tachinami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Weyl几何中构造曲率标量任意函数的引力理论，给出极大对称真空附近无鬼、无快子条件，并揭示Weyl矢量与标量扇区的耦合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将广义相对论通过Weyl不变性（局部标度对称性）进行推广，需厘清在Weyl几何下高阶曲率引力理论的稳定性（鬼与快子）条件及自由度结构，而此前缺乏对任意函数形式Lagrangian的系统分析。

Method: 以Weyl几何中标量曲率的任意函数构造Lagrangian；Weyl不变性要求除度规和Weyl矢量外引入补偿标量场。通过辅助场线性化作用量，分析Weyl矢量仅通过标量场的一个组合耦合，并将其平移对数梯度后成为Weyl不变的有质量矢量场；在Einstein框架下推导无鬼与无快子条件。

Result: Weyl矢量经平移后无需规范固定即成为Weyl不变的有质量矢量场。在引力子无鬼的前提下，Weyl矢量的无快子条件与标量子的无鬼条件归结为补偿标量场动能系数为正。当该系数为零时，标量子不传播，仅剩引力子和有质量矢量。对含常数、线性、二次曲率项的Lagrangian，整个稳定区必然同时存在有质量矢量与有质量标量子；若无线性项，标量子变为无质量。

Conclusion: Weyl几何中任意f(R)型引力理论的稳定性可由补偿标量动能系数的正性统一刻画，该框架为构建具有局部标度对称性的可行引力模型提供了判据与自由度图谱。

Abstract: Weyl geometry, in which the length scale can be chosen independently at each point of spacetime, provides a natural framework for extending general relativity through Weyl invariance, that is, local scale symmetry. We construct a gravitational theory whose Lagrangian is an arbitrary function of the scalar curvature in Weyl geometry and establish the conditions under which the theory is free of ghosts and tachyons around maximally symmetric vacua. For an arbitrary function, Weyl invariance requires a compensating scalar field in addition to the metric and the Weyl vector. By linearizing the action with an auxiliary field, we show that the Weyl vector couples to the scalar sector only through a single combination of the scalar fields. Consequently, the Weyl vector, shifted by the logarithmic gradient of this combination, becomes a Weyl-invariant massive vector field without gauge fixing. In the Einstein frame, given that the graviton is ghost-free, the no-tachyon condition for the Weyl vector, together with the no-ghost condition for the scalaron, the scalar mode associated with the higher-curvature terms, reduces to the positivity of the kinetic coefficient of the compensating scalar. When this coefficient vanishes, the scalaron does not propagate, leaving only the graviton and the massive vector. As an example, we show that for a Lagrangian containing constant, linear, and quadratic curvature terms, both a massive vector and a massive scalaron are necessarily present throughout the stability region, whereas without the linear term the scalaron becomes massless.

</details>


### [230] [Cosmological extra dimensions can mimic dark matter II. Growth of cosmological structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36943)
*Mattia Villani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是该系列第二篇论文，论证在额外维度紧致化为半径R的超球面的时空框架下，无需暗物质即可解释宇宙结构的形成，前提是额外维度处于收缩状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙学模型依赖暗物质来解释宇宙大尺度结构的形成与增长，但暗物质本质仍未探明。作者试图寻找一种不引入暗物质、而通过修改时空几何（额外维度）来替代其作用的解释方案。

Method: 采用具有额外维度的时空模型，将额外维度紧致化为半径为R的超球面，并在该几何框架下分析宇宙结构的增长过程，重点考察额外维度收缩情形对结构增长的影响。

Result: 证明即使在没有暗物质的宇宙中，宇宙结构依然能够增长，其条件为额外维度正在收缩。

Conclusion: 额外维度的收缩动力学可取代暗物质的功能，为宇宙结构的形成提供一种不依赖暗物质的几何解释，支持该系列论文关于"无需暗物质"的核心主张。

Abstract: This is the second paper of this series in which we prove we can do without dark matter using spacetime with extra dimensions compactified to a hypersphere with radius R. Here we prove cosmological structures can grow even in a Universe without dark matter, provided that extra dimensions are contracting.

</details>


### [231] [Post-Newtonian secular dynamics of hierarchical triples. I. Eccentricity-, inclination- and node-dependence of the multiple-scale formulation, and its canonical consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36990)
*Hideyoshi Arakida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文用双参数多尺度展开重新检验了Lim-Rodriguez (LR) 给出的层级三体系统领头后牛顿(1PN)交叉项，发现$\\delta\\varepsilon^{3/2}$项源于平均测度选择，在正则生成函数所需的时间测度下该项为零，与KST结果一致；$\\delta\\varepsilon^{7/2}$项仅在$m\\ll m_3$时质量依赖正确且角结构违反哈密顿性检验。数值积分表明ZLK共振调制在移除该项后仍存在，但加速并合消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lim和Rodriguez (LR) 计算的层级三体1PN交叉项在$\\delta\\varepsilon^{3/2}$和$\\delta\\varepsilon^{7/2}$阶与Kuntz、Serra和Trincherini (KST) 的有效场论结果存在分歧，需要厘清分歧来源并检验两种结果的正确性。

Method: 采用层级参数$\\varepsilon=a_1/a_2$与1PN参数$\\delta$的双参数多尺度展开，重构LR的计算；分析平均测度对四极矩周期解的影响，用单一相位变量进行自洽推导，并检验哈密顿性与合理性判据；最后用LR的长期演化代码做数值积分对比。

Result: $\\delta\\varepsilon^{3/2}$的"libration"项[LR Eq.(4.5)]源于平均测度：四极矩周期解对外真近点角均值为零，但被重新代入时间平均之下；闭式求值可精确复现该项，而在正则生成函数所需的时间测度下该项消失，与KST一致。Eq.(4.5)属于一个可移除哈密顿量流的单项，其伴随项缺失，故未通过合理性与哈密顿性检验。用单一相位变量自洽计算可得到完整的、规范等价的长期方程，而LR混合了真近点角四极矩解与偏近点角1PN解。实现的另外两个结构性缺陷不影响这些项。$\\delta\\varepsilon^{7/2}$项仅在$m\\ll m_3$时质量依赖正确，其角结构与KST不符并违反哈密顿性检验。数值积分显示LR报告的ZLK共振调制在移除Eq.(4.5)后依然存在，而八极矩导致的加速并合则消失。

Conclusion: LR与KST的分歧主要来自平均测度选择及相位变量使用不一致：$\\delta\\varepsilon^{3/2}$项在正确的正则时间测度下为零，支持KST结果；$\\delta\\varepsilon^{7/2}$项的质量依赖与角结构均存在问题。ZLK共振调制不依赖于Eq.(4.5)，但八极矩加速并合效应依赖于该项。

Abstract: We will re-examine the leading post-Newtonian (1PN) cross terms of hierarchical triples obtained by Lim and Rodriguez (LR) with a two-parameter multiple-scale expansion in the hierarchy parameter $\varepsilon=a_1/a_2$ and the 1PN parameter $δ$, which disagree with the effective-field-theory result of Kuntz, Serra, and Trincherini (KST) at the orders $δ\varepsilon^{3/2}$ and $δ\varepsilon^{7/2}$. Reconstructing the LR calculation, we show that the $δ\varepsilon^{3/2}$ ``libration'' term [LR Eq.~(4.5)] originates in the averaging measure: the quadrupole periodic solutions are made mean-free with respect to the outer true anomaly but re-substituted under the time average. The resulting term, evaluated in closed form, reproduces the implemented one exactly; with the time measure required of a canonical generating function it vanishes, in agreement with KST. Equation~(4.5) is one term of the flow of a removable Hamiltonian whose companion terms are absent, which is why it fails the rationality and Hamiltonicity tests. A calculation carried out consistently with a single phase variable yields the complete, gauge-equivalent secular equations, whereas LR's implementation combines a true-anomaly quadrupole solution with an eccentric-anomaly 1PN solution. Two structural defects of the implementation do not contribute to these terms. The $δ\varepsilon^{7/2}$ term of LR has the correct mass dependence only for $m\ll m_3$, and its angular structure disagrees with KST and violates the Hamiltonicity test. Finally, integrations with LR's secular code show that the resonant ZLK modulations reported by LR survive the removal of Eq.~(4.5), whereas their accelerated merger with the octupole disappears.

</details>


### [232] [Beyond circular and equatorial orbits: Bayesian constraint on vector charge with the adiabatic inspirals of generic Kerr geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37039)
*Tieguang Zi,Genliang Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了偏心且倾斜的Kerr轨道上轨道平均的电磁Carter常数通量，构建四维Chebyshev插值的绝热演化模型并集成到FastEMRIWaveform，实现对次级天体矢荷的贝叶斯推断，发现矢荷与源内禀参数强相关且约束对倾角呈非单调依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI通过引力波相位的累积效应可探测额外基本场，但一般Kerr轨道的矢量辐射建模需要Carter常数的演化，而该演化无法仅由能量和角动量平衡确定，因此需要建立更完整的绝热演化框架来研究矢量辐射通道。

Method: 推导偏心倾斜、非共振Kerr测地线的轨道平均电磁Carter常数通量（含无穷远与事件视界部分）；结合引力与电磁能量、角动量通量，采用四维Chebyshev插值方法构建一般绝热演化轨道；将相对论轨迹集成到FastEMRIWaveform包中，并对次级天体携带的矢荷进行贝叶斯推断。

Result: 分析揭示矢荷与源内禀参数之间存在显著相关性，且矢荷约束对轨道倾角呈现非单调依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了研究一般EMRI中额外矢量辐射通道及其对未来空间引力波观测检验引力理论意义的框架。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) offer a powerful probe of additional fundamental fields through their cumulative influence on gravitational-wave phases. However, modeling vector radiation from generic Kerr orbits requires the evolution of the Carter constant, which cannot be determined from energy and angular-momentum balance alone. Here we derive the orbit-averaged electromagnetic Carter-constant flux for eccentric and inclined, nonresonant Kerr geodesics, including sections at infinity and event horizon. Combining the gravitational and electromagnetic energy and angular-momentum fluxes, we construct generic adiabatic inspirals using a four-dimensional Chebyshev interpolation mathod. We incorporate the relativistic trajectories into the state of the art of FastEMRIWaveform package and perform Bayesian inference of vector charge carried by secondaries. Our analysis reveals substantial correlations between the charge and intrinsic source parameters, with vector charge constraints exhibiting a nonmonotonic dependence on orbital inclination. These results establish a framework for investigating additional vector-radiation channels in generic EMRIs and their implications for tests of gravity with future space-based gravitational wave observations.

</details>


### [233] [Self-Interacting Dark Matter-Induced Dynamical Friction and Its Imprint on EMRI Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37079)
*Yu Wang,Meilin Liu,Haiguang Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）对极端质量比旋近（EMRI）动力学与引力波相位演化的影响，构建了钱德拉塞卡动力学摩擦力的领头阶有效修正，并计算了由此产生的轨道演化、波形修改与累积相位偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI具有长时程引力波信号，是探测大质量黑洞周围暗物质环境的理想探针；但现有研究多针对无碰撞暗物质，SIDM的微观动量转移性质如何影响EMRI相位尚不清楚。

Method: 基于SIDM的碰撞玻尔兹曼描述，在传统钱德拉塞卡动力学摩擦力基础上引入领头阶有效修正项，该修正由动量转移截面σ_T与无量纲碰撞参数n_χσ_T r = r/λ_mfp控制；随后计算轨道演化、波形修正及累积引力波退相位，并区分暗物质总退相位与自相互作用额外贡献。

Result: 在增强阻力区间，SIDM自相互作用增强会提高有效动力学摩擦力，加速旋近过程，并相对于无碰撞暗物质情形产生逐渐增大的相位偏差；自相互作用诱导的额外相位修正可与总暗物质退相位相区分。

Conclusion: EMRI的累积引力波相位对暗物质微观动量转移性质具有敏感性，表明LISA等未来空间引力波探测器有望成为探测大质量黑洞附近SIDM的互补手段。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) provide a promising probe of the dark matter environment surrounding massive black holes through their long-duration gravitational-wave signals. In this work, we investigate the influence of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) on EMRI dynamics and gravitational-wave phase evolution. Motivated by the collisional Boltzmann description of SIDM, we construct a leading-order effective correction to the conventional Chandrasekhar dynamical-friction force, controlled by the momentum-transfer cross section $σ_T$ and the dimensionless collisionality parameter $n_χσ_T r=r/λ_{\rm mfp}$. We then compute the resulting orbital evolution, waveform modification, and accumulated gravitational-wave dephasing. In the enhanced-drag regime considered here, increasing the SIDM self-interaction strength increases the effective dynamical friction, accelerates the inspiral, and produces a progressively larger phase deviation relative to the collisionless dark matter case. We further distinguish the total dark-matter-induced dephasing relative to vacuum from the additional phase correction generated specifically by dark matter self-interactions. Our results demonstrate that the cumulative gravitational-wave phase of EMRIs can be sensitive to the microscopic momentum-transfer properties of dark matter, suggesting that future space-based detectors such as LISA may provide a complementary probe of SIDM in the vicinity of massive black holes.

</details>


### [234] [Closing the Loop: from EPRL-FK spinfoams to Regge dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37179)
*Matteo Bruno,Pietro Donà,Gowrisankar Sreeram*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将EPRL-FK自旋泡沫模型改写为楔-和乐变量，识别并施加"几何闭合"约束，可重建弯曲几何，使该模型在半经典极限下等价于Regge微积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EPRL-FK自旋泡沫模型在半经典、固定离散化下存在重大局限：其主导构型只能描述平坦几何，无法重现广义相对论弯曲时空的物理。作者希望找到缺失的额外条件，使变分原理能够给出Regge微积分（广义相对论的标准离散化）的运动方程。

Method: 将EPRL-FK模型重述为楔-和乐（wedge-holonomy）变量形式；把几何闭合（geometric closure）识别为缺失的条件，并将其作为与局部平坦性并列的额外约束施加于约束面。在规则、非退化、具有类空四面体的洛伦兹分支上（模去规范），分析该约束面与长度-Regge几何空间的关系；限制到该面后考察楔作用量及其切向变分。最后提出在自旋泡沫振幅中直接实现几何闭合的方案。

Result: 在规则、非退化洛伦兹分支（类空四面体）上，模去规范后，施加几何闭合与局部平坦性所定义的约束面与长度-Regge几何空间等价；限制到该面上的楔作用量化为Regge作用量，其切向变分给出长度-Regge方程。

Conclusion: 几何闭合是EPRL-FK模型半经典极限中缺失的条件。将其作为额外约束施加后，EPRL-FK模型在半经典区域与Regge微积分等价，从而能够重建弯曲几何。作者进一步提出在自旋泡沫振幅中直接实现几何闭合的途径。

Abstract: The EPRL-FK spinfoam model in its semiclassical regime at fixed discretization faces a big limitation: its dominant configurations describe only flat geometries. We ask what additional condition is needed for the variational principle to reproduce the equations of motion of Regge calculus, the standard discretization of General Relativity. We recast the EPRL-FK model in wedge-holonomy variables and identify geometric closure as the missing condition. We prove that imposing geometric closure as an additional constraint alongside local flatness allows us to reconstruct curved geometries. On a regular, nondegenerate Lorentzian branch with spacelike tetrahedra, modulo gauge, they define a constraint surface equivalent to the space of length-Regge geometries. The wedge action restricted to this surface becomes the Regge action, and its tangent variations yield the length-Regge equations. Finally, we propose a way to implement geometric closure directly in the spinfoam amplitude. With this additional constraint, the EPRL-FK model is therefore equivalent to Regge calculus in its semiclassical regime.

</details>


### [235] [The Irreversible Depletion of Dark Matter Spikes by Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37234)
*Charlie Sharpe,Yonadav Barry Ginat,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Alexander Heger,Bence Kocsis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: EMRIs在探测暗物质尖峰的同时也会通过引力弹弓效应将其破坏，大幅削弱了重力波相位偏差信号，缩小了通过该通道探测暗物质的 prospects。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为大质量黑洞周围的致密暗物质尖峰会在EMRI中产生可被LISA观测到的重力波相位偏差。但论文质疑：用于探测暗物质尖峰的EMRI本身是否会破坏这些尖峰，即探测器与被测对象之间的自反馈效应未被充分考虑。

Method: 将核星团的Fokker-Planck模型与EMRI-暗物质相遇的后牛顿三体模拟相结合，追踪重复引力弹弓（gravitational slingshots）在数十亿年时间尺度上对暗物质粒子从星团"损失锥"（loss cone）中被不可逆抛射的过程。

Result: 对于z=3、质量≲10^5 M⊙的MBH，即使保守的EMRI率O(1-10) Gyr⁻¹也足以使尖峰密度降低数个量级；对于更现实的O(100-300) Gyr⁻¹，该质量范围扩展到≲10^6 M⊙。因此预期的重力波相位偏差被极大削弱。仅质量较大、红移较高的系统（如z≳5、~10^6 M⊙、EMRI率≲100 Gyr⁻¹）才能产生超过1弧度的相位偏差。

Conclusion: EMRI对暗物质尖峰具有破坏作用，导致重力波相位偏差信号显著减弱，从而窄化了通过EMRI重力波去相位探测暗物质的前景。只有特定高质量、高红移、低EMRI率的系统仍可作为潜在探测目标。

Abstract: Dense dark matter spikes around massive black holes (MBHs) have been widely predicted to produce gravitational-wave dephasing in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) that are potentially observable by LISA. We show that EMRIs, the very objects used to probe these spikes, also destroy them. Combining Fokker-Planck models of nuclear star clusters with post-Newtonian three-body simulations of EMRI-dark matter encounters, we demonstrate that repeated gravitational slingshots irreversibly eject dark matter particles from the cluster's loss cone over gigayear time-scales. For MBHs at $z = 3$ with masses $\lesssim 10^5 \,M_\odot$, even conservative EMRI rates of $\mathcal{O}(1-10) \, \mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$ suffice to deplete the spike density by orders of magnitude; for more realistic rates of $\mathcal{O}(100-300) \, \mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$, the mass range extends to $\lesssim 10^6 \,M_\odot$. As a result, the gravitational-wave dephasing expected from such systems is greatly reduced, narrowing the prospects of detecting DM through this channel. Only heavy, high-redshift systems with sufficiently low EMRI rates -- for example, a $\sim 10^6 \,M_\odot$ MBH at $z \gtrsim 5$ with $\lesssim 100$ EMRIs per Gyr -- can induce dephasing that exceeds one radian.

</details>


### [236] [Gravitational-Wave Inference For Noise PSD Jumps Across Data Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37248)
*Jian-Ming Yan,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 针对长时引力波数据中短间隙且PSD大幅跳变的情形，提出两阶段"有效性驱动"补救方案：预白化（PW）+自适应间隙扩展（AGE），以在WDM时频域贝叶斯间隙增广框架下恢复局部对角近似的有效性，从而获得更紧、更准确的后验并保持计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时引力波推断必然面临数据间隙，且常伴随探测器噪声功率谱密度（PSD）的突变与非平稳变化。传统做法将间隙前、后数据分开分析，导致可避免的信息损失并可能降低参数推断质量。而现有WDM贝叶斯间隙增广方法在"短间隙+大PSD幅度跳变"这一重要失效模式下，间隙边缘沿时间方向的局部对角（类Whittle）近似不再成立，可能造成推断有偏或稳健性下降，因此需要在不做全分段的前提下恢复局部对角近似的有效性。

Method: 在Wilson–Daubechies–Meyer（WDM）时频域贝叶斯间隙增广基础上，提出两阶段、以有效性为驱动的补救方法：第一阶段为频域先验预测预白化（PW），利用端点信息引入噪声信息，以缓解导致对角行为不佳的主要失配，在保持计算可行性的同时获得更有利的WDM表示；第二阶段，当时间方向平滑性判据仍不满足时，采用自适应间隙扩展（AGE），仅在间隙边界周围最小邻域内选择性地扩大间隙，以恢复局部对角近似的有效性。通过具有嵌合式PSD过渡的玩具模型仿真进行验证。

Result: 玩具模型仿真（含嵌合式PSD过渡）表明，WDM+PW+AGE相比单侧（间隙前/间隙后）分析能给出显著更紧、更准确的后验分布，同时保持适合下一代任务的计算效率。

Conclusion: PW与AGE相结合的两阶段策略可在短间隙且PSD大幅跳变的情形下恢复WDM局部对角近似的有效性，避免整体分段，兼顾推断精度与计算效率，适用于下一代引力波探测任务的长时数据分析。

Abstract: Long-duration gravitational-wave (GW) inference inevitably encounters data gaps, which are often accompanied by abrupt, non-stationary changes in the detector noise power spectral density (PSD). Conventional practice typically analyzes the pre-gap and post-gap data separately, causing avoidable information loss and potentially degrading parameter inference. We build on Bayesian gap augmentation in the Wilson--Daubechies--Meyer (WDM) time--frequency domain and focus on a practically important failure mode: short gaps with large PSD amplitude jumps. In this regime, the local diagonal (Whittle-like) approximation that underpins the WDM likelihood can no longer be relied upon in the time direction near the gap edges, which may lead to biased and/or less robust inference unless the local diagonal validity is restored without resorting to full segmentation. We propose a two-stage, validity-driven remedy. First, frequency-domain prior-predictive prewhitening (PW) incorporates endpoint-informed noise information to mitigate the dominant mismatch responsible for poor diagonal behavior, enabling a more favorable WDM representation while retaining computational tractability. Second, when a time-direction smoothness criterion still fails, we apply adaptive gap expanding (AGE), selectively enlarging only the minimal neighborhood around the gap boundaries needed to restore local diagonal validity. Toy-model simulations with chimeric PSD transitions show that WDM+PW+AGE yields substantially tighter and more accurate posteriors than single-sided pre-gap/post-gap analyses, while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for next-generation missions.

</details>


### [237] [The alternative scalar field dark sector of the Universe: Cosmological inflation, quintessence and ekpyrotic model in the framework of scalar-tensor cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37293)
*Marcin Postolak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该博士论文系统研究暗部门（dark sector）的标量场描述，涵盖标量-张量宇宙学、暴胀有效场论、精质（quintessence）以及火劫/循环（ekpyrotic/cyclic）替代模型四大主题，并结合DESI DR2观测动机讨论动态暗能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗部门的物理本质尚不明确：宇宙学常数面临理论困难，且DESI DR2等观测暗示暗能量可能随时间演化。因此需要从标量场这一统一视角检验暗能量的动力学描述、暴胀的紫外敏感性，以及替代暴胀的循环图景。

Method: 论文采用理论分析与文献综合的方法，分四部分展开：（1）从Brans-Dicke理论推广到一般非最小耦合标量场，讨论Jordan与Einstein共形框架及共形变换；（2）以有效场论处理暴胀，分析时间平移自发破缺、Goldstone描述绝热扰动、高维算子、辐射修正与Planck压低项及eta问题；（3）研究精质的tracking、scaling、thawing、freezing行为及statefinder、$(ω_φ,ω'_φ)$平面、$Om(z)$等诊断；（4）分析火劫相作为超硬状态方程驱动的平滑机制，以及扰动、非高斯性、张量模、反弹条件、熵产生、测地不完备性与相分辨场空间距离界限。

Result: 论文给出了标量场作为修改物质或额外引力自由度的理论框架；阐明了暴胀有效场论中的紫外敏感性与eta问题；梳理了精质各类动力学行为并借助观测诊断与DESI DR2结果论证超越严格宇宙学常数的时变暗能量；并刻画了火劫/循环模型中扰动、非高斯性、反弹条件及场空间距离约束等关键性质。

Conclusion: 标量场为描述暗部门提供了统一且灵活的语言：既可充当修改引力自由度，也可实现暴胀、动态暗能量与循环宇宙图景。未来需借助观测诊断（如DESI DR2）区分动态暗能量与宇宙学常数，并进一步厘清暴胀有效场论的紫外完备性及循环模型的反弹与奇点问题。

Abstract: This dissertation investigates scalar field descriptions of the dark sector (of the Universe), with emphasis on scalar-tensor cosmology, inflationary effective field theory, quintessence, and ekpyrotic/cyclic alternatives to inflation.
  The thesis first reviews scalar-tensor theories of gravity, starting from Brans-Dicke theory and extending to general non-minimally coupled scalar fields. Particular attention is paid to the interpretation of the scalar field, the relation between the Jordan and Einstein conformal frames, and the role of conformal transformations in cosmology. This provides the theoretical basis for treating scalar fields either as modified matter or as additional gravitational degrees of freedom.
  The second part discusses cosmological inflation from the viewpoint of effective field theory. The spontaneous breaking of time translations, the Goldstone description of adiabatic perturbations, higher-dimensional operators, radiative corrections and Planck-suppressed terms are analyzed in the context of ultraviolet sensitivity and the eta problem.
  The third part is devoted to quintessence as the simplest dynamical dark energy model. 'Tracking', 'scaling', 'thawing' and 'freezing' behaviors are discussed, together with observational diagnostics such as the statefinder parameters, the $(ω_φ,ω'_φ)$ plane and the $Om(z)$ diagnostic. The DESI DR2 results are used as motivation for considering time-dependent effective dark energy beyond a strict cosmological constant.
  The final part studies ekpyrotic and cyclic cosmologies. The ekpyrotic phase is analyzed as a smoothing mechanism based on an ultra-stiff scalar field equation of state. Perturbations, non-Gaussianity, tensor modes, bounce conditions, entropy production, geodesic incompleteness and phase-resolved field-space distance bounds are discussed.

</details>


### [238] [Scaling RIFT 1: Extending RIFT to third-generation gravitational-wave analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37393)
*R. O'Shaughnessy,A. Jan,J. Lange,R. Mechum*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作为第三代引力波探测器的高信噪比致密双星信号参数估计，扩展了RIFT流水线，实现对任意波形模型的通用、稳健推断，无需中间预训练数据产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波探测器观测到的致密双星信号信噪比极高（可达数百至数千），且地球自转与探测器大尺度物理尺寸导致探测器响应随时间和频率变化，使信号解读复杂。现有方法依赖降阶近似或归一化流等预训练中间数据产品，缺乏通用性和稳健性。

Method: 借鉴此前针对LISA的工作，作者为RIFT参数估计流水线实现了通用且稳健的扩展，使其能够使用任意现有波形模型进行推断，无需依赖降阶近似或归一化流等中间预训练数据产品。

Result: 在三站点下一代探测器网络中，对有限尺寸双中子星注入信号在SNR=1000条件下实现端到端参数恢复，单块GPU上数分钟内将源的外禀参数定位到0.8×10⁻³ deg²。

Conclusion: 扩展后的RIFT流水线可高效、精确地处理第三代探测器的高信噪比信号，实现快速高精度定位，且不依赖预训练中间数据产品。

Abstract: Interpretation of compact binary signals observed by third-generation gravitational-wave detectors is complicated by their high signal-to-noise ratios, often reaching hundreds to thousands, and by time- and frequency-dependent detector responses arising from Earth's rotation and the detectors' large physical extent. Building on prior work for LISA, we implement generic, robust extensions of the RIFT parameter estimation pipeline that enable interpretation of these sources using any existing waveform model, without recourse to intermediate pretrained data products like reduced-order approximations or normalizing flows. We demonstrate end-to-end recovery of finite-size binary-neutron-star injections in a three-site next-generation network to $\mathrm{SNR}=1000$, localizing the source's extrinsic parameters to $0.8\times10^{-3}\,\mathrm{deg}^2$ in minutes on a single GPU.

</details>


### [239] [Asymptotics of Killing horizons: A Characterization in de Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37427)
*Carlos Peón-Nieto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文证明de Sitter时空可由其Killing视界与共形边界的交唯一刻画，并用共形不变量σ分类Killing视界、给出表面重力的内蕴共形不变公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Λ>0真空时空（de Sitter）的Killing视界与共形边界的关系，寻求从边界数据重构体时空Killing向量场并对视界进行分类和量化。

Method: 利用Killing向量场到共形边界𝓘的延拓，通过其在𝓘上的孤立本质零点刻画视界与边界的交；由边界值重构全部de Sitter Killing向量场，并引入共形不变量σ进行分类。

Result: 证明(n+1)维de Sitter时空是唯一（局部）满足Killing视界与𝓘相交的Λ>0真空时空；σ<0对应非退化分叉视界，σ=0对应退化非分叉视界；并得到表面重力的内蕴共形不变公式κ²=|σ|。

Conclusion: de Sitter Killing视界可由共形边界上的数据完全确定，共形不变量σ同时刻画视界类型并给出表面重力，建立了边界与体时空几何的对应关系。

Abstract: We prove that (n + 1)-dimensional de Sitter spacetime is locally the unique $(Λ> 0)$-vacuum spacetime whose Killing horizons intersect the conformal boundary $\mathscr{I}$. The intersection occurs at the isolated essential zeros of the extension of the Killing vector field to $\mathscr{I}$. We then reconstruct all de Sitter Killing vector fields from their boundary value and obtain a characterization of those admitting a Killing horizon in terms of a conformal invariant $σ$: $σ<0$ characterizes non-degenerate bifurcate horizons, whereas $σ= 0$ yields degenerate non-bifurcate horizons. Finally, we derive an intrinsic, conformally invariant formula for the surface gravity of the horizons in de Sitter, $κ^2 = |σ|$.

</details>


### [240] [Oscillatory dark energy with phantom crossings in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37437)
*Saddam Hussain,Sandip Biswas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Einstein-标量-Gauss-Bonnet引力框架下，通过混合指数-二次势与局域高斯GB耦合构建振荡暗能量模型，实现了既多次穿越phantom分界又无鬼模与梯度不稳定性、并趋向稳定de Sitter吸引子的可行参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI重子声学振荡与超新星联合观测显示暗能量状态方程可能随红移演化（高红移phantom、低红移转向quintessence）。除单调演化外，振荡型暗能量是另一种实现动力学暗能量的可能方案，本文旨在构建并检验此类振荡场景。

Method: 采用Einstein-标量-Gauss-Bonnet引力：标量场具混合指数-二次势，与Gauss-Bonnet不变量非最小耦合，GB耦合函数取围绕势能极小值局域化的高斯形式。分析晚期演化（场在势阱极小值附近振荡并趋于稳定de Sitter吸引子），并检验微扰稳定性（鬼模、梯度/声速稳定性）。

Result: 最小耦合极限下，有效状态方程在低红移z~0处于quintessence振荡且不穿越phantom分界；非最小耦合放大振荡并使有效状态方程多次穿越phantom分界。过强耦合会导致标量与张量声速为负，微扰模式不稳定。存在参数空间区域可同时满足多次phantom穿越、无鬼模与梯度不稳定性、并趋向稳定de Sitter吸引子。

Conclusion: Einstein-标量-Gauss-Bonnet框架中的振荡暗能量可作为动力学暗能量的一种可行实现，其参数空间允许多次phantom穿越且保持理论自洽与稳定未来演化。

Abstract: Recent baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), combined with supernova observations, show a mild preference for an evolving dark-energy equation of state, in which dark energy remains phantom-like at higher redshift while a transition toward the quintessence regime emerges around $z\lesssim0.5$. Beyond the monotonic evolution of the equation of state, an oscillatory dark-energy scenario provides a potential alternative realization of dynamical dark energy. We study such an oscillatory scenario within the Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity framework, where a scalar field with a hybrid exponential-quadratic potential couples nonminimally to the Gauss-Bonnet (GB) invariant. We choose a Gaussian GB coupling function localized around the minimum of the potential. During the late-time epoch, the field thereby oscillates around the potential minimum and eventually exits toward a stable de Sitter attractor phase. In the minimally coupled limit, the effective equation of state oscillates in the quintessence regime at low redshift, $z\sim0$, without crossing the phantom divide. The nonminimal coupling amplifies the oscillations and drives the effective equation of state across the phantom divide multiple times. Although a sufficiently strong coupling can induce negative scalar and tensor sound speeds, rendering the perturbation modes unstable. We identify a region of parameter space in which the model undergoes multiple phantom crossings while remaining free of ghost and gradient instabilities and approaching a stable de Sitter attractor in the future.

</details>


### [241] [Black objects in a five-dimensional swirling spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37511)
*Adriano Viganò*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将"旋涡宇宙"（swirling universe）推广到五维，并成功嵌入Myers-Perry黑洞与旋转黑环，证明两解均无锥形奇点，计算其质量与角动量并讨论唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋涡宇宙解多限于四维，缺乏高维推广；同时在旋转真空解族中，如何将Myers-Perry黑洞和旋转黑环统一纳入一个共同背景，是检验解结构与唯一性的重要问题。

Method: 构造五维旋涡宇宙的推广度规，将Myers-Perry黑洞与旋转黑环作为其中的特例嵌入；通过检验轴线/奇点条件证明无锥形奇点，分析几何性质，并利用Komar积分等方法计算质量与角动量，进而讨论唯一性。

Result: 所得五维度规可同时容纳Myers-Perry黑洞与旋转黑环；两解均无锥形奇点，几何性质（如渐近结构、视界与轴线行为）被明确刻画，并给出其质量与角动量表达式及唯一性结论。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了五维旋涡宇宙的统一框架，表明两个著名旋转解可嵌入同一背景，为高维旋转真空解的分类与唯一性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We construct a five-dimensional generalization of the swirling universe and embed in it the Myers-Perry black hole and the rotating black ring. We prove that both solutions are free of conical singularities, analyze their geometrical properties, compute their mass and angular momentum, and discuss their uniqueness.

</details>


### [242] [Deformation Quantization of Schutz Quantum Cosmology: Relational Time, Constraints, and Physical States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37585)
*Raphael Fracalossi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在FLRW宇宙学耦合Schutz流体的模型中，建立了约化关系动力学与扩展约束量子化之间的受控对应，通过正则变换得到去参数化约束 $\mathcal C=p_T+H$，并在Weyl--Wigner扩展框架下给出显式的分布映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系动力学（relational dynamics）与扩展约束量子化通常被视为两种不同框架，二者关系缺乏明确的对应性刻画；本文旨在给出二者的受控对应，厘清约化量子态如何在扩展约束体系中实现。

Method: 1）在FLRW宇宙学耦合Schutz完美流体的模型内，对物质扇区作正则变换，得到去参数化约束 $\mathcal C=p_T+H$，$T$ 作为内时；2）在扩展Weyl--Wigner表述中双向施加量子约束；3）分析反对称部分（Moyal演化）与对称部分（量子壳条件）；4）对固定自伴实现 $\hat H$，构造由负谱子空间支撑的约化密度算符到分布性扩展的显式映射。

Result: 反对称部分复现由约化哈密顿量生成的精确Moyal演化；对称部分给出独立的量子壳条件。谱对（能量 $E,E'$）被映射到支撑于 $p_T=-(E+E')/2$ 的clock Wigner分布，关系相位由 $E-E'$ 决定。该映射保持非对角相干性，同时满足两个星约束，并以约化Wigner态为其 $p_T$ 边缘分布，因此在可容许正密度扇区上是单射。平坦尘埃模型（$q=1/2$、Dirichlet数据）中，约化谱为 $(-\infty,0]$，可容许扇区包含全部可归一化约化态。

Conclusion: 约化关系动力学与扩展约束量子化之间存在显式、保持相干性的对应关系，该对应针对固定的约束代表与量子实现成立，但依赖所选实现而非对所有代表普遍成立。

Abstract: A controlled correspondence is established between reduced relational dynamics and extended constrained quantization in an FLRW cosmology coupled to a Schutz perfect fluid. A canonical transformation in the matter sector yields a deparametrized constraint of the form $\mathcal C=p_T+H$, with $T$ serving as an internal time. In the extended Weyl--Wigner formulation, the quantum constraint is imposed bilaterally. Its antisymmetric part gives the exact Moyal evolution generated by the reduced Hamiltonian, while its symmetric part imposes an independent quantum shell condition. For a fixed self-adjoint realization of $\hat H$, every reduced density operator supported on its negative spectral subspace admits an explicit distributional extension. A spectral dyad with energies $E$ and $E'$ is mapped to a clock Wigner distribution supported at $p_T=-(E+E')/2$, with relational phase determined by $E-E'$. The map preserves off-diagonal coherences, satisfies both star constraints, and has the reduced Wigner state as its $p_T$ marginal; it is therefore injective on the admissible positive-density sector. A flat dust model with $q=1/2$ and Dirichlet data provides an explicit example: its reduced spectrum is $(-\infty,0]$, so the admissible sector contains all normalizable reduced states. The correspondence applies to a fixed constraint representative and quantum realization.

</details>


### [243] [On more general Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary terms for f(R) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37692)
*Federico Scali,Sergio Luigi Cacciatori,Matteo Galaverni,Gabriele Gionti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在$f(R)$引力中引入比现有文献更一般的边界条件，使定态作用量原理适定，并显式计算了相应的Gibbons-Hawking-York边界项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知文献中的边界条件仅为特例，限制了可容许解的空间；需要更一般的边界条件以扩大适定变分问题的适用范围。

Method: 推广边界条件，使其涵盖已有情形，并在任意解析$f(R)$下显式推导对应的Gibbons-Hawking-York边界项。

Result: 所得边界条件包含已知情况，扩大了可容许解的空间；Gibbons-Hawking-York边界项对任意解析$f(R)$均成立。

Conclusion: 结论中展望了该结果在标量-张量映射及黑洞热力学定律方面的应用前景。

Abstract: More general boundary conditions are introduced - with respect to known literature - which produce a well-posed stationary action principle in $f(R)$ gravity. These conditions encompass the known cases, thus enlarging the space of admissible solutions. The corresponding Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary terms are explicitly computed and hold for any analytic f(R). The perspectives of application concerning the scalar-tensor mapping and the laws of black-hole thermodynamics are outlined in the conclusions.

</details>


### [244] [Quasinormal-mode analysis of a massive scalar field in a Schwarzschild background via the spectral method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37942)
*Davide Batic,Anna Chrysostomou,Alan S. Cornell,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 采用扩展Chebyshev谱方法求解Schwarzschild背景上的大质量标量准正规模谱问题，通过单值化处理双叶质量色散关系，将径向问题转化为七次多项式特征值问题，获得低多极矩、高阶泛音及跨越质量阈值的稳定谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量场准正规模计算在质量色散关系的双叶解析结构下存在困难，WKB与连分数方法在部分区域（如跨越质量阈值、高阶泛音）适用性受限，需要一个受控的基准框架来验证大质量场准正规模计算。

Method: 扩展Chebyshev谱方法。先对双叶解析的质量色散关系进行单值化处理，再进行离散化；将径向问题转化为七次多项式特征值问题，并在其中纳入视界与空间无穷远处的准正规模边界条件。

Result: 对低多极矩、高阶泛音以及标称质量阈值两侧的频率均获得稳定谱，包括WKB和连分数方法难以适用的区域。对于复频率，远场行为不能仅由频率实部判定，而取决于复波数及其Riemann叶。识别出阻尼率近似均匀间距的稳定纯虚根族。

Conclusion: 该Schwarzschild标量问题旨在作为大质量场准正规模计算的受控基准，而非张量型Kerr铃宕的直接模型。纯虚根族的物理解释仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We study the massive scalar quasinormal-mode spectral problem on a Schwarzschild background using an extended Chebyshev spectral method. The massive dispersion relation has a two-sheeted analytic structure, which we uniformise before discretisation. This converts the radial problem into a seventh-degree polynomial eigenvalue problem while incorporating the quasinormal-mode boundary conditions at the horizon and spatial infinity. The method yields stable spectra for low multipoles, high overtones, and frequencies on both sides of the nominal mass threshold, including regimes in which WKB and continued-fraction approaches become difficult to apply. For complex frequencies, the far-field behaviour cannot be classified from the real part of the frequency alone, but depends on the complex wavenumber and its Riemann sheet. We also identify stable families of purely imaginary roots with approximately uniform spacing in their damping rates, whose physical interpretation remains open. The Schwarzschild scalar problem is intended as a controlled benchmark for massive-field quasinormal-mode calculations rather than as a direct model of tensorial Kerr ringdown.

</details>


### [245] [Orbital stability, relativistic precession, and QPO constraints for constant-curvature black holes in $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37962)
*Kourosh Nozari,Sara Saghafi,Khadijeh Salahshour,Moisés Bravo-Gaete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在度规 $f(R)$ 引力的常曲率真空扇区中，分析圆轨道稳定性、本轮共振、相对论进动与 QPO 约束；贝叶斯 MCMC 结合 GRO J1655-40 的 QPO 三峰与动力学质量测量给出 $R_0M^2=-5.57^{+12.94}_{-16.15}\times10^{-4}$（68%），结果与 Kerr 一致，无显著偏离证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 Kerr 之外的引力修正是否必要。度规 $f(R)$ 引力在常曲率真空扇区可给出 Schwarzschild/Kerr-(反)de Sitter 背景，是检验强场引力与黑洞时空的简单可计算窗口；同时 QPO 观测与动力学质量测量提供了联合约束手段，可用以量化曲率参数 $R_0$ 是否被数据支持。

Method: 在满足代数迹方程的常曲率 $R_0$ 背景下，推导 Schwarzschild-(反)de Sitter 与 Kerr-(反)de Sitter 时空的静止坐标时轨道频率与本轮频率，确定稳定类时圆轨道区域；计算 3:2、2:1、3:1 共振分支与带符号节点进动（回到 Lense-Thirring 极限）；评估角动量加权刚体流转进动与粘性对齐。采用贝叶斯 MCMC，将 GRO J1655-40 同时 QPO 三峰与独立动力学质量测量联合拟合，条件于测地线相对论进动方案与所采用的时间归一化。

Result: 正曲率使稳定运动被限制在最内与最外稳定圆轨道之间，二者在临界曲率处合并，并在适当频率比下允许两个共振半径；负曲率允许稳定运动延伸至任意大半径，且 $Ω_θ/Ω_r\rightarrow1/2$，而渐近平坦极限为 1。所得曲率约束为 $R_0M^2=-5.57^{+12.94}_{-16.15}\times10^{-4}$（68% 置信度）。Kerr 仍被允许，非零曲率对拟合改善可忽略；质量不确定度加倍使曲率区间约展宽 2.1 倍，移除质量似然则出现先验依赖的扩展退化。

Conclusion: 该分析给出的是 QPO 与动力学质量的联合约束，而非纯 QPO 约束；数据对偏离 Kerr 没有显著证据，Kerr 仍完全兼容，$f(R)$ 曲率参数仅被弱限制且高度依赖先验与进动方案假设。

Abstract: We analyze circular-orbit stability, epicyclic resonances, relativistic precession, and quasi-periodic-oscillation (QPO) constraints in the constant-curvature vacuum sector of metric $f(R)$ gravity. The Schwarzschild--(anti-)de~Sitter and Kerr--(anti-)de~Sitter backgrounds are characterized by curvature $R_0$ satisfying the algebraic trace equation. We derive stationary-coordinate-time orbital and epicyclic frequencies and determine stable timelike circular-orbit domains. Positive curvature confines stable motion between innermost and outermost stable circular orbits, which merge at a critical curvature, and permits two resonance radii for suitable frequency ratios. Negative curvature allows stable motion to arbitrarily large radii, with $Ω_θ/Ω_r\rightarrow1/2$, compared with unity in the asymptotically flat limit. We determine the $3{:}2$, $2{:}1$, and $3{:}1$ resonance branches and signed nodal precession, recovering the Lense--Thirring limit. We also evaluate angular-momentum-weighted rigid-flow precession and viscous alignment, with the outermost stable orbit bounding positive-curvature flows. A Bayesian Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis combining the simultaneous GRO J1655--40 QPO triplet with an independent dynamical-mass measurement yields $R_0M^2=-5.57^{+12.94}_{-16.15}\times10^{-4}$ at $68\%$ credibility, conditional on the geodesic relativistic-precession prescription and adopted time normalization. Kerr remains allowed, with negligible fit improvement from nonzero curvature. Doubling the mass uncertainty broadens the curvature interval by approximately $2.1$; removing the mass likelihood reveals an extended, prior-dependent degeneracy. This is therefore a joint QPO and dynamical-mass constraint, with no significant evidence for departure from Kerr.

</details>


### [246] [Fast and Flow-rious: Gravitational Wave Background Bayesian Model Comparison using Normalizing Flows and Nested Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38122)
*David C. Wright,Aaron D. Johnson,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,William G. Lamb*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合归一化流、嵌套采样与边缘化PTA似然的贝叶斯模型比较方法，可在消费级硬件上数分钟内完成任意GWB谱模型的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA合作组已宣布纳米赫兹引力波背景证据，下一步核心目标是刻画GWB并识别其来源。现有PTA文献中的贝叶斯因子计算方法（如积空间采样、热力学积分）在模型差异大或非嵌套时计算代价高、效率低，亟需高效通用的模型比较方法。

Method: 该方法融合归一化流、嵌套采样，以及对所有非GWB参数边缘化的PTA似然。流模型由新开发的Python包coppuccino训练，通过学习数据的copula——即变换到均匀边缘分布后的依赖结构——来拟合似然。

Result: 在解析目标与模拟PTA数据上验证：解析证据在误差范围内被复现，且将贝叶斯因子作为注入模型的二分类器时，AUC得分达0.996。方法可在消费级硬件上数分钟内完成任意GWB谱模型的贝叶斯模型比较。

Conclusion: 所提方法高效、通用，显著加速PTA中GWB谱模型的贝叶斯模型比较，为刻画GWB及识别其来源提供了实用工具。

Abstract: In 2023, pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations around the world announced evidence for a nanohertz gravitational-wave background (GWB). Beyond increasing the detection significance, the next major goal is to characterize the GWB and identify its source(s). The typical model-comparison metric used in PTA analyses is the Bayes factor (BF). However, BF computation methods used in the PTA literature, such as product-space sampling and thermodynamic integration, are computationally expensive or inefficient if the models compared are dissimilar or non-nested. In this work, we introduce a PTA model-comparison method using a combination of normalizing flows, nested sampling, and a PTA likelihood that is marginalized over all non-GWB parameters. The flows are trained using a newly-developed Python package coppuccino, which learns the data's copula: the dependence structure after transforming to uniform marginals. We validate the method on both analytic targets and simulated PTA data, recovering the analytic evidence within error bars and achieving an area-under-the-curve (AUC) score of 0.996 when treating the BFs as binary classifiers of the injected models. The result is a method to perform Bayesian model comparison of arbitrary GWB spectral models in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [247] [Polar-metric/axial-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33511)
*Abhishake Sadhukhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究MMP可穿越虫洞的线性微扰，将极向度规微扰与规范场轴微扰约化为两个耦合的Zerilli-Moncrief主方程，精确计及费米子对微扰的响应与诱导Hall电流，得到O(α)修正的闭式解并讨论稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMP虫洞喉部由带电费米子最低朗道能级的Casimir能量维持张开，其线性稳定性与微扰结构尚需系统研究；此前MMP处理忽略了应力张量的迹部分，需检验自洽性。

Method: 在磁荷背景上处理极向度规微扰与规范场轴微扰耦合的扇区，约化为两个Zerilli-Moncrief型耦合主方程；从二维共形反常精确计算费米子响应（含MMP舍弃的迹部分）并引入诱导Hall电流；由费米子有效作用量导出Hall电流需带系数c/48π的Euler项（类似Wen-Zee项的磁通-曲率耦合）；在α=0时喉部为精确AdS₂×S²，模式解耦为两个Pöschl-Teller问题，并给出O(α)修正的闭式解。

Result: α=0时两模式质量分别为l(l-1)与(l+1)(l+2)，频率间隔为1/ℓ；同时包含费米子响应与Hall电流时线性化Bianchi恒等式在一阶α成立；O(α)修正混合两模式且依赖频率，并在重叠区与虫洞口匹配；势的对称部分在整个虫洞内为正，排除纯指数增长；修正降低正常频率并劈裂两模式共享的能级，一阶α为实位移；该扇区在l≥2时此阶无增长模式。

Conclusion: MMP虫洞的极向-轴微扰扇区在一阶费米子反作用α下自洽，O(α)修正给出实频率位移与能级劈裂且不产生l≥2的不稳定增长模式，表明该扇区线性稳定。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) traversable wormhole. On a magnetically charged background, polar metric perturbations couple to axial perturbations of the gauge field. We treat this sector and reduce it to two coupled master equations of Zerilli-Moncrief type. The wormhole has two regions. In the mouths the geometry is a nearly extremal magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole with no quantum source. In the throat the Casimir energy of the lowest Landau level of charged fermions holds the wormhole open. We show that the throat equations are consistent only if the fermions respond to the perturbation, computed exactly from the two-dimensional conformal anomaly with the full stress tensor including the trace part that MMP discard. This response must be accompanied by an induced Hall current of the fermions. With both effects included, the linearised Bianchi identities hold at first order in the fermionic backreaction $α$. Deriving the Hall current from the fermion effective action instead requires an Euler term with coefficient $c/48π$, which becomes a coupling between magnetic flux and curvature similar to a Wen-Zee term. At $α=0$ the throat is exact AdS$_2\times S^2$ and the modes decouple into two Poschl-Teller problems with masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$, with levels spaced by $1/\ell$ in frequency. We give the $O(α)$ corrections in closed form. They mix the two modes and depend on frequency, and match the mouths in the overlap region. The symmetric part of the potential is positive throughout the wormhole, excluding purely exponential growth. The corrections lower the normal frequencies and split the levels shared by the two modes, with real shifts at first order in $α$. This sector has no growing mode for $l\ge2$ at this order.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Solver Agent: an Agentic AI Framework for Theoretical Physics Computations Applied to F-theory Uplifts of O3-planes and S-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35958)
*Eliott Morgensztern,Cesar Fierro Cota,Alessandro Mininno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的 Solver Agent 框架，通过持久账本记录假设、推导与计算，由中心智能体分派子任务并进行独立验证，提升计算机辅助计算的溯源性与可复现性，并将其应用于 Type IIB orientifold 的 F-theory 全局 uplift 及 S-fold 推广研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学与理论物理中的计算和证明过程复杂，传统计算机辅助计算缺乏可追溯性、可复现性与独立验证机制，因此需要一个结构化的 AI 框架来管理推导流程并校验结果。

Method: 构建 Solver Agent：以持久账本记录假设、推导和计算；中心智能体将任务分派给专门子智能体，独立智能体分别验证中间步骤和最终结果。在物理层面，研究 Type IIB orientifold 的全局 F-theory uplift 及 S-fold 推广，借助弦论不变量与环面超曲面构造进行分析。

Result: 给出射影三维簇上具有终端 Z_k 商奇点（k∈{2,3,4,6}）的 Weierstrass 模型成为具孤立 Gorenstein 终端商奇点的 Q-阶乘射影椭圆纤维化 Calabi-Yau 四维簇的充分条件；这些几何实现 O3-平面与 S-fold，其局部 D3-膜探针给出四维 N=3 超共形场论；用弦论不变量导出对 Hodge 数据和 Euler 特征的固定点贡献，并表明 Euler 修正决定 tadpole 抵消所需的局域 D3-膜荷。

Conclusion: Solver Agent 提升了计算机辅助计算的溯源性与可复现性。所提出的充分条件刻画了实现 O3-平面和 S-fold 的几何，Euler 修正与 tadpole 抵消条件相关联；在环面超曲面构造中，单个三维多胞形同时决定 Type IIB Calabi-Yau 三维簇与 F-theory 底空间，orientifold 双覆盖自然构成具有离散 Z_2 规范对称性的另一 genus-one 纤维化 uplift 的双截面。最后给出四维 N=1 紧化中非阿贝尔规范部门的环面计算与四形式通量分析方法。

Abstract: We introduce Solver Agent, an AI framework based on large language models for calculations and proofs in mathematics and theoretical physics. The solution process is tracked through a persistent ledger that records assumptions, derivations, and computations. A central agent delegates tasks to specialized sub-agents, while independent agents verify both intermediate steps and the final result. This setup improves the traceability, reproducibility, and verification of computer-assisted calculations. Applying Solver Agent, we study global F-theory uplifts of Type IIB orientifolds and their S-fold generalizations. We establish sufficient conditions for Weierstrass models over projective threefolds with terminal $\mathbb{Z}_k$ quotient singularities ($k\in\{2,3,4,6\}$) to give $\mathbb{Q}$-factorial projective elliptically fibered Calabi-Yau fourfolds with isolated Gorenstein terminal quotient singularities. These geometries realize O3-planes and S-folds, where local D3-brane probes of the latter yield four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=3$ superconformal field theories. Using stringy invariants, we derive fixed-point contributions to Hodge data and Euler characteristics, and show that these Euler corrections determine the localized D3-brane charges required for tadpole cancellation. We illustrate these results using toric hypersurface constructions, where a single three-dimensional polytope determines both the Type IIB Calabi-Yau threefold and the F-theory base; here, the orientifold double cover naturally forms a bisection of an alternative genus-one-fibered uplift with discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge symmetry. Finally, we provide methods for toric computations and four-form flux analysis in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ compactifications with non-abelian gauge sectors.

</details>


### [249] [Diagrammatic construction of GKZ systems for polygonal functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38030)
*K. B. Alkalaev,Semyon Mandrygin,Y. M. Zalishchansky*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究多边形超几何函数，为其构造GKZ超几何系统，并给出图解解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多边形超几何函数被猜想可计算任意维度的多点参数化单圈共形积分，需要建立其与GKZ超几何系统的联系以系统化研究。

Method: 给定n点多边形幂级数，构造对应的GKZ超几何系统；利用编码图解数据的transianic矩阵确定环面矩阵和关系格基，从而得到一个由n(n-3)/2个环面方程组成的有限子系统。

Result: 该有限子系统由二阶和三阶方程组成，而完整环面族还包含更高阶方程；并用第四Appell函数和Srivastava–Daoust函数等例子说明该流程。

Conclusion: transianic矩阵蕴含全部相关图解数据，可唯一确定研究多边形超几何函数所需的GKZ子系统。

Abstract: We investigate the recently introduced polygonal hypergeometric functions [arXiv:2502.12127, arXiv:2507.01904], which are conjectured to evaluate multipoint parametric one-loop conformal integrals in arbitrary dimensions. These functions can be systematically constructed using a diagrammatic algorithm that operates in terms of simple planar shapes. Given an $n$-point polygonal power series, we formulate the corresponding GKZ hypergeometric system, which admits an explicit diagrammatic interpretation. In particular, the transianic matrix, which encodes all the relevant diagrammatic data, determines the corresponding toric matrix along with a basis of the lattice of relations, thereby singling out a finite subsystem of $n(n-3)/2$ toric equations. This finite subsystem consists of second- and third-order equations, whereas the full toric family also includes higher-order ones. We illustrate the general procedure with several prominent examples of polygonal functions, including the fourth Appell and the Srivastava--Daoust functions.

</details>


### [250] [Allowable complex saddles with real charges in the gravitational index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35961)
*Fabio Billiato,Gianmarco Esposto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了渐近AdS黑洞的一种新的超对称非极端复化解（实电荷、多个复视界），并用KSW判据检验其是否为引力路径积分的可容许超对称鞍点，同时与对偶超共形指标的收敛条件作比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断这些超对称复化黑洞解能否作为引力路径积分的可容许鞍点，并将其引力约束与对偶超共形指标的收敛条件进行对比，以理解体引力条件与边界指标收敛之间的关系。

Method: 构造带实电荷和多个复视界的超对称非极端复化渐近AdS黑洞解；应用Kontsevich-Segal-Witten（KSW）判据检验其可容许性；将所得引力约束与对偶超共形指标的收敛条件进行比较。

Result: 在多数研究情形中，渐近区域的KSW判据与指标收敛条件一致；而完整的体条件更为严格，会排除那些化学势位于指标收敛域内的鞍点。

Conclusion: 渐近KSW条件与指标收敛在多数情形下相符，但全体KSW条件更严格，排除了部分化学势处于指标收敛域内的鞍点，表明体引力可容许性与边界指标收敛性并不完全等价。

Abstract: We present a new supersymmetric, non-extremal complexification of asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}$ black holes, characterized by real charges and multiple complex horizons. We investigate whether these solutions define admissible supersymmetric saddles of the gravitational path integral by applying the Kontsevich-Segal-Witten (KSW) criterion, and compare the resulting gravitational constraints with the convergence conditions of the dual superconformal index. For most of the cases studied, the KSW criterion in the asymptotic region agrees with the index convergence, while the full bulk condition is more stringent and excludes saddles whose chemical potentials lie within the index-convergence domain.

</details>


### [251] [Type IIB supergravity solutions for polarized IKKT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35963)
*Eric D'Hoker,Michael Gutperle,Christoph F. Uhlemann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在已分类的复化IIB型超引力F(4)不变局部解基础上，构造通量势与场强，聚焦IIB$^\star$实形式，得到$dS_{1,5}\times S^2\times\Sigma$解，并推导可解析延拓为欧氏IIB型超引力中正则$S^6\times S^2\times\Sigma$解的边界与正则性条件，进而构造显式整体解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（arXiv:2508.14959）已导出并分类了复化IIB型超引力中F(4)不变的局部解，但缺少相应的通量势与场强构造，也缺乏将其与全息对偶（如极化IKKT模型）联系起来的整体/正则解及电荷分析。

Method: 补全F(4)不变解的通量势与场强；转向IIB$^\star$实形式，取warped product型时空$dS_{1,5}\times S^2\times\Sigma$；系统推导边界与正则性条件，使这些解可解析延拓为欧氏IIB型超引力中的正则$S^6\times S^2\times\Sigma$解；据此构造显式整体解。

Result: 给出了复化IIB型超引力F(4)不变解的通量势与场强；获得$dS_{1,5}\times S^2\times\Sigma$解及其解析延拓到$S^6\times S^2\times\Sigma$的条件；构造了显式整体解，包括极化IKKT模型全息描述的显式实现，并澄清其携带的电荷，还得到同一正则类中具有不同膜电荷的新解。

Conclusion: 该工作将F(4)不变复化解的研究推进到通量构造与实形式/欧氏整体解层面，为极化IKKT模型的全息对偶提供具体几何实现，并给出具有不同膜电荷的新解。

Abstract: The general local solutions to complexified Type IIB supergravity which are invariant under the complex Lie superalgebra $F(4)$, and which were derived and classified in arXiv:2508.14959, are supplemented here by the construction of their flux potentials and field strengths. We then focus on the Type IIB$^\star$ real form of complex Type IIB supergravity, where we obtain solutions with spacetime metric of the warped product form $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\timesΣ$. We systematically derive boundary and regularity conditions under which the $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\timesΣ$ solutions can be analytically continued to regular $S^6\times S^2\timesΣ$ solutions in Euclidean Type IIB supergravity. Using these results, we construct explicit global solutions. These solutions include explicit realizations of recent proposals for a holographic description of the polarized IKKT model, and we clarify the charges carried by these solutions. We also obtain novel solutions in the same regularity class with different brane charges.

</details>


### [252] [Helicity-Resolved Constructibility from Unified Ward Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35976)
*Jaehoon Jeong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究大$z$极限下大质量高自旋粒子的行为，证明其标度由螺旋度大小而非总自旋控制，并给出螺旋度分辨的可构造性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大质量高自旋粒子在全线横向移动（all-line transverse shifts）下的大$z$行为，澄清大质量高自旋振幅可构造性的适用条件，特别是非极端高自旋波函数出现的额外$z$增长问题。

Method: 将四维大质量动量及其质量嵌入辅助的零五维矢量，用其第五流分量刻画无质量Ward恒等式的破缺与恢复；借助在每个矢量指标上独立作用的统一Ward恒等式消除额外的$z$增长。

Result: 证明固定螺旋度的大$z$标度由螺旋度大小决定；在剥离后的运动学张量于无质量极限下保持有限的条件下，增加总自旋本身不会恶化可构造性，且五点及以上非负耦合量纲的振幅对所有螺旋度构型均可构造。

Conclusion: 大质量高自旋粒子的可构造性由螺旋度而非总自旋决定，所提出的螺旋度分辨判据适用于任意自旋，拓展了振幅可构造性分析至大质量高自旋情形。

Abstract: We show that the large-$z$ behavior of massive higher-spin particles under all-line transverse shifts is controlled by the helicity magnitude rather than the total spin. The apparent additional $z$ growth of nonextremal higher-spin wavefunctions is removed by unified Ward identities acting independently on each vector index. To expose this structure, we embed a four-dimensional massive momentum and its mass into an auxiliary null five-vector, whose fifth-current components characterize the breaking and restoration of the massless Ward identities. These identities determine the fixed-helicity large-$z$ scaling and yield a helicity-resolved constructibility criterion valid for arbitrary spin. Provided the stripped kinematic tensor remains finite in the massless limit, increasing the total spin does not by itself worsen constructibility, and amplitudes with nonnegative coupling dimension are constructible for all helicity configurations at five points and beyond.

</details>


### [253] [Control Problem of de Sitter Vacua with T-brane Uplift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35985)
*Daniel Junghans*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出T-brane de Sitter隆起作用在弦论中面临严重的控制问题：弦框架体积被Calabi-Yau定向轨形拓扑所限，导致大量模型无法自洽实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-brane被认为可在IIB弦论LARGE-volume scenario框架下提供一致的de Sitter真空提升，作者希望检验该构造是否真正可控、自洽。

Method: 通过推导弦框架体积的拓扑上界，结合h^{1,1}=2和h^{1,1}=4的多个模型分析，并研究约600个由gaugino condensation生成非微扰超势的模型，考察warping修正、tadpole、弦耦合与通量超势微调以及brane moduli稳定化。

Result: 发现体积上界常为O(1)或更小，直接排除许多h^{1,1}=2模型的受控de Sitter真空；h^{1,1}>2也给出类似证据及h^{1,1}=4的显式例子；在大体积gaugino condensation模型中，de Sitter解虽表面可行，但受不可控warping修正影响，其体积压低被大电荷等因素抵消；最小warping修正模型还伴随极小tadpole和需微调的弦耦合与通量超势。

Conclusion: 多种独立问题表明T-brane隆起作用难以在弦论中自洽实现，加上brane moduli稳定化的额外挑战，该场景很可能不可行。

Abstract: T-branes have been argued to provide a consistent uplift to de Sitter vacua in the context of the LARGE-volume scenario of type IIB string theory. We point out that this construction suffers from a serious control problem: the string-frame volume is bounded from above by a number that depends only on the topology of the Calabi-Yau orientifold and is often $\mathcal{O}(1)$ or smaller. This observation immediately rules out controlled de Sitter vacua in many models with $h^{1,1}=2$ if the non-perturbative superpotential is generated by D3-brane instantons. We give evidence that similar conclusions apply for $h^{1,1}>2$ and show this explicitly for two models with $h^{1,1}=4$. Our volume bound is seemingly less severe in models where the non-perturbative superpotential is generated by gaugino condensation. However, we study $\mathcal{O}(600)$ such models and find that, although de Sitter solutions with large volumes are naively possible there, they suffer from uncontrolled warping corrections whose volume suppression is cancelled by other factors, in particular large charges. We also observe that the models with the smallest warping corrections within our set have very small tadpoles and at the same time require a fine-tuned string coupling and flux superpotential, which may indicate an additional problem with the axio-dilaton/complex-structure-moduli stabilization. Finally, we point out an independent challenge related to the stabilization of the brane moduli. Our various results lead us to conclude that it is unlikely that the scenario of T-brane uplifting can be realized in string theory in a self-consistent manner.

</details>


### [254] [D-branes and orientifolds on quasicrystalline orbifold backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35997)
*Antony Wendels*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造并分析了六维type IIB Z12准晶体orbifold的orientifold后代，确定了闭弦谱、扭曲扇区轨道结构，构造相容的无向投影，计算Klein瓶、环面和Möbius带振幅，并研究tadpole抵消、反常约束及多个后代背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶体闭弦背景此前研究较少，其是否能容纳受控的微扰开弦完备化（含边界与crosscap扇区）尚不清楚，本文旨在探索这一问题。

Method: 以六维type IIB Z12准晶体orbifold为母背景（由在几何环面上非晶体学的Narain格自同构定义），计算闭弦谱与扭曲扇区轨道结构，构造无向投影并计算Klein瓶、环面、Möbius带振幅，通过tadpole抵消选择边界轨道，并分析其部分T对偶的D7膜描述。

Result: Tadpole抵消选出可作为成角D7膜部分T对偶描述的边界轨道，确定Chan-Paton规范群与无质量谱；所得超对称六维真空满足不可约反常约束，可约反常多项式经Green-Schwarz机制分解，反常格满足Kim-Shiu-Vafa弦探针界；另构造了具有微扰无快子刚性分支的Brane Supersymmetry Breaking后代，以及五维自由作用orientifold，其两个解紧化极限分别重现几何type IIA Z3 orientifold与反对称type IIB Z4 orientifold。

Conclusion: 准晶体闭弦背景可以容纳受控的微扰开弦完备化，并具有非平凡的边界与crosscap扇区。

Abstract: We construct and analyse orientifold descendants of a six-dimensional type IIB $\mathbb Z_{12}$ quasicrystalline orbifold. The parent background is defined by a Narain-lattice automorphism that is not crystallographic on the geometric torus. We determine the closed-string spectrum and twisted-sector orbit structure, construct a compatible unoriented projection, and compute the Klein bottle, annulus and Möbius strip amplitudes. Tadpole cancellation selects boundary orbits that admit a partially T-dual description as D7-branes at angles and fixes the Chan-Paton gauge group and massless spectrum. The resulting supersymmetric six-dimensional vacuum satisfies the irreducible anomaly constraints, its reducible anomaly polynomial factorizes through the Green-Schwarz mechanism, and its anomaly lattice obeys the Kim-Shiu-Vafa string-probe bound. We also construct a Brane Supersymmetry Breaking descendant with a perturbatively tachyon-free rigid branch, and a freely acting five-dimensional orientifold whose two decompactification limits reproduce a geometric type IIA $\mathbb Z_3$ orientifold and an antisymmetric type IIB $\mathbb Z_4$ orientifold. These examples show that quasicrystalline closed-string backgrounds can admit controlled perturbative open-string completions with non-trivial boundary and crosscap sectors.

</details>


### [255] [Signatures of Bulk Topology from the 't Hooft Worldsheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36015)
*Jackson R. Fliss,Alexander Frenkel*

Main category: hep-th
